# 五术联合 · 事业

同一个问题,几门各自怎么看,按主题并排。每条一行,原文在各门自己的主题文件里。

One question, each art side by side, by topic. One line per rule; the source text is in each art's own topic file.

已收:六爻。还没收:梅花易数、大六壬、奇门遁甲、紫微斗数。

## 总则(Principles: which line is the post, the document, the patron; strong and weak)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/总则.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.01** 求官的卦里不能没有官鬼：没有官鬼，功名难成，卦里纵然有贵人，也只是有贵人而没有位子。官鬼这一爻宜安静不宜发动：带着吉神动是福，加上凶煞动是祸。
  A divination for office must not lack the Pressure line: without it name and rank are hard to achieve, and even with a Noble in the hexagram one is a noble without a post. The Pressure line should be still and not moving: moving with an auspicious spirit it is a blessing, moving with a baleful one it is a calamity.
- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.06** 官鬼是克我的爻，凶的地方多、福的地方少，怕子孙、靠妻财，动起来克兄弟、生扶父母；卦里虽然凶，却不能没有它，宜静不宜动。父母是生我的爻，遇到财爻就伤到自己的根本，碰到官鬼就增添光辉。
  The Pressure line is the line that controls me: it is more often ill than a blessing; it fears Output and relies on Wealth; moving, it harms Peer and gives life and support to Resource; though it is ill in a hexagram it must not be missing, and is best still and not moving. The Resource line is the line that gives me life: meeting Wealth it harms its own substance, meeting Pressure it gains in lustre.
- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.07** 父母帮着官鬼，子孙帮着妻财；私事用妻财做主，公事用官鬼为尊，官鬼最怕乱动、最适宜安静。要打官司、求功名，必须凭官鬼。
  Resource assists Pressure, and Output assists Wealth. For private matters Wealth is the chief; for public matters Pressure is the honoured one, which above all fears moving about and is best still. To start a suit or to seek fame one must depend on Pressure.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.26** 道士、僧人来问占：身安稳、世安静最要紧；身爻克世，可以去谋划；世、应比和，自然安乐；世去克身，应当退守；财来克世，适合去求缘；六爻不动是清福；有一位交重发动，事情就有变迁。
  For Taoist priests and monks asking by divination: the Body at ease and the Self still comes first; the Body line controlling the Self, one may plan and act; Self and Other in accord, ease comes of itself; the Self controlling the Body, one should withdraw and hold; Wealth coming to control the Self, it is good to seek alms; the six lines still is pure blessing; one line moving means change in the matter.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.31** 断卦一开始先看天喜，其次看贵人所在，三合则百事都吉，禄、马最好。
  In judging a hexagram first look at the Joy star, next at where the Noble is; three-harmony makes all things auspicious, and the Emolument and the Horse are the best.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.32** 测官，父母爻兴旺，做官能做到侯王；父母爻又是印，印旺主大贵。
  In judging office, with the Resource line flourishing one holds office up to marquis and prince; Resource is also the seal, and a strong seal means great honour.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.79** 占推荐，以官爻、文书爻为主；占旌奖（表彰奖励），以财爻、官鬼为凭据。官吏贤能，盼望上司宪台的荐举嘉奖；百姓善良，依靠县令的旌表褒扬。所喜欢的是官鬼旺相、青龙兴旺；所忌讳的是世爻落空、子孙发动。凡是占嘉奖、荐举，都要官爻旺相、青龙得位，最忌世爻落在空亡、子孙发动。
  For a recommendation, take the Pressure line and the document line as the chief; for a commendation or award, take Wealth and Pressure as the ground. Officials who are worthy and able look to the supervising authorities for recommendation and award; common people who are good depend on the county magistrate for commendation and praise. What is welcomed is Pressure thriving and the Dragon flourishing; what is feared is the Self Void and Output moving. In every divination about award or recommendation, the Pressure line should be thriving and the Azure Dragon in its place, and what is most feared is the Self falling on Void and Output moving.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.88** 往上察看官府的喜怒，全由四象的兴衰决定；往下推究人事的亲疏，不出两仪的变化。
  Observing above the pleasure or anger of the officials all depends on the rise and fall of the four images; reckoning below the closeness or distance of human affairs does not go beyond the changes of the two modes.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.103** 谋求成事，以世爻、应爻为主，以内卦、外卦为凭据。要成事，必须查世、应：世是本主（自己），应是他人；空、冲、破、绝临到世爻，凡是去谋求，就不要动手；冲、破、绝、空落在应位，对方的心思不合，白白叹息。凡是成事办事、一切谋求，都取世、应为主：不论世还是应，如果落在绝地，或者犯旬空、月破、日冲，都难成就。其次把内卦当作谋事的人，不适宜空、破；如果旺相，一定亨通；再得到内克外卦，或者内外相生以及比和，都是美好的。
  In scheming to accomplish an affair, take the Self and the Other as the chief and the inner and outer trigrams as the ground. To accomplish an affair one must examine the Self and the Other: the Self is the principal and the Other is the other party; if Void, clash, break or extinction sits on the Self, do not set about scheming; if clash, break, extinction or Void is in the Other's place, the other party's mind does not agree and one sighs in vain. In every business and every scheme, take the Self and the Other as the chief: if either the Self or the Other falls on a place of extinction, or runs into the ten-day Void, Month-break or the Day's clash, it is hard to accomplish. Next, take the inner trigram as the one who schemes the affair, which should not be Void or broken; if it meets thriving it surely means success; if in addition the inner controls the outer trigram, or the inner and outer generate each other or are in accord, all is fine.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.137** 各种重大的托付，只凭一卦的细细审详；百里之外倾心相托、千金的重托，全凭一句承诺的寄托，都系于六爻的断定。
  For every kind of weighty entrusting, one relies only on the careful examination of one hexagram; a heart given from a hundred li away and a thousand pieces of gold entrusted, depending wholly on one man's promise, all hang on the verdict of the six lines.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.02** 官鬼爻被冲克，就是（占）求官，还有诉讼的事暗中相关，又可能是走失、盗贼，不要把爻里的情形当成一个例子来看。
  The Pressure line clashed and controlled means the seeking of office, and a lawsuit is hiddenly connected, or perhaps something strayed or lost and thieves; do not read what is in the line as a single case.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.06** 拜谒人，要问拜谒的是谁；世、应作鬼，白费心思；用爻出现却不乘旺，去见他必定不在家。世、应坐着官鬼，双方互相猜疑；如果用爻衰弱了，我还能凭靠什么呢？
  In calling on someone, ask whom one is calling on; with the Self or the Other as Pressure one wearies the mind in vain; the Subject line appearing but not riding strength, going to see him, he is surely not at home. With the Self or the Other sitting on Pressure, each suspects the other; with the Subject line weak, on what could I rely?
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.01** 占做官，妻财就是俸禄。还没做官而求功名的人，不要财爻；已经做官的人，要见财爻，因为有爵位就有俸禄，没有没俸禄就能做官的。妻财旺相，俸禄一定多；休囚就微薄；卦里没有财或财落空亡，还没有俸禄；财动遇冲，任职以后会因事减俸；日辰月建冲财、又刑害世爻和官爻，恐怕有停发俸禄、罢职的祸患。
  In divining an office, Wealth is the salary. For one not yet in office who is seeking a name, Wealth is not wanted; for one already in office, Wealth is wanted, since where there is rank there is a stipend, and no one gets an office with no pay. When Wealth is thriving the salary is surely large; resting or trapped, it is meagre; if there is no Wealth in the hexagram or it falls Void, no salary has yet been received; if Wealth moves and meets a clash, the salary is cut after taking up the post, on account of some matter; and if the Day or the Month clashes Wealth while also punishing or harming the Self and Pressure, there may be the calamity of salary being stopped and dismissal from office.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.02** 官鬼代表官职和爵位：旺相有气，官高爵大；休囚死绝，官小职卑；发动去生合世爻，或者得到月建、日辰的生扶，一定有升迁提拔，名声远播京城；安静不动而被月建、日辰克害，或者被世爻刑冲克害，一定没有声名威望。
  Pressure stands for office and rank: thriving and with breath, the office is high and the rank great; resting, trapped, dead or in extinction, the office is small and the post low; moving to generate or combine with the Self, or generated and supported by the Month or the Day, there will surely be promotion and the name spreads far to the capital; still, and controlled or harmed by the Month or the Day, or punished, clashed, controlled or harmed by the Self, there will surely be no fame or standing.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.28** 占仕宦，要按所任的职位和职分来推断，才能看出仕途的顺逆。这是仕宦章的结语：前面各联是按官职的种类分的（风宪、牧民、将帅、出巡、谏官、僧道医官），用的时候先判断问的是哪一类职位，再取相应的一联。
  In divining official posts, one must infer according to the post held and its duties, and then the smoothness or blockage of an official career can be known. This is the closing line of the chapter on holding office: the couplets before it are divided by the kind of office (censorate, shepherding the people, commander, touring, remonstrance, monk, Daoist and physician officials), and in use one first judges what kind of post is being asked about and then takes the corresponding couplet.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.29** 占书馆（去当塾师），以世爻代表西席，也就是教书的先生。世爻临父母，这位先生一定是通晓经书的人；世在乾、坤、离三宫也是这样；世临官爻又带贵人，或者本宫的官鬼伏在世爻下面，多半是秀才。
  In divining a school post, the Self stands for the tutor's seat, that is, the teacher. If the Self rests on Resource, the teacher is surely versed in the classics, and so too if the Self is in the Qian, Kun or Li palace; if it rests on Pressure with a noble, or the home palace's Pressure lies hidden under the Self, he is mostly a licentiate.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.01** 求官，官鬼爻是用爻，宜旺不宜衰，喜欢日辰扶持拱卫，怕刑、害、克、冲；宣敕（任命的诏书）看父母爻，卦中不可没有，最好旺相、扶助世爻、不加凶杀；若被刑冲，根基不正；加临大耗，要多花钱财。太岁、月建旺的，多半是「宣」；贵人、禄、马同在一处的，不过是「敕」；虽有吉神却散漫不当权的，无非是吏员的文书。冲世凶，生世吉；只要不落空亡，再辛苦费力，终归能成。
  In seeking office the Pressure line is the Subject line and should be strong, not weak; it likes the Day to support and gather round it and fears punishment, harm, control and clash. The proclamation and decree are read from Resource, which cannot be missing from the hexagram and is best strong, supporting the Self and with no baleful spirit added; if it is punished or clashed the footing is not sound, and if Great Loss is added much money will be spent. If Tai Sui and the Month are strong it is mostly a 'proclamation'; if the Noble, the Emolument and the Horse share one place it is no more than a 'decree'; if there are auspicious spirits but they are scattered and without authority it is no more than a clerk's papers. A clash on the Self is bad and a generating of the Self is good; so long as it does not fall Void, however hard and wearing the labour, it will in the end be accomplished.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.10** 听先贤说过：以正道得国、以德行守国的，国运常久；以武力得国、以暴虐守国的，不能长久。这是天命注定的，哪里还要等占筮？而且天所授的学问是仁与智，人所授的学问是象数，怎么能用人教的学问去测度天命的精微？这里只是转述旧时的见闻，以备昭示之用，占的人要谨慎。
  I have heard the worthies of old say: those who gain the state by what is right and keep it by virtue endure; those who gain the state by force and keep it by violence do not endure. It is decreed by Heaven, so why wait for the yarrow stalks? Moreover, what Heaven teaches is benevolence and wisdom, and what men teach is images and numbers; how can one use what men have taught to fathom the subtlety of Heaven's decree? I merely relate what was heard of old, to be ready for clear manifestation; let the diviner take care.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.28** 常说决策在人，成谋在天。蓍草所顺从的，是鬼神的情意：喜欢直而厌恶曲，辅助仁者而讨伐暴者。占的人要以此为鉴。
  It is often said that decisions rest with men and the success of schemes with Heaven. What the stalks follow is the feeling of the spirits: they like the straight and dislike the crooked, they aid the benevolent and strike down the violent. Let the diviner take this as a mirror.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.40** 已经在朝廷食禄的人占仕途，都以官为用神：喜它在日、月上，人们仰仗它的照临；喜它旺相，像万物沾到及时雨；喜它逢生、扶、拱、合，如同倚着长城奔驰在康庄大道上；忌它逢破、空、散、绝——空就失职，破就败事，绝就解任，散就削籍。子孙动，要防公议，按衰旺定轻重；兄弟发，要减俸粮，按喜忌说吉凶。官临长生，知道宦途远大通达；逢帝旺、临官，知道仕路有升迁；逢衰、墓、死、绝，知道官道不通；进神就升爵升阶，退神就降禄降级。变墓绝而动爻得生扶、被日月克而动爻变生，都是安稳之象。泄气的爻动而元神静，升迁的消息杳无；元神发动、用象摇动，调迁的音信就到了。
  Having already eaten the Son of Heaven's salary, in divining the official career one takes Pressure as the Subject throughout: it is good when it sits on the Day or the Month, for people look up to its shining down; good when it is thriving or strong, like things receiving the timely rain; good when it meets generation, support, enclosing and combination, like leaning on a great wall while racing along a broad highway; bad when it meets broken, Void, scattered or extinct: Void loses office, broken ruins affairs, extinct dissolves the post, scattered strikes the name from the rolls. Output moving, guard against public criticism, and tell heavy from light by decline and thriving; Peer issuing cuts the salary grain, and good or ill is told by what is welcome and what is feared. Pressure at Birth, one knows the official road will go far and clear; at Peak or at Taking Office, one knows there will be promotion; at decline, tomb, death or extinction, one knows the official way is blocked; Advancing means rank and grade go up, Retreating means salary and grade go down. Changing into tomb or extinction while the moving line is generated and supported, or being controlled by the Day and Month while the moving line changes into generation, are all signs of safety. If the line that drains it moves while its Support is still, news of promotion is faint; if the Support issues and the image of the Subject stirs, word of a transfer arrives.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.62** 太医、僧纲（僧官）、道纪（道官），所喜的只是官旺、财备；所戒的是鬼克世、世空，以及随墓助伤。凡是占杂职，都和这个相同。
  For court physicians, Buddhist registrars and Daoist registrars, what is welcome is only thriving Pressure and ready Wealth; what is to be guarded against is Pressure controlling the Self, the Self Void, and following into the tomb and helping the injury. All divinations about miscellaneous posts are the same as this.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.64** 古代治国的人，没有比识人更难的：认得清人又用得对，各种事情才办得成，何况是经营家业、谋生的人呢？任用仆人以财爻为用，任用一般的人以应爻为尊；三冲，很少能有始有终；应克世，心里怀着对我不利的念头。
  Of those who governed a state in antiquity, nothing was harder than knowing men: if one knows men and employs them well, all affairs are carried through, and how much more for one who runs a household and makes a living? To employ a servant the Wealth line is the Subject, and to employ anyone else the Other line is honoured; with the three clashes it is rare to see a thing through to the end, and with the Other controlling the Self he bears in his heart what is not to my advantage.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.84** 大抵当役而求利的，喜财、官；当役而求免害的，厌鬼旺，以及官动、官克世。避役的喜欢失去鬼，谋役、脱役的却不喜欢失去鬼。用神不要死守一个爻。谋役、脱役，没有鬼就难以做主。
  Broadly, for one who serves a duty to seek profit, Wealth and Pressure are welcome; for one who serves a duty and seeks to escape harm, Pressure thriving is hated, and so are Pressure moving and Pressure controlling the Self. In avoiding a duty it is welcome for Pressure to be lost; in seeking a duty or being freed from one it is not welcome for Pressure to be lost. The Subject is not held to one line. In seeking a duty or being freed from one, without Pressure it is hard to take charge.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.88** 凡是谋事要「来」的（要得到什么），办法是谋望，再同时求用神；谋事要「去」的（要摆脱什么），办法是谋望而不求用神。求于自己的看世，求于别人的看应；有交接的忌冲，没有交接的不忌冲；只有官鬼是谋事的主，都不可失去。
  For a scheme in which something is to 'come' (something is to be gained), the method is the scheme and hope, and also seeking the Subject; for a scheme in which something is to 'go' (something is to be shaken off), the method is the scheme and hope without seeking the Subject. What is sought from oneself is seen by the Self, what is sought from others by the Other; where there is a meeting, clash is feared, and where there is no meeting, clash is not feared; only Pressure is the master of the scheme, and neither may be lost.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.90** 孔孟之道，以五伦为体、以经世为用；困与亨、夷与险，各有各的尽道之法，何必占筮。如果是弃家入道、超拔流俗、修行求真，爱欲的网难除，得道的人难遇，这自然是大丈夫的事，哪里同于世俗人的占筮？然而道有道缘，业有业障，也常常靠占筮得知。割断爱欲、离开尘世，一念之间就是万年，哪里用得着占筮？然而所处的安危、择师的邪正，也仍要靠圣贤告诉我。本章分七问：择师、传法、主持、纲纪、募缘、法会、斋戒。
  The Way of Confucius and Mencius takes the five human relations as its body and ordering the world as its use; in hardship and success, ease and danger, each has its own way of exhausting what is right, and why should one divine? But for one who leaves home to enter the Way, rises above the common run and practises to find the truth, the net of love is hard to cut, and a perfected man is hard to meet; this is of course the affair of a great man, and how could it be the same as the divining of the ordinary world? Yet the Way has its affinity for the Way, and the vocation has its karmic obstruction, and these too can often be learned by divining. Cutting off love and leaving the dust, one thought is ten thousand years, and what use is divining? Yet the safety or danger of where one lives, and whether the teacher one chooses is straight or crooked, one still relies on the sages to tell me. The chapter takes seven questions: choosing a teacher, transmitting the teaching, being abbot, being in the administration, soliciting donations, ritual assemblies and fasting.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.91** 什么叫道缘？福神就是。什么叫业缘？系爻就是。所以子孙持世，道就成；官鬼交重，障就重。而世不可有病：病在破，多病多灾，修行落入傍门；病在散、绝，自暴自弃，半路上心里懒散；随墓、助伤，则身陷泥犁（地狱）之地；世被生、世旺，则心里打开日月的光明。这是它的大象。专忌鬼动，以及世爻破、散、绝，这是三戒；世动也主不安宁。
  What is called the affinity for the Way? The fortune spirit, Output, is it. What is called karmic affinity? The binding line is it. So with Output on the Self the Way is accomplished; with Pressure moving and doubled the obstruction is heavy. And the Self must not be sick: sick by being broken, there are many illnesses and calamities and the practice falls into side paths; sick by being scattered or extinct, one gives oneself up and the heart goes slack halfway; following into the tomb and helping the injury, the body sinks into the pit of hell; with the Self generated and thriving, the heart opens to the light of sun and moon. This is its great image. Moving Pressure is above all feared, and the Self line broken, scattered or extinct, are the three warnings; the Self moving also means unrest.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.97** 占持斋戒（守斋持戒），专取用神；自己占的以世为本。用旺、鬼静，就始终不变；三冲、用坏，就半路改辙。如果占开斋破戒，不会有吉占；世坏、鬼动尤其凶，只能戒（不要做）罢了。
  In divining about keeping the fast and the precepts, one attends only to the Subject; in divining for oneself the Self is the base. With the Subject thriving and Pressure still, one does not change from beginning to end; with the three clashes and the Subject damaged, one changes course halfway. If one divines about breaking the fast and the precepts, there will be no auspicious divination; a damaged Self and Pressure moving are especially ominous, and all one can do is refrain.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.101** 只有得到复、咸、艮、无妄而安宁的，是修行的吉占。孔孟之学几乎成了功名的捷径，自姚江（王阳明）以后，道风渺茫；如果有豪杰之士起来继承绝学、阐明圣道，也用这一章来占。复、咸、艮、无妄这几个卦，专门解释心学；大抵儒释道三教必以正心为根，占修行得到这几个卦的，不拘福神、鬼、冲合，直接说吉；但安静时才如此，变动了就不是了。圣学没有『求道』一说，所以道业只详述佛道二家；三教之道相同，占法也相同。
  Only getting Fu, Xian, Gen or Wuwang and being at peace is an auspicious divination for practice. The learning of Confucius and Mencius has almost become a short cut to fame and office, and since Yaojiang (Wang Yangming) the manner of the Way has grown faint; if there are heroic men who rise up to continue the lost learning and make clear the sages' Way, they too divine by this chapter. The hexagrams Fu, Xian, Gen and Wuwang are devoted to the learning of the heart; in general the three teachings all take rectifying the heart as the root, and one who divines about practice and gets these hexagrams, whatever the fortune spirit, Pressure, clash and combination, is simply told that it is auspicious; but this holds only when still, and if moving it is not so again. The sages' learning has no 'seeking the Way', so the monastic vocation treats only the two schools in detail; the three teachings are the same in the Way and the same in divining.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.102** 有人说阳是官、阴是鬼；又说旺是官、衰是鬼；又说官鬼是「系」爻、是「制」爻。大凡喜欢它的叫官，厌恶它的叫鬼；用在阳的一面就是官，用在阴的一面就是鬼：名称不同而用法相同。卦里不该没有它，也不宜发动。官鬼不必按旺衰、阴阳来分：只要取它来用就是官，忌它就是鬼；然而官也可能凶，鬼也可能吉。
  Some say that yang is the official and yin is the demon; some say that thriving is the official and weak is the demon; some say that Pressure is the 'binding' line and the 'controlling' line. In general what one welcomes is called the official and what one dislikes is called the demon; used on the yang side it is the official, used on the yin side it is the demon: the names differ but the use is the same. The hexagram should not lack it, and it should not move. Pressure need not be told apart by strength and weakness or by yin and yang: if it is taken for use it is the official, and if it is feared it is the demon; yet the official may be ominous, and the demon may be auspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.103** 所以官鬼是事情的主，也是祸的起头；出入行藏，贵在静而不动；兴词、构难（打官司、制造灾难），怕它一动就成。大凡防备伤害到自身的，喜欢它没有鬼；求事来对自己有益的，利在有官。要请学的人自己分辨。
  So Pressure is the master of a matter and also the beginning of calamity; in going out and in, going abroad and hiding, it is valued still and not moving; in bringing a suit or contriving a difficulty, one fears that once it moves the thing is done. In general, in guarding against harm to oneself, it is welcome for there to be no Pressure; in seeking a matter to one's own advantage, the benefit lies in having the official. Let the student distinguish them.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.01** 官禄占分两种情形：还没做官的，占将做什么官、哪个月选官、哪天领到凭证、任所在哪个方向；已经做官的，占赴任的吉凶、在任上的顺逆、哪天升迁任命、什么时候被人替代。至于做官是贫还是廉，以及失去职位、补缺任职、遭父母之丧、起复，是官禄占附带的占问。
  The divination of office and salary has two cases. For one not yet in office: what office he will take, in which month he will be selected, on what day he will receive his credentials, and in which direction the post lies. For one already in office: the good or ill of his journey to the post, how his term goes, on what day he will be promoted or appointed, and when he will be replaced. Whether an official is poor or incorruptible, and losing a post, being given a post to fill, mourning for a parent and being recalled to office, are subsidiary questions of this divination.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.82** 谋望占总述：想做一件事又不敢马上去做，就有了谋；想得到又不能一定得到，就有了望。所以先占它行不行，既然行了，再占它成不成，既然成了，再占它是快是慢，大致就是这些。分爻是：国事、官事、人事、家事、身事、心事。
  Overview of divining a scheme. When one wants to do a thing and does not dare to do it at once, there is a scheme; when one wants to get a thing and cannot be sure of getting it, there is a hope. So first divine whether it is feasible; once feasible, whether it will succeed; once successful, whether it will be slow or fast; that is the general outline. The division of the lines: affairs of state, official affairs, affairs of others, family affairs, personal affairs and affairs of the heart.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.01** 占升选补缺，官星要旺相、持世，妻财要来生身：旺相的官星持世，或者日月、动爻作官星来生合世爻，或者世爻发动变出官星来生世，都是吉兆；妻财发动生官星，能得到好缺；官星临在日月上，必定越级升迁。
  In a question about promotion or appointment the star of office should be thriving and hold the Self, and Wealth should generate the Body: a thriving star of office holding the Self, or the Day, the Month or a moving line acting as the star of office to generate or combine with the Self, or the Self moving and turning into the star of office to generate the Self, are all auspicious; Wealth moving to generate the star of office wins a fine post; the star of office on the Day or Month means promotion beyond one's rank.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.07** 看升选不能只看卦名：古人以晋、升两卦为吉，屯、蹇两卦为凶；野鹤说晋、升两卦确实屡次应验，但也要世爻得地，世爻失陷也不吉；屯、蹇两卦，只要世爻旺相就没有妨碍，他有一次世爻旺相占到屯卦，照样越级升迁。
  Promotion cannot be read from the hexagram name alone: the ancients took Jin and Sheng as auspicious and Zhun and Jian as ominous; the Old Man of Yehe says Jin and Sheng have indeed proved right many times, but the Self must also be well placed, and a Self that is lost or sunk is not auspicious; for Zhun and Jian there is no harm so long as the Self is thriving, and once he got Zhun with a thriving Self and was promoted beyond rank all the same.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.10** 道士、僧人来问卜：身安稳、世安静最要紧；身爻克世，可以信用；世、应比和，自然安乐；世去克身，当退守；财来克世，好求缘；六爻不动是清福，一位交重，事就变迁；财扶助世，能够施舍行利；官鬼交动，偏好去谒见官员；世临白虎，官司纠缠扰乱；身遇勾陈，心事牵缠；父母克身，有师父接引；青龙持世，有贵人周全；勾陈带杀，多有魔障；官鬼临在财上，被贼人冤害；兄弟和勾陈同位发动，谨防法眷在外勾连；贵人和父母交动，师父通达圣道；青龙并上子孙，徒弟贤能；父母重重、身受克，断定是被俗家牵累；财空，注解经疏徒劳力；鬼旺，谋求营运白费钱。
  For Taoist priests and monks asking by divination: the Body at ease and the Self still comes first; the Body line controlling the Self, one may be trusted and used; Self and Other in accord, ease comes of itself; the Self controlling the Body, one should withdraw and hold; Wealth coming to control the Self is good for seeking alms; the six lines still is pure blessing, one line moving means change; with Wealth supporting the Self one can dispense benefit; Pressure moving is especially good for calling on officials; the Self on the White Tiger, a lawsuit disturbs one; the Body meeting Hook Earth, worries entangle the heart; Resource controlling the Body, a master receives and leads one on; the Azure Dragon holding the Self, a patron sees one through; Hook Earth carrying a baleful spirit, there are many demonic obstructions; Pressure on Wealth, one is wronged by thieves; Peer and Hook Earth moving together in the same place, beware of Dharma kin colluding with outsiders; the Noble and Resource moving together, the master is versed in the sacred; the Dragon joined to Output, the disciples are worthy; Resource upon Resource and the Body controlled, it is certain one is dragged back by one's lay family; Wealth void, annotating the scriptures is wasted labour; Pressure strong, scheming is money wasted.

## 成否(Will it come: seeking a post, a commission, a recommendation; easy or hard)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/成否.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.02** 求官时遇到父母爻绝处逢生（它落在日辰的绝地，又得动爻来生）：文书上虽然有阻滞卡节，终究有贵人替你做主，事情必成。
  In divining for office, if the Resource line meets life in the place of extinction (it falls where the Day extinguishes it but a moving line gives it life): though the documents meet delays and hold-ups, a noble will in the end take charge and the matter will surely succeed.
- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.03** 合处逢冲（卦本来相合，却被日辰冲了）：求官时官鬼、文书被暗中冲着，事里还有反复和变动；谋事遇到这样，一定因为别人而受阻，眼看要成又有变。
  A clash at a place of combination (a hexagram of combination that is clashed by the Day): in divining for office, if the Pressure line and the documents are clashed in secret, the matter still has reversals and changes; in planning a matter, meeting this means being held up by someone, and just as it is about to succeed there is a change.
- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.08** 求官时，财爻、官鬼如果得到生扶，谋划的事没有阻滞；卦身和世爻没有救助，做的事一定彼此背离。用官鬼做用神的，忌子孙持世；子孙兴旺发动，求官、应举都是白费心。
  In seeking office, if Wealth and Pressure are given life and support, plans meet no obstruction; if the Body and the Self have no rescue or help, what one does will surely run to discord. When Pressure is the Subject, Output holding the Self is feared; if Output flourishes and moves, seeking office or taking the examinations is a waste of effort.
- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.12** 父母爻兴旺，做官能做到侯王；旧注说，印（父母）旺，主有大贵。
  If the Resource line flourishes, one who holds office rises to marquis or prince; the old note says that when the Seal (Resource) is strong, great nobility is indicated.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.06** 申、酉、辰、戌的爻持世是吉；做官又遇上禄，正好施展；其中申、酉持世又得禄，最吉。
  The branches Shen, You, Chen and Xu holding the Self are auspicious; taking office and meeting the Emolument gives room to act; Shen or You holding the Self and getting the Emolument is the most auspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.07** 官鬼落在卦身或世爻上、卦里各爻旺相，才是真的得官，可喜；驿马落空亡，白费力气；卦遇胎、没，白白劳神。
  Pressure standing on the Body or the Self, with the lines of the hexagram thriving, is the true sign of getting the office and is cause for joy; the Post Horse Void means effort spent for nothing; a hexagram meeting Embryo or Sunk means wearying the mind in vain.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.13** 不论文官武职，求官都要看唐符、国印。按年支取：寅、申年，巳是唐符、亥是国印（旧注）；巳、亥年取寅、申；子、午年取卯、酉；卯、酉年取子、午；辰、戌年取丑、未；丑、未年取辰、戌。旧注说「以下类推」，这里按此读成每一对里前一支是唐符、后一支是国印。
  Whether it is a civil or a military post, in seeking office one must look to Tangfu and the State Seal. By the year branch: in the years Yin and Shen, Si is the Tangfu and Hai the State Seal (the note); in Si and Hai years take Yin and Shen; in Zi and Wu years, Mao and You; in Mao and You years, Zi and Wu; in Chen and Xu years, Chou and Wei; in Chou and Wei years, Chen and Xu. The note says the rest follow by analogy, which is read here as the first branch of each pair being the Tangfu and the second the State Seal.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.27** 财旺，正适合收布施；官鬼发动，偏适合去谒见官员；财落空，注解经疏是白费力气；官鬼旺，营谋是白费钱。
  Wealth thriving is just right for receiving alms; Pressure moving is especially good for calling on officials; Wealth void, annotating the scriptures is wasted labour; Pressure strong, scheming is money wasted.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.32** 世空、身旺，是地上的行仙；身空而不动，事事都是道；应旺而没有魔，处处都是禅；应落偶位、世落奇位，又生又合，精神清明、身体安静，寿命绵长。
  The Self void and the Body strong makes an earth-wandering immortal; the Body void and still, every single thing is the Way; the Other strong with no demon, Chan is everywhere; the Other on an even place and the Self on an odd one, generating and combining, the spirit clear and the body at peace, life is long.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.03** 官鬼落在身、世上，卦里各爻旺相，得官是真，欢喜；驿马落空亡，白费力气；卦遇胎、没，白白劳神。旺相的爻克应，不好；应入墓的时候，要担心死亡；世爻被克，会遭贬谪；如果世爻去克别人，俸禄加倍。
  Pressure on the Body or the Self with the lines of the hexagram strong and thriving, the office is truly gained and there is joy; the Post Horse Void, effort is spent for nothing; the hexagram meeting Embryo or Sunk, the mind is wearied in vain. A strong line controlling the Other is not good; when the Other enters its tomb, worry about death; the Self line being controlled, one is demoted and exiled; if the Self controls others, the salary is doubled.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.11** 求官，压住身、扶助世就能得官；天驿互相生扶，允许不用拖延；再逢官鬼旺相，哪里还有怀疑——例如二月占得坤之升卦。
  In seeking office, with the Body held down and the Self supported the office is gained; with the Heavenly Post mutually generating, it is promised there will be no delay; and with Pressure also strong how could there be doubt — as for example in the second month the hexagram Kun changing to Sheng.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.24** 官鬼爻旺相、没有刑克，如果再遇扶持，就能得高位显达；杀神和世发动来克官，是凶，白费身心、求不到。
  With the Pressure line strong and not punished or controlled, if it also meets support, high and distinguished rank is gained; a baleful spirit and the Self moving to control Pressure is bad, and body and mind are spent in vain and it is not obtained.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.30** 求官，官鬼要有气、旺相，印绶（父母爻）不带空亡；忌讳子孙发动，也忌父母和官鬼落空。
  In seeking office, Pressure must have qi and be strong and the seal and ribbon (the Resource line) must not carry Void; Output moving is to be avoided, and so are Resource and Pressure falling void.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.48** 谋事，先看内卦：是阳卦还是阴卦；内卦克外卦，所求就通，外卦克内卦，难成；内卦的爻无气，即使外卦旺相，也不能共事、所求终不遂心；世、应空亡，身与心相违，与人谋事也难称意；内外卦相生、又都有气，事情都成就，像得到天公的庇佑。
  In planning a matter, first look at the inner trigram, whether it is a yang trigram or a yin one; if the inner controls the outer, what is sought goes through, and if the outer controls the inner it is hard to accomplish; if the lines of the inner trigram have no qi, then even if the outer is strong one cannot work together and what is sought is in the end not as one wishes; with the Self and the Other Void, body and heart are at odds, and in planning a matter with others it is hard to get what one wants; with inner and outer generating each other and both with qi, all matters are accomplished, as if blessed by Heaven.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.49** 出行、谒见贵人以及寻人：兄、鬼交重发动，不可亲近；子孙旺、财爻明，一定吉利；父母兴动，即使去了也会又回来。
  For travelling, calling on a noble, or looking for someone: with Peer and Pressure moving heavily one cannot get close; with Output strong and Wealth clear it is surely auspicious; with Resource rising, one who has gone will come back again.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.83** 推举时，官爻、父爻旺相发动，稳稳地盼得高升、奖励功劳；如果财爻、官鬼衰弱落空，想谋求赏赐也是白费。具体说：推荐的，官爻、父爻两样齐全才是好兆头，两样缺一，就不能稳稳办成；论奖励的，虽然用官爻，还适宜财爻旺相；财爻、官鬼如果落空，就一定没有奖励勤劳、给予赏赐的美事。
  For a recommendation, if the star of office and Resource are thriving and moving, one can securely hope for high promotion and an award for service; if Wealth and Pressure are weak or Void, to plan for a reward is vain. In detail: for a recommendation, to have both the star of office and Resource is a good omen, and if one of the two is lacking it is not securely accomplished; for an award, though the Pressure line is the one used, Wealth should also be thriving, and if Wealth and Pressure are Void there will surely be no fine thing like an award for diligence and the granting of a reward.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.86** 想求得牌坊、匾额来光耀门庭，必须凭官爻旺相。世爻落空就绝望，子孙发动就难谋求，财爻发动还要托人嘱托，官鬼落空就不必祈求了。凡是求牌坊、匾额来光耀门庭的，先看官爻，再推世爻；这两个只要有一个落空，始终没有指望；子孙发动，也求不到；财爻发动去生官爻，谋求就遂心如意。
  To seek an honorific archway or plaque that will bring glory to one's door, one must rely on the Pressure line being thriving. If the Self is Void there is no hope, if Output moves it is hard to arrange, if Wealth moves one must still entrust the matter to someone, and if Pressure is Void there is no need to pray for it. In every seeking of an archway or plaque to bring glory to one's gate, look first at the Pressure line and then reckon the Self image; if either of the two is Void, there is no hope to the end; if Output moves, it cannot be obtained either; if Wealth moves to generate the star of office, the scheming turns out as one wishes.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.87** 六合的卦，心里喜悦；六冲的卦，脸上带着忧愁。如果求扎帖（官府的帖文），又要看文书爻，反而嫌财爻发动；父爻、官鬼在而财爻落空，就准许发给；不论匾额还是帖文，都忌讳六冲。
  With a six-combine the heart is glad; with a six-clash the face is clouded with sorrow. If one seeks an official warrant, look also at the document line, and on the contrary dislike Wealth moving; with Resource and Pressure present and Wealth Void, it is allowed and can be issued; whether plaque or warrant, a six-clash is feared in all.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.90** 递表章、上奏本，父爻旺相、官爻兴旺，就会得到御笔批准。也忌讳六冲、财爻和子孙发动；其次怕印绶（父母）变成财爻，更嫌官鬼变成福神（子孙）；父爻绝、官爻空，就不要进朝。拜表、上本、启奏都是同一个取法：父旺官兴，一定蒙准奏；如果遇到六冲，或者财摇子动、或者父变妻财、或者官变福神、或者父绝官空，这几条里只要犯了一条，就不要奏朝廷；即使奏了，也白费心机，难以合君王的心意。
  In presenting a memorial and submitting a petition, if Resource is thriving and the star of office flourishes, the imperial brush will mark it for approval. Also fear a six-clash and Wealth and Output moving; next worry the seal line changing into the Wealth line; still more dislike the Pressure image changing into Output; with Resource extinct and the star of office Void, do not go to court. Presenting a memorial, submitting a petition and memorialising all take the same method: if Resource is thriving and the star of office flourishes, the memorial will surely be approved; if it meets a six-clash, or Wealth sways and Output moves, or Resource changes into Wealth, or the star of office changes into Output, or Resource is extinct and the star of office Void, then if even one of these points is violated, do not memorialise the court; even if one does, it is a waste of effort and hardly meets the sovereign's wish.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.93** 逢到冲，不要去朝见天子；遇到合，就该来奏对皇帝；没有官鬼，就沾不到雨露（恩泽）；世爻落空，怎能蒙受恩光？具体说：六冲的卦象，千万不要去朝见天子；六合的爻象，按理适宜面见圣上；爻象虽然聚合，但卦中没有官鬼，或者落在空乡，就不要指望丰厚的恩典；卦里即使有官爻，世爻落在陷地，也只能叹运气艰难，怎么谈得上沐浴天恩？
  Meeting a clash, do not go to court the Son of Heaven; meeting a combination, it is fitting to come and memorialise the emperor; with no Pressure one cannot share in the rain and dew of favour; with the Self Void how can one receive the glow of grace? In detail: with a six-clash image, on no account go to court; with a six-combine line, it is proper to meet the sage sovereign; even if the lines gather in combination, if the hexagram has no Pressure or it falls on empty ground, do not hope for rich favour; even if the hexagram has a star of office, if the Self sits in a pit one can still only lament one's hard fortune, and how could one bathe in heaven's favour?
- **LY.CAREER.EL.97** 论文书、消息，以文书爻为主，以官鬼为凭据。官府的印信文书，适宜父爻发动；民间立的票据契约，忌讳财爻兴旺；财爻临在身爻、世爻上，书契就白写；用爻不落空亡，就按这个情形断。凡是论文书、票据、契约，取父母爻为用爻：旺相就有成，落空就无用；财爻发动，或者世爻、身爻落空，就白费纸笔、枉费心思。
  In judging documents and tidings, take the document as the chief and Pressure as the ground. For official seals and documents it is fitting that Resource move; for bills and contracts drawn up by the people, avoid Wealth flourishing; if Wealth sits on the Body or the Self, the writing of the deed is in vain; if the Subject is not Void, hold to this situation. In judging documents, bills and contracts, take Resource as the Subject line: if thriving it succeeds, if Void it is useless; if the Wealth line moves, or the Self or Body is Void, the paper and brush are toiled for in vain and thought is wasted.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.98** 官鬼是元神，落空就办不成；财是忌神，发动就难成；父爻被冲克，真的也成了假的；父母爻变出生扶它的爻，就有信凭。具体说：卦里没有官鬼，或者官鬼落空亡，都主办不成；妻爻发动，契约必定难成；财动官也动，反而能成契约；父母爻如果被日月冲破，或者化绝、化空，这份契约不能取信于人；父母爻如果遇到生扶，或者变成有气，这份文书即使是假的也可以当真。
  Pressure is the Support, and if Void it does not succeed; Wealth is the Adversary, and if it moves it is hard to accomplish; if Resource is clashed or controlled, the genuine turns false; if Resource changes and is generated and supported, it gains credence. In detail: if the hexagram has no Pressure or it falls on Void, all mean it will not succeed; if the wife line, Wealth, moves, the contract will surely be hard to complete; if Wealth moves and the star of office also moves, on the contrary the contract can be completed; if Resource is clashed or broken by the Day or Month, or changes into extinction or Void, this contract cannot be believed by others; if Resource meets generation and support, or changes into something with qi, then even if this document is false it can be taken as true.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.104** 六冲的爻象难以谐成，卦里没有官爻，事一定出差错；世应如果逢日辰来合，任凭你不愿意也值得夸赞。六冲的卦，事一定不成；合的地方遇到冲，成了之后还会退回；官鬼如果空亡或者不上卦，都办不成；世、应二爻自相会合，或者得到日辰来合世、合应，即使心里不想，也能得到允准办成。
  A six-clash image is hard to bring to harmony, and with no star of office in the hexagram the affair is sure to go wrong; if the Self and the Other meet a combination from the Day, even if you do not wish it, it is worth praising. With a six-clash hexagram the affair will surely not succeed; if where there is a combination it meets a clash, after it succeeds it falls back again; if Pressure is Void or not in the hexagram, in neither case does it succeed; if the Self and the Other lines combine with each other, or the Day combines with the Self or the Other, even if one does not wish it in one's heart, it will be granted and accomplished.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.105** 升官迁职、兴讼，官鬼发动，名声更添；求利，还要财爻旺相；求书信，必须父爻有光彩；兄爻独自发动，样样都忌；子孙兴旺，事事都好。在官府里谋求的，断法同前，又喜官爻旺相，唯独忌讳子孙发动。谋求利息钱财，忌讳财空亡；如果是办理文书，不适宜财爻发动；卦里如果没有父爻，终究不能成。各种谋事，都嫌兄弟发动；只有子孙发动没有不好的，唯独功名一项忌讳。
  For promotion and transfer in office, and for starting a lawsuit, if Pressure moves one's name is doubled; for seeking profit, Wealth must also be thriving; for seeking a letter, Resource must be bright; if the Peer line moves alone, everything is taboo; if Output flourishes, everything is good. For scheming in official business the judgment is as before, and one is again glad for the Pressure line to be thriving and alone fears Output moving. In scheming for interest and wealth, Void is to be avoided for Wealth; if one is managing a document, Wealth moving is not fitting; and if the hexagram has no Resource it cannot in the end succeed. In every kind of scheming, Peer moving is disliked; only Output moving is never anything but good, and for fame and office alone is it a taboo.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.131** 用人，以用爻为主，以应象为凭据。凡是用别人，就在应爻上找；亲属各有各的用爻划分；日冲、月破、空亡落在这个爻上，就永远不亨通。凡是用人办事，或者请人代自己劳作，或者招人做生意，或者托付别人图利益，如果用的是外人，以应爻为主；如果是亲戚，以用象为凭据。用爻如果遇到日辰冲、月建破，或者落旬空，都主不成；即使成了，也没有力量，又不长久，千万不要用这个人。
  In employing a man, take the Subject line as the chief and the Other image as the ground. In employing an outsider, look to the Other; for one's own kin, each has a Subject line to itself; if the Day's clash, Month-break or Void sits on this line, it never prospers. Whenever one employs a man to act, or asks a man to labour in one's place, or takes on a man for a business, or entrusts something to another for gain, if one employs an outsider the Other line is the chief; if it is a relative, the Subject image is the ground. If the Subject line meets the Day's clash, the Month's break, or falls on the ten-day Void, in every case it does not succeed, and even if it succeeds it has no strength and does not last, so by no means employ him.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.132** 用爻遇到绝乡，终究不好；世爻被用爻所克，却是无情；六冲就反目、不成宾主；用爻变出刑伤，力量一定轻。用爻临绝又没有生扶，这个人一定没有能耐；用爻来克世，实在怀着欺骗主人的心，他不是仗义的人；卜得六冲的卦，终究反目，怎么能和谐？卦上用爻虽然有气，但变成克制它的地方、或者变入墓、绝，这个人必定先勤后惰，事情当是有始无终。
  If the Subject meets a place of extinction it is in the end not good; if the Self is controlled by the Subject, it is without feeling; with a six-clash they fall out and are not host and guest; if the Subject changes into punishment and injury, its strength is surely slight. If the Subject line is in extinction and has no generation or support, the man is surely incapable; if the Subject comes to control the Self, he truly harbours a mind to cheat his master and is not a man of loyalty and justice; if a six-clash hexagram is cast, they end by falling out, and how can there be harmony? Even if the Subject line in the hexagram has qi, if it changes into a place that controls it or into tomb or extinction, the man will surely be diligent at first and idle later, and the affair will have a beginning but no end.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.133** 以上七样都不犯，才可说他和悦又多能；如果世爻又得他的扶助，必定受他的恩，事情成功。应爻和用爻，不遇日冲、月破、空亡、死绝，又不克世，又不变出冲伤，本卦和之卦都不是六冲，就叫做宾主相投，用他有力；用爻如果来生世、合世，就靠他维持，替我周旋办事。
  If none of these seven things is violated, one may say he is amiable and very capable; if the Self also gets his support, one will surely receive his kindness and the affair will succeed. If the Other line and the Subject line do not meet the Day's clash, Month-break, Void, death or extinction, and do not control the Self, and do not change out a clash or injury, and neither the original nor the changed hexagram is a six-clash, it is called host and guest in accord, and the man is strong to employ; if the Subject line comes to generate or combine with the Self, one relies on him to hold things up and he manages my affairs for me.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.144** 没有官鬼，必定没有头绪；游魂，必定游去游回。世爻犯旬空，自己有改弦更张的意思；应爻遭月破，师父没有传授的心。不幸遇到六冲，彼此无情，难以学业；幸而逢六合，师徒相得，反倒成功。凡是徒弟向师父学业，不适宜卦内没有官鬼；如果逢空，事情没有头绪；卦值游魂，往返不定；又看世爻是自己、应爻是师父，都不可值旬空、月破，又不适宜卦值六冲；世应如果不空亡，又遇到爻逢六合，一定主师徒相得、事必成功。想选择明师，不知道曲直好坏，应当占一个易卦，就能看到真假。
  With no Pressure there is surely no clue to the matter, and a Wandering Soul surely means going off and returning; if the Self falls on the ten-day Void one is inclined to change one's course, and if the Other meets Month-break the teacher has no heart to pass on his teaching; meeting a six-clash is unlucky, for the two have no feeling for each other and it is hard to learn the trade; luckily meeting a six-combine, master and pupil suit each other and succeed. For all pupils learning under masters, it is not fitting for the hexagram to have no Pressure line in it; if it meets Void, the matter has no clue; if the hexagram falls on a Wandering Soul, one goes back and forth and nothing is settled; look also at the Self as oneself and the Other as the teacher, neither of which may fall on the ten-day Void or Month-break, and it is also unfitting for the hexagram to be a six-clash; if the Self and the Other are not Void and the lines also meet a six-combine, master and pupil will surely suit each other and the matter will surely succeed. To choose a bright teacher when one does not know the crooked from the straight, one should divine a hexagram, and the false and true will be seen at once.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.04** 拜见贵人、求谋、问立身：文书（父母）不动、又赶上时辰，可成；财动，文书白费力；子孙动，伤了官鬼，事情不亨通。事情现出两个爻，要再去找；官多，辗转曲折，恐怕艰难；最要紧的是日辰生合它、让它出来，那就处处遇到知音、得到前程。
  For calling on a noble, scheming for a post and asking about establishing oneself: with the documents (Resource) still and in step with the time it can be done; Wealth moving, the documents are spent in vain; Output moving injures Pressure and the matter does not go through. If the matter shows two lines, seek again; with many Pressure lines it winds and turns and may be hard; what matters most is that the Day generates or combines and brings it out, and then one finds those who know one everywhere and a future.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.05** 六爻中如果只有一爻动，最忌讳兄弟爻动，事情不实在、难成。如果文书和贵人在卦中原本没有，也难成；出现了、有气，也怕空亡——所说的动爻急得像火攻，急着落入空亡。求官和文书，要旺相、持世才有成。应爻不受克，事体就分明；乾、兑、坎宫，谋事不专一；见官员，用爻发动，那人多半出门，叫也是白叫。
  If only one line moves among the six, what is most to be avoided is Peer moving: the matter is not real and is hard to accomplish. If the documents and the Noble are originally absent from the hexagram, it is also hard to accomplish; if they appear and have qi they still fear Void, as is said, a moving line is urgent like an attack by fire, hurrying into Void. For seeking office and documents, they must be strong and hold the Self to succeed. If the Other line is not controlled, the matter is clear; in the palaces Qian, Dui and Kan the planning is not single-minded; in seeing an official, if the Subject line moves, the man has mostly gone out, and calling for him is in vain.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.07** 子孙发动，谒见贵人、求官不要进取（主用爻被伤），守旧倒还可以顺其自然；妻财发动克了文书，应举、求官总是落空（文书受伤）；官鬼发动，更改、动谋都不行（鬼动，所以受阻隔）。
  With Output moving, do not press forward in calling on a noble or seeking office (the Subject line is injured), and holding to the old can be left to take its course; with Wealth moving and controlling the documents, taking the examination or seeking office is all empty (the documents are injured); with Pressure moving, neither changes nor schemes will do (Pressure moving brings obstruction).
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.08** 官化官，病没有安好；拜见贵人、求官，事事都难（有名无实）；官化文书，官运还不顺（官位高过文书，文书就难）；纵然忧愁、疾病，也还算吉。
  Pressure turning into Pressure, the illness is not yet settled; in calling on a noble or seeking office everything is hard (name without substance); Pressure turning into the documents, the office does not yet go smoothly (with Pressure ahead of the documents, the documents are hard); even so, worry and illness are still auspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.09** 父化同人，多有口舌；一起求谋，要曲折婉转、重重叠叠（纵然成了也要重新更改）；父化财，做买卖有利（先难后易），家里长辈不安宁、求事笨拙（化成财克了父）；父化官，家里有耽误、有失（化成鬼，不适宜），求官必定得到靠近高位的职务（官、父两个月），卦里没有父母，事情没有头绪（父母是总体），再在休囚，白费力气（父母衰，后来劳苦）。
  Resource turning into a companion (Peer), there is much quarrelling; seeking together, it winds and turns and must be layered (even if it succeeds it must be changed again); Resource turning into Wealth, trading is profitable (hard first, then easy), but the elders of the house are unsettled and seeking a matter goes clumsily (turning into Wealth controls Resource); Resource turning into Pressure, the household suffers delay and loss (turning into Pressure is unsuitable), and seeking office one is sure to get a post near a high one (the two months of Pressure and Resource); with no Resource in the hexagram the matter has no head (Resource is the whole body), and if it is also at rest or trapped, effort is spent in vain (Resource weak, labour afterwards).
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.10** 官化兄，朋友有欺诈，安心托付的人心思已不像当初；官化财，钱财自然得到，赌博、抽签拈阄总不如意；官员里没有官鬼，难以谋事，官员不出现，事情落空；托人情办事，人不一条心（不适宜托人）。
  Pressure turning into Peer, friends are deceitful and the one trusted with a favour no longer has the heart he had at first; Pressure turning into Wealth, money comes of itself, but gambling and drawing lots are never as one wishes; with no Pressure in the office, planning a matter is hard, and with the official not appearing the matter is empty; relying on favours, hearts are not as one (it is not suitable to entrust it to others).
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.11** 朱雀交重发动，文书印绶旺（朱雀属火）；和杀神并在一起，就白劳功夫（并上凶神就没有文）；是非口舌都由此而来；持水、临空，反而有利于公事。白虎交重，是惊恐怪异之事（白虎属金）；求官时临在官鬼上，反而丰盛隆盛（虎与官有利于名）。
  With the Vermilion Bird moving heavily, the documents and the seal and ribbon are strong (the Vermilion Bird belongs to Fire); joined with a baleful spirit, the labour is spent in vain (joined to an ill spirit there are no documents); quarrels and gossip all come from this; holding Water or standing in the void, it is on the contrary favourable to public business. With the White Tiger moving heavily it is fright and strange things (the White Tiger belongs to Metal); in seeking office, standing on Pressure, it brings abundance and prosperity on the contrary (the Tiger and Pressure favour a name).
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.05** 父母爻是印绶、文书、诰牒、宣敕、奏疏、表章，卦里不能没有它，宜旺不宜衰，扶助世爻最吉。它持太岁、有气、生合世爻，主有朝廷的宣召；再加月建，多是敕令制命和上司的奖励；最怕冲、空、化空，那就多半不实；衰弱安静又空亡，必定没有宣敕，也没有差遣任命；卦里没有父母，别指望迁升选用。
  Resource is the seal and ribbon, documents, patents, imperial edicts, memorials and petitions. It cannot be missing from the hexagram, should be thriving and not weak, and supporting the Self is the most auspicious. If it holds the Tai Sui with breath and generates or combines with the Self, there will be a summons from the court; with the Month added, it is mostly an edict or a commendation from superiors; what is most feared is a clash, Void or turning into Void, and then it is mostly unreal; if weak, still and Void, there will surely be no edict and no appointment; if Resource is not in the hexagram, do not hope for promotion or selection.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.35** 占馆不把父母当作老师，要当作书馆来看。父母旺相有气，就有好书馆；卦里没有父母或者落空，必定没有书馆，事情也难成。占那一年有没有书馆，全要这一爻出现、有气不空；应爻来生合世爻，自然有人来聘请，世爻生合应爻和父爻，就必须自己去访求；卦里没有父母而动爻化出父母的，意想不到有人推荐；父化父必有两处书馆；本宫化入他宫由近及远，他宫化入本宫从外到内。
  In divining a school post, Resource is not taken as the teacher but as the school itself. If Resource is thriving and has breath, there is a good school; if there is no Resource in the hexagram or it falls Void, there will surely be no school and the matter is hard to bring about. To divine whether there will be a school that year everything depends on this line appearing with breath and not Void; if the Other comes to generate or combine with the Self, someone naturally comes to engage one, and if the Self generates or combines with the Other and with Resource, one must go and seek on one's own; if there is no Resource in the hexagram but a moving line turns into it, someone unexpectedly recommends one; Resource turning into Resource means two schools; from the home palace into another palace is from near to far, and from another palace into the home palace is from outside to inside.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.37** 占学馆，应爻持兄弟发动，必有同行的人来争谋这个馆，兄弟临卦身也是这样；如果在间爻上发动、冲克世爻，就主有人从中说坏话，事情难成。
  In divining a school post, if the Other holds Peer and it moves, there will surely be fellow teachers contending for the post, and likewise if Peer rests on the Body; if it moves on a between line and clashes or controls the Self, it means someone undermines it by talk, and the matter is hard to bring about.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.38** 官鬼能生扶父母，所以占馆以官鬼这一爻代表招集率领学生的人。官鬼不出现或者落空，必定没有人聚集学生来成学馆，事情难成，也没有人推荐；如果空而逢冲、伏而被提起，必须央求那个人，他才肯出来招集。
  Pressure can generate and support Resource, so in divining a school post the Pressure line stands for the person who gathers and leads the pupils. If Pressure does not appear or falls Void, there will surely be no one to gather pupils to make up a school, the matter is hard to bring about, and no one will recommend one; if it is Void and meets a clash, or is hidden and drawn up, one must beg that person before he is willing to come forward and gather them.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.39** 应爻空亡，没有人来聘请；再如果父母不出现或者落空，必定难成；应爻动空、化空，是说空话、假装作主，不然也不能把事做完。父母临身，或者动爻来生合世身，是大吉的兆头，这个馆容易成；有这种象而应空、官鬼动，虽然有招集学生的人，却没有聘请的主人，适合自己开馆；官鬼空伏而应来生合，虽然有聘请的人家，却没有招集学生的人，适合自己去招来学生。
  If the Other is Void, no one comes to engage one; if Resource also does not appear or falls Void, the matter will surely be hard to bring about; if the Other moves into Void or turns into Void, he only says it in words and pretends to be the master, and otherwise the matter is not seen through to the end. If Resource rests on the Body, or a moving line comes to generate or combine with the Self or the Body, it is a greatly auspicious sign and the school comes easily; with this image but the Other Void and Pressure moving, though there is someone to gather pupils there is no master to engage one, and it is fitting to open a school oneself; if Pressure is Void and hidden while the Other comes to generate or combine, though there is a household that would engage one there is no one to gather pupils, and it is fitting to draw in pupils oneself.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.40** 文书（父母）是占馆的用神，遇到财爻发动就被克坏：没成的不能成，已经成的也不称意；世爻、身爻、日辰、月建都忌讳临着财爻；如果父母持月建，或者临在生旺之地，或许还能成。
  Resource, the document, is the Subject in divining a school post, and if Wealth moves it is controlled and spoiled: what has not yet come about cannot, and what has come about is not to one's liking; the Self, the Body, the Day and the Month all dislike resting on Wealth; if Resource holds the Month or rests in a place of birth or thriving, it may perhaps still come through.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.41** 卦里父母出现、应爻来生合，而世爻空亡的，不是馆不成，是自己不肯上前，或者虽然成了却不去；应爻不生合、父母重叠而世爻空亡的，是另有别的馆，所以不成这一个；世爻无缘无故自己空亡，必定不能成，即使成了也终究不如意。
  If Resource appears in the hexagram and the Other comes to generate or combine while the Self is Void, it is not that the school will not come about but that one does not step forward oneself, or that even if it comes about one does not go; if the Other does not generate or combine, Resource is doubled and the Self is Void, there is another school, and so this one does not come about; if the Self falls Void by itself without cause, it will surely not come about, and even if it does it is in the end not as one wishes.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.43** 间爻（世应之间的爻）发动，事情多有阻隔，纵然卦象吉也难成。
  If a between line (a line between the Self and the Other) moves, the matter is often obstructed, and even if the hexagram is auspicious it is hard to bring about.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.02** 求职、请上司批判，宜官鬼出现，忌发动；已在任的，宜鬼静，鬼发动就有变动，子动就有人来替。如果六爻中只有一爻动，最吉。兄动，事情不实在，难成。如果（用爻）出现而有气，可以很快成，怕落空。易说：动爻急如火。其次，是文书和贵人出现。但如果卦中本来没有、或不入卦、或落空，那件事就难。官和文书都旺相，也要持世才可以成。应又不克世，事情就清楚。乾、兑、坎宫，谋事不专一；见官爻发动，那个人多半外出，见了也生气。
  In seeking a post or asking a superior for a ruling, it is right for Pressure to appear and wrong for it to move; for one in office, it is right for Pressure to be still, Pressure moving means a change, and Output moving means someone to take his place. If only one of the six lines moves, it is most auspicious. If Peer moves, the matter is not solid and is hard to accomplish. If it appears and has force, it can be accomplished quickly, but fear Void. The Yi says: a moving line is urgent as fire. Next come the document line and the Noble appearing. But if the hexagram originally lacks it, or it does not enter the hexagram, or it is Void, the matter is hard. If Pressure and Resource are both strong, they must also hold the Self before it can be accomplished. If the Other does not control the Self either, the matter stands clear. In the palaces of Qian, Dui and Kan, the scheming is not single-minded; when Pressure is seen moving, the man is mostly out, and if one sees him he flares up in anger.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.02** 印绶（父母）旺，职位就高；官鬼衰，名声就卑小。父母、官鬼是占官的根本，缺一个事情就难成；两个都逢生逢旺，官职必然高大，休囚则只是卑小的职分；父母强旺而官鬼无气，职位虽平平，身处的是清静冷淡的衙门，没有威光权柄；遇进神，名声更大；逢退神，必减损前任的政声。
  With the seal and ribbon (Resource) strong the post held is high; with Pressure weak the name is humble and small. Resource and Pressure are the root of divining for office, and lacking either the matter is hard to accomplish; if both meet birth or strength the post is surely high and great, and if they are at rest or trapped it is only a humble station; if Resource is strong but Pressure has no qi, the rank may be ordinary but one sits in a quiet, cold office without glory or authority; meeting an Advancing spirit the name grows greater, meeting a Retreating spirit it will surely lessen the reputation for government of the previous post.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.03** 禄、马扶助自身，万里风云际会。禄是以旺位为禄（如甲的禄在寅，在临官、帝旺的地方），比喻人长大后食禄；马是驿马，落在身、世，或官鬼、父母上，以及发动来生扶身、世，是大吉，唾手成名的象；最忌空亡和刑、害。
  With the Emolument and the Horse supporting the self there is a meeting of wind and cloud across ten thousand li. The Emolument takes strength as the Emolument (for Jia it is at Yin, at the Office and Peak stages), likening a person grown up and eating a salary; the Horse is the Post Horse, and standing on the Body or the Self, or on Pressure or Resource, or moving to generate or support the Body and Self, it is greatly auspicious, the image of making a name as easily as spitting into one's palms; Void and punishment or harm are the great taboos.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.05** 青龙发动，事业容易成；朱雀落空，文书难成。青龙是吉庆福德的喜神，有年建、月建、日建三种，发动必定加官进禄，与天马并立自有升腾进益之喜，不宜落空，落空只是空欢喜；朱雀是占官的用爻，宜旺不宜衰，见空亡文书必难成，被刑克文书必遭伤坏。
  When the Azure Dragon moves the undertaking is easily accomplished; when the Vermilion Bird is void the documents are hard to complete. The Azure Dragon is the spirit of joy of auspicious celebration, blessing and virtue, of three kinds, that of the year, of the Month and of the day; moving, it surely adds rank and emolument, and standing together with the Heavenly Horse there is the joy of rising and gain; it must not fall void, for then it is only empty joy. The Vermilion Bird is the Subject line in divining for office and should be strong and not weak; seen Void the documents are sure to be hard to complete, and punished or controlled they are sure to be spoiled.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.08** 妻财持世，任命的文书哪能称心？兄弟扶身，俸禄哪能称意？妻财持世克了文书（父母），就算成了也不如意；兄弟是克剥的神，俸禄必定不丰厚。
  With Wealth holding the Self, how can the patent of appointment go as one wishes? With Peer supporting the self, how can the salary be as one wishes? Wealth holding the Self and controlling the documents (Resource) means that even if it succeeds it is not as one wishes; Peer is the spirit that strips and takes, so the salary will surely not be generous.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.10** 卦里有两个父母、两个官鬼，一定是「鸳鸯求仕」：心里有两个打算，是此失彼得的兆头。前面的官鬼旺、后面的官鬼空，只宜守旧，不可改谋；前面的衰而后面的旺，宜舍旧图新；前后都旺，所求遂意。
  With two Resource lines and two Pressure lines in the hexagram it is surely 'mandarin ducks seeking office': there are two intentions, a sign of losing here and gaining there. If the former Pressure is strong and the latter void, one should only hold to the old and not change the plan; if the former is weak and the latter strong, give up the old and plan for the new; if both are strong, what is sought is attained as wished.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.32** 已经聘定，问去就馆吉不吉：三冲，教席难以做满；财陷，酬金不丰；应空，东家没有尊师重道的志向；应克世，有怠慢先生的嫌疑；应破、散、绝，有懈怠学业的心；只有六合、财旺、应生世才是吉。馆已经定了，要问我在那里能不能身安心定：随墓、助伤不可犯；世用空、破、散、绝不可遇；官鬼不可动；动了就用五行、八宫、六神、诸星细看。游魂主不长久，三冲主不安定。
  Once the engagement is made, in asking whether taking up the post is auspicious: the three clashes, and the seat at the inkstone is hard to keep to the end; Wealth fallen, and the tuition gift is not generous; the Other Void, and the family has no will to honour the teacher and the Way; the Other controlling the Self, and there is a suspicion of slighting the teacher; the Other broken, scattered or extinct, and there is a heart of idleness about study; only Six-Combine, thriving Wealth and the Other generating the Self are auspicious. The post is settled; in asking whether I shall be at ease in body and settled in mind there, following into the tomb and helping the injury must not be committed, the Self or the Subject Void, broken, scattered or extinct must not be met, and Pressure must not move; if it moves, examine it by the five phases, the eight palaces, the six spirits and the stars. Wandering Soul does not last, and the three clashes are unsettled.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.33** 设帐（开馆）会不会有人来跟着学：应空，就没有背着书箱来的学徒；财虚，馆谷（教书的酬金）就不丰盛。问学生来附读的，和设帐一样断；问代人坐席的，和成馆一样断。占辞馆，以应为主人：应克世，主人不依从；应空、散、破、绝，事情做不成；主人不依从，即使六冲也解不开；事情做不成，即使游魂又怎么辞得掉？只有应爻实（不空）、又不克世的，才可以离开。
  In asking whether, on opening a school, anyone will come to study: the Other Void, and no pupils come carrying their book-chests; Wealth empty, and the stipend for the post is not rich. In asking about pupils who come to attach themselves, judge as for opening a school; in asking about standing in for another, judge as for a post that comes off. In divining about resigning from a post, the Other is the master: the Other controlling the Self, and the master does not comply; the Other Void, scattered, broken or extinct, and it does not go through; when the master does not comply, even a six-clash cannot dissolve it; when it does not go through, even Wandering Soul, how can one resign? Only when the Other is solid and does not control the Self may one leave.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.48** 有指定一个地方来占，问能不能得到这个地方：本卦六冲、合处逢冲、官坏世空，就不是食禄的地方。占考选、升调，问能不能得到某个官职：世爻实、官旺，就是应当拜受的职位。考职选官、升任调缺，或者指定哪个地方、哪个官来占，都用这个办法。
  If one points to a particular place and divines whether one will get that place: if the original hexagram is a six-clash, a combination is clashed, Pressure is damaged and the Self is Void, it is not a place to draw salary. If one divines about selection by examination, promotion or transfer and asks whether one will get a certain office: when the Self is solid and Pressure thriving, it is the post one is due to be appointed to. Taking a post by examination, selecting an official, being promoted into or transferred to a vacancy, or pointing to a certain place or a certain office, are all divined by this same method.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.85** 谋事在人，营求的办法不一，都是物与物相交才成。所以六冲就交不到一起；合处逢冲，就将要交合却又（此处疑脱一字，当是「散」）；应空、散，对方的心就不依附；世空、散，我自己的意思先懒了；只有世应破、绝，还能得到一半；官鬼失去，谋事就没有主。这是五种忌。
  Scheming rests with men; the ways of seeking are not one, and all of them are accomplished by one thing meeting another. So with a six-clash they do not meet; with a combination clashed, they are about to join and then (a character seems lost here, presumably 'come apart'); with the Other Void or scattered, his heart does not attach to me; with the Self Void or scattered, my own will first turns slack; only with the Self and the Other broken or extinct is half still obtained; with Pressure lost, the scheme has no master. These are the five taboos.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.86** 所以靠自己的力量能办成的，不用看应；靠别人才能成的事，不用看世。只问成不成，只忌空、冲；兼问吉凶，就要同时求用神旺。谋文书（功名）用父，谋利用财，解除祸患用子。占自己谋的事不论应，占别人谋的事不论世；问成不成看谋（世应、冲空），问吉凶看用（用神）。
  So for what can be done by one's own strength, one need not look at the Other; for what is done through others, one need not look at the Self. If one asks only whether it comes off, one fears only Void and clash; if one also asks about good and ill, one must also seek the Subject thriving. For documents (a name) use the father line, for profit use Wealth, and for relieving trouble use Output. In divining a scheme of one's own, the Other is not discussed; in divining another's scheme, the Self is not discussed; asking whether it comes off one looks at the scheme (Self, Other, clash, Void), asking about good and ill one looks at the Subject.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.89** 凡是专问嘱托的，重体；专问所谋的事的，重用；问嘱托而又兼求用神的，体用两全，事情没有不吉的。然而嘱托的体有病，即使用神得位也不成。所以以眼前这一占为得，不可有二心。
  When one asks only about the entrusting, the body counts for most; when one asks only about the matter schemed for, the use counts for most; when one asks about the entrusting and also seeks the Subject, with body and use both whole, there is no matter that is not auspicious. But if the body of the entrusting is sick, even if the Subject is well placed it does not come off. So the divination at hand is what is to be taken, and one must not be of two minds.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.92** 然而世空有喜与恶的分别，三冲有宜与忌的分别：出家而三冲、世空，是超然物外，像孤鹤横空；求道而三冲、世空，是尘世杂务缠心，像困鱼失水。占出世，三冲是解开（俗世的）网，世空是没有牵挂；若占入道，那么空就成不了。
  Yet the Self Void has a distinction of welcome and unwelcome, and the three clashes a distinction of suitable and unsuitable: for one leaving home, the three clashes and the Self Void are being detached from the world, like a lone crane crossing the sky; for one seeking the Way, the three clashes and the Self Void are worldly business tangling the heart, like a trapped fish out of water. In divining about leaving the world, the three clashes are undoing the net and the Self Void is having nothing hanging on one's mind; if one divines about entering the Way, then Void means it does not come off.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.94** 限定日期取证（证道）、指日还丹（炼丹成功）：世空就无成，六冲就多阻碍，世病就像掘井九仞而放弃，鬼发就是魔高一丈；只有世旺、福兴，才丹成悟彻。只问求道，世空、世病、六冲、鬼动，这是四戒。
  For fixing a date for attaining realisation and counting the days to the return of the elixir: the Self Void, and nothing comes of it; a six-clash, and there are many obstructions; a sick Self, and it is digging a well nine fathoms deep and giving it up; Pressure issuing, and the devil stands ten feet high; only with the Self thriving and Output rising is the elixir completed and the realisation thorough. In asking only about seeking the Way, the Self Void, the Self sick, a six-clash and Pressure moving are the four warnings.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.104** 功名没有官鬼（官鬼不上卦），进取就没有成就，升迁也不能如愿。这是说取官鬼为用的占问，官鬼不宜没有。
  In seeking a name and office with no Pressure line in the hexagram, advancement comes to nothing and promotion is not achieved. This speaks of divinations in which Pressure is taken as the Subject, and Pressure should not be absent.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.07** 候选（等待选官）——能选上：官星临在身爻、世爻上，财爻单独发动；或者世爻、官星加文书、贵人，带青龙或朱雀发动；或者太岁、月建带着官星、印星来生合世身。选得迟：官星伏在世爻下，或伏着驿马、印绶。选在下等：世爻持官星、贵人遇升爻，但休囚受克。选不上：卦里没有官星、印星，或它们空绝，或身爻世爻空绝，或子孙持世旺动、子孙单独发动；官鬼变官鬼，事情多反复；官鬼发动而财空绝，选官的衙门没有力量；官星入绝但逢生，事虽有阻挠，终得贵人之力而成；身爻世爻随官鬼入墓，选不成，成了也不如意；遇丁未、戊戌二杀之墓，即使选了官、到了任，也要带着亲人回乡；官鬼带亡神劫杀大杀克世身，选了官也不得赴任；有官星而无父母，或父母空死墓绝胎，没有任所。
  Awaiting selection. Selected: the star of office sits on the Body or the Self and the Wealth line alone moves; or the Self and the star of office carry the document and the Noble and move with the Azure Dragon or the Vermilion Bird; or Tai Sui and the Month carry the star of office and seal and generate or combine with the Self and Body. Late selection: the star of office or the Post Horse or seal lies hidden under the Self. A low selection: the Self holds the star of office and the Noble on a promotion line but is resting or trapped and controlled. Not selected: the hexagram has no star of office or seal, or they are Void or extinct, or the Body and Self are Void or extinct, or Output holds the Self and is thriving and moving, or Output alone moves. Pressure turning into Pressure: the matter goes back and forth; Pressure moves while Wealth is Void or extinct: the selecting office has no power; the star of office extinct but meeting generation: the matter meets obstruction but is in the end achieved through a patron's help; the Body and Self following Pressure into the tomb: the selection fails, and if it succeeds it is not as wished; meeting the tombs of the two baleful spirits Dingwei and Wuxu: though selected and arrived at the post, he travels home with his kin; Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit, Robbery and great baleful spirits and controlling the Self and Body: though selected he cannot go to his post; the star of office present but no Resource, or Resource Void, dead, in tomb, extinct or in the womb stage: there is no post.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.10** 领凭证：以文书（父母）为用神。文书旺相，到它入墓的日子可以领；衰弱，到它生旺的日子可以领；伏藏，到它当值的日子领。卦里没有父母，或者父母临空、死、墓、绝、胎，等很久也领不到；父母变出父母，文凭不实在。
  Collecting the credentials: the document line is the Subject. If it is thriving, the credentials can be collected on the day it enters the tomb; if weak, on a day of its birth or strength; if hidden, on the day in which it rules. If the hexagram has no Resource line, or Resource sits on Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, one waits long and does not get them; if Resource turns into Resource, the credentials are not genuine.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.29** 起复，重新起用：官星遇到三传（太岁、月建、日辰）生扶的，必定起复；世爻下伏着官星，或伏着印星、驿马的，必定在官星、印星当值的年月日起复；官星、印星持身爻、世爻，旺相安静的，必定在冲动官星、印星的年月日起复；旁边的爻带贵人、驿马，旺相发动，来生身爻、世爻上的官星、印星的，必得贵人提拔，在相生的年月日起复；旁边的兄弟爻旺相发动，带亡神、劫杀、隔神来刑、冲、克、害身爻、世爻上的官星、印星的，必有小人从中阻隔、坏事。
  Being recalled to office. If the star of office meets generation and support from the three rulers, he is surely recalled; if the star of office lies hidden under the Self, or the seal and the Horse lie hidden, he is recalled in the year, month or day when the star of office and seal rule; if the star of office and seal hold the Body and Self, thriving and still, he is recalled in the year, month or day that clashes them into motion; if a side line carrying the Noble and the Horse is thriving and moving and generates the star of office and seal on the Body and Self, he gets a patron's promotion and is recalled in the year, month or day of generation; if a side Peer line is thriving and moving, carrying the Lost Spirit, Robbery and the obstructing spirit, and punishes, clashes, controls or harms the star of office and seal on the Body and Self, there will surely be bad men who stand between and spoil the matter.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.71** 文书的占问，有公事和私事的分别：虽然都以父母爻为用神，但公事占以官星旺相为助，私事占以子孙旺相为助。三传（太岁、月建、日辰）刑、害、克、破父母爻的，不成；卦里没有官星、父爻，或官星、父爻临空、绝，以及化成空、绝的，不是发不下来，就是遗失；父爻值墓、胎，以及化成墓、胎，或者加勾陈、土爻而旺相发动的，必定被阻滞耽搁拖延月日。在官的人占文凭、诰敕、公文、差委的札付，只要身爻、世爻坐着禄、驿马、贵人、官星、印绶，或者三传值官星、印星发动来生合身爻、世爻的，必定可以得到。为经商占盐引、钞文，以及居家占契券、田地凭帖，只要父母爻旺相发动生合世爻、身爻，而财爻、子孙旺相安静的，有；应爻空、绝、墓、胎，以及世爻持财爻加玄武发动的，没有（财爻克文书，玄武克朱雀）。
  Documents differ between public and private matters: though Resource is the Subject in both, in a public matter the star of office being thriving helps, and in a private matter Output being thriving helps. If the three rulers punish, harm, control or break Resource, it does not come about; if the hexagram has no star of office or seal line, or they sit on Void or extinction or turn into Void or extinction, it is either not issued or lost; if the seal line meets tomb or womb, or turns into tomb or womb, or carries Hook Earth and an Earth line and is thriving and moving, it will surely be blocked and delayed for months and days. For one in office divining credentials, imperial edicts, official letters and orders of appointment, if the Body and Self sit on Emolument, the Horse, the Noble, the star of office and the seal and ribbon, or the three rulers on the star of office and seal move to generate or combine with the Body and Self, they can surely be had. For a merchant divining salt vouchers and notes of credit, or a householder divining contracts, deeds and land certificates, if the Resource line is thriving and moving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Wealth and Output are thriving and still, there will be one; if the Other is Void, extinct, in tomb or in womb, or the Self holds Wealth with the Dark Tortoise added and moving, there will be none (Wealth controls the document and the Dark Tortoise controls the Vermilion Bird).
- **LY.CAREER.YY.83** 谋事能不能成，以世爻、应爻为主，内卦是谋事的人。内卦克外卦、世爻克应爻，外卦、应爻生内卦、世爻，或者内外卦、世应都旺相比和的，事情可以谋，谋也容易成；月建、日辰合世、应的，对方心里虽然不愿意，也会答应成全；间爻、官鬼发动生合世、应的，有人劝说成全。谋官，逢官星旺相生合世身、子孙衰弱安静的，成；谋财，逢财爻旺相生合世身、兄弟衰弱安静的，成；谋文书，逢父爻旺相生合世身、财爻衰弱安静的，成。凡事都嫌兄弟发动，但如果兄弟带着贵人、禄一起生世爻，就是替你吹嘘推荐的人；凡事都喜欢福神发动，求名（功名）却忌讳它。这是可谋而成的占。
  Whether a scheme can succeed takes the Self and Other as the chief, and the inner trigram is the man who schemes. If the inner controls the outer, the Self controls the Other, the outer and the Other generate the inner and Self, or the inner and outer and the Self and Other are all thriving and in harmony, the matter can be planned and is easily done; if the Month and Day combine with the Self and Other, though the other side is unwilling at heart he will agree and see it through; if a line between or Pressure moves to generate or combine with the Self and Other, someone persuades him to see it through. Scheming for office, if the star of office is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Output is weak and still, it succeeds; scheming for wealth, if Wealth is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Peer is weak and still, it succeeds; scheming for a document, if Resource is thriving and generates or combines with the Self and Body while Wealth is weak and still, it succeeds. In everything a moving Peer line is disliked, but if it carries the Noble and Emolument and generates the Self, it is one who talks you up and recommends you; in everything the fortune spirit moving is liked, but in seeking fame it is avoided. This is the pattern of a scheme that can be planned and will succeed.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.84** 不可谋、难成的占：内外卦、世爻、应爻、身爻，逢空、死、墓、绝、胎，被刑、害、克、破的，或者卦是六冲，或者世、应都发动，或者外卦克内卦、应爻克世爻，或者官鬼、兄弟临在间爻、带隔神、退神、退悔杀发动的，都不可谋，谋也不成。又，合中犯了刑、害、克、破的，事情多半恩中生怨，成了之后又毁，虽然是自己的事，也被旁人阻隔。又，用神不上卦的（比如求官而卦里没有官爻、官爻空绝；求财、求文书而没有财爻、印爻，或财爻、印爻空绝），都谋不成。又凡是卦里没有官星，以及官星逢空、绝的，诸事不成。所以经书说：卦中没有官鬼、伏着的谋事，父爻发动，终究要费力才成。这是不可谋而难成的占。
  The pattern of a scheme that cannot be planned and is hard to complete. If the inner and outer trigrams, the Self, Other and Body meet Void, death, tomb, extinction or womb and are punished, harmed, controlled or broken, or the hexagram is a six-clash, or the Self and Other both move, or the outer controls the inner and the Other controls the Self, or Pressure or Peer sits on a line between carrying the obstructing spirit, the Retreating spirit or the regret baleful spirit and moves, none can be planned, and if planned will not succeed. Again, if within a combination there is punishment, harm, control or breaking, the matter often turns from favour to resentment, and is spoiled after succeeding, and though it is one's own matter it is blocked by others. Again, if the Subject does not appear on the board (as when seeking office there is no Pressure line, or Pressure is Void or extinct; seeking wealth or a document, there is no Wealth or seal line, or they are Void or extinct), the scheme will not succeed. Again, when the hexagram has no star of office, or the star of office is Void or extinct, nothing succeeds. So the classic says: if there is no Pressure in the hexagram and it lies hidden, in a scheme, with the Resource line moving, it is in the end completed only with effort. This is the pattern of a scheme that cannot be planned and is hard to complete.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.85** 谋事的几种格局：内外卦纯阳、用爻旺相出现的，适宜公事，不适宜私事；内外卦纯阴、用爻休囚伏藏的，适宜私事，不适宜公事。内卦生外卦、世爻生应爻，是我去求人；外卦生内卦、应爻生世爻，是人来就我。本卦冲而变卦合、发动克世爻而变出来生世爻的，先难后易；本卦合而变卦冲、发动合世爻而变出来刑世爻的，先易后难。
  Some patterns in a scheme. If the inner and outer trigrams are pure yang and the Subject line is thriving and appears, it is fit for a public matter and not a private one; if the inner and outer are pure yin and the Subject line is resting or trapped and hidden, it is fit for a private matter and not a public one. If the inner generates the outer and the Self generates the Other, I go to ask others; if the outer generates the inner and the Other generates the Self, others come to me. If the original hexagram clashes and the changed hexagram combines, or a moving line controls the Self and changes into one that generates the Self, it is hard at first and easy after; if the original hexagram combines and the changed hexagram clashes, or a moving line combines with the Self and changes into one that punishes the Self, it is easy at first and hard after.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.86** 想知道事情成败在哪个人身上，就用六亲来定：本宫的内卦，是至亲；本宫的外卦，是远亲；他宫的内卦，是邻里；他宫的外卦，是远方的人；官鬼加贵人，是缙绅，否则是牙人、保人、媒人；想知道是哪个方向的人，用八卦来定。又有托人办事的，外人看应爻，亲人看用爻。凡应爻、用爻犯日破、月破、旬空的，都主受托的人没有能力帮忙，空、死、墓、绝、胎也一样；生旺而变成空、死、墓、绝、胎的，必有始无终；冲、克、刑、害世爻的，受托的人必定怀着欺骗，难以托付；六冲的卦，终究反目成仇。白虎发动的，损害；螣蛇发动的，变心；勾陈发动的，愚钝；玄武发动的，欺诈；朱雀旺相落空而发动的，多说谎话。只有旺相、带青龙、喜神、成神，发动来生合世爻、身爻的，才靠他扶持把事办成。
  To know through whom the matter succeeds or fails, fix it by the six kin: the inner trigram of the home palace is the nearest kin; the outer trigram of the home palace, distant kin; the inner trigram of another palace, neighbours; the outer trigram of another palace, men from afar; Pressure with the Noble is gentry, otherwise a broker, a guarantor or a go-between; the direction is fixed by the eight trigrams. If one entrusts someone with the matter, outsiders are seen through the Other and kin through the Subject line. Whenever the Other or the Subject line suffers Day-break, Month-break or decade Void, the man entrusted has no ability to help, and so too for Void, death, tomb, extinction and womb; if thriving and turning into Void, death, tomb, extinction or womb, there is a beginning without an end; if the Self is clashed, controlled, punished or harmed, the man entrusted surely harbours deceit and cannot be relied on; a six-clash hexagram ends in a falling out. If the Tiger moves, he does harm; the Snake, his heart changes; Hook Earth, he is dull; the Dark Tortoise, he cheats; the Bird thriving and Void and moving, he tells many lies. Only if he is thriving and carries the Dragon, Joy and the Completion spirit and moves to generate or combine with the Self and Body does one rely on his support to get the matter done.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.02** 子孙持世，或者在卦中发动，升迁选官没有指望；官星落在空、绝、墓、破上，或者发动变出凶爻，补缺升迁还早得很。
  If Output holds the Self or moves in the hexagram, promotion and selection have no hope; if the star of office falls on Void, extinction, tomb or break, or moves and turns into a bad line, it is still early for an appointment or promotion.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.03** 世爻与官星三合成官局或财局，或者这个局生世爻，或者世爻在局中，或者官星持世、又与日月动爻相合，得到六合卦，以及发动变成六合卦的，都是得到皇恩的日子。但如果官局生的是应爻、不是世爻，这叫「出现无情」，这个缺得不到。
  If the Self and the star of office form a three-harmony Pressure frame or Wealth frame, or the frame generates the Self, or the Self is within the frame, or the star of office holds the Self and combines with the Day, the Month or a moving line, or one gets a Six-Combine hexagram or a hexagram that moves into Six-Combine, it is the day of receiving the ruler's favour. But if the Pressure frame generates the Other and not the Self, this is called 'appearing but without feeling', and the post cannot be had.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.04** 时机未到的征兆：世爻、官星都不旺，又得六冲卦，或者官星被日月相冲，或者六冲变六冲；元神衰弱安静，或者泄元神之气的爻发动，问升选就没有日期。反过来，世爻发动、官星变成进神，升迁就在眼前；世爻发动或变吉，或禄、马、贵人临世，或官星发动变吉、生合世爻，都可以收拾行装等着。
  Signs that the moment has not come: the Self and the star of office are not thriving and the hexagram is Six-Clash, or the star of office is clashed by the Day or Month, or a Six-Clash hexagram turns into Six-Clash; or the Support is weak and still, or the line that drains it moves, and then promotion has no date. The reverse: if the Self moves and the star of office turns into an Advancing line, promotion is at hand; if the Self moves, or turns auspicious, or Emolument, the Horse and a Noble sit on the Self, or the star of office moves, turns auspicious and generates or combines with the Self, one may pack one's bags and wait.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.05** 世爻或官星被破就不要指望，世爻上的子孙落空却也能升。世爻落在月破上，即使官星持世也升不了，还要防别的变故；子孙持世又遇旬空，也不该升。但如果占的是两三天之内的事，可以答应一定得到，因为子孙还没有出空、没有克官星。作者占过三个人，都得了官，得官后随即死了。
  If the Self or the star of office is broken, give up hope; if the Self sits on an Output line that is Void, one is promoted all the same. If the Self is Month-broken, one cannot be promoted even when the star of office holds the Self, and one must guard against other changes; if Output holds the Self and meets Void, one should not be promoted either. But if the question concerns the next two or three days, one may grant that it will surely be had, since Output has not yet left the Void and does not yet control the star of office. The author divined for three men; all got office, and all died soon after getting it.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.22** 用钱买官（援例）：妻财旺相、世爻旺相，但官星落在月破、旬空上，或被日月冲克，以及发动变凶，官星像朽木枯枝，有再旺相的财也生不起它，到头来只是个有钱的平民，吃不上国家的俸禄。
  Buying office by precedent: if Wealth and the Self are thriving but the star of office falls in Month-break or Void, or is clashed or controlled by the Day or Month, or moves and turns bad, the star of office is like a rotten log and a dry branch: though Wealth is thriving it cannot raise it, and in the end one is only a rich man in a commoner's house who cannot eat the heavenly salary of the royal house.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.23** 援例捐官，世爻、官星、财星这三样不能有一样失陷：妻财、官星都旺而世爻失陷，终究得了官也没有福气安享；官星、世爻都旺而财星失陷，是有例可援却没有钱。
  In buying office by precedent, of the Self, the star of office and the star of Wealth, not one may be lost: if Wealth and the star of office are both thriving but the Self is lost, one may in the end get office but has no fortune to enjoy it in peace; if the star of office and the Self are both thriving but the star of Wealth is lost, there is a precedent to appeal to but no money.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.24** 援例捐官，子孙持世，不管财爻动不动，都不可以去办；财爻落空落破，也不可以去办；但子孙、财、官一起发动又可以去办，因为子孙动生财、财动生官。
  In buying office by precedent, if Output holds the Self, whether or not the Wealth line moves it should not be done; if Wealth falls in Void or break, it should not be done either; but if Output, Wealth and the star of office all move together, it may be done, because Output moves to generate Wealth and Wealth moves to generate the star of office.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.25** 援例捐官，兄弟爻持世，或者兄爻发动，是破耗的神，白花钱财，功名终究不成；如果和官星一起发动，又可以去办，因为官鬼能制住兄弟。野鹤说：卦中官星不动，只要遇到兄弟持世、兄爻和父母爻发动、以及财爻持世变出兄弟，百件里没有一件成，即使成了也必有别的缘故，到头来没有地方吃俸禄。
  In buying office by precedent, if the Peer line holds the Self or a Peer line moves, it is a spirit of waste and loss: the money is spent for nothing and the rank is never gained; if it moves together with the star of office it may be done, since Pressure restrains Peer. The Old Man of Yehe says: if the star of office does not move in the hexagram, and Peer holds the Self, or Peer and Resource lines move, or the Wealth line holds the Self and turns into Peer, not one in a hundred succeeds, and even if it succeeds there will surely be some other cause, and in the end one has no place to eat a salary.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.28** 上书奏事，父母爻就是文书、奏章：旺相的父母爻持世、合世，奏章必定被批准；再得到太岁、五爻和日月、动爻来生合的，被采用无疑。
  In submitting a memorial the Resource line is the document and the memorial: if a thriving Resource line holds or combines with the Self, the memorial will surely be approved, and if Tai Sui, the fifth line, the Day, the Month or a moving line also generates or combines with it, it will be adopted without doubt.
