- **LY.CAREER.ZS.29** 兄弟临身时，喜欢父母爻变吉并来拱合：兄弟持世，有日月生它，或者世爻、父母爻发动变吉，或者世爻变出父母爻来生世，都能使皇上龙颜大悦，因为奏章被采用。
  If Peer sits on the Body, it is good that the Resource line turns auspicious and holds up and combines: if Peer holds the Self and the Day and Month generate it, or the Self or the Resource line moves and turns auspicious, or the Self turns into Resource to generate the Self, all of these please the dragon countenance, and one is employed because of the document.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.33** 条陈将相、诤谏君过、弹劾奸臣这类事，和一般的奏事不一样：这里子孙要当福神用，所以反而喜欢子孙持世，即使当面诤谏也可以保证没有祸。但有官职的人如果只是为了保全功名，子孙发动时千万不要去做。古人以子孙在乾宫发动为吉，是不对的，子孙是制住官鬼的福神，没有听说只在乾宫才是福神。
  For proposals about the generals and chancellors, remonstrance against the ruler's faults and the impeachment of a treacherous minister, the rule differs from ordinary memorials: here Output is used as the lucky spirit, so Output holding the Self is welcome instead, and even if one contends to the ruler's face one can be sure of no misfortune. But an official who only wants to preserve his rank must not do it when Output moves. The ancients took Output moving in the Qian palace as auspicious, which is wrong: Output is the lucky spirit that restrains Pressure, and it has never been heard that it is so only in the Qian palace.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.36** 上书求赠爵封官：父母旺相、官星生它，或者旺相的官星持世，或者旺相的父母持世、官星发动生它，或者旺官持世、有日月生它，做官的就官上加官，读书人就位至台阁，平民得到这个卦，立刻平步青云。野鹤说：断卦最看重的是来占的人心里的那个念头；借上书当阶梯来求功名的人，官星旺相而文书不旺，也可以答应他得到赠官封职，因为他本来的念头是为了得官，文书只是敲门砖，所以看重官星而不看重父母。
  On submitting a memorial to win a bestowed rank or office: if Resource is thriving and the star of office generates it, or a thriving star of office holds the Self, or a thriving Resource holds the Self and the star of office moves to generate it, or a thriving star of office holds the Self and the Day and Month generate it, an official gets office added to office, a scholar reaches the high ministries, and a commoner who gets it at once climbs to the clouds. The Old Man of Yehe says: the most important thing in judging a hexagram is the one thought of the person who comes; for one who uses a memorial as a ladder to seek a name, if the star of office is thriving though the document is not, one may still grant that he will be given a post and title, because his first thought was to gain office and the document is only the tile to knock at the door, so the star of office is weighed and not Resource.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.51** 看工程牢不牢：工程都以父爻为用神；用神不旺，或者受冲克，又得六冲卦的，必定不坚固长久，变冲的也一样；还要看世爻旺不旺，世爻如果休囚被克，将来终究要遭它的害；只有父爻旺相、又得六合卦的，才永远坚固牢靠。
  To tell whether a work will be firm: in construction the Resource line is always the Subject; if the Subject is not thriving, or is clashed or controlled, and the hexagram is also Six-Clash, the work will surely not be firm and lasting, and so also when it turns into a clash; one must also see whether the Self is thriving, for if the Self is resting or trapped and controlled one will in the end suffer harm from it; only when the Resource line is thriving and the hexagram is also Six-Combine will it be firm and secure for ever.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.55** 儿子占父亲的功名：父母旺相、官星发动相生，或日月作官星来生父母，或父母旺相变出官星，或父母、官星发动变吉，都可以答应成名；财克父母、子孙发动伤官星、父母和官星发动变凶、日月克官星克父母，或者父爻、官爻衰弱入墓被破入绝，都主没有益处。
  A son divining about his father's career: if Resource is thriving and the star of office moves to generate it, or the Day or Month acts as the star of office and generates Resource, or a thriving Resource turns into the star of office, or Resource and the star of office move and turn auspicious, he may be granted a name; if Wealth controls Resource, Output moves to injure the star of office, Resource and the star of office move and turn bad, the Day or Month controls the star of office and Resource, or the Resource line or the Pressure line is weak, in tomb, broken or extinct, all mean no gain.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.01** 占求官时，遇到姤卦（求官，官位荣耀）、观卦（求官最好）、渐卦（求官渐渐升迁）、鼎卦（求官十全）、家人卦（谒见贵人、求官，获福无量），都是求官有利。
  In seeking office, meeting the hexagram Gou ('seeking office: the office is glorious'), Guan ('seeking office: the best'), Jian ('seeking office: gradual promotion'), Ding ('seeking office: complete in all ten parts') or Jiaren ('calling on nobles and seeking office, one wins blessing beyond measure') is favourable to seeking office.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.04** 遇到屯卦，见贵人、求官先难后易；遇到师卦，求官虽吉，见贵人却会遭到嗔怪；遇到井卦，不宜妄动，求官谒贵要藏好器具等待时机。
  Meeting the hexagram Zhun, in seeing a noble and seeking office it is first hard, then easy; meeting Shi, seeking office is auspicious but seeing a noble draws his anger; meeting Jing, rash action does not suit, and in seeking office and calling on nobles one should keep the tools hidden and wait for the time.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.06** 官鬼持世，必定得官，文书、印信两样一起看；官化官是得禄，求官宜赶快；化财，占病凶；化父，文书相随；化子，必伤官；化兄，家里不和。子孙持世是福神，事成忧散，占胎问病重重吉利，谒见贵人、求官反而不亨通。父母持世及身宫，旺相，文书、喜信通达；父母化父母，文书一定应许；化子添人口；化鬼，自身得迁升荐举；化财，住宅长忧；兄弟，要从本身去取。
  With Pressure holding the Self one is sure to get office, and the documents and the seal are looked at together; Pressure turning into Pressure is gaining emolument, and in seeking office one should be swift; turning into Wealth, in illness it is bad; turning into Resource, the documents follow one after another; turning into Output, it surely injures office; turning into Peer, the household is not harmonious. Output holding the Self is the spirit of blessing: matters succeed and worries scatter, and in pregnancy and illness it is good upon good, but in calling on a noble or seeking office it does not go through. With Resource holding the Self and the palace of the Body, strong, documents and glad news go through; Resource turning into Resource, the documents are sure to be granted; turning into Output, people are added; turning into Pressure, one is promoted and recommended; turning into Wealth, the dwelling long worries; for Peer, take it from oneself.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.07** 青龙发动，万事通达，进财进禄，临在凶上、遇杀都无妨碍，只忌落在金上和落空；朱雀交重，文书印绶旺，和杀神并在一起就白劳功夫，是非口舌都由此而来，持水、临空反而有利于公事；白虎交重，是惊恐怪异之事，持金、世上带杀妨碍人口，求官时临在官鬼上，反而丰盛隆盛。
  With the Azure Dragon moving, all matters go through and wealth and emolument come in, and standing on ill or meeting a baleful spirit it meets no obstruction, only avoid its standing on Metal or falling void; with the Vermilion Bird moving heavily the documents and the seal and ribbon are strong, joined with a baleful spirit the labour is spent in vain, quarrels and gossip all come from this, and holding Water or standing in the void it is on the contrary favourable to public business; with the White Tiger moving heavily it is fright and strange things, holding Metal with a baleful spirit on the Self harms the household, and in seeking office, standing on Pressure, it brings abundance and prosperity on the contrary.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.09** 玄武落在官鬼上，是奸和盗；两重官鬼，必有忧愁丧事；但如果占的是官府（的事），反而是吉：福气厚、官位高、名誉远扬。
  The Dark Tortoise on Pressure means treachery and theft, and two layers of Pressure bring worry and mourning; but if one divines about officialdom it is on the contrary auspicious: blessing thick, office high and fame spread.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.12** 子孙发动、妻财交动，是要还俗的汉子；世空、身旺，是地上的行仙；身空而不动，事事都是道；应旺而没有魔，处处都是禅；应落偶位、世持住，又生又合，精神清明、身体安静，寿命绵长；忽然有负债纠结、财和身一起发动，免不了去偿还俗世的债和冤；世、应不和、身又乱动，想了身达命要等到驴年（永远等不到）；本身、太岁落在身和命上，当天就遇上神人、得到真传；太岁、火神加在父母上，天边的恩命一定会来宣布。
  Output moving with Wealth also moving: a man who will return to lay life; the Self void and the Body strong makes an earth-wandering immortal; the Body void and still, every single thing is the Way; the Other strong with no demon, Chan is everywhere; the Other on an even place and the Self holding, generating and combining, the spirit clear and the body at peace, life is long; if suddenly there is a debt-knot and Wealth and the Body move, one cannot avoid paying back worldly debts and grievances; with Self and Other at odds and the Body moving wildly, to finish with the body and reach one's destiny one must wait for the Year of the Donkey; the person himself and Tai Sui on the Body and one's fate, on that very day one meets a divine person and receives the true transmission; Tai Sui and the Fire spirit added to Resource, a favour commanded from the edge of heaven will surely be proclaimed.

## 动变(Moving lines and what they turn into)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/动变.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.05** 安静的爻被日辰冲到，叫暗动，有吉有凶。求官遇到暗动：吉的话有意想不到的机会，凶的话暗中有人损害。
  A still line clashed by the Day is said to move in secret, and it may be auspicious or inauspicious. In seeking office, meeting this: if auspicious there is a chance one did not seek; if inauspicious someone harms one in secret.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.84** 官鬼化成子孙爻，推荐贤能就不被听从；官爻变成财的象，奖励就能被应允。荐举和奖励，都以官爻为主：旺的来得快，衰的来得迟；官爻发动化成子孙，即使有人推荐也不被听信；财爻来帮助官鬼，奖励就能如愿。
  If Pressure changes into an Output line, the recommendation of the worthy is not heeded; if the star of office changes into the Wealth image, the award can be granted. Both recommendation and award take the star of office as the chief: if it is thriving it comes quickly, if weak it comes late; if it moves and changes into Output, even if one is recommended it is not heeded and believed; if Wealth comes to aid Pressure, the award is obtained as one wishes.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.09** 父母化父母，文书是虚的、不实在；官鬼化官鬼，事情翻腾反复。
  Resource turning into Resource, the documents are hollow and not solid; Pressure turning into Pressure, the matter churns and reverses.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.06** 古人把「随官入墓」「助鬼伤世」一概当凶，作者多次试验，结果是：世爻旺相的反而升官；世爻衰弱的不吉；助鬼伤身而元神同时发动的，官职必定升；世爻如果休囚，必有灾祸。
  The ancients took 'following the star of office into the tomb' and 'helping Pressure to injure the Self' as ominous without exception; the author tested it many times and found: where the Self is thriving the man is promoted instead; where the Self is weak it is not auspicious; where one helps Pressure to injure the Body but the Support moves at the same time, the office is surely raised; if the Self is resting or trapped, disaster is sure to come.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.16** 爻遇反吟或入三墓，主反复不定、昏庸糊涂。卦得反吟：世爻、官爻旺相，一定升迁；世爻、官星衰弱，必遭降职处罚，降后还要焦虑；世爻或官星被冲被克，有想不到的灾祸。世爻休囚被克、变墓或入墓，立刻见到灾祸是非；世爻有气而变墓、入墓，不过是糊里糊涂、昏聩罢了。
  Lines in Reversed Chant, or entering the three tombs, mean going back and forth, muddle and dullness. If the hexagram gets Reversed Chant: with the Self and the Pressure line thriving one is surely promoted; with the Self and the star of office weak one is demoted and punished and anxious after the demotion; if the Self or the star of office is clashed or controlled, there is unforeseen calamity. If the Self is resting or trapped and controlled, turns into the tomb or enters the tomb, disaster and trouble appear at once; if the Self has strength and turns into the tomb or enters the tomb, it is no more than being hazy and befuddled.

## 升迁(Promotion, transfer, posting and rank: which way, how far)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/升迁.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.10** 月建落在用爻上，求官一定升到高的职位。
  When the Month falls on the Subject line, one seeking office will surely be promoted to a high post.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.01** 占进职、升官，先看父母爻发动，其次看官鬼；父母和官鬼旺相兴盛，这事才有你的份；兄弟、妻财、子孙发动，只是白欢喜一场。
  To divine advance in post or promotion in rank, look first for Resource moving and then at Pressure; where Resource and Pressure are strong and flourishing there is a share for you, and where Peer, Wealth or Output moves the joy is in vain.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.03** 驿马去扶助世身，或者落在官位上，一定升迁；世、应和驿马相合，求官大吉大利。
  The Post Horse supporting the self, or standing on the seat of office, means promotion for sure; Self and Other combining with the Post Horse is greatly favourable for seeking office.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.14** 官鬼落在唐符、国印上最好，日后可在朝中做辅佐；唐符、国印这两爻在上卦发动，一定符印到手，不久随诏书起用；「宜静而动必升迁，宜动而静多掩抑」。
  Pressure standing on the Tangfu or the State Seal is the best, one who will serve as a support at court; when these two lines move in the upper trigram the tally and seal are surely in hand and one will soon be raised by imperial edict; 'what should be still but moves will surely rise; what should move but stays still is mostly held down'.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.20** 卦里再得雷火杀、重重叠叠，又逢发动，是大吉的征兆，眼看名位将到王公。旧注给了雷火杀的起法，但前后有两种说法并存。
  If the hexagram further gets the Thunder-Fire baleful spirit, layer upon layer, and it moves, it is a sign of great fortune, and soon rank and position reach prince and duke. The note gives the method for finding the Thunder-Fire spirit, but two accounts of it stand side by side.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.02** 求官时，官鬼落在吏位（初爻）来克身，是贤官之象；驿马相扶，官职迁动；坎、离二卦宜在冬天三个月，震、巽二卦应在申、酉年；世、应和驿马相合是吉；坎、咸、复三卦主狐疑；申、酉、辰、戌持世是吉，做官又遇到禄，好施展作为。
  In seeking office, Pressure on the clerk line (the first) and controlling the self is the sign of a worthy official; the Post Horse lending support, the post is moved up; the two trigrams Kan and Li suit the three winter months and Zhen and Xun the years of Shen and You; Self and Other combining with the Post Horse is auspicious; the hexagrams Kan, Xian and Fu bring doubt and hesitation; Shen, You, Chen and Xu holding the Self is auspicious, and taking office and meeting the Emolument gives room to act well.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.05** 不论文官武职，求官要看唐符、国印：寅、申年看巳、亥，巳、亥年看寅、申，子、午年看卯、酉，卯、酉年看子、午，辰、戌年看丑、未，丑、未年看辰、戌。注文说：寅、申年遇到巳、亥爻发动、旺相，是吉；巳、亥年遇到寅、申爻发动、旺相，是吉。官鬼落在符、印上最好，可以做朝廷的辅佐；「宜静而动必左迁，宜动而静多掩抑」。
  Whether it is a civil or a military post, in seeking office look to Tangfu and the State Seal: in the years Yin and Shen look to Si and Hai, in Si and Hai years to Yin and Shen, in Zi and Wu years to Mao and You, in Mao and You years to Zi and Wu, in Chen and Xu years to Chou and Wei, in Chou and Wei years to Chen and Xu. The notes say: in the years Yin and Shen, if the Si or Hai line moves and is strong, it is auspicious; in the years Si and Hai, if the Yin or Shen line moves and is strong, it is auspicious. Pressure standing on the tally and the seal is best, one who will serve as a support at court; 'what should be still but moves will surely be demoted; what should move but stays still is mostly held down'.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.10** 卦里再得雷火杀、重重叠叠，眼看名位将到王公；天乙贵人再来相合，世世代代做官、受封赠。这是天纲玄妙的法门，请你仔细抽绎，不要匆忙。注文给了鬼谷子的雷火杀例：雷火在寅、亥、申、巳里找；它的算法是正月起寅、二月起丑、三月起子、四月起亥、五月起戌、六月起酉、七月起申、八月起未、九月起午、十月起巳、十一月起辰、十二月起卯，逆数十二位。
  If the hexagram further gets the Thunder-Fire baleful spirit, layer upon layer, soon rank and position reach prince and duke; if the Tianyi Noble further combines, one holds office and receives imperial honours generation after generation. This is the profound method of Tiangang, and I ask you to unravel it carefully and not in haste. The note gives the Guigu-zi rule for the Thunder-Fire spirit: look for it among Yin, Hai, Shen and Si; its method is Yin in the first month, Chou in the second, Zi in the third, Hai in the fourth, Xu in the fifth, You in the sixth, Shen in the seventh, Wei in the eighth, Wu in the ninth, Si in the tenth, Chen in the eleventh and Mao in the twelfth, counting backwards through the twelve places.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.16** 占加官（加官进爵），看两个官鬼：一个鬼来持卦身，太岁和鬼并在一起，月建和鬼并在一起（原文「基阁」「外基」难解）。注文说：卦中官鬼爻持世或持卦身，求官可以中选，已有职位的人还可能升官；比如升卦，两个官都是酉金，临月卦身，世爻丑土是金的墓库，是有职位的人可以加官的兆头。
  In divining the adding of office, look at two Pressure lines: one Pressure line comes to hold the Body of the hexagram, Tai Sui joins with Pressure, and the Month joins with Pressure (the 'jige' and 'outer ji' of the text are unclear). The note says: if the Pressure line in the hexagram holds the Self or holds the Body, one seeking office can be selected, and one who already has a post may be promoted; for example in the hexagram Sheng the two Pressure lines are You Metal standing on the monthly Body, and the Self line Chou Earth is the tomb of Metal, a sign that one with a post can be given more office.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.23** 驿马、龙德和青龙，最旺的时候，加官升迁、禄位荣耀；飞、伏的六爻要细细取用，要知道四直，得用心去研究；还要有印绶和各种德神，逢生旺、值时，必定显达亨通。例：从前道明占得剥之晋卦，癸酉（日）无望，丙子（日）才遇上。
  The Post Horse, Dragon Virtue and the Azure Dragon, at their strongest, bring promotion and glory of emolument and rank; the six lines, flying and hidden, should be taken with care, and one must study the four rulers closely; besides, with the seal and ribbon and the various Virtues, meeting birth and strength at the right time one is sure to rise to prominence. For example, in the past Daoming got the hexagram Bo changing to Jin, with no prospect on Guiyou and meeting it on Bingzi.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.29** 印绶落在青龙上，飞上高位，求官很快就能得到，不会蹉跎。
  With the seal and ribbon on the Dragon, one flies up to a high place, and the office is soon gained, without delay.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.55** 占官职升迁，以官爻为主，以世爻为凭据。子孙是官鬼的忌神，不宜刚旺发动；只求官鬼发动才好。官鬼得生、得旺，就该迁转升调；官鬼落陷、落空，就暂且守着原职、照常。官鬼是主象，最有利的是它发动；如果临生旺，一定主高升；如果遇到空亡，就不能迁转。
  In divining promotion in office, take the Pressure line as the chief and the Self image as the ground. Output is the Adversary of Pressure and must not be strong or move; one asks only that Pressure move, which is good. If Pressure is generated or thriving, one should expect to be promoted and transferred; if Pressure is sunk or Void, for the time being one keeps to the usual and holds one's post. Pressure is the chief image and is most favoured when it moves; if it meets generation or thriving, it surely means a high promotion; if it meets Void, there will be no promotion or transfer.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.56** 官鬼化成子孙，就要担忧被调、被降；官鬼连着财位（化成财），就沐浴恩光。具体说：官鬼化成子孙，不是降职就是调走，卦里财爻、子孙发动也是这样；但如果财爻、子孙一起发动，转过来帮助官爻，就又不降不调；官鬼化成财爻，是在原品级上平升；官爻化成进气的官爻，又主连升、超升到好的职位。
  If Pressure changes into Output, fear being transferred or demoted; if Pressure is joined to the Wealth place, one bathes in the glow of favour. In detail: if Pressure changes into Output it is either demotion or transfer, and the same if Wealth and Output move in the hexagram; but if Wealth and Output move together and turn to aid the Pressure line, there is then no demotion or transfer; if Pressure changes into Wealth, one is raised on a level in grade; if Pressure changes into an advancing-qi Pressure line, it again means a double promotion to a fine post.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.57** 妻财是俸禄：财爻化成福象（子孙），就加官衔、赐俸禄；财爻变成兄爻（兄弟），就罢俸、减粮。
  Wealth is the salary: if Wealth changes into the fortune image, Output, rank is added and pay is granted; if Wealth changes into a Peer line, salary is stopped and grain is cut.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.58** 卦得归魂，是回来再任原职；卦得游魂，是迁转到别的地方去。具体说：卜得归魂，还会恢复原任；归魂化成归魂，按理应当退休（致仕）；遇到游魂而官又旺相的，必定升到别的省；游魂化成游魂，升了之后再升到远处。
  A Returning Soul hexagram means returning to the former post; a Wandering Soul means being moved and transferred elsewhere. In detail: if a Returning Soul is cast, one will return to the former post; a Returning Soul changing into a Returning Soul means that by right one should retire from office; if one meets a Wandering Soul and the star of office is again thriving, one is surely raised to another province; a Wandering Soul changing into a Wandering Soul means being promoted and then promoted again to a far place.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.59** 世爻是自己本人的官职：自己占卜，要察看世爻，世爻落空，就不能升迁；替别人占卜，就不取世爻为主，专看官爻，如果得到财爻发动生官爻，自然显达，能去辅佐帝王。
  The Self line is one's own post: in divining for oneself, look at the Self line, and if the Self is Void one cannot be promoted or transferred; in divining on behalf of another, do not take the Self as the chief but look only at the Pressure line, and if Wealth moves to generate Pressure, one naturally rises to eminence and goes on to assist the sovereign.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.60** 官鬼变成退神，适宜退休（致仕）；变入墓、绝，就早早回乡。官鬼爻如果化成退气，或者变入墓、绝之中，就不要指望升迁，只能远远地回到乡里。
  If Pressure changes into a retreating spirit it is fitting to retire from office; if it changes into tomb or extinction, one returns home early. If the Pressure line changes into a retreating qi, or changes into tomb or extinction, do not hope for promotion, but go far away and return to the countryside.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.70** 子孙发动伤克官爻，难以实现参谋的计策；财爻兴旺帮助官鬼，能成就辅佐到底的功劳。官鬼变到福乡（子孙），就顺着降职、调动；官爻变到财地（财爻），就渐渐升迁。官鬼是官职，最怕子孙发动来伤它，偏偏喜欢财爻兴旺帮助官鬼；官鬼变成子孙，不是降职就是调走；官鬼变成财的象，就一再高升。细细玩味占易道理的玄妙精微，就可以断定武官的吉凶。
  If Output moves to hurt the star of office, it is hard to carry out one's counsel as a staff adviser; if Wealth flourishes and aids Pressure, one can accomplish the merit of assisting to the end. If Pressure changes into the fortune quarter, Output, it follows that one is demoted or transferred; if the star of office changes into the Wealth ground, one is gradually promoted. Pressure is the official post, and it most dislikes Output moving to hurt it but particularly likes Wealth flourishing to aid it; if Pressure changes into Output, it is either demotion or transfer; if Pressure changes into the Wealth image, one is promoted again and again. By savouring the subtle principles of the Yi divination, one can decide the good and ill of a military officer.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.03** 赴诏、面见君主，宜官鬼出现；官鬼的墓发动，不必再推。除迁（任命调迁），以官鬼爻持世为宜，官鬼发动、或落在鬼墓上，就不吉利。求职、请求考核评定，在任的人宜官鬼爻安静；官鬼发动，或子孙发动出来，就有人接替。
  For going to court on summons or seeing the sovereign, it is fitting that Pressure appear; the tomb of Pressure moving needs no further reckoning. For appointment and transfer it is right that the Pressure line hold the Self, and if Pressure moves or stands on the tomb of Pressure it is not auspicious. For seeking a post or an appraisal, one in office should have the Pressure line still; if Pressure moves, or Output moves out, there is a replacement.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.06** 官爻是占官的象。它临持身世，或者来生合世爻，又不受月建、日辰冲克，凡有谋求必然称心；如果不上卦，或者落空亡，或者虽然出现却墓绝无气、受克制，都不如意；世爻、身爻被冲克，也凶。
  Pressure is the image in divining an office. If it settles on the Body or the Self, or comes to generate or combine with the Self, and is not clashed or controlled by the Month or the Day, whatever is sought will surely turn out to one's liking; if it is absent from the hexagram or falls Void, or even though it appears is in a tomb or extinction and without breath, or controlled, nothing will be as one wishes; a clash or control on the Self or the Body is also ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.12** 太岁发动，在任的人就有任命、调动；劫杀落空，遇凶也没有祸咎。太岁是地杀的主宰，各神都不敢当它；如果碰上青龙、月建，加在父母、官鬼上发动，即使已在任所，也该升迁到好职位；如果加劫杀，就该受贬责；如果落空亡，遇凶也无咎。
  When Tai Sui moves, one in office gets an appointment or transfer; when the Robbery Killing is void, meeting calamity brings no fault. Tai Sui is the master of the earthly baleful spirits, which no spirit dares withstand; if it meets the Azure Dragon and the Month, added to Resource and Pressure and moving, even one already at his post will be promoted to a fine office; if the Robbery Killing is added, one is to be demoted and reprimanded; if it falls Void, meeting calamity brings no fault.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.42** 问升迁：占得官爻在日、月上，就是越级升迁；得到旺相的官，就是按常规升；得到休囚的官，就是还没转动。旺相又动，尤其快；休囚又静，更迟。遇空就失望，遇破就阻碍图谋，遇散就白求，遇绝就难开始，遇世空，这一占就不会如愿。不要说官伏就不升，得到生助也升；不要说子动就不升，得日月相助也升；不要说被冲被合就不升，有动爻也升；不要说随墓助伤就不升，得旺相也升。只看官爻；替别人占，就不忌世空。
  In asking about promotion: if one gets the Pressure line on the Day or the Month, it is promotion over the heads of others; if one gets thriving, strong Pressure, it is promotion in the ordinary course; if one gets resting, imprisoned Pressure, there is not yet a move. Thriving and moving is especially fast; resting and still is slower still. Meeting Void, one is disappointed; meeting broken, the plan is obstructed; meeting scattered, the seeking is in vain; meeting extinct, it is hard to begin; and with the Self Void the divination does not come to pass. Do not say that Pressure hidden means no promotion: with help it is promoted too; do not say that Output moving means none: with the Day and Month it is promoted too; do not say that being clashed or combined means none: with a moving line it is promoted too; do not say that following into the tomb and helping the injury means none: with thriving strength it is promoted too. Look only at the Pressure line; in divining for another, the Self being Void is not feared.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.109** 于是占出去求事的，也察看用爻：求事于科第，就考察文书；求事于升迁，就考察官鬼，而不要世空；求事于寻访，考察用爻；求事于货利，考察财象；这是专就事来占，而不专就出行来占。
  So in divining about going out to seek something, one also examines the Subject line: for seeking something in the examinations, one examines the document line; for seeking something in promotion, one examines Pressure, and the Self must not be Void; for seeking something by searching and visiting, one examines the Subject line; for seeking something in goods and profit, one examines the Wealth image; this goes by the matter and not by the travelling.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.11** 任所的远近：世爻在一、二位的近，三、四位的远，五、六位的更远；六合卦近，六冲卦远。
  The distance of the post: if the Self is in the first or second place it is near, in the third or fourth far, and in the fifth or sixth farther still; a Six-Combine hexagram is near and a Six-Clash hexagram far.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.12** 任所在什么地方，按世爻所属的地支和星宿分野来定：世属子，是坎宫、女宿虚宿危宿三宿的分野，今山东济南等五府；世属丑，是艮宫、牛宿斗宿的分野，今南京的应天、扬州等府，浙江、江西、福建、广东诸府；世属寅，是艮宫、尾宿箕宿的分野，今北京顺天等府和辽东沈阳；世属卯，是震宫、氐房心三宿的分野，今南京徐州、淮安；世属辰，是巽宫、角亢二宿的分野，今山东兖州府；世属巳，是巽宫、翼轸二宿的分野，今湖广诸府、四川夔州府、贵州铜仁黎平、广东廉州、广西诸府；世属午，是离宫、柳星张三宿的分野，今河南河南府、南阳府和湖广郧阳、襄阳、德安三府；世属未，是坤宫、井鬼二宿的分野，今陕西、甘肃、四川、云南、贵州的诸府州；世属申，是坤宫、毕觜参三宿的分野，今山西太原、平阳等地；世属酉，是兑宫、胃昴二宿的分野，今北直真定、顺德二府和山西大同府；世属戌，是乾宫、奎娄二宿的分野，今南京凤阳、庐州二府和徐州；世属亥，是乾宫、室壁二宿的分野，今河南开封、彰德、卫辉、怀庆、汝宁等府和北直大名府。
  The region of the post is fixed by the branch the Self belongs to and the allotted lunar mansions: Zi is the Kan palace and the mansions Nü, Xu and Wei, today five prefectures of Shandong; Chou is the Gen palace and the mansions Niu and Dou, today the prefectures of the southern capital region such as Yingtian and Yangzhou and those of Zhejiang, Jiangxi, Fujian and Guangdong; Yin is the Gen palace and the mansions Wei (Tail) and Ji, today the prefectures of Beijing such as Shuntian, with Liaodong and Shenyang; Mao is the Zhen palace and the mansions Di, Fang and Xin, today Xuzhou and Huai'an in the southern capital region; Chen is the Xun palace and the mansions Jiao and Kang, today Yanzhou prefecture in Shandong; Si is the Xun palace and the mansions Yi and Zhen, today the prefectures of Huguang, Kuizhou in Sichuan, Tongren and Liping in Guizhou, Lianzhou in Guangdong and the prefectures of Guangxi; Wu is the Li palace and the mansions Liu, Xing and Zhang, today Henan and Nanyang prefectures in Henan and Yunyang, Xiangyang and De'an in Huguang; Wei is the Kun palace and the mansions Jing and Gui, today the prefectures and departments of Shaanxi, Gansu, Sichuan, Yunnan and Guizhou; Shen is the Kun palace and the mansions Bi, Zi and Shen, today Taiyuan, Pingyang and other places in Shanxi; You is the Dui palace and the mansions Wei (Stomach) and Mao, today Zhending and Shunde in the northern metropolitan region and Datong prefecture in Shanxi; Xu is the Qian palace and the mansions Kui and Lou, today Fengyang and Luzhou in the southern capital region and Xuzhou; Hai is the Qian palace and the mansions Shi and Bi, today Kaifeng, Zhangde, Weihui, Huaiqing and Runing in Henan and Daming prefecture in the northern metropolitan region.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.16** 在任，差使和升黜：道路这一爻落空，出外巡视的使者多受惊吓；子孙旺相发动，负责剿捕的官员奏报凯旋；太岁伤官星，在朝为官的必被罢黜；月建、日辰生世爻，外任的官可以升迁。
  In office, commissions, promotion and dismissal. If the road line is Void, the envoy touring his district is often alarmed; if Output is thriving and moving, the officer in charge of suppressing bandits reports victory; if Tai Sui injures the star of office, an official at court is surely dismissed; if the Month and Day generate the Self, an official in a post outside can be promoted.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.21** 升迁，世应论君臣：世爻是臣子，应爻是君上；世爻、应爻相生、相合、比和的，升迁；世爻生应爻，是我自己谋求进用，应爻生世爻，是别人荐举我；应爻冲克世爻，是外放任命，应爻生合世爻，是在京内任命。
  Promotion, the Self and the Other as subject and ruler. The Self is the subject and the Other is the ruler; if the Self and the Other generate, combine or are in harmony, he is promoted; if the Self generates the Other, it is I who seek advancement, and if the Other generates the Self, others recommend me; if the Other clashes with or controls the Self, he is appointed outside, and if the Other generates or combines with the Self, he is appointed within the capital.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.22** 升迁的格局：世爻、官星旺相发动的，升；财爻、子孙一起发动的，升；财爻单独发动的，升；太岁、月建生扶身爻、世爻的，升；官星临唐符、国印而发动的，升；官鬼带朱雀、带雷火杀发动的，升；官星变财爻的，平稳而升；官鬼变官鬼遇到进神的，越级升迁；遇到退神的，调到事务简单的缺；归魂卦、官爻旺相安静的，必定复任；游魂卦、官爻旺相发动的，必定升到远处；游魂变游魂的，升了再升，而且在远处；卦里有两个父爻、两个官爻旺相发动的，是鸳鸯求仕。
  The patterns of promotion. If the Self and the star of office are thriving and moving, he is promoted; if Wealth and Output move together, he is promoted; if Wealth moves alone, he is promoted; if Tai Sui and the Month generate or support the Body and Self, he is promoted; if the star of office sits on Tangfu or the State Seal and moves, he is promoted; if Pressure with the Bird and the Thunder-Fire spirit moves, he is promoted; if the star of office turns into Wealth, he is promoted smoothly; if Pressure turns into Pressure and meets an Advancing spirit, he is promoted beyond his rank; if it meets a Retreating spirit, he is transferred to an easier post; in a Returning Soul hexagram with the Pressure line thriving and still, he returns to his post; in a Wandering Soul hexagram with the Pressure line thriving and moving, he is promoted far away; if a Wandering Soul turns into a Wandering Soul, he is promoted and then promoted again, in a distant place; if the hexagram has two seal lines and two star-of-office lines thriving and moving, it is 'mandarin ducks seeking office'.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.23** 升迁，守旧还是图新，以及不升的情形：内卦旺相、外卦衰弱，适宜守着旧职；内卦衰弱、外卦旺相，适宜谋求新职；内外都旺相，两头都称心；朱雀临在门户爻上发动的，喜报到来；世爻落空、官星落空，或者官爻安静伏藏，以及身爻发动、刑到太岁月建的，都不升迁。
  Promotion, keeping the old post or seeking a new one, and when there is no promotion. If the inside is thriving and the outside weak, it is fitting to keep the old post; if the inside is weak and the outside thriving, it is fitting to seek a new post; if both are thriving, either way goes as wished; if the Bird sits on the door-and-gate line and it moves, good news arrives; if the Self is Void and the star of office is Void, or the Pressure line is still and hidden, or the Body moves to punish Tai Sui and the Month, in every case he is not promoted.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.08** 按官星的五行断去哪个方位上任：金官应西，木官应东，水应北，火应南，土官升到中土。野鹤说这是古法，他验证过的有出入：木官应在山东，也有应在广东的；金官应在山西，也有应在江西、广西的；还有按所住的地方来分东西南北的。
  To judge in which direction the post lies from the element of the star of office: a Metal one is due in the west, a Wood one in the east, Water in the north, Fire in the south, and an Earth one is promoted to the central land. The Old Man of Yehe says this is the old method and that what he has confirmed does not quite agree: a Wood star of office is due in Shandong, but sometimes in Guangdong; a Metal one in Shanxi, but sometimes in Jiangxi or Guangxi; and sometimes the directions are divided according to the place where one lives.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.09** 看是内地还是边远：初爻代表内地，五爻、六爻代表边远地方。野鹤解释：「应在初爻」是世爻和官星都在初爻；「应在五、六爻」是世在五爻官在六爻，或世在六爻官在五爻，或官星持世、一起在五、六爻上；如果在二、三、四爻，仍然按五行来决断。
  To tell whether the post is inland or on the frontier: the first line is the inner land and the fifth and sixth lines are the frontier. The Old Man of Yehe explains: 'due in the first line' means the Self and the star of office are both in the first line; 'due in the fifth or sixth line' means the Self is in the fifth line and the star of office in the sixth, or the Self in the sixth and the star of office in the fifth, or the star of office holds the Self and both are in the fifth or sixth line; if they are in the second, third or fourth line, one still decides by the five elements.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.10** 占升选，与其问「升到哪个方向」，不如指明具体的缺来问：这个缺我能得吗？我能进翰林吗？我能做部属吗？我能做科道官吗？我能做正印官吗？官星持世、官星发动生合世爻、三合官局生世，都主必得；世爻落空落破、官星落破落空、子孙持世、子孙发动、官星临在应爻上、三合官局生应爻，都主得不到。指明了缺就已经知道在哪里，不必多此一举。
  In a question about promotion, rather than asking 'in which direction', it is better to name the post and ask: can I get this post? Can I enter the Hanlin Academy? Can I get a post in a ministry? Can I become a supervising censor? Can I become the chief official holding the seal? If the star of office holds the Self, or the star of office moves to generate or combine with the Self, or a three-harmony Pressure frame generates the Self, it will surely be had; if the Self is Void or broken, the star of office is broken or Void, Output holds the Self, Output moves, the star of office sits on the Other, or a three-harmony Pressure frame generates the Other, it will not be had. Once the post is named its place is already known, and the extra step is unnecessary.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.15** 在任时，变出进神，或冲变成合，都是加官晋爵的征兆：世爻旺相、官星变进神；官星旺相、世爻变进神；世爻与官星都得地、财爻变进神。
  In office, turning into an Advancing line, or a clash turning into a combination, are signs of added office and higher rank: the Self thriving and the star of office turning into an Advancing line; the star of office thriving and the Self turning into an Advancing line; or the Self and the star of office both well placed and Wealth turning into an Advancing line.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.54** 断官能做到几品：古法按五行的成数，水一、火二、木三、金四、土五，官星临在火上官位二品，官星属水是一品的尊位。野鹤说这个古法不够用：水只有一个数，官位到公侯的怎么确定？官分九品，数只到五，六品、七品的怎么确定？他的办法是指明了去占：我能做公侯将相吗？我能做一品大员吗？我能中鼎甲吗？旺相的官星临日月，指日可待；官星被破或被刑冲，终身失望；子孙持世，就像望梅止渴；官星发动生身，恰似探囊取物。问鼎甲这类具体的事，要另占一卦指明了问：古人以蹇卦为不吉，他只看用神，世临巳火、卯月生申日合、青龙持世，中鼎甲无疑；世爻变出辰土之官，应在辰年，后来果然在甲辰科殿试得传胪第一。
  To judge to what grade an official will rise: the old method uses the numbers of the five elements, Water one, Fire two, Wood three, Metal four, Earth five, so that a star of office on Fire gives the second grade and a Water star of office the honoured first grade. The Old Man of Yehe says this old method is not enough: Water has only the number one, so how is the rank of dukes and marquises to be fixed? There are nine grades of office and the numbers stop at five, so how are the sixth and seventh to be fixed? His method is to name the matter and divine: can I become a duke, marquis, general or chancellor? Can I become a first-grade minister? Can I take a place among the top three? A thriving star of office on the Day or Month can be expected any day; if the star of office is broken, punished or clashed, one is disappointed for life; if Output holds the Self, it is like quenching thirst by gazing at plums; if the star of office moves to generate the Body, it is as easy as taking something from a bag. For a concrete matter such as the top three, another hexagram is cast with the matter named: the ancients took Jian as ominous, and he looked only at the Subject, where the Self on Si Fire generated by the Mao month and combined by the Shen day with the Azure Dragon holding the Self left no doubt of a top place; the Self turned into the Chen Earth star of office, so it fell in a Chen year, and at the palace examination of the Jiachen session he did come first at the announcement.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.56** 占升迁，问什么时候和问什么地方，必须分开占；凭一个卦兼着断，假如官星是申金，必定七月升迁，按分野申是西方晋地，难道七月升迁的缺就一定在西方？这是古法的荒谬。又有按八宫来定的（如离南坎北），有用二十八宿定分野的，有用禄来定方位的，有说游魂卦远、归魂卦近的，都靠不住。《易冒》以五行、六神、八宫为经，众星为纬，可以讲但不能行：五行、八宫、二十八宿还算近理，如果用六神再加星煞，丁日占得恒卦，酉金官星持世，必掌兵权；六神得螣蛇，螣蛇是奔走差遣的吏；丁日以酉为文昌，文昌是翰林院；按这个方法断，就成了又掌兵权、又在翰林、又听差遣的人了，岂不是胡说。
  In divining about promotion, when it comes and where it is must be divined separately; judging both from one hexagram, suppose the star of office is Shen Metal, one would be promoted in the seventh month, and by the division of regions Shen is the land of Jin in the west, but does a promotion in the seventh month have to be a post in the west? This is the absurdity of the old method. Then there is fixing it by the eight palaces (such as Li south and Kan north), or by the twenty-eight lunar mansions, or by the Emolument, or saying a Wandering Soul hexagram is far and a Returning Soul hexagram near, none of which can be relied on. The Yi Mao takes the five elements, the six spirits and the eight palaces as the warp and the stars as the weft, which can be talked about but cannot be practised: the five elements, the eight palaces and the twenty-eight mansions are rather more reasonable, but if the six spirits are joined with the stars and baleful spirits, then on a Ding day with Heng and the You Metal star of office holding the Self he must hold military power; the spirit is the Coiling Snake, an official who runs on commissions; on a Ding day You is the Wenchang star, which is the Hanlin Academy; by this method he would be a man who holds military power, sits in the Hanlin and awaits commissions all at once, and is that not idle talk?
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.02** 占官职、升迁，遇到乾卦（占官转职）、晋卦（进必有升）、大畜卦（加官转职）、中孚卦（官贵升迁）、损卦（官旺财兴），都主官职有转迁、升进。
  In divining office and promotion, meeting the hexagram Qian ('asking about office: a change of post'), Jin ('advancing surely brings promotion'), Daxu ('promotion and transfer of post'), Zhongfu ('officials and nobles are promoted') or Sun ('office flourishes and wealth rises') all point to a change or advance of post.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.08** 年建是天符（子年子爻动才断）：天符是青龙木时，发动，加官进禄，在外卦盗去资财，在内卦摇动，忧虑家眷。月建是直符：月建是青龙，动了就不与众相同，在内卦摇动，人口兴旺，在外卦动，禄和财丰厚；月建向前数第三位是朱雀，文书不用等约定，有吉神帮助，就有升迁，杀神交加，遭到拘系束缚。
  The Year branch is the Year Tally (judge when the line of the year branch moves, as in a Zi year with the Zi line moving): when the Year Tally is the Azure Dragon of Wood and moves, it adds office and emolument, outside wealth is stolen, and shaking inside there is worry for the family. The Month branch is the Month Tally: if the Month is the Azure Dragon, when it moves it is unlike the common run, shaking inside the household prospers, moving outside emolument and wealth are abundant; the third place ahead of the Month is the Vermilion Bird, the documents need not wait to be agreed, helped by auspicious spirits there is promotion, and with baleful spirits crossing one is bound and confined.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.13** 青龙持世，功名大；天喜同官，道业亨通；朱雀并财，宜开馆；勾陈同鬼，不要出游。世、应既和睦、身又旺，上书献策必定能成；日辰、龙德都得生旺，县令、州官会出力相助。禄、马、贵人落在月建上，公侯担任重责，官职不轻；天符值土，应当没有阻碍；月建持金，必有惊恐。父母最宜逢六合，世、身切忌犯三刑；刑、杀克身又克世，会陷身受辱、耽误一生。朝见君主，不可遇上屯、蹇；面圣偏适宜见到晋、升；更何况天符持木德，高升、担重任，俸禄光荣。
  With the Azure Dragon holding the Self, fame and rank are great; with the Joy star together with the office, the Way and its work go through; the Vermilion Bird joined to Wealth suits opening a school; Hook Earth with Pressure, do not go travelling about. With the Self and the Other in accord and the Body strong as well, submitting a memorial or a plan is sure to be accomplished; with the Day and Dragon Virtue both born and strong, the county magistrate and prefect will lend their strength. With the Emolument, the Horse and the Noble standing on the Month, dukes and marquises bear heavy charge and the post is weighty; with the Year Tally on Earth there is probably no hindrance; with the Month holding Metal there is sure to be fright. Resource is best meeting the six combinations, and the Self and the Body must avoid the three punishments; punishment and baleful spirits controlling the Body and the Self, one falls into disgrace and ruins one's life. To attend the sovereign one must not meet Zhun or Jian; to face the sage ruler it suits meeting Jin or Sheng; and moreover the Year Tally holding Wood virtue, there is high promotion, heavy charge and glorious emolument.

## 任所(In office: how the post goes, the term, dismissal, impeachment, leaving office)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/任所.md`

- **LY.CAREER.CA.11** 卦（宫）所属的五行去克世爻和身位，职位不得安稳；身、世去克初爻（人吏），吏员都躲避退让，各样事业都有忧患聚集的征兆。
  If the element the hexagram belongs to controls the Self line and the Body place, the post cannot be held in peace; if Body and Self control the first line, the clerk, all the clerks draw back, and anxieties gather over every undertaking.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.15** 巽象发动时，方位在东和南；乾位上有动爻，出在西北。
  With Xun stirring the direction is east and south; with moving lines in the Qian place it goes out to the northwest.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.17** 要知道任所的好坏和官职的高低，看卦的生旺来定分寸：旺相，名声高、官尊贵；休囚，位置低劣，更是白劳。一、二、三世卦，说是咫尺之近；四、五、六世卦，是千里之外的驿程；若问注定在哪里做官，官鬼爻落在乾，就是去西北。注文说：鬼爻逢长生、帝旺，官职高；逢衰、病、死、墓、绝，官职卑。
  To know whether the post is good or bad and whether it is high or low, look at the strength of the hexagram to settle the degree: strong, the name is high and the office honoured; at rest or trapped, the position is poor and effort is all the more in vain. The First, Second and Third Generations are said to be a hand-span away, the Fourth, Fifth and Sixth a post-road of a thousand li; if one asks where one is destined to hold office, with the Pressure line in Qian one goes to the northwest. The note says: if the Pressure line meets the Birth or Peak stage the post is high, and if it meets Decline, Sickness, Death, Tomb or Extinction the post is low.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.21** 占求官有没有任所：父母爻是诰牒（任命书），任官的地方就是它的财；财落空亡，就没有任所，空握着诰牒、心意徘徊；父母被危，诰身就破；财爻被破，任官就危险；岁月的冲要加重来说，日辰的克可以看轻。注文举例：甲辰旬占得乾宫卦，甲寅为财，甲辰旬里寅、卯空，财落空亡，表示没有俸禄、没有任职之处。
  On divining whether one seeking office will have a post to go to: the Resource line is the patent of appointment, and the place of the post is its Wealth; with Wealth fallen Void there is no post, and holding the patent in vain one's mind wavers; with Resource endangered the patent is spoiled; with the Wealth line broken, taking office is perilous; a clash by the year or the month is to be weighed heavily and a control by the Day can be taken lightly. The note gives an example: if a hexagram of the Qian palace is cast in the Jiachen decade, Jiayin is Wealth, and in the Jiachen decade Yin and Mao are void, so Wealth falls Void, which shows no salary and nowhere to take office.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.22** 占在任的官安不安：世宫是官，应是百姓；主首、监临，世就是自身；如果有子孙爻来克官鬼，在位的人心神彷徨，担忧退位；旺相相生，六位都安，居官平安无事、吏民欢喜；财动，必定担忧丢失官位；遇休囚、墓、杀，要仔细推看。注文说：父母爻是主首，月卦是自己的身；得度就安，退度就失。
  On divining whether an official in post is secure: the Self palace is the official and the Other is the people; for the headman and the overseer the Self is oneself; if Output comes to control Pressure, the one in post is uneasy and worries about leaving his place; strong and generating one another, all six places are secure, the official in post is without trouble and clerks and people are glad; with Wealth moving one is sure to worry about losing the post; meeting rest, trapped, tomb or baleful spirits, reckon with care. The note says: the Resource line is the headman and the monthly hexagram is oneself; gaining the degree is secure and the retreating degree is loss.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.26** 官爻在内卦，任所就近；在外卦，就要被差到远地。太岁和官并在一爻、进入内卦，或者居在九五，就能踏上金阶（入朝）。
  With the Pressure line inside, the post is near; outside, one is sure to be sent to a distant place. With Tai Sui and Pressure joined on one line and entering the inner part, or standing on the fifth line, one steps up the golden stairs.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.08** 世爻发动又遇空亡：没做官的，卦中世爻发动，必定得不到京官、牧守一类的职位，不过如果是出巡的职位，反而顺利；已经做官的遇到这种情形，居官不久，再遇日辰、动爻相冲，必定不能长久任职理政。
  If the Self moves and meets Void: for one not yet in office, with the Self moving in the hexagram, one will surely get no post as a capital official or a governor or magistrate, though if it is a post of touring and inspecting it goes smoothly on the contrary; for one already in office, meeting this means one will not stay long in office, and if the Day or a moving line also clashes it, one will surely not hold the post and govern for long.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.10** 出巡的官，适合官鬼发动；牧守一类的官，适合官鬼安静。如果官鬼发动、化出子孙，必有别的官员来代理职务，不然也会被别人抢在前面；子孙发动化出官鬼，就是先难后易，或者先凶后吉；官鬼和子孙都发动，也主有官员来接替。
  For an officer on tour, it is fitting that Pressure moves; for a governor or magistrate, it is fitting that Pressure is still. If Pressure moves and turns into Output, another officer will surely come to take over the post, or one is otherwise forestalled by someone else; if Output moves and turns into Pressure, it is hard first and easy later, or ominous first and fortunate later; if both Pressure and Output move, it likewise means an officer comes to replace one.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.14** 兄弟是僚属，也就是同僚、下属一类的官。卦中官鬼发动、化出兄弟，冲克世爻，主同僚不和；或者兄弟化出官鬼而刑冲克害，或者兄弟带三刑六害伤害世爻，也都是这样；世爻克兄弟，是我欺负他，因而不和。
  Peer stands for the officers of one's staff, that is, colleagues and subordinates. If in the hexagram Pressure moves and turns into Peer and clashes or controls the Self, it means discord among colleagues; or if Peer turns into Pressure and punishes, clashes, controls or harms, or if Peer carries the three punishments or the six harms and injures the Self, it is the same; if the Self controls Peer, it is I who bully him, and so there is discord.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.16** 卦静而世爻空亡，是退休的兆头；身爻空亡而煞发动，是躲避祸事的征兆。世爻空亡，没做官的没有选用的日期；已经做官的，如果六爻安静、月建日辰太岁没有伤它，就是休官改任的象；如果官鬼发动、被月建日辰太岁伤克，是躲避祸患、脱离灾难的兆头；卦静而日辰冲，想回家却被留住；煞动而日辰合，想躲避却躲不开。
  A still hexagram with the Self Void is a sign of retirement; the Body Void and a killer moving is a sign of avoiding misfortune. When the Self is Void, one not yet in office has no date of selection; for one already in office, if the six lines are still and the Month, the Day and the Tai Sui do not injure it, it is the image of retiring from office and changing post; if Pressure moves and the Month, the Day or the Tai Sui injures or controls it, it is a sign of avoiding misfortune and escaping calamity; if the hexagram is still and the Day clashes, one wants to go home but is not let go; if a killer moves and the Day combines, one wants to escape but cannot.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.17** 遇到凶兆的时候，如果官鬼爻临身持世，或者本宫的官鬼伏在世爻下面，虽然被责罚，官职还在；如果不临身持世，也不伏在世爻下面，或者虽然伏着仍遇到空亡，必定被革去官带贬为平民，不只是责罚而已。
  When an ominous sign appears, if Pressure settles on the Body or holds the Self, or the home palace's Pressure lies hidden under the Self, then although one is blamed and punished, the office remains; if it neither settles on the Body nor holds the Self, nor lies hidden under the Self, or lies hidden but still meets Void, one will surely be stripped of the official belt and reduced to a commoner, which is more than blame and punishment.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.18** 官鬼发动，来生合世爻，日辰、月建、动爻的变化又没有冲克它的，做官必定有声名威望；再得到财爻生扶合助、有气不空，就既会做官又会赚钱，内里实际贪图贿赂，外面不丧失名声；如果财爻空亡、伏藏或者临死绝，就是声名虽有，贿赂却没有。
  If Pressure moves to generate or combine with the Self and the changes of the Day, the Month and the moving lines do not clash or control it, one will surely have fame and standing in office; if Wealth also generates, supports and helps it, with breath and not Void, one both knows how to hold office and how to make money, taking bribes within while not losing one's name without; if Wealth is Void, hidden, or in death or extinction, one has the fame but not the bribes.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.22** 担任牧民的职位，要财爻旺而不动，父母爻扶助而不空，必是富庶的好地方；财爻空绝、父爻受制，就是地瘠民贫；父母发动临世，政务必定繁重；兄弟持世，赋税征不起来；日辰带兄弟冲克世爻，手下人必定舞文弄法，败坏我的政事；兄弟在旁边的爻上发动来冲克，顽劣的百姓难治；兄弟化子孙、子孙化兄弟而刑克世身，恐怕有百姓告我；世应冲克，和乡绅也多不和睦。
  In a post that shepherds the people, Wealth should be thriving and not moving and Resource should support and not be Void, and then it is surely a rich and prosperous place; if Wealth is Void or in extinction and Resource is controlled, the land is barren and the people poor; if Resource moves and settles on the Self, the business of government will surely be heavy; if Peer holds the Self, taxes cannot be raised; if the Day carries Peer and clashes or controls the Self, the underlings will surely twist the law and ruin my government; if Peer moves on a side line and comes to clash or control, the unruly people are hard to govern; if Peer turns into Output or Output into Peer and punishes or controls the Self or the Body, there may be the sign of the people bringing a suit against me; and if the Self and the Other clash or control each other, there is also much discord with the local gentry.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.23** 钦差出巡，或者奉命去封王、采木，都怕世爻、应爻遇空；如果是路爻空亡的卦，主途中惊险；世爻在五爻而自己空亡，须提防死在外面，有冲克就不会这样；世在五爻发动，以及游魂卦世爻发动的，都是出巡的职位。
  For one sent by imperial commission on a tour, or to invest a prince or to gather timber, a Void Self or Other is much feared; if it is a hexagram with the road line Void, there will be danger and fright on the way; if the Self is on the fifth line and Void by itself, beware of dying abroad, though with a clash or control it is not so; and when the Self on the fifth line moves, or the Self moves in a Wandering-Soul hexagram, it is a post of touring.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.33** 世应两爻刑冲克害，日后宾主一定不合，再加上兄弟、官鬼发动，是大凶的兆头；世应生合比和，情意必定相投；生而化出克，开始和睦、最后不和；冲而化出合，开始疏远、后来亲密；世爻和子孙也适宜生合，师生之间就恩义兼尽，见到冲克也多不睦。
  If the Self and the Other punish, clash, control or harm each other, the guest and host will surely be at odds in time, and if Peer and Pressure also move it is a greatly ominous sign; if the Self and the Other generate, combine or are in harmony, their feelings will surely be congenial; generation turning into control is harmonious at the start and not at the end; a clash turning into a combination is distant at first and close afterwards; it is also fitting that the Self and Output generate or combine, and then the teacher and his pupils fulfil both kindness and duty, while with a clash or control they are mostly not on good terms.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.34** 占馆以妻财为束修（学费）。财旺相就多，休囚就少；要知道有多少，用生成数来推，生旺加倍，绝死减半，在阳宫用生数，在阴宫用成数；只怕兄弟发动或者财化出兄弟，束修不能都归自己，必有人抽成分用，不然就有名无实；财爻无气而遇月建日辰动爻生扶的，束修虽然不多，四季节礼反而周全。
  In divining a school post, Wealth is the tuition fee. If thriving it is much, and if resting or trapped, little; to know how much, infer by the generating and completing numbers, doubled if in birth or thriving and halved if in extinction or death, using the generating number in a yang palace and the completing number in a yin palace; what is feared is Peer moving or Wealth turning into Peer, and then the fee does not all go to oneself, someone takes a share, or it is in name only; if Wealth has no breath but is generated or supported by the Month, the Day or a moving line, the fee, though not large, is made up for by gifts at the four seasons' festivals.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.46** 应爻不克世爻，父母不空，兄弟、官鬼不发动，而月建、日辰、动爻都不生合世爻的，这个事可以成，只是主人不敬重，都用白眼对待。
  If the Other does not control the Self, Resource is not Void, Peer and Pressure do not move, and the Month, the Day and the moving lines do not generate or combine with the Self, the matter can come about, only the master does not respect one and treats one with disdain.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.47** 占馆以子孙爻代表门生（学生）：旺相就多，休囚就少；空亡、不上卦，虽是这家人作主，未必是他家的子弟；子孙化出子孙，学生必定多；带煞发动来克世爻，日后恐怕有动手作乱、入室反目的人。
  In divining a school post, Output stands for the pupils: if thriving there are many, if resting or trapped, few; if it is Void or absent from the hexagram, though this household is the master the pupils are not necessarily the sons of that family; Output turning into Output means many pupils; if it carries a killer and moves to control the Self, there may later be one who takes up arms and turns on his master within the room.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.48** 子孙旺相却被日辰、动爻刑克，学生开始时虽然很多聚集在馆，日后渐渐减少；子孙休囚却被日辰、动爻生合扶起，开始时虽然不多，开馆后日渐增加。
  If Output is thriving but is punished or controlled by the Day or a moving line, then although many pupils gather in the school at first, they gradually dwindle later; if Output is resting or trapped but is generated, combined or raised up by the Day or a moving line, then though there are not many at first, they increase day by day after the school opens.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.49** 子孙爻发动，本来是吉兆，如果被日辰、动爻冲散，他的学生必定有背弃老师而去的；如果被世爻冲散，是先生呵斥退了学生，不是弟子自己背弃老师；子孙动而变出空亡，弟子中有半途而废的，那是歇学，不是叛离。
  If the Output line moves, it is of itself an auspicious sign, but if it is clashed apart by the Day or a moving line, some of his pupils will surely turn their backs on him and leave; if it is clashed apart by the Self, it is the teacher who scolds his pupils away, and not the disciples who abandon their teacher; if Output moves and changes into Void, some of the disciples give up halfway, which is leaving off study and not defection.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.52** 卦里有两个子孙爻，都发动而相冲，弟子之间必定多有不合，再加白虎、螣蛇，时常有争斗，如果来伤世爻，必定连累到先生；如果两个爻都来生合世爻，门生就自己尽弟子的礼，尊师重道，必定不会轻易背弃老师。
  If the hexagram has two Output lines and both move and clash with each other, there will surely be much discord among the disciples, with the White Tiger and the Coiling Snake added there are often fights, and if they come to injure the Self it will surely reach the teacher; if both lines come to generate or combine with the Self, the pupils will fulfil the duty of disciples on their own, respect their teacher and value what is handed down, and will surely not lightly turn their backs on him.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.53** 世爻临着财爻发动，是自己做饭，不是东家供给膳食；如果占的是供膳，又主供得成，只怕兄弟发动，或者虽然不动而持身世的，都主供不成；大凡占馆，遇到财爻持世，又主先生的家眷一同到馆。
  If the Self rests on Wealth and it moves, one cooks for oneself and meals are not provided by the host; if one divines whether meals will be provided, it also means they will be provided, and what is feared is Peer moving, or Peer holding the Self or the Body even if it does not move, in all of which cases they will not be provided; in general in divining a school post, Wealth holding the Self also means the teacher's family comes along.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.54** 财爻临在应爻上、生合世爻身爻，一定供给膳食，因为财就是饮食；月建、日辰、动爻都带妻财，一定不是一家做东供膳，而是各位学生轮流供给；财旺相款待丰厚，休囚款待菲薄，财化子孙丰盛洁净，化官鬼没有美味，化父母清淡，化兄弟不过是平常饭食。
  If Wealth rests on the Other and generates or combines with the Self or the Body, meals will surely be provided, because Wealth is food and drink; if the Month, the Day and the moving lines all carry Wealth, it is surely not one family acting as host but the pupils providing in turn; thriving Wealth means generous hospitality and resting or trapped Wealth meagre, Wealth turning into Output means plentiful and clean food, into Pressure no delicacies, into Resource plain fare, and into Peer no more than everyday food.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.55** 要讨取束修，以财爻为主。财爻不出现，不管伏在哪个爻下，都主费力难讨，必须央求朋友一同去讨，也许才能得到，因为伏藏的爻必须有月建、日辰、动爻把它提起来，像飞神一样，然后才能出来起作用；财爻出现旺相，月建日辰动爻不来伤克，就不会欠缺；财爻出现却被克，或者绝、空、入墓，都不如意。
  To collect the tuition fee, Wealth is the main line. If Wealth does not appear, whatever line it lies hidden under, collecting is hard work, and one must beg friends to go along and ask for it, and then it may perhaps be had, for a hidden line must be drawn up by the Month, the Day or a moving line, like a flying line, before it can come out and act; if Wealth appears and is thriving, and the Month, the Day and the moving lines do not injure or control it, there will be no shortfall; if Wealth appears but is controlled, or in extinction, Void or in a tomb, nothing is as one wishes.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.56** 占束修，财爻出现、旺相不空，月建、日辰、动爻又不带兄弟来伤克，就不缺欠，再得财爻生世、合世，是大吉的兆头；如果财爻虽然出现，却被克被劫，或处在绝地，或空亡，或变出死、墓、绝的爻，都不如意；世爻临着兄弟来克财，尤其难讨；得子孙旺相发动，也好。
  In divining the tuition fee, if Wealth appears, thriving and not Void, and the Month, the Day and the moving lines do not carry Peer to injure or control it, there is no shortfall, and if Wealth also generates or combines with the Self it is a greatly auspicious sign; if Wealth appears but is controlled or robbed, or stands in a place of extinction, or is Void, or turns into a line of death, tomb or extinction, nothing is as one wishes; if the Self rests on Peer and it controls Wealth, collecting is especially hard; and if Output is thriving and moves, that is also good.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.57** 财爻发动化出父母，或者父母发动化出财，束修一定没有金银财宝，多半是用货物折价，是有名无实的象；卦里兄弟再发动，连杂货物也不能全部得到。
  If Wealth moves and turns into Resource, or Resource moves and turns into Wealth, the fee will surely not be in gold and silver but mostly in goods taken at a set value, the image of name without substance; if Peer also moves in the hexagram, even the miscellaneous goods cannot all be had.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.58** 兄弟是劫财的神，发动或持世，束修都难到手；财爻化出兄弟，有名无实，或者只得一半；卦里没有财而兄弟化出来，就有人抽成分去。
  Peer is the spirit that robs wealth, and when it moves or holds the Self the fee is hard to lay hands on; Wealth turning into Peer is in name only, or only half is had; if there is no Wealth in the hexagram and Peer is changed out, someone takes a share.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.59** 占束修，遇到卦身、应爻，以及子孙、妻财，有的空亡或者不上卦，束修就拿不到，因为财是用神，身是事主，应是学生的父兄，子孙又是生财的神，现在都空、都休囚，怎么能拿到呢，所以白白虚度。
  In divining the fee, if the Body, the Other, Output and Wealth are Void or absent from the hexagram, the fee will not be obtained, for Wealth is the Subject, the Body is the one the matter belongs to, the Other is the pupils' fathers and brothers, and Output is the spirit that generates Wealth, and now all are Void and resting, how can it be obtained, and so the time is spent in vain.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.61** 卦里没有财，而兄弟、官鬼、文书乱动，有化出财爻来生合世爻的，必须到公堂上打官司，然后才能得到束修；官爻单独发动、生世合世，也是这样。
  If there is no Wealth in the hexagram but Peer, Pressure and Resource move wildly and one of them turns into a Wealth that generates or combines with the Self, one must go to the court and bring a suit before the fee can be had; Pressure moving alone to generate or combine with the Self is likewise so.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.13** 官鬼爻持世，应当知道到任后要掌权；印绶（父母）扶身，必定临堂掌管案牍。官鬼持世、印绶扶身是卦的主宰，再有用爻加在上面，一定到任把权、临堂掌案。
  With the Pressure line holding the Self, know that on taking up the post one will hold power; with the seal and ribbon (Resource) supporting the self, one will surely sit in court and take charge of the cases. Pressure holding the Self and the seal supporting the self are the masters of the hexagram, and if the Subject line is added to them one surely takes power on taking up the post and presides in court over the cases.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.14** 卦里没有父母，终究没有可任职的地方；妻财隐伏不现，还拿不到养廉的俸禄。父母是任所所在的地方，不出现或落空，恐怕没有任职之处；妻财隐伏或落空，养廉的俸禄就不够。
  With no Resource in the hexagram there is in the end no place to take up office; with the Wealth line hidden one does not yet get the stipend that fosters integrity. Resource is the place of the post, and if it does not appear or falls void there may be nowhere to take office; with Wealth hidden or void the stipend that fosters integrity is not enough.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.15** 一世、二世卦，任所不远；五世卦、八纯卦，仕途要远行。一、二、三世的世爻在内卦，所以说不远；四、五、八纯在外卦，所以说要远行。
  In a First- or Second-Generation hexagram the post is not far; in a Fifth-Generation or Eight-Pure hexagram the career takes one far. The First, Second and Third Generations are in the inner trigram, so it is said not to be far; the Fourth, Fifth and Eight-Pure are in the outer trigram, so it is said one travels far.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.39** 代理印信、受委派镇守的人，占成不成，类似谋差使；世应空、本卦合处逢冲，以及官爻有病，都不可。占有没有利，和服役相同：官坏、财虚，只是白费奔走；官来克世，还要担心（此处一字不清）生出事端。
  For one acting with the seal or commissioned to a garrison, divining whether it comes off is like seeking a commission; the Self and the Other Void, a combination in the original hexagram being clashed, and Pressure being sick are all unacceptable. Divining about profit is the same as for serving in a duty: with Pressure damaged and Wealth empty one only wears oneself out galloping about; if Pressure comes to control the Self, one must also fear that (one character is unclear) strife arises.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.44** 升到哪个方向，以官爻所属的五行为法：属金，该到西方上任；属木，该在东方治事；属水，该在北海一带治民；属土，该在中央执政；属火，该在南方一隅施行教化。不论官爻的衰旺，也不论十二支神所主的方位，大略按五行来分，多半应验。例如戌月戊申日占升到哪个方向，得革卦；若按地支说，丑官在东北、未官在西南，后来升到了河南中土。
  As to which direction one will be promoted to, the rule is the phase to which the Pressure line belongs: if Metal, one should take up office in the west; if Wood, one should govern in the east; if Water, one should rule the people by the northern sea; if Earth, one should hold the government in the centre; if Fire, one should carry out the transforming teaching in a corner of the south. Whether the Pressure line is strong or weak, and the directions governed by the twelve branch spirits, do not matter; dividing by the five phases in rough outline is mostly confirmed. For example, in a Xu month on a Wu-Shen day, divining in which direction one would be promoted, one got Ge; going by the branches, a Chou Pressure line is north-east and a Wei one south-west, and later one was promoted to the central earth, Henan.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.45** 官爻单独发动，就按它所在的宫来论方位：独发在离宫，是关南的柱石；单动在坎卦，是塞北的屏藩；在巽，在东南兴教化；在乾，在西北立功名；兑当陇蜀，震偏齐鲁，艮往辽蓟的沙漠，坤到秦晋的疆土。凡独发，用八宫来断方位；其他的情形用五行来断地利；这是就天下而言，不拘泥于本乡、本任。
  When the Pressure line moves alone, direction is discussed by the palace it is in: moving alone in the palace of Li, one is a pillar south of the passes; moving alone in Kan, a screen and bulwark north of the frontier; in Xun, one spreads teaching in the south-east; in Qian, one makes one's name in the north-west; Dui corresponds to Long and Shu, Zhen leans to Qi and Lu, Gen goes to the deserts of Liao and Ji, and Kun comes to the borders of Qin and Jin. When a line moves alone, one judges the direction by the eight palaces; in other cases one judges the lie of the land by the five phases; this speaks of the whole realm, and is not tied to the home district or the present post.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.46** 只有一个官爻、又落在世爻上的，用演禽二十八宿的所属来定分野；只有一个官爻、伏藏着的，用它上面的飞神来定地方。不过这是占官，不是占世；已经做官的，以官爻为象，不拘泥于世。例如亥月戊寅日卜升到哪个方向，得同人卦，亥官居世，京房降宿是危星，后来升到齐地的分野；又如酉月乙巳日得颐卦，丙戌世下伏着酉官，后来升到西北。
  When there is a single Pressure line and it sits on the Self, the assignment of the twenty-eight mansions of the Yanqin system is used to fix the field-allocation; when there is a single Pressure line and it is hidden, the flying line above it is used to fix the place. But this is divining the Pressure line, not the Self; for one already in office the Pressure line is the image, and one does not hold to the Self. For example, in a Hai month on a Wu-Yin day, divining which direction one would be promoted, one got Tongren, with a Hai Pressure line on the Self, and the mansion that Jing Fang assigns to it is Wei, and later one was promoted to the field-allocation of Qi; or in a You month on a Yi-Si day one got Yi, with a hidden You Pressure line under the Bing-Xu Self, and later one was promoted to the north-west.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.47** 归魂卦，常常回到旧任；游魂卦，常常走上新路。六冲而官伏藏，就远；六合而官出现，就近。内卦是近处，外卦是远方；初爻在接壤的近处，六爻在边远之地。卦中只有一个官爻，在内卦为近、在外卦为远，初爻近、六爻远，其余爻照这样类推；若有内外两个官爻，就看卦：游魂、六冲、官伏藏，应在远；归魂、六合、官出现，应在近。因为一个官爻以内外分远近，两个官爻以卦象分远近。
  A Returning Soul hexagram often goes back to the old post; a Wandering Soul hexagram often walks a new road. A six-clash with Pressure hidden is far; a six-combine with Pressure showing is near. The inner trigram is near, the outer far; the first line borders on the near, the sixth is the remote frontier. When there is only one Pressure line in the hexagram, in the inner trigram it is near and in the outer far, the first line near and the sixth far, the other lines by analogy; if there are two Pressure lines, inner and outer, one looks at the hexagram: Wandering Soul, a six-clash and Pressure hidden answer far; Returning Soul, a six-combine and Pressure showing answer near. For one Pressure line distance is divided by inner and outer; for two Pressure lines it is divided by the image of the hexagram.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.55** 占能不能久任：三冲、游魂就不长久；官坏就罢职；世空就解印离任；官克世就被参劾、贬谪。占到任后身体平安与否：随墓、助伤，就是一到任就生病；世破、鬼发，就是临政时抱灾祸。占久任，以官爻为官职本身；占身安，以官爻为病；占卜取用爻，妙处在于变通。
  In divining whether one will keep the post long: the three clashes or Wandering Soul mean it will not last; Pressure damaged means dismissal; the Self Void means laying down the seal and leaving the post; Pressure controlling the Self means impeachment and demotion. In divining whether one will be safe in body on taking up the post: following into the tomb and helping the injury means falling ill on arriving at the post; the Self broken and Pressure moving means bearing calamity while in charge of government. For a long tenure the Pressure line is taken as the office itself; for bodily safety it is taken as illness; in divining, the wonder of using a line lies in the change.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.59** 问大计（定期考核官员），要官鬼没有瑕疵，要世爻有气；官临日、月又得生扶，必定被选在卓异、廉能的一等；子孙动，就有降职的忧虑；兄弟摇动，就有罚俸的议论；再遇官破，就致仕还乡。为奉养双亲请假、告病辞官：如果官克世而被厌恶，官爻破、绝、空、散，谁能明白你高蹈归隐的心怀；世象随墓、助伤，反而被世俗的尘缘束缚。
  In asking about the grand evaluation of officials, one wants Pressure without flaw and the Self line with force; if Pressure sits on the Day or Month and is generated and supported, one will surely be chosen among the outstanding, upright and capable; Output moving, there is the worry of demotion; Peer stirring, there is talk of a fine of salary; and if Pressure is also broken, one retires and goes home. In asking leave to support one's parents or retiring on grounds of illness: if Pressure controls the Self and one is disliked, and the Pressure line is broken, extinct, Void or scattered, who will understand a heart set on lofty retreat; if the Self image follows into the tomb and helps the injury, one is instead bound by the dust of the world.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.60** 到乱地去任官而求身平安，随墓、助伤、世穷、鬼发，都不吉祥；到危邦去任官而求爵位显赫，官旺、世实，就吉。例如治河防决口，忌鬼动、鬼克；抽税征商，忌应空、财坏；考卷有瑕疵，用父母；征徭役，不用妻财；占的人志在这件就用这个爻，志在那件就用那个爻。
  Taking office in a disordered region and seeking personal safety, following into the tomb, helping the injury, the Self at its end and Pressure moving are all inauspicious; taking office in a perilous state and seeking a distinguished title, thriving Pressure and a solid Self are auspicious. For example, in managing a river and guarding against a breach, Pressure moving or controlling is to be avoided; in levying taxes on merchants, a Void Other and damaged Wealth are to be avoided; a flaw in the examination papers uses Resource, and levying labour service does not use Wealth; where the diviner's aim lies, that line is used, and if the aim is elsewhere, the other line is used.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.96** 此后再往前走，顺应因缘倡导道法，就多有名利的追求了。住持（主持寺观）如占宅：三冲、鬼动，就该退；纲纪（寺观的管事）如筮仕：世空、官坏，就无成；募缘（化缘募捐）同求财：应空、财陷，就白去一趟；法会如设馆：应空、鬼动，就多有损失。
  Beyond this, to go along with the occasion and promote the Way brings many pursuits of fame and profit. Being abbot (presiding over a temple) is like divining about a house: the three clashes and Pressure moving mean one should withdraw; holding an administrative post is like divining about taking office: the Self Void and Pressure damaged mean nothing comes of it; soliciting donations is the same as seeking wealth: the Other Void and Wealth fallen mean the trip is in vain; a ritual assembly is like opening a school: the Other Void and Pressure moving mean many losses.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.13** 赴任：对应的那一爻旺相又逢生合的，吉；遇上官鬼、落空，又被刑、害、克、破的，必有灾难。又，世爻是做官的人，应爻是任所：世克应，路上没有阻碍；应克世，到任不利；世生应，因为做官而耗损；应生世，因为做官而得益。世爻落空必有灾难：走陆路担心跌伤，走水路担心风浪；如果是旺相的空，只是生病而已，或者得到日辰、动爻来冲克，才大概能免于灾咎。
  Going to the post: the matching line is auspicious when it is thriving and meets generation or combination; if it meets Pressure and is Void, and is punished, harmed, controlled or broken, there will surely be disaster. Again, the Self is the official and the Other is the post: if the Self controls the Other the road is unobstructed; if the Other controls the Self, taking up the post is unfavourable; if the Self generates the Other, one is drained because of the office; if the Other generates the Self, one gains because of the office. A Void Self means disaster: going by land fear a fall, by water fear wind and waves; if it is a thriving Void it is only illness, or if the Day or a moving line comes to clash with or control it one may perhaps escape blame.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.14** 在任，到任之后的权势和声望：官星带贵人、禄，临到并扶助身爻、世爻的，到任就掌实权；印星带贵人、驿马，临到并扶助身爻、世爻的，上堂就掌印；官星带青龙、贵人，落在阳宫的阳爻上又旺相的，政绩的名声很大。
  In office, power and repute after taking up the post. If the star of office carrying the Noble and Emolument comes to the Body and Self and supports them, he holds real power on taking up the post; if the seal line carrying the Noble and the Horse does so, he holds the seal when he takes his seat; if the star of office carrying the Dragon and the Noble sits on a yang line in a yang palace and is thriving, his name for government is great.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.15** 在任，任所的民情和属吏：印星受伤而财爻落空、绝的，任所地瘠民贫；印星、身爻发动，而兄爻、世爻也发动的，政事繁杂而赋税不足；日辰合着发动的兄爻伤世爻的，属吏多半舞文弄法；兄爻变出子孙来克世爻的，百姓多半顽梗，不服教化。
  In office, the people and the clerks of the post. If the seal line is injured and Wealth is Void or extinct, the land is barren and the people poor; if the seal line and the Body move, and the Peer line and the Self also move, government business is heavy and taxes fall short; if the Day joins a moving Peer line in injuring the Self, the clerks mostly twist the written law; if a Peer line turns into Output and that controls the Self, the people are mostly stubborn and refuse to be reformed.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.11** 在任吉凶：官星旺相、妻财兴旺，仕途显赫。官星旺相又得生扶，或发动变吉，世旺财旺，或财发动生世，官民称颂，宦途平稳；官星临日月生合世爻，或三合官局生合世爻，或官星持世又得日月生扶、太岁五爻相生合，在君主身边的特别受宠，居高位的不久越级升迁，外任的被评为卓异。反过来，兄弟持世或发动，不是破财就是罚俸；子孙持世或在动爻中，有被剥官削职的忧虑，只有官星休囚而有扶、子孙发动而被制住时，才不过降级。
  How one fares in office: with the star of office thriving and Wealth flourishing, the official career is brilliant. If the star of office is thriving and gets generation and support, or moves and turns auspicious, with the Self and Wealth thriving or Wealth moving to generate the Self, soldiers and people praise his virtue and the sea of officialdom is calm; if the star of office sits on the Day or Month and generates or combines with the Self, or a three-harmony Pressure frame generates or combines with the Self, or the star of office holds the Self and the Day and Month generate and support it with Tai Sui or the fifth line generating and combining, then one near the ruler receives uncommon favour, one in high office is soon promoted beyond rank, and one serving outside is rated outstanding. The reverse: if Peer holds the Self or moves, there is either a loss of goods or a fine of salary; if Output holds the Self or is among the moving lines, there is cause to fear being stripped of office and rank, and only when the star of office is resting or trapped but supported and Output moves but is restrained does it come to no more than a demotion.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.12** 官星旺相而兄爻发动，不一定是坏事：兄爻发动虽然是耗财、减俸的神，但不必拘泥；官星旺相的话，那是做官清正、不义之财不取，并不是没有财；官星旺相而财落空破，也这样断。旺相的父母爻临世，官星发动临在太岁、五爻上，或有日月生扶，外任的必得上司推荐，在君主身边和居高位的，恩赐荣誉一再加身。
  A moving Peer line with a thriving star of office is not necessarily bad: a moving Peer line is indeed a spirit that wastes money and cuts salary, but one need not cling to that; when the star of office is thriving, it means an upright official who takes nothing unrighteous, not one who has no wealth; the same judgment holds when the star of office is thriving and Wealth is Void or broken. If a thriving Resource line holds the Self and the star of office moves on Tai Sui or the fifth line, or the Day and Month generate and support it, an official serving outside will surely be recommended by his superiors, and those near the ruler and in high office receive honours again and again.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.13** 兄弟、官鬼安静，管辖的地方上事情少；兄弟、官鬼在卦中乱动，是旱涝、兵荒，地方不安宁的征兆。
  When Peer and Pressure are still, the district under one's charge has few troubles; when Peer and Pressure move in disorder in the hexagram, it is a sign of drought, flood, war and famine, and of a district at unrest.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.18** 世爻变出官星又遇官星，多代理职务：世爻旺相，变出的官星生合、比助世爻，代理有益；变出的官星刑克世爻，代理反受其害；变出的官星泄世爻之气，代理要赔钱受累；官星持世旺相、爻中又发动出官星来拱扶、比合，也主多次代理政务。
  If the Self turns into the star of office and meets the star of office again, one often takes charge of the administration in an acting capacity: if the Self is thriving and the star of office it turns into generates, combines with or helps the Self, acting in a post benefits one; if the star of office it turns into punishes or controls the Self, acting in a post brings harm instead; if it drains the Self's strength, one loses money and is burdened by acting; if the star of office holds the Self and is thriving and another star of office moves in the lines to hold up, help or combine with it, one also often takes charge in an acting capacity.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.42** 世爻安静又落空，官星又失陷，终究要回到山林做个闲人：世爻落空是退休的征兆，官星又落空、被破、休囚的，削职无疑。
  If the Self is still and Void and the star of office is also lost, one must in the end go back to the woods as an idle man: a Void Self is a sign of retirement, and if the star of office is also Void, broken, or resting or trapped, dismissal is certain.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.46** 养亲、告病、辞官：子孙持世、子孙发动，就没有官职的牵累，必定能如愿偷闲；官星持世，以及官星发动克世、合世的，难以摆脱名利的缰绳和锁链。
  To care for a parent, take sick leave or resign: if Output holds the Self or Output moves, there is no burden of office and one will surely get the leisure one wants; if the star of office holds the Self, or the star of office moves to control or combine with the Self, one can hardly break free from the reins and chains of rank and profit.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.47** 告假、告病、奉养父母是暂时回去的事，得反吟卦不忌讳（应了将来还会再任职）；辞官的人不宜见反吟，得伏吟一定走不成；官星或世爻变退神，告假、辞官都能如意；世爻与官星变进神，公事缠身，难以歇手。
  Taking leave, sick leave and caring for a parent are temporary returns home, and a Reversed Chant hexagram is not feared (it means one will take office again); for one resigning Reversed Chant is not suitable, and with Hidden Chant one surely cannot go; if the star of office or the Self turns into a Retreating line, leave and resignation are both granted as wished; if the Self and the star of office turn into Advancing lines, state business holds one fast and it is hard to stop.

## 贵人(Patrons and superiors: calling on the great, recommendations, who helps or blocks)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/贵人.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.11** 有禄、有马的爻落在世爻或应爻上，求官可以进，名和利都能成。禄是日干的禄所在的地支（甲禄在寅、乙禄在卯），马是日支三合的驿马所在的地支（申子辰马在寅）。
  When a line that is the Emolument or the Horse stands on the Self or Other line, one seeking office can advance and both name and profit are achieved. The Emolument is the branch where the day-stem has its Emolument (Jia at Yin, Yi at Mao); the Horse is the branch of the Post Horse of the day's three-harmony group (Shen-Zi-Chen has its horse at Yin).
- **LY.CAREER.CA.18** 喜神、驿马落在官鬼上，荐举的文书一封接一封，知己很多。
  With the Joy spirit and the Post Horse on Pressure, recommending letters come one after another and those who know you are many.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.21** 天乙贵人再来相合，不只自己享俸禄，而且世世代代都能做官、受封赠。旧注里的天乙贵人按月份取：正月在巳、二月在申、三月在亥、四月在寅，五、九月仍在巳，往后三位轮转；逢动一定吉，子孙代代做公卿。
  If the Tianyi Noble further combines, one enjoys the emolument oneself and, generation after generation, holds office and receives imperial honours. The note finds the Tianyi Noble by the month: Si in the first month, Shen in the second, Hai in the third, Yin in the fourth, Si again in the fifth and ninth, the three places turning on after that; if it moves it is surely auspicious, and descendants will be dukes and ministers generation after generation.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.29** 父母克身，有师父接引；青龙持世，有贵人周全；贵人落在父母上，师父通达圣道；福德、青龙交动，弟子贤能；父母重重叠叠、身受克，断定是被俗家牵累。
  Resource controlling the Body, a master receives and leads one on; the Azure Dragon holding the Self, a patron sees one through; the Noble on Resource, the master is versed in the sacred; Output and the Dragon moving together, the disciples are worthy; Resource upon Resource and the Body controlled, it is certain one is dragged back by one's lay family.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.33** 木神、太岁落在身和命上，当天就遇上神人、得到真传；太岁、火神加在父母上，天边的恩命一定会来宣布。
  The Wood spirit and Tai Sui on the Body and one's fate: on that very day one meets a divine person and receives the true transmission; Tai Sui and the Fire spirit added to Resource: a favour commanded from the edge of heaven will surely be proclaimed.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.08** 喜神、驿马兼有官鬼，荐举的文书一封又一封，被知己看重；再就这当中用唐符、国印两爻来推，早晚会随着诏书起用。
  With the Joy spirit and the Post Horse together with Pressure, recommending letters follow one another and one is valued by those who know one; then reckon from the two lines Tangfu and State Seal, and sooner or later one will be raised on the strength of an imperial edict.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.25** 太岁（扶）求官，常常赴诏入朝；如果太岁在宫来，得台省之力；太岁和官爻并在九五，或者持世，有威名声势；再得印绶相符合，圣人常常挂念记着你的名字。注文说：官爻有气，遇升印卦利武职、升阳卦利文官；官鬼爻和印绶相生相扶，就是得地；如果杀神和世爻发动克官鬼，白劳一场、空手而返；太岁动扶官，主赴诏。太岁在九五与官爻并，或者持世，这人威名显赫；再得印绶相辅，君王时常记挂他。
  With Tai Sui supporting one seeking office, one is often summoned to court by edict; if Tai Sui comes in the palace, one gets the help of the censorate and secretariat; with Tai Sui and the Pressure line joined on the fifth line, or holding the Self, one has authority and fame; and if the seal and ribbon are also in accord, the sage ruler often keeps your name in mind. The notes say: with the Pressure line having qi, meeting a 'rising seal' hexagram favours military office and a 'rising yang' hexagram civil office; the Pressure line and the seal and ribbon generating and supporting each other is being well placed; if a baleful spirit and the Self line move to control Pressure, one labours in vain and returns empty-handed; Tai Sui moving to support the Pressure line means going to court on summons. Tai Sui on the fifth line together with the Pressure line, or holding the Self, makes the man famous and eminent, and with the seal and ribbon also helping, the sovereign often keeps him in mind.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.34** 想去参见高贵的人，要把爻位仔细推详（按鬼谷子爻位法推：如贵人落在初爻，宜谒乡里的贵人）；内、外卦相生，喜事加倍；如果遇到青龙发动，主有吉庆；如果遇到白虎发动，主拜谒适当。
  To call on a person of high rank, examine the lines closely (by the Guigu line-position method: if the noble stands on the first line it is fitting to call on a noble of the township); inner and outer generating each other, joy is doubled; if the Azure Dragon moves there is good fortune; if the White Tiger moves, the call is fitting.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.35** 世应和同、子孙（爱子）发动，轻快地兴起，主吉利；屯滞，最嫌官鬼慢慢扬起（发动），主困顿难行；外卦是阳又被内卦所克（如既济这一类），到门前拜谒，只见凄惶而归。
  With Self and Other in harmony and the beloved son (Output) moving, one rises lightly and gaily, which is auspicious; stagnation: Pressure slowly rising (moving) is disliked and means being stuck and hard to proceed; with the outer trigram yang and also controlled by the inner (as in a hexagram such as Jiji), at the door where one calls one meets only dismay and goes home in distress.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.36** 喜神、驿马兼有财、禄，如果发动，就知道大吉昌：天喜、驿马、财爻和禄这四样发动，谒见贵人如意，并且有得财的吉利。
  With the Joy spirit and the Post Horse together with Wealth and the Emolument, if they move, know that it is greatly auspicious: if the Joy star, the Post Horse, the Wealth line and the Emolument, these four, move, the call on the noble goes as one wishes and there is also the good fortune of gaining wealth.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.37** 拜谒，内卦（贞）和外卦（悔）相生，吉利恰当；内外互相欺凌（相克、相冲），凶而且受伤；世克应，是众位尊长发怒，再加官鬼爻，就不祥。
  In a call, the inner trigram (zhen) and the outer (hui) generating each other is fitting and auspicious; inner and outer bullying each other (controlling or clashing) is bad and brings harm; the Self controlling the Other means the elders are angry, and with the Pressure line besides it is inauspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.38** 悔（外卦）克贞（内卦），是说主人惦念你，但也要担忧谗言带来的刑害；贞克悔，是伤了主人的心，进退俯仰只须自己谦卑恭敬。注文说：终究不如相生好。
  The outer trigram controlling the inner means the host is thinking of you, but worry too about punishment and harm through slander; the inner controlling the outer means one has hurt the host's heart, and in every bearing one must humble oneself and show respect. The note says: in the end it is not as good as mutual generation.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.39** 应克世，是说主人喜爱你；内外相生，吉利最好；世、应比和，有吉无凶；官鬼旺、身（卦身）旺，欢乐无阻碍。注文说：内外相生、世应比和、官鬼生世爻，都是吉利。
  The Other controlling the Self means the host is fond of you; inner and outer generating each other is the best auspice; Self and Other in accord is auspicious without ill; with Pressure strong and the Body strong, joy is without obstruction. The note says: inner and outer generating each other, Self and Other in accord, and Pressure generating the Self line are all auspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.40** 要见贵人，看月卦（月的卦身）：卦里有着落，就能相见；占卦要见到官鬼爻，官鬼生身更有益；卦里如果没有身、又没有鬼，想去见人临出发一定又作罢；卦中官鬼落空亡，终究见不到。注文举例：假如正月占得泰卦，月令寅和卦身上的官鬼寅并在一起，就是和那个人平等相交。
  To see a noble, look at the monthly hexagram and its Body: if the hexagram has a footing in it one can meet; in divining one must see the Pressure line, and Pressure generating the Body is all the more helpful; if the hexagram has no Body and no Pressure, one who wishes to go and see will surely stop again as he sets out; if Pressure in the hexagram falls Void, in the end one cannot see him. The note gives an example: suppose in the first month one gets the hexagram Tai, and the month branch Yin joins the Pressure Yin on the Body of the hexagram: one meets that person as an equal.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.41** 占见平常人是喜是怒：内卦阳、外卦阴，相见美好，容貌像是嗔怒、心里却欢喜；阳爻动，是他出外游走，阴爻动，是他在家里；外卦阴、内卦见阳，九五相承，求见吉昌；内阴外阳，见不到面，即使见到，他嗔怒、在外面有伴。注文说：内卦是心，外卦是貌；阴主见不到、阳主见得到；阴主喜、阳主嗔；外卦阴主近、能相见，外卦阳主远、见不到；外阳内阴主不吉，见了也不相喜。
  On divining whether an ordinary person will be glad or angry on being seen: inner yang and outer yin, the meeting is fair, the face looks angry but the heart is glad; a yang line moving says he has gone out roaming, a yin line moving says he is at home; the outer trigram yin and the inner yang, with the fifth line yang on top, the call is auspicious; inner yin and outer yang, one does not meet, and even if they meet he is angry and has company outside. The notes say: the inner is the heart and the outer the countenance; yin means not meeting, yang means meeting; yin means gladness, yang means anger; outer yin means near and one can meet, outer yang means far and one cannot; outer yang with inner yin means ill luck, and even if they meet they do not take to each other.
