- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.42** 占进献书信、奏章：旺相、相生，都主顺利敏捷；休废、不和，终究不被应允；一阴又一阳、上下相生，也算相顺；内外都是阳、应生世，吉庆无疑；阴居休囚、刑杀并见、相克，要防忧愁滞碍；外卦阳，他听从，阴，他不从；有气相生就好，无气就凶；纯阳相生，他欢喜就听从；纯阴相凌，他像是装聋。注文逐条解释了阴阳相生为顺、相克为逆，以及应生世、父母爻是书信等。
  On divining the presenting of a letter or memorial: strong and generating one another, all go swiftly and smoothly; at rest or abandoned and at odds, in the end it is not granted; a yin and then a yang, upper and lower generating each other, it is also in accord; with inner and outer both yang and the Other generating the Self, good fortune is beyond doubt; yin in a place at rest or trapped with punishment and baleful spirits together, and mutual control, guard against worry and obstruction; the outer trigram yang, he listens and complies, yin, he does not follow; with qi and generating it is good, without qi bad; pure yang generating, he is glad and listens; pure yin bullying, he seems to feign deafness. The notes explain item by item that yin and yang generating each other is accord and controlling each other is opposition, that the Other generating the Self, and that the Resource line is the letter.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.43** 占想去见人该不该出门：阴气兴起的时候得阴卦，适宜见小人；阳气生起之后得阳卦，适宜见士人、贤者；阴卦遇上阳的时节就灭没，出门会被小人所害；阳卦遇上阴的时节不顺，出门也难得到君子的喜爱。注文说：夏至后阴气生，得阴卦是小人得时；冬至后阳气生，得阳卦是君子得时；得时则吉，失时则凶。
  On divining whether one who wishes to see someone may go out or not: when yin rises and one gets a yin hexagram it suits seeing petty people; after yang is born and one gets a yang hexagram it suits seeing scholars and the worthy; a yin hexagram meeting a yang season is extinguished, and going out one will be harmed by petty people; a yang hexagram meeting a yin season is not in accord, and going out one will hardly win the regard of the gentleman. The notes say: after the Summer Solstice yin qi is born, and getting a yin hexagram is the petty person's time; after the Winter Solstice yang qi is born, and getting a yang hexagram is the gentleman's time; in its time it is auspicious, out of its time it is bad.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.44** 参见拜谒，先要看卦名；稍遇爻动，利于生成；相扶相合，见面终究美好；有气，就如心遂意。只忌世在内卦克应在外卦，其余都可以指望受到迎接；阴爻旺相，利于见武职的人；阳爻升旺，利于见文人。注文说：世爻克应爻、内卦克外卦，都不利相见；应生世、外克内，则他欢喜、盼我去求；阴爻旺相利见武职和平辈，阳爻旺相利见文职和尊长。
  In calling on someone, first look at the name of the hexagram; a little movement of a line favours bringing it about; with mutual support and combination the meeting is in the end fair, and with qi it goes as the heart wishes. Only avoid the Self in the inner trigram controlling the Other in the outer; all the rest can hope to be welcomed; a strong yin line favours seeing military men and a rising, strong yang line favours seeing literati. The notes say: the Self line controlling the Other line, or the inner trigram controlling the outer, is unfavourable for meeting; if the Other generates the Self or the outer controls the inner, he is glad and hopes I will come and ask; a strong yin line favours seeing military officials and equals, a strong yang line civil officials and elders.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.45** 世、应、本宫、内外卦都旺，就不怕旬中碰上空亡，纵然刚到的时候没能见到，不久也会遇上，很相当；指望得财，必须有官鬼，伏神在卦里也算强；休囚的卦就不要指望，有喜神发动相助也不过寻常；休囚、空亡，不要打算；旺的杀伤身，反而受伤；生合扶助的卦如果不旺，寻常相见，见到的正是忠良之人。
  With the Self, the Other, the palace and the inner and outer trigrams all strong, one need not fear meeting Void within the decade, and even if on first arriving one cannot see him, one will soon meet and it is very fitting; hoping for wealth, there must be Pressure, and a hidden spirit present in the hexagram is also strong; a hexagram at rest or trapped, give up hope, and with the Joy spirit moving to help it is still only ordinary; at rest, trapped or Void, do not plan on it; a strong baleful spirit injuring the self brings injury instead; a hexagram generated, combined and supported but not strong, one meets as usual and the one met is just a loyal and good man.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.46** 拜谒人，要问拜谒的是谁；世、应坐着官鬼，白费心思；用爻出现却不乘土，去见他必定不在家。又说：凡拜谒人，以外卦来取：外卦阳，可以见到，外卦阴，见不到；阴化阳，宜再次去见，阳化阴，人已经出去了；外卦要出现，他才在家；忌讳外卦单独发动或伏藏，应爻发动，都见不到；第一看财爻，旺相、出现，忌讳发动；要见官员，看官爻；忌讳世、应坐着鬼。
  In calling on someone, ask whom one is calling on; with the Self or the Other sitting on Pressure, one wearies the mind in vain; the Subject line appearing but not riding Earth, going to see him, he is surely not at home. It also says: in calling on anyone, take it from the outer trigram: outer yang, one can see him, outer yin, one cannot; yin turning into yang, it suits seeing him again, yang turning into yin, he has already gone out; the outer trigram must appear for him to be at home; avoid the outer trigram moving alone or hidden, and with the Other moving in all cases one cannot see him; first look at the Wealth line, strong and appearing, and it should not move; to see an official, look at the Pressure line; avoid the Self or the Other sitting on Pressure.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.80** 臣子蒙受君王的恩典，必须得到岁君（太岁）来生世爻。要求恩宠，要看五爻是天子、太岁是朝廷：这两个象里如果有一个来生世爻或者生官爻，就沾到天子和朝廷所给的恩泽；如果落在空亡，就不要指望得到恩宠；不落空而来克世爻、伤克官爻的，本来就不是福。
  A minister who receives the sovereign's favour must have Tai Sui, the sovereign of the year, generating the Self. To seek favour, look at the fifth line as the Son of Heaven and Tai Sui as the court: if either of these two images generates the Self or generates the star of office, one shares in the grace given by the Son of Heaven and the court; if it falls on Void, do not hope for favour; if it is not Void but comes to control the Self or to hurt the star of office, it was never a blessing.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.81** 下级要承蒙上级的荐举，要求外卦的官鬼来扶持卦身。占荐举，专看外卦的官爻；外卦没有，内卦的官爻也可以用；内卦的官鬼再落空，就不必谋求指望；不论内卦外卦，卦中如果有官爻来生卦身、生世爻，一定承蒙提拔的恩典。
  A subordinate who is to receive a recommendation from above must seek the outer Pressure line to support the Body. In divining for a recommendation, look only at the Pressure line in the outer trigram; if the outer trigram has none, the inner star of office can be used; if the inner Pressure too is Void, there is no need to scheme and hope; whether inner or outer, if the hexagram has a star of office that generates the Body or the Self, one will surely receive the grace of being raised.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.82** 卦里有两个官爻，六部就一再推荐、一再举用；爻里一个官鬼也没有，三司就不奖励也不褒扬。如果旺相的官爻接连出现，又来生合世爻，一定得到一重又一重的荐举；六爻里如果没有官鬼，一定没有推举的官员。
  If the hexagram has two stars of office, the Six Boards recommend and raise one again and again; if the lines have not even one Pressure, the Three Offices neither commend nor praise. If thriving stars of office appear one after another and come to generate or combine with the Self line, one will surely receive recommendation upon recommendation; if there is no Pressure among the six lines, there is surely no official to recommend one.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.94** 占恩封诰命，以文书爻为主，以官鬼为凭据。想知道诰命要赠给谁，卦里要分六亲：父爻当作皇帝的封诰，世爻是自己，官爻是吏部，五爻是君王，再把太岁当作天子，乾卦应当称作帝王的尊位；君位怕落空和绝，五爻忌克制世爻和身爻。凡是占诰命，父母爻是用爻，不绝不空，终究有指望；又看世爻是自己、官爻是吏部、五爻是天子、太岁是朝廷，都不要落空亡；代表君位的爻，千万不要伤身、克世；乾卦也代表君王，不论内卦外卦得到这个象的，一定受到恩封。
  In divining about an honorific patent, take the document as the chief and Pressure as the ground. To know on whom the imperial patent will be conferred, one must distinguish the six kin in the hexagram: Resource is taken as the imperial patent, the Self is oneself, the star of office is the Board of Personnel, the fifth line is the sovereign, and Tai Sui is further taken as the Son of Heaven; the Qian hexagram is called the honoured emperor; the sovereign's position fears Void and extinction, and the fifth line must not control the Self and the Body. In every divination about a patent, Resource is the Subject line, and if it is not extinct and not Void there must in the end be hope; look also at the Self as oneself, the star of office as the Board of Personnel, the fifth line as the Son of Heaven and Tai Sui as the court, none of which may be Void; the line of the sovereign's position must on no account hurt the Body or control the Self; the Qian hexagram also stands for the sovereign, and whoever gets this image, inner or outer, will surely receive the conferment.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.95** 君爻生合哪一爻，就看到天恩赐给那个人：财爻遇生，封赠妻子；子爻逢合，荫及儿孙；世爻、身爻如果与君爻相合，品级加增，做到大臣。具体说：五爻和太岁生合父母爻，一定赠封父母（椿萱）；生合财爻，一定封赠妻室；生扶福德（子孙），应当荫及儿孙；生合世爻、身爻，再加官爻旺相，自然品级职位高升。
  Wherever the sovereign's line generates or combines, there one sees heaven's favour conferred on that person: if the Wealth image meets generation, the wife is enfeoffed; if the Output line meets combination, descendants receive privilege; if the Self or Body is combined with the sovereign's line, one's rank is increased and one becomes a great minister. In detail: if the fifth line and Tai Sui generate or combine with Resource, the parents will surely be honoured with a title; if they generate or combine with Wealth, the wife will surely be enfeoffed; if they generate and support Output, the fortune virtue, descendants should receive privilege; if they generate or combine with the Self or Body and the Pressure line is further thriving, one's rank and post will naturally be raised high.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.96** 官鬼发动、父爻兴旺，就迎来诰命；父爻落空、财爻发动，就断绝皇恩；官鬼休囚，适宜托人嘱托；文书（父母）被破、被绝，就不要费心。被召、被宣同这个取法；去朝见、去上奏，按它的缘由来断。卦里官爻、父爻都旺，一定沐浴洪恩；父爻如果空亡，难迎诰命；妻财独自发动、克制文书，难沾雨露；官鬼休囚，托人走门路为好；父爻临空、绝，白费机谋。君王来宣召、臣子去朝见，都喜欢君爻生世爻；官爻旺相为好，各自都忌帝位克制身爻，官鬼落空不吉。
  If Pressure moves and Resource flourishes, one welcomes the patent; if Resource is Void and Wealth moves, the sovereign's favour is cut off; if Pressure is resting or imprisoned it is fitting to entrust the matter to someone; if the document is broken or extinct, do not trouble your heart; being summoned or called follows this same rule, and going to court or memorialising follows its causes. If in the hexagram the star of office and Resource are both thriving, one will surely bathe in great favour; if Resource is Void, it is hard to receive the patent; if Wealth moves alone and controls the document, it is hard to share in the rain and dew of favour; if Pressure is resting or imprisoned, going through connections is best; if Resource is Void or extinct, scheming is in vain. Whether the sovereign summons or the minister goes to court, in all cases it is welcome for the sovereign's line to generate the Self; a thriving star of office is good, in each case the throne's position controlling the Body is feared, and Pressure Void is ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.99** 去谒见贵人，以世爻、应爻为主，以外卦、月卦为凭据。外卦本来就是用爻：兴旺并且出现，就能相会；没有卦身、没有官鬼，都见不到。凡是去谒见贵人，最嫌外卦空亡；外卦出现而且旺相，人一定能见到；如果外卦三个爻都动，一定有变更；一、二个爻动，不这样论，还要取卦身和官鬼之爻，缺了其中一个，又见不到。
  In going to call on a noble, take the Self and the Other as the chief, and the outer trigram and the month hexagram as the ground. The outer trigram is originally the Subject line: if it flourishes and appears, one gets to meet; with no Body and no Pressure, one does not meet. In every call on a noble, what is most disliked is the outer trigram falling on Void; if the outer trigram appears and is thriving, one will surely meet the person; if all three lines of the outer trigram move, there will surely be a change; if one or two lines move, it is not judged so, but one must also take the Body and the Pressure line, and if one of these is lacking, one again does not meet.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.100** 主人如果伤了宾，情意就丢开了。再看世爻是主、应爻是宾，内卦是主、外卦是宾：内外卦、世应相生，或者比和，或者应克世、外克内，都主相逢称心；唯独世克应、内克外，主方反而冒犯宾方，即使见面，也不相投。
  If the host injures the guest, the feeling is thrown away. Look also at the Self as the host and the Other as the guest, and the inner as the host and the outer as the guest: if the inner and outer trigrams or the Self and the Other generate each other, or are in accord, or the Other controls the Self, or the outer controls the inner, in every case it means meeting to one's satisfaction; only if the Self controls the Other, or the inner controls the outer, does the host on the contrary offend the guest, and even if they meet they will not get on.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.101** 世爻落在空地，事难成；应爻落到空亡，白费辛劳。拜访客人，喜欢遇到三合的卦；会见宾客，怕遇到六冲的爻。趋谒的事，世、应和官鬼都不要落空亡，这三个里如果有一个落空，一定彼此不合；即使不遇空，如果遇到六冲，也一定没有好意；如果得到三合、六合的卦，宾主自然和谐；如果应象又发动，就说是人不遇。
  If the Self falls on empty ground the matter is hard to accomplish; if the Other falls on Void the toil is wasted. In calling on a visitor one is glad to meet a three-harmony hexagram; in meeting a guest one fears a six-clash line. In a call on a noble, the Self, the Other and Pressure must none be Void; if one of the three is Void, the two will surely not agree; even if none is Void, if there is a six-clash there will surely be no good will; if the hexagram is a three-harmony or a six-combine, guest and host are naturally in accord; if the Other image moves as well, it is said that the person is not met.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.102** 去求书信、帖文，忧的是财爻发动，偏宜父母爻发动；去排解是非、脱出官司，子孙爻动，祸殃就消；求利、打抽丰，财爻、官爻两旺，就乐得很。具体说：去求文、取帖，以及送书、递柬，不适宜父母爻空亡，更忌妻财发动；想排解词讼、平息官司，反而适宜官鬼休囚，又怕应爻来伤世爻；遇到子孙持世或发动，祸一定消散；如果借公事来成全私情、或者打抽丰对自己有利，必须财、官两旺，切忌兄弟发动。
  If one seeks a letter or a note, one worries about Wealth moving and it is fitting for Resource to move; if one goes to settle a dispute or escape a lawsuit, when the Output line moves calamity is dispelled; if one seeks gain by drawing a rich man's bounty, Wealth and the star of office both thriving make one overflow with joy. In detail: in going to ask for a text or take a note, and in sending a letter or handing in a card, it is not fitting for Resource to be Void, and still more Wealth moving is avoided; if one wants to clear up a dispute and stop a lawsuit, it is on the contrary fitting for Pressure to be resting or imprisoned, and one fears the Other coming to hurt the Self; if Output holds the Self or moves, the calamity will surely vanish; if one makes use of a public affair to serve a private feeling, or draws a bounty for one's own benefit, Wealth and the star of office must both be thriving, and Peer moving is by all means to be avoided.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.07** 太岁是君主的象，月建是执政的官。它们如果生合世爻、身爻，必有好处；只怕冲克世身，必定遭到贬谪。月建扶出官爻、世爻，必是风宪的职位；太岁加在父母上、扶出官鬼和世爻的，必有天恩，再得日辰在生旺之地更好；衰绝、遇空就没有用。
  The Tai Sui is the image of the sovereign, and the Month is the officer who holds government. If they generate or combine with the Self or the Body, there will surely be benefit; what is feared is that they clash or control the Self or the Body, and then one will surely be demoted and exiled. If the Month supports Pressure or the Self, it is surely a post in the censorate; if the Tai Sui is added to Resource and supports Pressure and the Self, there will surely be the Emperor's favour, better still if the Day then stands in a thriving place; if weak, in extinction or Void, it is of no use.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.36** 官鬼发动，应当有阻隔；但如果它来生合世爻、身爻，又主这个馆必得贵人推荐才能成；应爻如果克世，或者父母落空，即使有人推荐也不成。
  If Pressure moves there will be obstruction; but if it comes to generate or combine with the Self or the Body, it also means the school can be had only with a noble's recommendation; if the Other controls the Self, or Resource is Void, then even with a recommendation it does not come through.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.44** 官鬼发动化出兄弟，必须拿出礼物先酬谢推荐馆的人，才能成；兄弟临世身，也主要先花费财物；兄弟临在应爻上，是东家好利，必须有礼品馈送，然后才能成。
  If Pressure moves and turns into Peer, one must give gifts first to repay the person who recommended one to the school, and then it can come through; Peer resting on the Self or the Body likewise means that one must first spend money and goods; if Peer rests on the Other, the host is fond of profit, and gifts must be sent before it can come through.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.45** 官鬼爻出现而世爻又化出官鬼的，必须再得到推荐才能成；卦里没有官鬼而动爻化出官鬼的，当初没有人推荐，也必须等央求人去推荐才能成；官鬼化官鬼、父母化父母，又主反复难成。
  If Pressure appears and the Self also turns into Pressure, one must obtain a further recommendation before it can come through; if there is no Pressure in the hexagram but a moving line turns into Pressure, no one recommended one at first and one must wait for someone to be begged to recommend one before it can come through; if Pressure turns into Pressure or Resource into Resource, it also means going back and forth and being hard to bring about.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.03** 占谒见贵人，以官鬼为主，世是我、应是他：世应相生，就能遇到和气喜悦。凡占谒贵，以外卦来取：外卦阳爻，见得到；外卦阴爻，见不到。阴的鬼遇阳的世，再来相见；阳的鬼遇阴的世，他已经外出。（用爻）出现，是在家；忌外卦独发。伏藏、应动，都见不到。
  In divining about calling on a great man, Pressure is the master; the Self is me and the Other is he: if the Self and the Other generate each other, one meets with harmony and pleasure. In all divinations about calling on a great man, take it from the outer trigram: a yang line in the outer trigram means he can be seen, a yin line in the outer trigram means he cannot. With a yin Pressure line and a yang Self, one will see him later; with a yang Pressure line and a yin Self, he has already gone out. If it appears, he is at home; the outer trigram moving alone is feared. If it is hidden, or the Other moves, he is not seen in either case.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.04** （为求财去谒见）看财爻旺相、出现，忌讳它动；（要见的是官员，用官）就看官爻，忌世、应坐鬼；又必须问清要见的是什么人，以那个人的用爻为主。谒见的时候，用爻的地支出现、旺相、不动，他在家；如果空、冲、散，他不在。
  (For one who calls to seek money) look at the Wealth line thriving and showing, and fear it moving; (if the one to be seen is an official, so that Pressure is used) look at the Pressure line, and fear the Self or the Other sitting on Pressure; and one must also ask whom one is to see, and the Subject line of that man is the master. In calling on him, if the branch of the Subject line appears, is thriving and does not move, he is at home; if it is Void, clashed or scattered, he is not in.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.05** 世应相生、相合就吉，相克必然凶。世克应，或克用爻，都是招致怨恨之象，应当处处小心。应克世，或用爻克世，（他）都愿意见，但也担忧（受）刑责。应和用爻相克，不如相生。卦有身，见面时更要看用爻，用爻生身尤其好；卦无身，又无用爻，或者用爻空亡，终究见不到。
  If the Self and the Other generate or combine with each other it is auspicious, and if they control each other it is surely ominous. The Self controlling the Other, or controlling the Subject line, are all signs of drawing resentment, and one should be careful in every move. The Other controlling the Self, or the Subject line controlling the Self, he is willing to see one, but there is also the worry of punishment. The Other and the Subject line controlling each other is not as good as generating each other. If the hexagram has a Body, in the meeting one looks further at the Subject line, and the Subject line generating the Body is especially good; if the hexagram has no Body and no Subject line, or the Subject line is Void, one is never seen.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.04** 贵人持世，承受九天雨露般的恩泽。贵人有两种：天乙贵人和福星贵人，宜旺相，怕刑、冲；落在官鬼和文书上，主官职高升、必登相位；福星、贵人持世，这人有福德，一生遇险不用忧虑，临到凶事也无祸咎。
  With the Noble holding the Self one receives favour like the rain and dew of the ninth heaven. There are two kinds of Noble, the Tianyi Noble and the Fortune-star Noble; it should be strong and fears punishment and clash; standing on Pressure or on the documents it means a high promotion and surely a seat as chancellor; with the Fortune star or the Noble holding the Self the person has blessing and virtue, meets danger all his life without anxiety and meets calamity without fault.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.11** 爻上有月德、月建，必定戴上獬豸冠（监察执法之冠）。月德、月建都是清正的吉神，各种杀都不敢抬头看；若加在贵人上、扶持官鬼和世爻，必定不是州县的官，而是风宪（监察）的职位；再看眼下的衰旺，就能断他升官赴任；这种情形不忌凶杀，遇杀反而威风凛凛、一举一动惊人、掌权轰轰烈烈。
  With the Month Virtue and the Month on the line one will surely wear the Xiezhi cap (the cap of the censor and judge). The Month Virtue and the Month are both upright auspicious spirits at which no baleful spirit dares look up; added to the Noble and supporting Pressure and the Self line, it is surely not a post in a prefecture or county but a censorate office; look then at the present strength or weakness to judge his promotion and taking up the post; here baleful spirits are not feared, and meeting one makes one awe-inspiring, every move startling and one's wielding of power resounding.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.34** 占荐馆（托人推荐馆席），类似仗托（托人办事）：本卦六冲、应坏，他就不出力；应克世，他就不忠。占攘馆（有人谋夺馆席），如同占夺婚：应破、散、空，他的图谋不成；应克世，就被他夺走。
  In divining about recommending someone to a post it is like entrusting a matter to someone: if the original hexagram is a six-clash and the Other is damaged, he does not exert himself; if the Other controls the Self, he is not loyal. In divining about snatching a post it is like snatching a marriage: if the Other is broken, scattered or Void, his scheme does not succeed; if the Other controls the Self, one is robbed of it by him.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.37** 请荫（请求让子孙凭父祖的官阶得官）、请封（请求给父母妻室封赠诰命），要戒备五爻伤世，以及官鬼克身。父爻是玉牒（封荫的凭据），不可破、空；世是为自身谋划的，不可虚、陷。如果是替父母、子、妻求的，世空就不忌。
  In asking for hereditary privilege (a descendant entering office on the strength of a forefather's rank) or for the conferment of titles (honours for parents and wife), guard against the fifth line harming the Self and against Pressure controlling the Body. The Resource line is the jade register (the document on which such grants rest), and must not be broken or Void; the Self is the one who plans for himself, and must not be empty or fallen. If it is sought on behalf of parents, sons or wife, the Self being Void is not feared.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.56** 求荐举、嘉奖，而遇到官爻、文书破坏，恐怕荐贤的人不出力；鬼象克世，担心嘉奖不是真心实意；世遇空亡，求荣不成，盼升也难迁。被贬谪的官员请求开复（恢复官职），也同此推断。民间的牌坊匾额、旌表、冠带、衣巾、乡贤、乡饮等事，也属同类。
  In seeking recommendation or commendation, if Pressure and Resource (the document line) are broken and damaged, one fears that those who recommend the worthy do not exert themselves; if the Pressure image controls the Self, one fears the commendation is not genuine; if the Self meets Void, seeking honour does not succeed and hoping for promotion one cannot move. For a demoted official asking to be reinstated, judge in the same way. Memorial arches and tablets in the villages, commendation, caps and sashes, scholars' robes, the shrine of local worthies, and the village drinking ceremony are of the same kind.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.87** 占嘱托（托人办事），随所取的用神：或者用官，或者用应，或者用六亲，都忌空，空就不出力；都忌破，破就没有功效；都忌六冲克世，那就生疑、受阻、生出嫌隙；随墓、助鬼，就不利于公堂。旺衰来分他出力的大小，合冲来定他情意的向背，化进就勇于办事，化退就倦于行动；五行、六神用来断他的性情，但不要拿它来主断吉凶。金坚直，木从容，水曲折，火燥烈，土诚信；龙和，虎勇，雀再三，武婉转，勾端实，蛇缠绵——这是五行、六神按生旺说的，空、破就反过来。
  In divining about entrusting a matter to another, one follows the Subject taken: whether Pressure, or the Other, or one of the six relations, all fear Void, and Void means he does not exert himself; all fear broken, and broken means no achievement; all fear a six-clash controlling the Self, which breeds suspicion, obstruction and quarrel; following into the tomb and helping Pressure is unfavourable at the public court. Strength and weakness tell how much force he has; combination and clash fix whether his feeling is toward or against me; changing into an advance means he is bold in the matter, changing into a retreat means he tires of acting; the five phases and the six spirits are used to judge his temper, but one does not make them the master of good and ill. Metal is firm and straight, Wood is easygoing, Water is roundabout, Fire is fierce and hasty, Earth is sincere and faithful; the Azure Dragon is mild, the White Tiger bold, the Vermilion Bird says it again and again, the Dark Tortoise is indirect, Hook Earth is upright and solid, the Coiling Snake clings; this is said of the phases and the six spirits when they are strong, and Void or broken turns it round.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.17** 在任，荐举、宣敕和征召：官星变出财爻，不是受嘉奖就是被荐举；官星、贵人旺相发动、来生合世爻的，眼前就蒙人荐举；太岁、月建带着印星旺相发动的，朝廷有宣读的敕命；太岁与五爻发动、来生合世爻的，被征召入朝；官爻、世爻临在五爻或太岁上，又得印星发动来生合的，名字上了御榻；三传带着财爻发动、来生合世爻的，受赏赐。
  In office, recommendation, edicts and summons. If the star of office turns into Wealth, he is either commended or recommended; if the star of office and the Noble are thriving and moving and generate or combine with the Self, he is recommended right away; if Tai Sui and the Month carry the seal line, thriving and moving, an edict is proclaimed at court; if Tai Sui and the fifth line move to generate or combine with the Self, he is summoned to court; if the Pressure line and the Self sit on the fifth line or on Tai Sui and a moving seal line generates or combines with them, his name is written on the imperial couch; if the three rulers carrying Wealth move to generate or combine with the Self, he receives gifts.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.18** 在任，封赠：太岁与五爻生合哪一亲的爻，哪一亲就受恩典：生合父母爻，父母受封赠；生合妻财，妻子受诰命；生合子孙，儿孙得到荫庇；生合官鬼，已故的祖母得恩荣。
  In office, honours conferred on kin. Whichever kin line Tai Sui and the fifth line generate or combine with, those kin receive the favour: if Resource, his parents receive honorary titles; if Wealth, his wife receives an imperial patent; if Output, his sons and grandsons gain hereditary privilege; if Pressure, his late grandmother receives the grace of honours.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.73** 拜谒显贵的占问：人去拜谒显贵，必定有所求、有所托，起初担心见不到，见到了又担心他不照所请求的办，所以不能不占。看所拜谒的那位贵人是哪一爻（大贵、中贵、朝贵、州郡、省贵、县贵、乡贵），这一爻出现、旺相发动、生合身爻、世爻的，去了必定见得到，见了必定如我所请；如果伏藏、空、死、墓、绝、胎，又刑、害、克、冲世爻、身爻的，去了必定见不到，见了必定不遂我的求。
  Divining a call on a noble. A man calling on a noble surely has something to ask and something to entrust, and at first fears he will not be seen, and once seen fears the noble will not do as asked, so one cannot do without a divination. Look at which line the noble called on is (the great noble, the middle noble, the court noble, prefecture and commandery, the provincial noble, the county noble, the village noble): if this line appears, thriving and moving, and generates or combines with the Body and Self, going he will surely be seen and once seen it will surely be as I ask; if it lies hidden or is Void, dead, in tomb, extinct or in womb, and punishes, harms, controls or clashes with the Self and Body, going he will surely not be seen and if seen it will surely not fulfil my request.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.74** 见不见得到，先看他在不在家：外卦和应爻、官爻属阴的，他在家；属阳的，他出外；阳变阴的，刚从外地回来；阴变阳的，刚要出外。应爻、官爻出现的，在家；伏藏的，不在。
  Whether he can be seen, first whether he is at home. If the outer trigram and the Other and Pressure lines are yin, he is at home; if yang, he is out; yang changing into yin, he has just returned from elsewhere; yin changing into yang, he is just going out. If the Other and Pressure lines appear, he is at home; if they lie hidden, he is not in.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.75** 见得到见不到：官星发动的，难见；落空的，见不到；空而旺相的，过了这一旬才见得到；出现、旺相发动而不带土爻的，见不到；阴爻变阳爻的，见到而且被接待；阳爻变阴爻的，第二次去见时被拒绝。占得六合的，见得到；占得六冲的，见不到。卦里没有身爻，或没有官鬼，或世、应都落空，世、应都是官鬼的，不必去见。凡世、应、内外卦的官鬼相生相合比和的，等应爻、官爻生世爻、合世爻的日子可以见到，或者应爻、官爻生旺的日子见面。
  Whether he is seen. If the star of office is moving, he is hard to see; if Void, he is not seen; if Void but thriving, he is seen only after the decade is past; if it appears, thriving and moving, without carrying Earth, he is not seen; a yin line changing into yang: seen and received; a yang line changing into yin: refused on the second visit. If the divination is a six-combine, he is seen; if a six-clash, not. If the hexagram has no Body, or no Pressure, or the Self and Other are both Void, or the Self and Other are both Pressure, there is no need to go. Whenever the Pressure lines of the Self, the Other, and the inner and outer trigrams generate, combine or are in harmony, wait for a day when the Other or the Pressure line generates or combines with the Self, or a day when the Other or the Pressure line is born or thriving, to meet him.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.76** 见面时他是喜是怒：外卦和应爻、官爻，生合世爻、身爻和内卦的，见面时他高兴；刑、害、克、冲世爻、身爻和内卦的，见面时他恼怒。应爻、官爻虽然生合身爻、世爻，而变爻刑、害、克、冲的，先高兴后恼怒；先刑、害、克、冲身爻、世爻，而变爻来生合的，先恼怒后和善。应爻、官爻受日辰、动爻刑、害、克、破，或变成死、墓、绝、胎的，他有灾病祸事。加青龙发动的，和气多情；加朱雀发动的，话多易怒；加勾陈发动的，直率没有文采；加螣蛇发动的，多疑少信；加白虎发动的，狠毒没有恩情；加玄武发动的，狡诈多端。
  Whether he is pleased or angry at the meeting. If the outer trigram and the Other and Pressure lines generate or combine with the Self, Body and inner trigram, he is pleased at the meeting; if they punish, harm, control or clash with the Self, Body and inner trigram, he is angry. If the Other and Pressure lines generate or combine with the Body and Self but the changed line punishes, harms, controls or clashes with them, he is pleased first and angry after; if they first punish, harm, control or clash with the Body and Self and the changed line comes to generate or combine, he is angry first and kind after. If the Other and Pressure lines are punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the Day or a moving line, or turn into death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, he has illness or disaster. With the Azure Dragon moving, he is genial and warm; with the Bird, talkative and quick to anger; with Hook Earth, blunt and unrefined; with the Snake, suspicious and little trusting; with the Tiger, fierce and without kindness; with the Dark Tortoise, deceitful in many ways.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.77** 所求的事不同，取的用神也不同：为攀援求助、打官司，以及上书献策而去谒见贵人的，以官鬼为用神——官鬼旺相发动、生合世爻、身爻的，吉；官鬼逢空、死、墓、绝、胎的，没有力量；官鬼发动来刑、害、克、冲身爻、世爻的，不允；加白虎、凶煞来伤的，不但不允，反而遭到罪责贬谪。为平息官司、开脱罪名而谒见贵人的，以子孙为用神——应爻持子孙旺相发动，带喝散、解神生合世爻的，或者世爻持子孙，或者月建、日辰是子孙来生合世爻的，吉；应爻上的子孙逢空、死、墓、绝、胎，或被刑、害、克、冲的，没有力量。为求书信、恳求荐举而谒见贵人的，以父母为用神——官鬼旺相发动、生合父爻，以及父爻旺相发动、生合身爻、世爻的，有；父爻逢空、死、墓、绝、胎的，没有；父爻被刑、害、克、破的，有也像没有一样。财爻持世旺相发动，以及财爻单独发动的，所求必定得不到，得到也没有力量。
  What is asked decides the Subject. For one calling on a noble to scheme for help, to sue, or to present a memorial with proposals, take Pressure as the Subject: if Pressure is thriving and moving and generates or combines with the Self and Body, it is auspicious; if Pressure meets Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, it has no strength; if Pressure moves to punish, harm, control or clash with the Body and Self, it is not granted; with the Tiger and a baleful spirit coming to injure, not only is it not granted, but he is instead blamed and demoted. For one calling on a noble to end a lawsuit and clear a charge, take Output as the Subject: if the Other holds Output, thriving and moving, with Hesan and Jiexie generating or combining with the Self, or the Self holds Output, or the Month or Day is Output coming to generate or combine with the Self, it is auspicious; if Output on the Other meets Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, or is punished, harmed, controlled or clashed, it has no strength. For one calling on a noble to ask for a letter or beg for a recommendation, take Resource as the Subject: if Pressure is thriving and moving and generates or combines with Resource, or Resource is thriving and moving and generates or combines with the Body and Self, there will be one; if Resource meets Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, there will be none; if Resource is punished, harmed, controlled or broken, it is as if there were none though there is. If Wealth holds the Self and is thriving and moving, or if the Wealth line alone moves, what is asked will surely not be had, and if had it will have no force.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.78** 带着礼物去谒见贵人：以应爻为用神。应爻逢空、死、墓、绝、胎的，人遇不到，遇到了也不投合；财爻带退神发动，以及财爻发动变成退神的，礼物不被收纳；应爻的财旺相发动、生合世爻的，送得薄而回报厚；应爻发动刑、害、克、冲世爻，而间爻又发动的，被人从中挑拨而情分疏远。
  Calling on a noble with a gift. Take the Other as the Subject. If the Other meets Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, the man is not met, and if met he does not take to the visitor; if Wealth carries the Retreating spirit and moves, or Wealth moves and turns into a Retreating spirit, the gift is not accepted; if the Wealth of the Other is thriving and moving and generates or combines with the Self, the gift is slight and the return generous; if the Other moves to punish, harm, control or clash with the Self and a line between also moves, someone comes between them and the feeling cools.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.87** 托贵人办事：只要官星、贵人受刑、害、克、破，就不可以向贵人求事，因为贵人自己受克制，不能成就我。如果官星、贵人刑、冲、克、害身爻、世爻的，求情难得许诺，后来被别人插队抢先，有始无终，反而要花费钱财。《毕法赋》说：凡是书占得夜贵人，叫作脱气，凡事被贵人哄骗；这是谋望可否成败的附带占断。
  Entrusting a noble with the matter. If the star of office and the Noble are punished, harmed, controlled or broken, one must not ask the noble for the matter, because the noble himself is under restraint and cannot see me through. If the star of office and the Noble punish, clash with, control or harm the Body and Self, a plea is hard to get granted, and later someone else jumps ahead, so it begins and does not end, and one even spends money for it. The Bifa Fu says: when a document divination gets the night Noble, it is called a drained breath, and in everything one is cajoled and cheated by the noble; this is an appended judgment on whether a scheme can and will succeed.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.20** 官星相合、青龙兴旺，恩命就到了：青龙官星持世，或世爻临日月，或日月、动爻生合世爻，或世爻临太岁、五爻，或太岁、五爻生合世爻，或世爻与官星三合，都是吉兆；居高位的蒙皇上特别重用，职位一般的加级越级升迁。
  When the star of office combines and the Dragon flourishes, a gracious order arrives: the star of office on the Azure Dragon holding the Self, or the Self on the Day or Month, or the Day, Month or a moving line generating or combining with the Self, or the Self on Tai Sui or the fifth line, or Tai Sui or the fifth line generating or combining with the Self, or the Self and the star of office in three-harmony, are all auspicious signs; for one in high office the emperor's favour brings a special employment, and for one in an ordinary post a rise in grade and promotion beyond rank.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.03** 占官、见贵人，遇到丰、豫、升、随、巽、无妄、离、同人、复、临、夬、比这十二个卦，官职和贵人都顺遂有利：见贵得贵、求名得名、官贵随顺、官贵有喜、贵官俱遂。
  In divining office and calling on nobles, meeting any of the twelve hexagrams Feng, Yu, Sheng, Sui, Xun, Wuwang, Li, Tongren, Fu, Lin, Guai and Bi, office and nobles alike go smoothly and favourably: seeing a noble one gains the noble, seeking fame one gains fame, officials and nobles comply, officials and nobles have joy, nobles and office are both fulfilled.

## 差役(Clerks, runners and stewards: enlisting, serving, leaving service)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/差役.md`

- **LY.CAREER.EL.106** 谋差使、顶替衙门的役，以官爻为主，以世爻为凭据。书办、捕快、兵、民壮、皂隶、差役这些人谋差顶役，要细看世爻；都忌六冲、官爻害世爻，都适宜财爻、官鬼旺相才强。凡是衙门里的差事、生意、顶役、谋差，如果遇到六冲，一定难以成就，即使成了也不长久；官鬼如果伤了身爻、世爻，终究遭到罪责，难以逃脱；得到财、官两旺在其中，一定见到兴家。
  In seeking a commission or taking over a post in a yamen's service, take the Pressure line as the chief and the Self image as the ground. For clerks, scribes, constables, soldiers, militiamen, lictors and runners seeking a commission or taking over a service, examine the Self line in detail; all fear a six-clash and the star of office harming the Self, and all are fitly strong when Wealth and Pressure are thriving. For every job in a yamen, whether a livelihood, taking over a service or seeking a commission, if it meets a six-clash it will surely be hard to accomplish, and even if it is accomplished it will not last; if Pressure injures the Body or the Self, one will in the end be blamed and punished and cannot escape; if Wealth and the star of office are both thriving within it, one will surely see the household prosper.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.107** 更怕身、命随着官爻入墓，世爻坐空亡，谁来帮助？没有官鬼，就不要来谋这份役；官爻临在身爻、世爻上，却适宜担当。具体说：身爻、世爻、本命随着官爻入墓，祸就挡不住；世爻落在空亡，哪能长久？卦中没有官鬼，所做的不成，即使成了也始终无益；如果官爻临在身爻、世爻上，本命又遇到生扶，就适宜充任这份役。
  Still more fear the Body and the fate following the star of office into the tomb, and with the Self sitting on Void who will assist? With no Pressure do not come to seek this service; with the star of office on the Body or the Self it is on the contrary fitting to take it up. In detail: if the Body, the Self and the native fate follow the star of office into the tomb, the calamity cannot be withstood; if the Self falls on Void, how can it last long? If the hexagram has no Pressure, what one does will not succeed, and even if it succeeds it is of no benefit from first to last; if the star of office rests on the Body or the Self and the native fate meets generation and support, it is fitting to fill this service.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.108** 财爻落空、官鬼衰弱、兄爻发动，只得到虚名，利益不旺；世爻不遇冲、官爻不绝，差使办成、役位到手，心里欢喜。财爻落在空中、官爻居衰地，事情虽看似成了，一定没有钱财；卦中即使有衰弱的财爻，或者遇到兄动，也不吉祥；世爻不冲不破、官爻不绝不空，就可以说谋求能成、求财得利。
  With Wealth Void, Pressure weak and the Peer line moving, one gets only an empty name and profit does not flourish; if the Self does not meet a clash and the star of office is not extinct, the commission is won and the service is taken up, and the heart is glad. If Wealth falls into Void and the star of office sits in a weak place, then although the matter seems to be accomplished there will surely be no money; even if there is a weak Wealth line in the hexagram, or Peer moves, it is not auspicious; if the Self image is not clashed or broken and the Pressure line is not extinct or Void, one may say that the scheme will succeed and that seeking wealth will bring gain.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.109** 百姓承当户役，有轻重的不同；卦里出现的官爻，看它的旺衰就可以验证。官爻居于旺相，役一定高大强劲；休囚，才细小；官克世爻，役厚而不薄；伤身爻，役重而不轻；带青龙是吉，加白虎是凶。要知道户役的轻重，必须看官爻的衰旺：旺就重大，衰就轻微；或者落空，或者入绝，都主没有忧虑；官鬼即使休囚，如果克世，还是繁重；官爻虽然旺相，但生世爻，赔费还轻；值青龙，终究没有责罚；临白虎，必定触犯官刑。
  The people bear household services of lighter and heavier kinds, and the Pressure line that appears in the hexagram can be tested by its strength or weakness. If it is in a thriving place the service will certainly be high and strong; if it is resting or imprisoned it is small and fine; if it controls the Self the service is thick and not thin; if it hurts the Body it is heavy and not light; carrying the Azure Dragon it is called auspicious, and adding the White Tiger it is called ominous. To know whether the household service is light or heavy, look at the strength of the Pressure line: if thriving it is heavy and large, if weak it is light and slight; if Void or extinct, in either case there is nothing to worry about; even if Pressure is resting or imprisoned, if it controls the Self the burden will still be heavy; even if the star of office is thriving, if it generates the Self the cost of making up shortfalls is still light; on the Azure Dragon there is in the end no blame or punishment, and on the White Tiger one will surely run into official punishment.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.110** 月建临在官爻上，应被派作魁首（最重的役）；日辰克官鬼，应审定为轻微。官爻值世，估定是高名（高等户，役重）；福德加身，登下榜（下等户，役轻）。具体说：官临月建，是最重的差；鬼值世爻，是次重的役；官鬼遇到日月克伤，役轻是可以断定的；子孙持世或者发动，这个户也轻。
  If the Month rests on the star of office, one should be assigned as the headman; if the Day controls Pressure, one should judge it light and slight. If the Pressure line holds the Self, one is reckoned of high name; if Output, the fortune virtue, is added to the Body, one ascends the lower roll. In detail: the star of office on the Month is the heaviest commission; Pressure on the Self line is the next heaviest service; if Pressure meets the control and injury of the Day and the Month, lightness can be affirmed; if Output holds the Self or moves, the household too is light.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.111** 子孙化成官，就把轻变成重；官化成子孙，就转祸为祥。兄弟发动时，多破费；子孙旺相处，少亏损。要细看用爻的强弱，解户的深浅就预先定了。具体说：子孙变成官爻，解运看着轻，赔补赊欠却有；官鬼变成子孙的象，役大、赔补却小；兄弟发动或者持世，一定多赔补、多赊欠；子孙旺相或者临在身爻上，花费钱财还算可以。
  If Output changes into the star of office, light is made heavy; if the star of office changes into Output, calamity is turned to fortune. When Peer moves there is much expense; where Output thrives there is little loss. Examine the strength of the Subject line, and the depth or shallowness of the conveyor household's burden is settled in advance. In detail: if Output changes into the Pressure line, the conveyance is light but there are shortfalls and arrears; if Pressure changes into the Output image, the service is great but the shortfall is slight; if Peer moves or rests on the Self, there will surely be many shortfalls and arrears; if Output is thriving or rests on the Body line, the cost in wealth is somewhat tolerable.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.112** 要把自己的役扳到别人身上（求帮户），以世爻、应爻为主，以官鬼为凭据。把役扳给别人，忌讳应爻落空；六冲又没有官鬼，对方难以服从；应爻克世爻，他没有过错；官鬼落空亡，官司就像风一样（落空）。想求得帮户，必须看应爻和官爻：应爻如果空亡，「师来帮贴」；世爻被应爻克制，没有力量去扳他；如果卜得六冲，一定难以遂意；没有官鬼，怎么能扳到人？官鬼的象临空、临绝，官的心里没有主意，不会有作为。
  To shift a service onto others and so seek help from another household, take the Self and the Other as the chief and Pressure as the ground. In passing the service on to another, the Other being Void is to be avoided; with a six-clash and no Pressure the other party finds it hard to comply; if the Other controls the Self, he is not at fault; if Pressure falls on Void, the lawsuit is like the wind. To seek a helper household one must look at the Other and the Pressure line: if the Other is Void, 'the master comes to help with a subsidy'; if the Self is controlled by the Other, one has no strength to drag him in; if a six-clash is cast it will surely be hard to achieve one's wish; with no Pressure how can one drag another in? If the Pressure image meets Void or extinction, the official's mind has no direction and he does nothing.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.113** 应爻被世爻伤、被官鬼克，一定能把役扳累过去，获得全功；卦爻相生相合、妻财旺相，一定来贴补费用，双方和同；应爻上临官爻、临月破，这个人受责、遭凶。世克应爻，或者官伤应位，必定如意；世应相生相合、财爻逢生旺之乡，不必官府来扳，自然来津贴；官爻值应、或者应临月破的，官府一定佥派帮户，违背选定的就遭责罚。
  If the Other is hurt by the Self and controlled by Pressure, one can surely drag the service onto the other and gain complete success; if the lines generate and combine and Wealth is thriving, they will surely come with a subsidy for the expenses and both sides will be in accord; if the Other rests on the star of office or on Month-break, that man is reproved and meets ill. If the Self controls the Other line, or the star of office hurts the Other's place, it will surely be as one wishes; if the Self and the Other generate and combine with each other and Wealth meets a place of generation and thriving, there is no need for the officials to drag anyone in, and a subsidy comes of itself; if the Pressure line is on the Other, or the Other rests on Month-break, the officials will surely assign helper households, and whoever goes against the selection is reproved.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.114** 求脱役（解除差役），以子孙为主，以世爻为凭据。官爻克世爻、又临在世爻上，用尽机谋也脱不成；卦得六冲，应该能脱役；子孙在世爻上，一定除名。凡是求脱役，专看官爻：官如果克世、临世，想退掉这份役，难以推开；如果得到六冲，或者子孙持世，或者子孙发动，都主役免名消。
  In seeking release from service, take Output as the chief and the Self image as the ground. If the Pressure line controls the Self and also rests on the Self, however much scheming one uses one cannot get free; if the hexagram is a six-clash, one should be freed from the service; if Output is on the Self, one will surely be struck from the rolls. In every seeking of release from service, look especially at the Pressure line: if it controls the Self or rests on the Self, it is hard to push off the service one wants to leave; if one gets a six-clash, or Output holds the Self, or Output moves, in every case the service goes and the name is cleared.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.115** 世爻落空，自己就能退役，没有忧虑；应爻落陷，别人就不能上籍当丁；世应都空而官又发动，旁人顶役，两边都没有刑害。世爻值空亡，自然解散；应爻值空亡，他难以顶替；世、应都空、官又发动的，自己能解脱；他担当不了，一定有旁人代役，自己就免了忧虑。
  If the Self is Void one retires of oneself and has no worry; if the Other is sunk, the other man cannot be entered on the rolls as an able-bodied adult; if the Self and the Other are both Void and the star of office moves, another man takes over the service and neither side is punished. If the Self falls on Void, one is naturally released; if the Other falls on Void, he can hardly take one's place; if the Self and the Other are both Void and the star of office moves, one can free oneself; if he cannot bear it, another man will stand in for the service, and one is free of the worry.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.116** 官鬼爻立在应爻上、又伤克对方，他必定承担下来，永不更改；官鬼如果兴旺、财爻接连发动，还须自己进公堂。官爻临在应爻上又克应爻，都主他人代役；官爻临旺相发动、又不伤他，何况遇到财爻发动来生官爻，一定难脱；官爻即使发动，如果来生助世爻，反而有益于我，必定得脱。
  If the Pressure line stands on the Other and also hurts him, he will surely take it on and it will never change; if Pressure flourishes and Wealth moves one after another, one must still go oneself into the public court. If the Pressure line rests on the Other and controls it, in every case another man takes over the service; if the star of office is thriving and moving and does not hurt him, and still more if Wealth moves to generate the star of office, release is surely hard; even if the Pressure line moves, if it comes to generate and aid the Self line, it is on the contrary of benefit to me and I will surely get free.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.134** 要仗托别人出力，以应爻为主，以应爻不遇绝、空、冲、破为好。被派解送钱粮到京的差使，常有人包办完纳粮税；在县里当差，常有人替自己出公庭；或者央求邻里替自己谋划，或者请亲朋替自己做事，承受他们的庇佑，事情靠他们办成。前面列出的这些人的事情，都附在卦的应象上。粮从产业出，好像有差徭的重担；产业卖了、粮税还在，更多力役的征调；或者山长水远路途遥远，或者人胆小、丁口单薄，不能没有亲属的托付，必须央求旧交的情面；事情有万般祸患，要靠卦来一决；一切仗托别人的事，都看应爻。
  In relying on other people's strength, take the Other line as the chief and it is best if it meets no extinction, Void, clash or break. When one is assigned a commission to convey grain and money to the capital, there is often someone who contracts to complete the grain and taxes; when one is on duty in the county, there is often someone who goes to the public court in one's place; or one asks the neighbours to scheme on one's behalf, or begs relatives and friends to act in one's place, receiving their protection, so that the matter is accomplished through them. The affairs of the people listed above are all attached to the Other image of the hexagram. The grain comes out of the property and there seems to be the weight of service and corvée; if the property has gone but the grain tax remains, there are still more levies of labour; or the mountains are long and the waters far, a distant journey, or the man is timid and the adults of the household few and weak; one cannot but entrust the matter to relatives and must entreat old friends for their kindness; the matter has ten thousand troubles and the hexagram must settle it; in all kinds of relying on others, look at the Other line.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.135** 应爻动、应爻被冲，这个被托的人有更改、有变化；应爻空、应爻破，这个人无力无能；最嫌世爻受应爻伤，更忌内卦被外卦克。应爻发动、或者逢冲而动，仗他办事终究要改变；应爻落旬空、或临月破，托人办事决不能有始有终；如果应克世，或者外卦伤内卦，都主他心地不善，不可误用。
  If the Other moves or is clashed, the man entrusted has changes and alterations; if the Other is Void or broken, the man has no strength or ability; what is most disliked is the Self being hurt by the Other, and still more the inner being controlled by the outer. If the Other moves or meets a clash that sets it moving, relying on him will in the end bring a change; if the Other falls on the ten-day Void or meets Month-break, the man entrusted will surely not see it through from start to finish; if the Other controls the Self, or the outer hurts the inner trigram, in every case his heart is not good and one must not employ him by mistake.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.136** 财爻、福神生身，有益处；见官爻克世，就亏损钱财。六冲就心意混乱、没有恒心，哪里能和谐、哪里能成；六合就心平气和、有信用，能有始有终。妻财、子孙发动，生助世爻，终究有益；如果见兄弟、官鬼发动，冲克世象，怎么不受伤？六冲，作事不成，又没有信实；六合，所谋遂意，更有始有终。
  If Wealth and Output generate the Body, it is beneficial; if the star of office controls the Self, it costs wealth. With a six-clash the mind is confused and there is no constancy, so how could it be in harmony and how could it be accomplished; with a six-combine the heart is calm and there is good faith, so it can both begin and end. If Wealth and Output move and generate and aid the Self line, in the end it is beneficial; if Peer and Pressure are seen moving and clashing with or controlling the Self image, how could one not be hurt? With a six-clash, what is undertaken does not succeed and there is no good faith; with a six-combine, what is schemed turns out as one wishes and moreover there is a beginning and an end.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.138** 想顶替别人的户籍和姓名，以世爻为主，以官鬼为凭据：遇到六冲，一定听到叹息声；世爻空、世爻破，都办不成；官爻绝、官爻亡，全都不成。凡是改换姓名、顶替别人户籍的，主不成。
  In wanting to take over another's register and name, take the Self line as the chief and Pressure as the ground: with a six-clash one will surely hear sighs; if the Self is Void or broken, in neither case is it accomplished; if the star of office is extinct or lost, it all fails. Changing one's name and taking over another's household register is judged not to succeed.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.139** 顶补官名（官职），适宜官鬼旺相；顶替别人的店铺产业，要财爻兴旺。兄爻临朱雀发动，伤克身爻、世爻，还怕旁人出来争斗；世爻不空不冲、官不落陷，事情当可圆成，永不更改。在官府顶补姓名，尤其要官爻旺相，又不可官鬼克世爻、身爻；顶替别人的手艺行业，还要财象兴隆；不论公家还是私人的更改，兄爻独发就难成；即使乱动，如果兄爻带朱雀发动来克世，或者应爻带朱雀来克世，必有旁人出头争夺；世爻兴旺、官爻都不空、不冲、不破、不绝，事事都成。
  To take over an official name, it is fitting for Pressure to be thriving; to take over another's shop and business, Wealth must flourish. If Peer on the Vermilion Bird moves and hurts the Body or the Self, one still fears bystanders rising to contend; if the Self is neither Void nor clashed and the star of office is not sunk, the matter will be fully accomplished and never changed. In taking over a name in an official post, it is especially necessary for the Pressure line to be thriving, and Pressure must not control the Self or the Body; in taking over another's craft or trade, one still wants the Wealth image to flourish; whether the change is public or private, if the Peer line moves alone it is hard to accomplish; even if lines move in confusion, if Peer carrying the Vermilion Bird moves to control the Self, or the Other carrying the Vermilion Bird comes to control the Self, there will surely be bystanders who come forward to contend; if the Self is thriving and the Pressure line is not Void, clashed, broken or extinct, everything succeeds.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.61** 凡是公开揭发、参劾上疏的事，就像词讼一样：先占上面的意思是依从还是违背，再看应爻的生克。问什么时候交代（与继任人交接）：以官鬼入墓、入绝，子孙生旺值日（月）为期。问什么时候新官来：不论是读书人、百姓、官员、衙役，都以官鬼生旺为应验。找人代理署事：应空，谁来代他？或者从官（爵位）看，或者从人（那个人）看，用爻应当分清。
  A public denunciation or impeaching memorial is like a lawsuit: first divine whether the higher authority will comply or go against it, then examine the generating and controlling of the Other line. When will the handover be: the date is set by Pressure entering the tomb or extinction and Output being born or on duty. When will the new official come: whatever sort of person, scholar, commoner, official or runner, it is confirmed by Pressure being generated and thriving. In seeking someone to act in the post: if the Other is Void, who will stand in for him? Either look from the office (its rank) or from the man, and the line to use should be made clear.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.65** 任用年长的用父爻，任用年幼的用子爻，任用读书人用福爻，任用臣属用应爻，依他所担的任来取用爻。生世的是忠良，克世的是奸恶；空就无能，或者不为我出力；散就无心，或者不听我用；破就不一心向着我，绝就不肯先出力，纵然生世也得不到他的力。任用文人用父爻，任用商人用财爻，任用养牲畜的用子孙，任用交游往来的用兄弟，而应象都不可有病。因为应是根本，技艺是枝叶，应空、应破，即使父爻旺，也做不成文事。
  For employing an elder, use the father line; for a youth, the child line; for a scholar, the Output line; for a subordinate, the Other line: the line is taken according to the charge he is given. One that generates the Self is loyal and good, one that controls the Self is treacherous and wicked; Void means incapable, or not exerting himself for me; scattered means no heart, or not following my use; broken means not wholly turned toward me; extinct means not willing to labour first, and even if it generates the Self one does not get his strength. For a literary man use the Resource line, for a merchant the Wealth line, for a herdsman the Output line, and for a man of social contacts the Peer line, and in all of these the Other image must not be sick. For the Other is the root and the skill is the branches and leaves: if the Other is Void or broken, even with a thriving Resource line he cannot do literary work.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.69** 凡是占收雇，三冲就不长久。收仆是为了备驱使差遣的人，以财为用神：所以财神空，人虽然跟着我，心却不向着我；财神破、散，轻则疾病、重则丧亡；财神绝、衰，用他不上；财神旺而生合世身的，这是手足来奉养腹心；财神旺而克冲世的，这是干练的仆人欺凌软弱的主人。所以未收时占好坏，只忌三冲、财有病，即使克（世）也不嫌；已经收了占去留，也忌三冲、财动，克世就该让他走。
  In all divinations about taking on and hiring, the three clashes do not last. Taking on a servant is to have someone to be driven and sent about, and Wealth is the Subject: so with the Wealth spirit Void, the man follows in person but his heart is not toward me; with the Wealth spirit broken or scattered, there is illness at the lightest and death at the worst; with the Wealth spirit extinct or weak, one cannot use him; with the Wealth spirit thriving and generating or combining with the Self, this is hands and feet serving heart and belly; with the Wealth spirit thriving and controlling or clashing with the Self, this is a capable servant bullying a weak master. So when divining before taking him on whether he is good or bad, one fears only the three clashes and a sick Wealth, and even controlling is not objected to; when divining after taking him on whether he will go or stay, one also fears the three clashes and Wealth moving, and when it controls the Self he should go.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.72** 雇请人工（雇工），就以应为用神：应破、绝，就不能善终；应空、散，就不能开好头；三冲，没有定准；克世，没有良善。雇请养蚕织布的妇女，妇女按财取：财不可坏；雇请（别的）按应取：应不可有病。应来克世，恐怕妨害我的事；合处逢冲、变出冲，不可久留；八纯卦、六冲卦，连暂时用也不可。
  In hiring labour, the Other is the Subject: the Other broken or extinct, and it cannot end well; the Other Void or scattered, and it cannot get a good start; the three clashes, and there is nothing settled; controlling the Self, and there is no goodness. In hiring a woman who raises silkworms and weaves, a woman is taken by Wealth, and Wealth must not be damaged; hiring is otherwise by the Other, and the Other must not be sick. If the Other comes to control the Self, I fear it harms my affairs; a combination meeting a clash, or a changed clash, means he must not be kept long; a pure hexagram of eight or a six-clash, he must not be used even briefly.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.74** 立户头成年丁、置办产业入册，说到底只是怕征收赋税的牵累，所以官鬼是徭役：不可动、不可克（动就烦，克就累）；不可随墓、助伤（随墓助伤就有飞来的差役、突然的摊派）；只有鬼静、福兴，才能安居乐业。
  Establishing a household and registering adult males, acquiring property and entering it on the rolls are in the end only out of fear of the burden of tax-levying; so Pressure is the corvée, and it must not move or be controlled (moving means vexation, being controlled means burden); it must not follow into the tomb or help the injury (following and helping bring sudden duties and abrupt levies); only with Pressure still and Output rising does one live in peace and enjoy one's work.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.75** 占寄户（把户口寄在别人名下）、投图（投入某个里图），专看应象：应空，对方不容；克世，多有牵累；世空，成不了；三冲，不长久。至于分丁产、收回图甲、清漏粮赋，都以助伤、随墓为恶，鬼动克世为凶。
  In divining about lodging a household or joining a registry one looks only at the Other image: the Other Void, he will not take one in; controlling the Self, there are many burdens; the Self Void, it will not come off; the three clashes, it will not last. As for dividing the males and property, taking back the registry and tithing, and clearing omitted grain and levies, all take helping the injury and following into the tomb as bad, and Pressure moving to control the Self as ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.76** 交纳（缴纳钱粮）容易吗？官克世，就难；散派（摊派）难吗？世克应，就容易；违限（逾期）安稳吗？鬼动、官克，就危险。
  Is paying in easy? With Pressure controlling the Self it is hard. Is apportioning hard? With the Self controlling the Other it is easy. Is missing the deadline safe? With Pressure moving and Pressure controlling, it is dangerous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.77** 役有不同：有我自己求的役，有官府派的役，有有利的役，有不利的役，都是替官府当役。所以喜忌都用官爻：求而有利的，以役为利，就以财旺、官备为吉，以官坏、克世为凶。
  Service differs: there is service one seeks oneself, service the authorities assign, service that pays, and service that does not, all of it service to the authorities. So what is welcome and what is feared are all taken from the Pressure line: for service one seeks and which pays, taking the service as the profit, thriving Wealth and a complete Pressure line are auspicious, and damaged Pressure and Pressure controlling the Self are ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.78** 凡是盐、漕、河、赋这些差使，以及吏胥、吏卒一类的人：官能使他荣，所以官不可空；官能使他辱，所以官不可被克；官能使他得利，所以财不可没有。随官入墓、助鬼伤身，是公门的大忌。
  For duties connected with salt, grain transport, river works and taxes, and for clerks, runners and attendants: the official can bring him honour, so Pressure must not be Void; the official can bring him disgrace, so Pressure must not be controlled; the official can bring him profit, so Wealth must not be absent. Following Pressure into the tomb and helping Pressure to injure the Body are the great taboos of the public offices.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.79** 占谋差使能不能成：本卦六冲、合处逢冲、世空、官失（官爻失去），就是不成。顶替差役能不能成：世空、应空、本卦六冲、合处逢冲、官失，就是合不到一起、办不成。
  In divining whether a bid for a duty will come off: the original hexagram clashed, a combination clashed, the Self Void, or Pressure lost, mean it does not come off. Whether taking another's place in a duty will come off: the Self Void, the Other Void, the original hexagram clashed, a combination clashed, or Pressure lost, mean the two do not tally and it cannot be done.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.80** 被摊派而不利的，以役为害。所以问役的轻重：官克世，役就加重；官持日辰、月建而旺相，役必多、徭必苛；休囚就少，空、散就免。破要分动静：动就有役，静就无役；随墓助伤，也应当大凶，但遇到空、破，反而得免。
  When one is assigned a duty to one's disadvantage, the duty is a harm. So in asking whether the duty is light or heavy: Pressure controlling the Self, the duty is increased; Pressure holding the Day or Month and thriving, the duty is sure to be many and the corvée harsh; resting or imprisoned, it is little; Void or scattered, it is escaped. Broken is divided into moving and still: moving, there is duty; still, there is none; following into the tomb and helping the injury should also be greatly ominous, but meeting Void or broken one escapes instead.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.81** 脱役、除名：官克（世），就不被宽宥；官空、破、散，又嫌没有主事的人放我；官临旺相，又忌它制住我的范围。所以凡是官，不可被克，不可太旺（极），不可失去（没）。「极」是极旺，「没」是失没。补遗说：官鬼克身，难脱役；子孙持世，易除名。
  For being freed from duty and struck off the rolls: Pressure controlling the Self, one is not pardoned; Pressure Void, broken or scattered, one fears that there is no one in charge to release me; Pressure thriving, one fears it holds my bounds in check. So Pressure must not be controlled, must not be extreme, and must not be lost. 'Extreme' is extremely thriving, and 'lost' is vanished. The Buyi says: when Pressure controls the Body, it is hard to be freed from duty; when Output holds the Self, it is easy to be struck off.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.82** 如果求人帮助服役，须应生世，如应空、破、散的就得不到帮助；如果找人替我卸役，须世克应，如应空、官克的就无门；如果为役告官而求得理，官克世不适宜，如官空、破、散的就无益。因为公事看官，私事看应。口诀说：私下求人要应生世，公断要戒官克世；应空谁来怜惜，应克谁来出力。
  If one asks someone to help with a duty, the Other must generate the Self, and if the Other is Void, broken or scattered there is no help; if one looks for someone to unload a duty for me, the Self must control the Other, and if the Other is Void or Pressure controls, there is no door; if one sues the official over the duty and wants to be in the right, Pressure controlling the Self is unsuitable, and if Pressure is Void, broken or scattered it is no use. For public matters are seen by Pressure and private matters by the Other. The verse says: in a private plea, one needs the Other to generate the Self; in a public judgment, beware of Pressure controlling it; if the Other is Void, who will pity one; if the Other controls, who will exert himself.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.83** 占躲起来逃避差役吗？鬼旺，差役就来；鬼克世，就来抓我了。占当差防备出事吗？官动、官克，就多牵累；只有卦静而子动，才是这两种占的吉。避役、防患，都喜子、忌官。
  In divining about hiding one's tracks to avoid a duty: with Pressure thriving the duty comes; with Pressure controlling the Self, they come to arrest me. In divining about serving a duty and guarding against trouble: with Pressure moving and Pressure controlling there are many entanglements; only with the hexagram still and Output moving are the two divinations auspicious. Avoiding service and guarding against trouble both welcome Output and fear Pressure.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.110** 如果是大官奉敕命、小官奉上司之命、吏民奉派出差，官爻不可有病，并且不可冲克世位。如果是官吏因河工（治河工程）占利不利，就反过来以官为重：官旺、世实，就被记功、升荐；官伤、世空，以及随墓、助伤，就有罪过、降责。
  If a great official acts on an imperial edict, a lesser official on an order, or clerks and commoners on an assignment, the Pressure line must not be sick, nor clash with or control the Self's place. If an official or clerk asks about gain or loss in connection with river works, then on the contrary Pressure counts for most: with Pressure thriving and the Self solid, he is recorded for merit and recommended for promotion; with Pressure injured and the Self Void, and following into the tomb and helping the injury, there is guilt and demotion.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.111** 如果是民夫服苦役占吉凶，就以官为忌：官旺动克世，劳苦没有尽头；命墓、助伤，忧虑难测。如果担忧触犯妨碍，以及受苦于徭役的，都以鬼为忌象：旺动克世，以及随墓助伤，就凶。
  If a labourer in hard service divines about good and ill, Pressure is the taboo: Pressure thriving, moving and controlling the Self, the toil has no end; the life in the tomb and helping the injury, there is fear of the unforeseen. If one worries about offending and obstruction, and for those who suffer under labour service, Pressure is the taboo image in all cases: thriving, moving and controlling the Self, and following into the tomb and helping the injury, are ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.112** 凡是占差委、雇工，都以应为用：应旺实，就有成；克世，就不良善；六冲，就难成。
  In all divinations about commissioned workmen and hired labour, the Other is the Subject: with the Other thriving and solid it comes off; controlling the Self, it is not good; a six-clash, it is hard to accomplish.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.19** 世爻临官星又遇驿马，会有差遣，烦劳辛苦：官星持世，明动、暗动又加临驿马的，必定有差遣；日月、动爻作财来生扶的，在差遣中能得到利益；卦中兄爻发动，日月、动爻刑克世爻的，辛苦还要赔钱；心里盼着得到差遣的人，不用等驿马加临，只要世爻发动就有差遣了。
  If the Self sits on the star of office and meets the Post Horse, there will be commissions and toil: if the star of office holds the Self and moves openly or in secret upon the Post Horse, there will surely be a commission; if the Day, Month or a moving line acts as Wealth and generates or supports it, one profits from the commission; if a Peer line moves in the hexagram and the Day, Month or a moving line punishes or controls the Self, one toils and loses money as well; for one who hopes in his heart for a commission, there is no need to wait for the Post Horse, for as soon as the Self moves there is a commission.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.27** 谋署理印务、谋差事：财星和官星不能有一个不旺相，子孙和兄弟不能有一个发动摇动；世爻失陷、财落空，不如守住自己；官星落空、世爻被破，暂且让给别人；用钱去谋的，和援例一样推断。衰弱的世爻随官鬼入墓，世爻发动变克、变鬼，子孙或兄弟持世，这时去谋署印、营求差事，是自己投进罗网。
  On seeking to act in a seal-holding post or to scheme for a commission: of the star of Wealth and the star of office neither may fail to be thriving, and of Output and Peer neither may move or sway; if the Self is lost and Wealth is Void, it is better to keep to oneself; if the star of office is Void and the Self is broken, let others have it for now; for one who seeks it with money, judge as for buying office by precedent. If a weak Self follows Pressure into the tomb, or the Self moves and turns into a control or into Pressure, or Output or Peer holds the Self, then seeking to act in a seal-holding post or to scheme for a commission is walking into the net oneself.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.48** 修陵修河等营造公务防患：福神摇动、子孙发动没有忧虑，官鬼克、兄弟冲就有祸患；子孙持世或在卦中发动，事情没有忧虑，有始有终；官鬼克世会受累，兄弟克世、发动会赔钱，官鬼、兄弟持世的也同样推断。
  Guarding against trouble in works such as repairing tombs and rivers: with the lucky spirit swaying and Output moving there is nothing to worry about, and with Pressure controlling and Peer clashing there is trouble; if Output holds the Self or moves in the hexagram, the matter has nothing to worry about and has a beginning and an end; if Pressure controls the Self one is burdened, if Peer controls the Self or moves one loses money, and the same judgment holds when Pressure or Peer holds the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.49** 父母持世，官星发动生合世爻，必定因为办公事而加官。
  If Resource holds the Self and the star of office moves to generate or combine with the Self, one will surely gain office through public business.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.50** 财兴旺、官星旺相，既得利益又得荣名：旺相的官星持世，财发动来生它，不但因公事获利，还将得到名誉荣耀；旺相的财持世，或者世爻发动变出财来生世，也同样断。
  With Wealth flourishing and the star of office thriving, one wins both gain and honour: if a thriving star of office holds the Self and Wealth moves to generate it, one not only gains from public business but will in due time win honour; the same judgment holds when a thriving Wealth line holds the Self, or the Self moves and turns into Wealth to generate the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.52** 占公务防患要分两个阶段：刚开始进入工程而防备祸患的，适合子孙动摇，子孙发动就没有忧虑；如果已经出现了疏漏失误而防备祸患的，必然是在为功名担忧，子孙发动反而伤官星，怎么能说子孙动就没有忧虑呢。觉子补充：刚开始进入工程，兄弟发动要担心赔钱；已经出现疏漏，兄弟兴旺要防备罚俸。
  In divining public business and guarding against trouble one must tell two stages apart: for one who has just begun a works project and guards against trouble, Output swaying is good, for when Output moves there is nothing to worry about; but for one who has already seen a lapse and guards against trouble, he is surely anxious for his rank, and Output moving then injures the star of office, so how could it be said that when Output moves there is nothing to worry about? Juezi adds: at the start of a works project, when Peer moves one fears losing money; once a lapse has appeared, when Peer flourishes guard against a fine of salary.

## 军旅(Soldiers, campaigns and military posts)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/军旅.md`

- **LY.CAREER.CA.24** 出兵交战，子孙要强；官鬼旺，对方赢、我必败亡；财是粮草，不要被兄弟克；父母是旌旗，忌讳摇动飘扬。
  In sending out troops to battle, Output should be strong; if Pressure is strong the other side wins and we are surely lost; Wealth is the fodder and grain and should not be controlled by Peer; Resource is the banner and standard and should not be shaken and flying.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.25** 水爻带着官鬼来克我，要防备劫寨和攻营；还要看有气没气，才能断兵机的输赢。决胜负，以世、应、日辰、官星来论：世是我方将领，应是对方军队，日辰是我方士卒，官鬼是对方兵；我克他，我胜；他克我，我负。财动克父，必定斩将夺旗；兄弟交重发动，必定夺粮劫草。
  A Water line carrying Pressure and coming to control us: guard against the raiding of the camp and the assault on the encampment; then see whether there is qi or none, and only then judge the winning or losing of the military affair. To decide victory, reckon by the Self, the Other, the Day and the star of office: the Self is our general, the Other is their army, the Day is our soldiers, Pressure is their troops; if we control them, we win; if they control us, we lose. If Wealth rises and controls Resource, the general is surely cut down and the banner seized; if Peer moves, grain is surely seized and fodder raided.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.06** 乾位上有动爻，出在西北；训练出行适宜前往，可以兴起功绩。遇到卦中世、应相生，有奉命训练兵马的差事，就适宜前往有利的方位，必然立功。
  With moving lines in the Qian place it goes out to the northwest; for a training mission it is fitting to go, and merit will arise. When Self and Other generate each other in the hexagram and there is an order to drill troops and horses, it is fitting to go toward the favourable direction, and merit will surely be won.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.12** 出师征伐，以世爻、应爻为主，福德（子孙）为凭据。杀敌擒俘全凭勇气谋略，观察时机、应付变化各用机智谋划，传自黄石公、姜太公的《三略》《六韬》，诸葛孔明推演的八门九遁；虽然知道奇门的胜负，还要凭易卦的吉凶来断。
  In sending out troops and campaigning, take the Self and the Other as the chief and Fortune Virtue (Output) as the ground. Killing the enemy and taking prisoners rest wholly on courage and strategy, and watching the moment and mastering change each use wit and planning; the Three Strategies and the Six Secret Teachings handed down from Huang Shigong and Jiang Taigong and the Eight Gates and Nine Escapes spread out by Zhuge Kongming are known, but though one may know the victories and defeats of Qimen, one still relies on the good and ill of the hexagram.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.13** 为什么奇门要和易卦并用：在帷幄中运筹、在千里外决胜，功成名就就在史册上留给后世；如果有勇无谋、见识浅短，一着失误，小则陷身失职，大则国破家亡，祸患就从这里开始。所以说，虽然知道奇门的胜负，还要评断易卦的吉凶。奇是三奇乙、丙、丁；门是八门，各配八卦方位，但并非固定，有超神、接气的起例，每时改换，再与九星配合来定吉凶；九星是天心、天柱属金，天冲、天辅属木，天蓬属水，天英属火，天芮、天禽、天任属土。决断交战胜负，奇门、易卦两样都要精通。只用奇门，如果彼此都知道、双方都从吉门进兵，难道能一起获胜吗？何况奇门以接气、超神为主，前后难评，差之毫厘就谬以千里。此外还有太乙统宗是天之数，奇门遁甲是地之数，大六壬是神人之数，这几种数总归于八卦，各种变化不离五行；军中虽用这些数，哪比得上易卦把衰旺动静生克看得清清楚楚，不但推断军务的一个方面，而且能指点万民的趋避。易卦上应天时、下通人事，彰明往事、察知未来，开物成务、验证休咎存亡的机微，衡量判断的人必须精细详审，才不会有差错。
  Why Qimen is used together with the hexagram. Planning within the tent and deciding victory a thousand miles off, one who succeeds and wins fame leaves a record in the annals for later ages; but with courage and no wit, and shallow knowledge, a single slip means at the least personal ruin and loss of post, and at the most the fall of the state and ruin of the house, and calamity begins from there. So it is said: though one may know the victories and defeats of Qimen, one still judges the good and ill of the hexagram. The Wonders are the three Wonders Yi, Bing and Ding; the gates are the Eight Gates, each matched with a trigram direction, but not fixed, for there are the rules of leaping the spirit and joining the qi, and they change with every hour, and are then combined with the nine stars to fix good and ill; the nine stars are Tianxin and Tianzhu in Metal, Tianchong and Tianfu in Wood, Tianpeng in Water, Tianying in Fire, and Tianrui, Tianqin and Tianren in Earth. To decide the victory or defeat of a battle one must be expert in both Qimen and the hexagram. If one uses Qimen alone and both sides know it and both advance by auspicious gates, how can both win? Besides, Qimen depends on joining the qi and leaping the spirit, which is hard to judge forwards and backwards, and a hair's breadth of error becomes a thousand miles. Besides these there are the Taiyi Tongzong, the number of Heaven, Qimen Dunjia, the number of Earth, and the Da Liu Ren, the number of spirits and men; these numbers all return to the eight trigrams, and all changes do not leave the five elements; though the army uses these numbers, how do they compare with the hexagram, which sees plainly strength and weakness, motion and stillness, generation and control, and can not only work out one side of military affairs but also point the way for the myriad people to seek or avoid. The hexagram answers above to the seasons of Heaven and below reaches human affairs, shows the past and discerns the future, opens things and completes affairs, and tests the springs of good and ill and survival and loss; one who weighs and judges must be fine and thorough, and only then will there be no error.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.14** 官鬼和凶煞当权，纵然走开、休、生三个吉门也不吉；福神（子孙）当道，即使走伤、死、杜三个凶门也没有灾殃。《烟波钓叟歌》说：八门遇到开、休、生，诸事遇到都称心。凡是占出兵的卦，最嫌官鬼重叠发动，官鬼如果发动，纵然往吉门去，一定难以取胜；如果走子孙旺相发动或持世爻的方位，即使踏上凶门，也没有妨害。
  If Pressure and baleful spirits hold power, even going by the three auspicious gates, Open, Rest and Life, is not auspicious; if the fortune spirit, Output, holds the road, even going by the three ill gates, Harm, Death and Block, brings no disaster. The Yanbo Diaosou Ge says: if the Eight Gates meet Open, Rest and Life, all matters met are to one's mind. In a hexagram for sending out troops, what is most disliked is Pressure moving in double; if Pressure moves, even going by an auspicious gate one surely finds it hard to win; if one goes by the quarter where Output is thriving and moving or holds the Self, even stepping on an ill gate does no harm.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.15** 八门要和官鬼、子孙的位置合着看：官鬼值在坤宫，嫌死门；子孙临在兑卦，只利惊门。伤门说是凶，但震宫发动带将星，出征反而得胜；休门说是吉，但坎宫兴起大杀，作战反而输。艮宫发动青龙，往生门的方向大利；乾宫摇动白虎，走开门的地方不适宜；离宫福神重叠发动，走景门能得喜悦；巽宫发动，走杜门去作战相差很远。将军和兵卒要知道忌讳进哪个门、利于走哪个方向，须推算官鬼值在哪一宫、官星临在哪个卦象，不要进那一门；又看子孙在哪一卦、福神在哪一爻，宜从那个门走。比如坤宫官鬼发动，最忌死门；兑卦子孙摇动，惊门可以进；伤门是祸害，但震宫有将星、福德，就是好的。
  The Eight Gates are read together with the positions of Pressure and Output. Pressure on the Kun palace dislikes the Death gate; Output on the Dui trigram favours only the Fright gate. The Harm gate is said to be ill, yet if Zhen moves with the General star, a campaign wins instead; the Rest gate is said to be auspicious, yet if Kan raises a great baleful spirit, the battle is lost instead. If the Azure Dragon moves in Gen, the direction of the Life gate is greatly favourable; if the White Tiger stirs in Qian, the place of the Open gate is unsuitable; if the fortune spirit doubles in Li, going by the Scene gate brings joy; if Xun moves, going to battle by the Block gate goes much against one. Commanders and soldiers who want to know which gate to avoid and which direction to take must work out in which palace Pressure sits and on which image the star of office lies, and not enter that gate; again, see in which trigram Output lies and on which line the fortune spirit is, and take that gate. For example, if Pressure moves in the Kun palace, the Death gate is most to be avoided; if Output stirs in Dui, the Fright gate may be entered; the Harm gate is a calamity, but if there is the General star and Fortune Virtue (Output) in the Zhen palace it is good.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.16** 将星的起例：正月在午，二月在卯，三月在子，四月在酉，五月又到午，只有这四个位置，周而复始；休门于是吉祥。坎卦有大煞，官鬼爻难得福。大煞的起例说：正月在犬（戌），二月在蛇（巳），三月在马（午），四月在未，五月在寅，六月在卯，七月在辰，八月在亥，九月忌鼠（子），十月在牛（丑），十一月在申方，「十一」金鸡（酉）为大煞；国家遇到这个，怎么能安邦？
  The setting out of the General star: in the first month at Wu, the second at Mao, the third at Zi, the fourth at You, the fifth again at Wu; only these four places, going round and round; the Rest gate then is auspicious. Kan has the great baleful spirit, and the Pressure line there finds it hard to gain fortune. The setting out of the great baleful spirit says: the first month the dog (Xu), the second the snake (Si), the third month the horse (Wu), the fourth Wei, the fifth Yin, the sixth Mao, the seventh Chen, the eighth Hai, the ninth month beware the rat (Zi), the tenth the ox (Chou), the eleventh in the quarter of Shen, and 'the eleventh' the golden cock (You) is the great baleful spirit; a state meeting this, how can it keep the peace?
- **LY.CAREER.EL.17** 福神带青龙在艮宫丙位摇动，如果进生门，人获全功；官鬼临白虎在乾宫中发动，纵然进开门也未能取胜。离宫子孙发动，宜走景门；巽宫土爻官鬼兴起，不要往杜门去。
  If the fortune spirit carrying the Azure Dragon stirs at Bing in Gen and one enters the Life gate, one wins complete success; if Pressure on the White Tiger moves in Qian, even entering the Open gate one cannot win. If Output moves in Li, it is fit to take the Scene gate; if an Earth Pressure line rises in Xun, do not go by the Block gate.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.18** 乙、丙、丁三奇，遇到没有不利的；庚、辛、癸三煞，遇到没有不凶的。乙是日奇，丙是月奇，丁是星奇，甲变成三奇自然吉利；如果变成庚、辛、癸之象，都不吉祥。卦配十干，要看规则：天禄诀说「壬甲从乾起，乙癸向坤求，丙向艮方取，丁出兑家游，戊自坎中觅，己用离为头，庚震及辛巽」。诀里震、巽、坎、离、艮、兑各属一个干，只有乾、坤两卦每个属于两个干，又要接合阴阳之道：冬至后阳令之时，乾是内卦甲、外卦壬，坤是内卦乙、外卦癸；夏至后阴升的节令，乾是内壬外甲，坤是内癸外乙。又说用兵：冬至后，卜得乾变遁卦，就是甲变成月奇；乾变履卦，就是甲变成星奇；乾变否卦，就是甲变成日奇；这样的卦爻得到大胜。又说夏至后，卜得否变归妹，甲被庚所伤；乾变巽卦，甲遭辛所克；凡是得到这类卦爻，人宜出战（原文如此）。
  The three Wonders Yi, Bing and Ding are never unfavourable when met; the three baleful stems Geng, Xin and Gui are never other than ominous when seen. Yi is the Sun Wonder, Bing the Moon Wonder, Ding the Star Wonder, and when Jia changes into the three Wonders it is naturally auspicious; if it changes into the images of Geng, Xin and Gui, none is auspicious. How hexagrams match the ten stems must be seen from the rule: the Tianlu rhyme says 'Ren and Jia start from Qian, Yi and Gui seek Kun, Bing is taken from Gen, Ding goes out to Dui, Wu is found in Kan, Ji takes Li as head, Geng is Zhen and Xin is Xun'. In the rhyme Zhen, Xun, Kan, Li, Gen and Dui each belong to one stem, and only Qian and Kun each belong to two, and the way of yin and yang must also be joined: after the Winter Solstice, in the season when yang commands, Qian has Jia inside and Ren outside and Kun Yi inside and Gui outside; after the Summer Solstice, in the season when yin rises, Qian has Ren inside and Jia outside and Kun Gui inside and Yi outside. Again for troops: after the Winter Solstice, if one casts Qian changing into Dun, Jia has changed into the Moon Wonder; Qian changing into Lu, Jia has changed into the Star Wonder; Qian changing into Pi, Jia has changed into the Sun Wonder; such lines bring great victory. Again after the Summer Solstice, if one casts Pi changing into Guimei, Jia is injured by Geng; Qian changing into Xun, Jia is controlled by Xin; whoever gets such lines, men are fit to go out and fight (as the text reads).
- **LY.CAREER.EL.19** 犯了伏吟、反吟的，急须回避；大煞、劫煞发动的时候，切莫交战。占交战最忌伏吟、反吟。伏吟有两种：卦犯伏吟（如乾见乾、坎见坎一类的八纯卦），爻犯伏吟（如寅变寅、辰变辰一类，随变夬、需变屯就是）。反吟也有两种：卦犯反吟（下巽上乾的姤卦一类，或者乾变巽、巽变乾），爻犯反吟。用兵的时候又忌大煞、劫煞重叠。劫煞的起例：正月逢亥，二月申，三月随蛇（巳），四月寅，五月循环又到亥，周而复始。伏吟、大煞、劫煞，犯一样就不吉祥，都不犯才好。
  Those who have hit Hidden Chant or Reversed Chant must withdraw urgently; when the great baleful spirit and Robbery move, on no account join battle. In divining for battle, Hidden Chant and Reversed Chant are most to be avoided. Hidden Chant is of two kinds: the hexagram hitting Hidden Chant (the eight pure hexagrams, such as Qian meeting Qian or Kan meeting Kan) and a line hitting Hidden Chant (such as Yin changing into Yin or Chen into Chen, as Sui changing into Guai or Xu into Zhun). Reversed Chant is also of two kinds: the hexagram hitting Reversed Chant (Gou with Xun below and Qian above, and the like, or Qian changing into Xun and Xun into Qian) and a line hitting Reversed Chant. In moving troops one also fears the great baleful spirit and Robbery doubled. The setting out of Robbery: in the first month at Hai, the second Shen, the third following the snake (Si), the fourth Yin, the fifth going round again to Hai, going round and round. Hidden Chant, the great baleful spirit or Robbery, if any one is hit it is inauspicious; to hit none is best.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.20** 易卦里最忌六爻里兴起两个官鬼，奇门里最喜三奇游行在六仪上。军中的卦，最嫌官鬼爻兴起，如果遇到两个官都发动，祸不可当；纵然只有一个官鬼发动变出官鬼爻，也有祸害。奇门的办法，却喜欢三奇，如果在六仪之上，好处加倍。六仪是甲子、甲辰、甲申、甲午（原文只列这四个）；又（门）开甲午、甲戌，利于遇见星奇；甲子、甲申，喜欢遇见日奇；甲寅、甲辰，遇月奇更吉。虽说是三奇游六仪，也就是说三奇得使者。经书说：三奇得使确实可取，六甲遇到它不是小补。譬如夏至后，乾变泰、遁变艮就是。这类卦中，丙得到子孙临日、月，凡是出兵布阵必有吉祥。
  In the hexagram the great taboo is two Pressure lines rising among the six lines; in Qimen what is best liked is the three Wonders roaming over the six Instruments. In a hexagram for the army, what is most disliked is a Pressure line rising; if both Pressure lines move the calamity cannot be withstood; even a single Pressure moving and changing into a Pressure line brings harm. The method of Qimen likes the three Wonders, and if they are over the six Instruments the benefit is doubled. The six Instruments are Jiazi, Jiachen, Jiashen and Jiawu (the text lists only these four); again, with Jiawu and Jiaxu open, it favours meeting the Star Wonder; with Jiazi and Jiashen, the Sun Wonder is pleasing to meet; with Jiayin and Jiachen, the Moon Wonder is better still. Though called the three Wonders roaming over the six Instruments, it also means the three Wonders getting the envoy. The classic says: the three Wonders getting the envoy are truly worth taking, and for the six Jia to meet them is no small help. For example, after the Summer Solstice, Qian changing into Tai or Dun changing into Gen. In such hexagrams, if Bing has Output on the Day or Month, any sending out of troops or drawing up of lines is sure to be auspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.21** 卦如果兴隆，七纵七擒都能如意；门如果旺相，百战百胜都能称心。旺衰能辨出盈亏，动静能知道兴息。八卦的旺衰不取乾兑金、震巽木、坎水、离火、坤艮土（那样的五行），而是由八个节气生出八卦的状态，各按它的时节：立春到春分，艮旺、震相；春分到立夏，震旺、巽相；立夏到夏至，巽旺、离相；夏至到立秋，离旺、坤相；立秋到秋分，坤旺、兑相；秋分到立冬，兑旺、乾相；立冬到冬至，乾旺、坎相；冬至到立春，坎旺、艮相。
  If the hexagram flourishes, seven releases and seven captures go as wished; if the gate is thriving, a hundred battles and a hundred victories are all to one's mind. Strength and weakness can tell waxing from waning, and motion and stillness can tell rising from ceasing. The strength of the eight trigrams is not taken from the elements Qian and Dui Metal, Zhen and Xun Wood, Kan Water, Li Fire, Kun and Gen Earth, but from the eight solar terms which produce the states of the eight trigrams, each by its season: from the Start of Spring to the Spring Equinox, Gen thrives and Zhen assists; from the Spring Equinox to the Start of Summer, Zhen thrives and Xun assists; from the Start of Summer to the Summer Solstice, Xun thrives and Li assists; from the Summer Solstice to the Start of Autumn, Li thrives and Kun assists; from the Start of Autumn to the Autumn Equinox, Kun thrives and Dui assists; from the Autumn Equinox to the Start of Winter, Dui thrives and Qian assists; from the Start of Winter to the Winter Solstice, Qian thrives and Kan assists; from the Winter Solstice to the Start of Spring, Kan thrives and Gen assists.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.22** 占出战，以内卦为我，外卦为他。如果得到内卦旺相、外卦衰弱，又遇吉神发动，东征西讨，威镇华夏和夷狄。奇门也有旺衰，也都按八个节气：立春后，生门太旺；春分后，伤门当道，只嫌大象（卦象）少加，反而取木能生火，用景门；立夏后，杜门虽旺也不吉祥，所以取火相的景门，用它得胜；夏至后，正值景门大吉；立秋后，虽然现在死门当令，不宜前进，应当取金相的惊门；秋分后，惊门当令，进兵没有不亨通的，水相的开门也吉；立冬后，开门正旺，休门作相门，两个门都好；冬至后，休门旺、生门相，两个门都能导向吉祥。
  In divining for going out to fight, take the inner trigram as me and the outer as them. If the inner is thriving and the outer weak, and an auspicious spirit also moves, campaigning east and west one overawes the Chinese and the barbarians. Qimen too has its strength and weakness, also by the eight solar terms: after the Start of Spring the Life gate is very strong; after the Spring Equinox the Harm gate holds the road, but as the great image adds little, one instead takes the fact that Wood can generate Fire and uses the Scene gate; after the Start of Summer the Block gate, though strong, is not auspicious, so one takes the Scene gate that Fire assists and wins by it; after the Summer Solstice the Scene gate is exactly in season and greatly auspicious; after the Start of Autumn, though the Death gate is now in season, it is not fit to advance, and one should take the Fright gate that Metal assists; after the Autumn Equinox the Fright gate commands and none who advance fail to prosper, and the Open gate that Water assists is also auspicious; after the Start of Winter the Open gate is exactly thriving, and the Rest gate acts as the assisting gate, both gates being good; after the Winter Solstice the Rest gate thrives and the Life gate assists, and both gates can lead to good fortune.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.23** 六爻安静，宜守不宜攻；内外两卦都发动，利于出征、不利于停留。卦爻安静、又没有暗动的，应爻又不克世爻，只宜坚壁不出；「二卦」就是内外卦，如果内卦动、外卦摇，要赶快出战，停留下来就增长了对方的智谋。
  If the six lines are still, it is fit to hold and not to attack; if both trigrams, inner and outer, move in double, it favours going out and does not favour staying.
