If the lines are still and nothing moves secretly and the Other does not control the Self, it is only fit to hold the walls and not go out; the 'two trigrams' are the inner and outer, and if the inner moves and the outer shakes, one should go out to fight quickly, since staying only increases the enemy's wit.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.24** 内卦是本城、本寨，外卦是对方的营。内卦和本宫都是本国，也是我的营寨；他宫和外卦都是他国，也是对方的营寨。内卦兴旺，我的营寨就坚固；外卦衰弱，对方的寨子就萧条。
  The inner trigram is my own city and camp, and the outer is their camp. The inner trigram and the home palace are both my own state and also my camp; another palace and the outer trigram are both another state and also their camp. If the inner trigram flourishes my camp is firm; if the outer trigram is weak their camp is desolate.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.25** 世爻是主帅的谋略，应爻是贼首的计策。交兵对敌必须分清彼我，才能决定输赢，所以以世爻为主将，应爻为贼首。世旺，主将忠心有力、勇敢，如果旺相又发动，就耀武扬威、勤于厮杀；世衰弱，就见闻低下、智谋浅陋；世衰而动，谋划孤立、势力单薄，却勉强行事。应旺相，对方必定强横而善于谋略；应休囚，对方就怯懦无能。所以经书说：一卦中间的主宰，无非凭世、应。
  The Self is the commander's strategy and the Other is the bandit chief's scheme. In engaging and facing the enemy one must tell them from us before deciding victory or defeat, so the Self is the commander and the Other is the bandit chief. If the Self is thriving, the commander is loyal, strong and brave, and if thriving and moving he parades his prowess and is diligent in slaughter; if the Self is weak, his knowledge is low and his wit shallow; if the Self is weak and moves, his plans are lonely and his strength thin, yet he forces himself to act. If the Other is thriving, they are surely violent and good at strategy; if the Other is resting or trapped, they are cowardly and incapable. So the classic says: what rules a hexagram is nothing other than the Self and the Other.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.26** 安营立寨，要推八卦的显与幽；监阵交锋，要阐明各爻的生克。行兵出战，先要选地安营，易卦中自有九宫八卦：乾宫利于西北之地，巽卦利于东南的方位，其余照此推。各爻，是一卦上下的六爻，却不知道还有变出的六爻、有伏出的六爻、又有互卦起作用的爻；六爻之内，有彼生我、我生彼，有我克彼、彼克我，必须详察彼此的吉凶，来判定输赢的道理。
  In pitching camp and setting up stockades, work out the manifest and hidden of the eight trigrams; in supervising the formation and engaging, make clear the generation and control of each line. In moving troops to battle, first choose the ground for the camp, and in the hexagram there are of itself the nine palaces and eight trigrams: the Qian palace favours the northwest and Xun the southeast, and the rest follow this. The lines are the six lines above and below in a hexagram, but one does not know that there are also the six lines of the changed hexagram, the six hidden lines and the lines acting in the nuclear hexagram; among the six lines, there is them generating me and me generating them, me controlling them and them controlling me, and one must examine the good and ill of both sides to decide the principle of winning and losing.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.27** 世爻克应爻，师勇兵强，可以剿伐；应爻伤世象，贼多势凶，不要触其锋。世克应爻，我必定取胜；世爻旺相发动而带福神克应、克官鬼的，势如破竹，一战就奏凯；世爻旺相克应但没有吉神发动帮助，反而被日辰、动爻克世的，虽然能取胜却没有获得全功；世衰应旺，对方虽然受克，也只可以抵御；（这样）没有得到奇谋善策，不能大规模专门征伐。世变子孙、应变官鬼的，宜用借路灭敌的计策，又不可再战邀功；子孙伏在世爻下面，而冲应、克应的，必须暗渡陈仓。应克世，必定对方赢，暂且按兵守候；应旺而克世的，来势必定不可轻视；应旺相发动克世，或者同旺相的官鬼、凶煞一起来克的，对方必定合谋夹击，机关难测。应爻上如果加兄弟，（对方）非常诡诈；加玄武，必定偷营；加白虎，猖獗突击得很厉害。
  If the Self controls the Other, the troops are brave and strong and one can campaign; if the Other injures the Self image, the bandits are many and fierce and one must not touch their edge. If the Self controls the Other, I surely win; if the Self is thriving and moving and carries the fortune spirit, Output, and controls the Other and controls Pressure, the force is like splitting bamboo and one wins in a single battle; if the Self is thriving and controls but no auspicious spirit moves to help, and instead the Day or a moving line controls the Self, though one can win one does not gain complete success; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, though they are controlled, one can only fend them off, and without marvellous plans and good strategies one cannot make large expeditions. If the Self turns into Output and the Other into Pressure, it is fit to destroy the enemy by borrowing a road, and one must not fight again to seek credit; if Output lies hidden under the Self and clashes with or controls the Other, one must secretly cross at Chencang. If the Other controls the Self, they surely win, and for the moment one holds the troops and waits; if the Other is thriving and controls the Self, its force is surely not to be made light of; if the Other is thriving and moving and controls the Self, or thriving Pressure and baleful spirits come to control together, they will surely plot together and attack from both sides, and their stratagems are hard to fathom. If a Peer line is added on the Other, they are extremely crafty; with the Dark Tortoise, they will surely raid the camp; with the White Tiger, they are fiercely and violently aggressive.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.28** 煞不克身，即使重叠发动也没有大害；煞如果伤世，虽然在安静中也暂且不要出征。「杀」就是大煞，有劫煞，也有亡神，又有小白虎、大白虎，官鬼也当作杀神。如果各煞或旺相或发动、但不克身爻、世爻，必定没有大害；如果不旺不动，而所克的（爻）有害于身爻、世爻，必定生祸，要谨慎提防；如果各煞或旺相或发动来克身、世，祸会骤然来到，绝非小事。如果煞和日辰一起冲克安静的世爻，叫作暗动，必有阴谋窃劫的祸患；想察觉什么时候来害，须推官鬼和煞生旺的时期。所以经书说「福来而不知，祸来而不觉」，正是说这个。大煞、劫煞的起例已经注在前面；亡神煞的起例说：正月起亥顺行十二位；小白虎，就是庚、辛日起白虎；大白虎，是正月起申顺行十二位。
  If a baleful spirit does not control the Body, even if it moves in double there is no great harm; if a baleful spirit injures the Self, even if it is still, for the time being do not set out. 'Sha' is the great baleful spirit, and there are Robbery, the Lost Spirit, the Lesser White Tiger and the Greater White Tiger, and Pressure too is counted as a baleful spirit. If the various baleful spirits are thriving or moving but do not control the Body and Self, there is surely no great harm; if they are neither thriving nor moving but what they control does harm to the Body and Self, disaster surely arises and one must guard carefully; if the baleful spirits are thriving or moving and come to control the Body and Self, the disaster comes suddenly and is no small matter. If a baleful spirit joins the Day in clashing with and controlling a still Self, it is called secret motion, and there will surely be the danger of plots and plunder; to find out when the harm will come, work out the period when Pressure and the baleful spirit are born or thriving. So the classic says 'fortune comes and one does not know, disaster comes and one does not feel', which says exactly this. The setting out of the great baleful spirit and Robbery is noted earlier; the setting out of the Lost Spirit says: from the first month start at Hai and go through the twelve places; the Lesser White Tiger is the White Tiger starting on Geng and Xin days; the Greater White Tiger starts at Shen in the first month and goes through the twelve places.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.29** 世爻陷落，我军有难；应爻落空，贼寇陷于危险。世是主将，要靠雄强来对垒折冲，哪里适宜落空陷落？如果值旬空，或者临月破的宫位，不可妄自争先，争先就有害，迟缓就有益。单爻属阳，发动成为老阳，主过去的缘由；又说阳主迅速，所以世居阳象，用兵贵在神速，迅速行动大利，迟缓就大害。这是大道理，所以不再琐碎细说。
  If the Self sinks, my army is in trouble; if the Other is Void, the bandits fall into danger. The Self is the commander, and must face the enemy by strength and stoutness; how can it be fit for it to be Void or sunk? If it meets decade Void or sits in a Month-break palace, one must not recklessly press ahead, for pressing ahead brings harm and delay brings benefit. A single line that is yang becomes old yang when it moves and governs the reasons of the past; again it is said that yang governs speed, so when the Self sits on a yang image, in using troops swiftness is prized, and acting swiftly is greatly favourable and delay greatly harmful. This is a great principle, so it is not detailed trivially.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.30** 父爻是旌旗，舒展卷收要凭它的动静；财爻是粮草，盈满亏缺可以由它的兴衰来判断。营中的旌旗以父爻为用爻，旺相又带青龙，就绣金华丽；加朱雀，就色彩鲜明；旺相又发动，就大旗高张、导引飘飘。如果衰、墓而逢白虎，就腐朽破旧；（父爻）空而又绝，旗帜或少或没有，即使有也必然损坏；只要得到安静，世事就都安宁了。谚语说：三军未发，粮草先行，所以卦中的妻财当作车马的粮草：财爻旺相，是兵粮丰盛、马草有余；玄武兴起，要谨防偷窃；兄弟兴起，必定抢夺；如果财爻衰、墓，粮不多、草不肥；卦内没有财，如果有子孙变出财爻，必去邻邦借贷；这一爻如果空、绝，粮草就或者丢失或者没有。
  The Resource line is the banners, and whether they are unfurled or furled depends on its motion or stillness; Wealth is the grain and fodder, and fullness and lack can be decided by its flourishing or decline. The banners in the camp take the Resource line as the Subject line; if it is thriving and carries the Azure Dragon, they are embroidered with gold and splendid; with the Vermilion Bird added, the colours are bright and clear; thriving and moving, great banners are raised high and wave about to lead the way. If it is weak or in tomb and meets the White Tiger, they are rotten and worn; if Void and extinct as well, the banners are few or none, and even if there are some they are sure to be damaged; if it is only still, all will be at peace. The proverb says: before the three armies set out, grain and fodder go ahead, so the Wealth in the hexagram is taken as the grain and fodder of carts and horses: if the Wealth line is thriving, the troops' grain is plentiful and the horses' fodder has surplus; if the Dark Tortoise rises, guard against theft; if Peer rises, there will surely be plunder; if Wealth is weak or in tomb, the grain is not much and the fodder not fat; if the hexagram has no Wealth and Output turns into a Wealth line, one must go to a neighbouring state to borrow; if this line is Void or extinct, the grain and fodder are either lost or lacking.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.31** 子孙当先锋，旺相就强，衰弱就弱；兄弟是埋伏的贼，出现就有，落空就没有。子孙是现有的雄兵，这是当先锋的说法；子孙旺相，必定是智勇两全的将领，如果带吉神而能克应爻，就能冒险冲锋、争先首捷，唾手成功；子孙如果遇到衰、墓，就柔弱怯懦无谋，难以胜任。兄弟是奸佞之神，就是埋伏的贼，如果逢冲动，必定有埋伏的兵，须防暗害；想知道什么时候来犯，必推它生旺的日子、时辰为期；如果绝、没有，就不必忧虑了。
  Output acts as the vanguard, strong if thriving and weak if declining; Peer is the hidden bandit, there if it appears and absent if Void. Output is the troops that are present, which is the argument for acting as the vanguard; if it is thriving, it is surely a general complete in both wit and courage, and if it carries an auspicious spirit and can control the Other, it can risk a charge, be first to press ahead and win the first success as easily as spitting on the palm; if Output meets weak or tomb, it is soft, timid and without plans and cannot fill the post. Peer is a crafty and flattering spirit, the bandit lying in ambush, and if it meets a clash into motion there are surely troops in ambush and one must guard against secret harm; to know when they will attack, work out the day or hour it is born or thriving as the term; if it is extinct or absent, there is no need to worry.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.32** 父母兴旺，机谋炯炯；妻财发动，兵甲纷纷。子孙是兵的用神，父母是子孙的忌神：如果父母旺相而兴起，就机谋迭出、诡计多端，这是伤兵的征兆；如果子孙旺相、父母衰弱，就是将领勇敢、兵卒强壮，可以有所作为。官鬼是对方的用神，妻财是官鬼的助力：对方本来不兴兵，但财爻一发动，官鬼就逞强，所以士卒纵横、兵戈扰乱；只要财爻安静，官鬼就孤立无助，自然太平了。
  If Resource flourishes, stratagems are bright and clear; if Wealth moves, arms and armour are everywhere in motion. Output is the Subject for the troops and Resource is the Adversary of Output: if Resource is thriving and rises, stratagems pour out and tricks are many, which is a sign of the troops being injured; if Output is thriving and Resource weak, the commander is brave and the troops strong, and one can act. Pressure is the Subject for the enemy and Wealth is the aid of Pressure: the enemy had not meant to raise troops, but as soon as the Wealth line moves Pressure shows its strength, so soldiers swarm and weapons are in disorder; if only Wealth is still, Pressure stands alone without aid and peace comes of itself.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.33** 兄象单独兴起，恐怕有断粮的忧患；父爻独自发动，难道没有伤兵卒的忧虑？兄弟专主奸险的事，如果在卦中独自发动，旺相又带白虎，对方就行凶劫掠；衰弱而临玄武，必被阴谋抽窃；如果得到日辰克制兄弟，即使见到有人行劫，也决不会失脱，就像伏着木牛流马一样稳固。父母能克子孙，如果在爻间单独发动，对我的兵大为忌讳，又旺相带凶煞，必定被刑伤；如果休囚又加吉神，对方的势头稍微（强）也不可小看啊。
  If the Peer image rises alone, fear the danger of cut-off grain; if the Resource line moves alone, how can there be no worry of injured soldiers? Peer governs crafty and dangerous matters, and if it moves alone in the hexagram, thriving and carrying the White Tiger, the enemy commits violence and plunder; if weak and sitting on the Dark Tortoise, there will surely be secret plots and pilfering; if the Day controls Peer, even if one sees plundering there will surely be no loss, as firm as if wooden oxen and gliding horses were hidden away. Resource can control Output, and if it moves alone among the lines, it is a great taboo for my troops, and if thriving and carrying baleful spirits, they are surely punished and injured; if it is resting yet carries auspicious spirits, even a slight strength on the enemy's side must not be made light of.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.34** 福神变成文书，兵必定骄傲，后悔也来不及；子孙变成兄弟，将领虽弱，也大获全功。父母是子孙的大忌，如果子孙变父母，就是用爻变成忌神，而克伐就像雪狮向着太阳，起初张扬威势，日渐消融，大有刑伤，噬脐莫及；兄弟是子孙的元神，子孙变兄弟，就是用爻化出元神来扶助，如同猛虎添翼，越发精神，百战百胜，大获全功。
  If the fortune spirit changes into the document, the troops are surely proud and regret will be too late; if Output changes into Peer, though the commander is weak he wins a great and complete success. Resource is the great Adversary of Output, and if Output turns into Resource, the Subject changes into the Adversary, and the control is like a snow lion facing the sun, at first putting on a show of might and day by day melting away, with much punishment and injury and the regret of biting one's own navel too late; Peer is the Support of Output, and if Output turns into Peer, the Subject changes into its Support to assist, like a fierce tiger adding wings, ever more spirited, winning a hundred battles in a hundred and gaining a great and complete success.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.35** 福神遇到青龙，必定出忠良的将领；子孙监临白虎，应当差遣勇猛的军队。子孙遇到青龙，必是赤胆忠心的胜将，再加禄、驿马、贵人，必是智勇谋略之士；子孙是兵卒，如果临小白虎，虽然衰弱也是强壮的兵卒；如果临大白虎，必是骁勇猛烈的雄兵。
  If the fortune spirit meets the Azure Dragon, loyal and good commanders are sure to appear; if Output sits over the White Tiger, bold and fierce troops should be sent. If Output meets the Azure Dragon, he is a victorious general of true loyalty, and with Emolument, the Horse and the Noble added, he is a man of wit, courage and strategy; Output is the soldiers, and if it sits on the Lesser White Tiger, though weak they are still strong soldiers; if it sits on the Greater White Tiger, they are surely valiant and fierce picked troops.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.36** 劫煞是凶星，怕临官鬼发动；亡神是恶曜，忌和官鬼同时兴起。官鬼是贼魁：休衰，就可以战、容易剿灭；旺相，必是强横而有谋勇的贼；如果发动，就无法抵挡，哪里还能再加凶煞一起行动呢？凶煞就是劫煞、太煞、亡神，起例已注在前面。如果官鬼发动、勾结各煞来克身爻、世爻，必定对方势头猖獗，我军大有损伤。
  Robbery is an ill star, and fears sitting with a moving Pressure; the Lost Spirit is an evil luminary, and must not rise together with Pressure. Pressure is the bandit chief: if resting or weak, one can fight and it is easy to destroy; if thriving, it is surely a fierce, wise and brave bandit; if it moves it cannot be withstood, so how could ill spirits be added to move with it? The ill spirits are Robbery, the Great Baleful Spirit and the Lost Spirit, whose setting out is noted earlier. If Pressure moves and colludes with the baleful spirits to control the Body and Self, the enemy is surely rampant and my army suffers heavy losses.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.37** 煞陷落、官鬼摇动，虽然凶也振作不起来；官鬼落空、煞发动，纵然有乱也不必忧虑。官鬼和煞同时兴起，兵马云集，成为大战；官鬼和凶煞都绝，战事顿时止息，庆贺太平。征战的爻里，最忌官鬼和煞两个神：如果煞神空、绝，官鬼纵然发动，也难成大害；如果官鬼逢空、绝，各煞虽然兴旺，也没有重大的祸害；如果官鬼和煞都发动，官鬼就随着煞的势头，煞趋附官鬼的威势，云集聚拢、大肆苦战；或者克世、冲世的，必被刑杀，不轻；如果官鬼和煞两个神衰弱而又安静，或者空或者绝，才是弓箭收起、盔甲解下的太平之世。
  If the baleful spirit sinks and Pressure stirs, though ominous it cannot rally; if Pressure is Void and the baleful spirit moves, even if there is disorder there is no need to worry. If Pressure and the baleful spirit rise together, horses and men gather like clouds and it becomes a great battle; if Pressure and the ill spirits are both extinct, arms are suddenly stilled and one celebrates peace. In the lines of war, what is most feared is the two spirits Pressure and the baleful spirit: if the baleful spirit is Void or extinct, even though Pressure moves it can hardly do great harm; if Pressure meets Void or extinction, though the baleful spirits flourish there is no heavy disaster; if Pressure and the baleful spirit both move, Pressure follows the baleful spirit's momentum and the baleful spirit joins Pressure's might, they gather like clouds and fight a bitter battle on a large scale; if they control or clash with the Self, one is surely punished and slain, and not lightly; if both Pressure and the baleful spirit are weak and still, or Void or extinct, only then is it an age of peace with bows put away and helmets removed.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.38** 官鬼和煞克身，我军难以抵敌；重叠发动去伐应爻，对方的贼寇容易诛灭。鬼是官鬼，煞是大煞、劫煞之类。官鬼和煞两个神如果来克世爻，我军大受损伤；如果官鬼和煞安静而又衰弱，来克旺相的世爻，是旺相不受克，料想没有大害；官鬼和煞旺相而克衰弱的世爻，这是旺相能克衰弱，我军大被讨伐杀戮；如果官鬼和煞旺相发动克应爻，对方已经受制，容易剿伐。
  If Pressure and the baleful spirit control the Body, my army can hardly stand against them; if they move in double to attack the Other, the enemy bandits are easily destroyed. 'Gui' is Pressure, and 'sha' is the great baleful spirit, Robbery and the like. If the two spirits Pressure and the baleful spirit come to control the Self, my army is badly injured; if Pressure and the baleful spirit are still and weak and come to control a thriving Self, a thriving line is not controlled and no great harm is expected; if Pressure and the baleful spirit are thriving and control a weak Self, this is the thriving controlling the weak, and my army is greatly attacked and slaughtered; if Pressure and the baleful spirit are thriving and moving and control the Other, the enemy is already restrained and easily destroyed.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.39** 细究是哪一爻克应爻，再查哪一个象伤官鬼；用这种奇方妙计，自然剿灭凶恶、锄除强梁。官鬼落空，只取应爻；应爻陷落，可以从官鬼（入手）。要懂得行兵的办法，必须找出克应爻、伤官鬼的爻：应爻如果受伤，对方就遭屠戮；官鬼如果被克，贼必定生擒。比如火爻临在应爻或官鬼上，须找水来制火，用黑旗为号，并且临水作战，自然得胜，其余的照这样推。官鬼如果空亡，就评断应象；应爻如果落陷，必须看官爻。
  Examine closely which line controls the Other, and then see which image injures Pressure; with this marvellous method and clever plan, one naturally destroys the evil and weeds out the strong. If Pressure is Void, take only the Other; if the Other is sunk, one can start from Pressure. To know the method of moving troops, one must find the line that controls the Other and injures Pressure: if the Other is injured, the enemy is slaughtered; if Pressure is controlled, the bandits are surely taken alive. For example, if a Fire line sits on the Other or on Pressure, one must seek Water to control the Fire, use black flags as the signal and fight beside water, and one naturally wins; the rest follow this. If Pressure is Void, judge the Other image; if the Other is sunk, one must look at the Pressure line.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.40** 子孙爻旺相、官鬼爻休囚，官鬼虽然克我，我也无忧；应爻强、世爻弱，世爻纵然伤他，他也难被讨伐；世爻遇到生扶、子孙象旺相，就是将领勇敢、兵卒骁勇；应爻遭到克破、官鬼爻落空，就是贼衰寇退。子孙能制官鬼：如果子孙旺相、官鬼休囚，对方先已被制住，哪里能克世爻呢？就像大盗正想打劫别人，已经被官府搜捕擒获，哪有工夫行劫，我还忧虑什么呢？如果应爻旺相、世爻衰弱，虽然克应爻，无奈对方猖獗，难以取胜，「旺不受克」说的正是这个。如果日辰、月将生世、合世，旺相的子孙又来扶助，就是我兵精良、粮草充足、机谋深远、声势浩大，决胜千里；如果应衰弱受克受冲，官鬼又落空亡，对方必定丧胆、倒戈、拔寨逃走。
  If the Output line is thriving and the Pressure line resting, though Pressure controls me I have nothing to fear; if the Other is strong and the Self weak, though the Self injures them, they are hard to attack; if the Self meets generation and support and the Output image is thriving, the commander is brave and the troops valiant; if the Other is controlled and broken and the Pressure line Void, the bandits decline and withdraw. Output can control Pressure: if Output is thriving and Pressure resting, the enemy has first been restrained, so how can it control the Self? It is like a great robber who is about to rob someone and has already been seized by the officers, so how has he time to rob, and what have I to fear? If the Other is thriving and the Self weak, though the Self controls the Other, the enemy is rampant and hard to beat, and 'the thriving is not controlled' says exactly this. If the Day and the Month General generate or combine with the Self, and a thriving Output also comes to support, my troops are well trained, grain is plentiful, plans are deep and strength great, and victory is decided a thousand miles away; if the Other is weak and controlled and clashed, and Pressure also falls into Void, the enemy will surely lose heart, turn their spears, pull up their stockades and flee.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.41** 金是兵甲，金值官鬼兴旺，要防战斗；土当城池，土临官鬼发动，就动起干戈。兵器铠甲都用金来造，如果金临官鬼，或者旺相、或者发动，对方必定甲坚刃利（与众不同），谋划奇策、出其不意地猛然冲突，不要小看；土旺在四季，分布四个角落，又作城墙、台堡，如果官鬼带土发动，就干戈扰攘，漫山遍野，无地不有。
  Metal is arms and armour, and if Metal meets a flourishing Pressure, guard against fighting; Earth is the city walls, and if Earth sits on Pressure and moves, spears and shields are raised. Weapons and armour are all made of Metal, and if Metal sits on Pressure and is thriving or moving, the enemy surely has strong armour and sharp blades, unlike the common, and plans unusual strategies to charge suddenly and unexpectedly, which must not be made light of; Earth is thriving in the four seasons and spread over the four corners, and also serves as city walls, terraces and forts, and if Pressure carries Earth and moves, spears and shields are in turmoil, covering the hills and spreading over the fields, with no place free of them.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.42** 密密的刀枪，是金宫空而发动；飘飘的旗帜，是因为火的父爻旺相逢冲。刀剑本属金，又属官鬼，如果值空亡又逢发动，必定振扬，所以经书说「金空则鸣」，必是刀枪光芒耀日、金鼓声震如雷，营寨密密重重是提防有备的缘故。旌旗以父母爻为用爻，又属离火，如果旺相，更遇日辰冲，所以日冲火发就有焰，必是黄幡悬着豹尾、彩旗上写着麒麟，飘飘摇摇，引导军队、行阵，号令严明，军机整肃。
  Dense blades and spears are Metal palace Void and moving; fluttering banners are the Fire Resource line thriving and clashed. Swords and blades belong to Metal, and also to Pressure, and if they meet Void and also move they surely rise up, so the classic says 'Metal Void rings', and surely the blades and spears flash against the sun, the gongs and drums shake like thunder, and the camps are dense and layered out of watchfulness and readiness. The banners take the Resource line as the Subject line and also belong to Li Fire, and if it is thriving and also meets the Day's clash, then when the Day clashes Fire moves there is flame, and surely yellow pennants hang with leopard tails and bright flags are written with the qilin, fluttering and waving, leading the army and the lines, orders strict and clear and military secrets well guarded.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.43** 水是江河，木是舟楫；两爻都旺相，水战特别适宜；两象都受伤，乘船渡水不利。水是江海的源流，木是舟船的根本，水战各有各的用处：水旺则满，水绝则竭；木旺则船大，木衰则只有小舟，木绝则没有船；两爻旺相又发动，万事齐备，利于操舟；如果两爻衰弱又绝，一切都不成，难以找到渡口了！
  Water is rivers and Wood is boats and oars; if both lines are thriving, a naval battle is especially fit; if both images are injured, going by boat is unfavourable. Water is the source and flow of rivers and seas, and Wood is the root of boats, each having its use in a naval battle: thriving Water is full, extinct Water dries up; thriving Wood means big boats, weak Wood only small boats, extinct Wood no boat; if both lines are thriving and also move, everything is ready and it is favourable to handle boats; if both lines are weak and also extinct, nothing succeeds and it is hard to find a crossing!
- **LY.CAREER.EL.44** 火多是营寨，土是城墙，这两个象兴旺，最宜陆战；这两爻空、绝，不利出征。营垒以火为用，城台靠土生成：火空则营寨危险，土空则城池陷落；两爻如果值旺相、动、冲，可以取得西域沙漠（那样的）胜利；（两象）如果遇到衰、休、空、绝，必有华容小道（曹操败走）那样的危险。
  Fire is mostly the camp and Earth is the city wall; if these two images flourish, they are most fit for a land battle; if these two lines are Void or extinct, it is not favourable to set out. Camps and ramparts take Fire as the Subject, and city terraces rely on Earth for their making: if Fire is Void the camp is in danger, if Earth is Void the city falls; if the two lines meet thriving, moving and clashing, one can win a victory such as that in the western deserts; if they meet weak, resting, Void or extinct, there will surely be danger like that of the small road at Huarong.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.45** 火爻官鬼伤身，须防劫寨；土爻官鬼克世，恐怕掉进陷坑。官鬼带火克世的，须慎防火攻、劫寨；官鬼和煞以土伤世的，恐怕有掉进壕沟的危险。
  If a Fire Pressure line injures the Body, guard against a raid on the stockade; if an Earth Pressure line controls the Self, one may fall into a pit. If Pressure carrying Fire controls the Self, one must take care against attack by fire and a raid on the stockade; if Pressure and a baleful spirit injure the Self by Earth, there may be the danger of falling into a trench.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.46** 火发动，利于迁移营寨；父爻落空，宜改换旌旗。军营以火为用，火如果发动，动就是变化，所以营寨利于迁移才吉；如果被刑、冲，或者加玄武、加兄弟，必遭侵劫，急宜迁改。旌旗以父爻为用，如果值旬空、月破，必然损伤，急须改换。
  If Fire moves, it favours moving the camp; if the Resource line is Void, it is fit to change the banners. The army camp takes Fire as the Subject, and if Fire moves, moving means change, so it is auspicious to move the camp; if it is punished or clashed, or the Dark Tortoise or Peer is added, the camp will surely be raided, and it should be moved and changed urgently. The banners take the Resource line as the Subject, and if it meets decade Void or Month-break they are surely damaged and must be changed urgently.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.47** 土爻官鬼发动克世爻，对方兴用炮石；火爻官鬼发动伤身象，对方用火攻。官鬼兴旺带土来克世的，须防对方制备擂木、炮石来攻；如果官鬼发动、加火一起克世，他必定举火烧营，旺相则太厉害，衰弱则稍微好一些。
  If an Earth Pressure line moves and controls the Self, the enemy uses catapult stones; if a Fire Pressure line moves and injures the Body image, they attack by fire. If Pressure is flourishing and carries Earth to control the Self, one must guard against their preparing rolling logs and catapult stones to attack; if Pressure moves and, with Fire added, controls the Self together, they will surely raise fire and burn the camp, and if it is thriving it is very serious and if weak it is somewhat better.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.48** 官鬼藏在世爻下面克世爻，切记忧虑自己的兵谋害主将；子孙伏在身爻中合应象，当心离开我的将领、投降夷人。世爻下面伏着官鬼、而又克世爻的，就是家兵杀主：如果世旺鬼衰，奸心已经存着、无隙可乘；世衰鬼旺，则叛逆潜谋，乘虚而入，所以张飞受到范疆的刺杀。如果世爻下面伏着子孙而合应爻，就是军心已经离散，各怀去意；如果世衰应旺，就是他国强横、我军卑弱，所以匈奴逼迫李陵投降。如果日辰生子孙，又加朱雀、白虎临在上面，必定被人暗中进谗、公开唆使，背弃道义、投靠仇人。
  If Pressure lies hidden under the Self and controls the Self, take care to worry about one's own troops plotting against the commander; if Output lies hidden in the Body and combines with the Other image, beware of the generals leaving me and surrendering to the foreigners. If a Pressure line lies hidden under the Self and also controls the Self, it is household troops killing their master: if the Self is thriving and Pressure weak, treacherous thoughts have been stored but no gap is open; if the Self is weak and Pressure thriving, the rebellion plots in secret and enters where there is a gap, as Zhang Fei was stabbed by Fan Jiang. If Output lies hidden under the Self and combines with the Other, the hearts of the army have already left and each harbours thoughts of leaving; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, the other state is strong and my army low, as the Xiongnu forced Li Ling to surrender. If the Day generates Output and the Vermilion Bird and White Tiger sit on it, one will surely be slandered in secret and incited in the open, betraying righteousness and following the enemy.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.49** 内外两个官鬼兴起伤世爻，祸患由内应外通而起；前后（内外）的子孙旺相生身，利于前攻后击。内卦的官鬼是自己人，外卦的官鬼是对方的兵：如果发动而克世爻，祸患出自自己人勾结外人，这就是内应外合的残害；如果世旺鬼衰，虽然发动也难以伤克；如果世爻变子孙或官鬼变子孙，就是官鬼受制，谋划不会决裂，没有夭折的祸害，经书说「有人制鬼，鬼动何妨」；本卦子孙之卦，子孙旺相而生世爻的，先攻后取，顺着心意行军，都获得全功；如果内卦的官鬼旺相、外卦的官鬼兴旺，就彼此对抗，难以判断胜负。
  If Pressure rises both inside and outside and injures the Self, the disaster comes from collusion between inside and outside; if Output is thriving before and behind and generates the Body, it favours attacking in front and striking behind. The inner Pressure is one's own men and the outer Pressure is the enemy's troops: if they move and control the Self, the disaster is one's own men joining others, which is the injury of inside and outside in collusion; if the Self is thriving and Pressure weak, though they move it is hard for them to injure or control; if the Self turns into Output or Pressure turns into Output, Pressure is restrained, the plot does not break out and there is no untimely harm, as the classic says 'if someone restrains Pressure, what harm is there in its moving'; if in the original hexagram Output is thriving and generates the Self, one attacks first and takes after, and moving troops as one wishes one gains complete success; if the inner Pressure is thriving and the outer Pressure flourishing, they resist each other and it is hard to judge victory.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.50** 官鬼乘着长生、帝旺，是贼寇兴起、世道大乱的时候；官鬼落在墓库、空亡，是国泰民安的景象；内外都没有官鬼，盗兵不来；世爻、应爻都空，战事已经断绝。战征的事，以世爻为主将，子孙当兵卒；以应爻为对方，官鬼为贼：如果官鬼值在长生、帝旺的位置，就盗寇纵横、西夷肆虐；如果卦中内外没有官鬼，或者逢墓、绝，世、应都空，就干戈止息，国泰民安了。
  If Pressure rides Birth or Emperor's Peak, it is a time of bandits rising and the world in disorder; if Pressure sits in the tomb or in Void, it is the image of a state at peace and a people secure; if there is no Pressure inside or outside, bandit troops do not come; if the Self and Other are both Void, war has ceased. In matters of war and campaign, the Self is the commander and Output is the soldiers; the Other is the enemy and Pressure is the bandits: if Pressure sits in the position of Birth or Emperor's Peak, bandits run wild and the western barbarians rage; if the hexagram has no Pressure inside or outside, or it meets tomb or extinction, and the Self and Other are both Void, arms are laid down and the state is at peace and the people secure.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.51** 胜负我先知道，不是靠揣测；吉凶神明已经告知，要依据道理推究详察。
  Victory or defeat I know beforehand, and it is not by guesswork; good and ill the spirits have already told, and one should work them out by principle.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.62** 占承袭爵位、谋求武职，以官爻为主，以世爻为凭据。承袭爵位、接任官职，都是沾受君王的恩典和祖先的荫庇；想知道利和名是显达还是暗淡，要靠卦象去推详。
  In divining about inheriting a title or seeking a military rank, take the Pressure line as the chief and the Self image as the ground. Inheriting a title and succeeding to an office are all through the sovereign's grace and the shade of one's ancestors; to know whether gain and name will be bright or dim, one must work it out from the images of the hexagram.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.63** 向朝廷献策、自请从军，要看父母爻：父爻旺相，一定高中；父爻落空，怎么能成名？父爻即使不空，还忌讳财爻发动。
  To submit a plan to the court and volunteer for service, look at Resource: if Resource is thriving, one will surely pass high; if Resource is Void, how can one make a name? Even if Resource is not Void, one still dislikes Wealth moving.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.64** 从军、比试武艺，世爻喜欢旺相，应爻适宜衰弱。如果是临阵交锋，又凭世、应：世旺应衰，对方一定弱；世衰应旺，对方就强；世克应是大胜，应克世就难以取胜。
  For joining the army or a military trial, the Self line likes to be thriving and the Other should be weak. For those who cross swords in battle, rely again on the Self and the Other: if the Self is thriving and the Other weak, the other side is surely weak; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, the other side is strong; if the Self controls the Other it is a great victory, and if the Other controls the Self it is hard to win.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.65** 靠祖荫承袭封爵，喜欢遇到贵人乘着禄、马；捐买官职、进升爵位，喜欢遇到青龙聚着财爻、官爻。所忌讳的是六冲、世爻落陷落空，白费力气；所喜欢的是六合、官鬼兴旺、财爻旺相，事事称心。军中占承袭职位、民间卜官职，各自都忌世爻空、官空和六冲的卦；如果官与世都旺相，一定显出威风；官爻或世爻带青龙、贵人，以及卦遇六合，威势和福分更加增添。
  To inherit a title by ancestral privilege, one is glad to meet the Noble riding on the Emolument and the Horse; to buy an office and advance in rank, one is glad to meet the Dragon gathering Wealth and the star of office. What is feared is a six-clash with the Self sunk or Void, which is wasted effort; what is welcomed is a six-combine with Pressure flourishing and Wealth thriving, everything as one wishes. In the army divining for inheriting a post, or among the people divining for office, each fears the Self Void, the star of office Void, and a six-clash hexagram; if the star of office and the Self are both thriving, one will surely show authority; if the Pressure line or the Self line carries the Azure Dragon or the Noble, or the hexagram meets a six-combine, authority and fortune are further increased.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.66** 官鬼在酉爻、申爻，显耀武功、扬起威风，享受世袭的俸禄；官爻落在乾卦、兑卦，抵御外族、振奋华夏，树立非凡的功劳。官爻或者世爻如果落在乾、兑卦中，或者在酉、申爻上，都会名声震动华夏和外族。
  If Pressure is on a You or Shen line, one displays martial prowess and raises one's awe, and receives a hereditary emolument; if the star of office is in the Qian or Dui trigram, one drives out the barbarians, raises up the Hua-Xia, and sets up extraordinary merit. If the Pressure line or the Self line falls in the Qian or Dui trigram, or is on a You or Shen line, in every case one's name shakes both the Chinese and the barbarians.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.67** 武职之中，内卦、外卦都兴旺，功劳就高、名誉就远；世爻旺相又逢生，勇冠三军、蒙受上司的赏赐；官爻兴旺又得帮助，爵位尊到一品、沐浴特殊的恩典；卦身遇到太岁生合，在战场上拔得头魁。内外卦兴旺、世应旺相，威势一定压倒邻近的疆界；世旺再遇太岁，或者日辰、月建生扶的，勇冠三军。
  Among military posts, if the inner and outer trigrams are both flourishing, merit is high and fame far; if the Self is thriving and is generated, one's courage tops the three armies and one receives the superior's reward; if the star of office flourishes and gains aid, one's rank is of the first grade and one bathes in special favour; if the Body meets generation or combination from Tai Sui, one becomes the top man in the field of war. With the inner and outer trigrams flourishing and the Self and the Other thriving, one's power will surely overawe the neighbouring borders; if the Self is thriving and also meets Tai Sui, or the Day and the Month generate and support it, one's courage tops the three armies.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.68** 身爻、世爻衰弱，就力量怯弱、机会稀少；世爻遇到日、月克制或冲，在拿戈戟的人当中也是最下等的。世爻如果休囚、卦又不旺相，就该断为没有力量、没有才能；又被日辰、月建冲克世爻的，完全没有提拔，反而遭到弹劾，哪里能在军队中出名？这中间还要看官爻：旺相就声名显赫，落空就被弹劾、削去兵权。
  If the Body and Self lines are weak, one's strength is timid and one's chances sparse; if the Self meets control or clash from the Day and the Month, even among bearers of spear and halberd one is of the lowest rank. If the Self is at rest or imprisoned and the hexagram is not thriving, one should be judged as without strength or ability; if the Self line is again clashed or controlled by the Day and the Month, there is no advancement at all and on the contrary impeachment, so how could one win fame in the ranks? Among these, look also at the Pressure line: if it is thriving, fame is eminent; if it is Void, one is impeached and stripped of military power.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.69** 世爻带青龙，名声传遍朝廷和民间；官爻坐着白虎，威势震动华夏和外族。青龙是大贵的星，白虎是兵权的煞，这两兽如果能临在世爻上、官爻上，一定主威名传扬海内。
  If the Self carries the Azure Dragon, one's name runs through court and countryside; if the star of office sits on the White Tiger, one's might shakes the Chinese and the barbarians. The Azure Dragon is the star of great nobility and the White Tiger is the baleful spirit of military power; if these two beasts come to the Self or to the star of office, one's might will surely spread throughout the land.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.124** 投军当兵，以世爻、身爻为主，以财爻、子孙（福）为凭据。投兵必须细看世爻：逢旺、逢生，去了必定兴旺；如果遇到旬空，就没有对手可打；如果逢月破，就死在他乡。凡是占投充当兵的，以世爻为用，只要得到日辰生世、或者世旺相，就功成利厚、力量增加、身体康健；如果值旬空，就没有人对敌；如果逢月破，就大有刑伤，非常不吉利。
  In enlisting as a soldier, take the Self and the Body as the chief and Wealth and Output as the ground. In joining the army one must examine the Self line in detail: if it meets thriving or generation, going will surely bring prosperity; if it meets the ten-day Void, there is no enemy to face; if it meets Month-break, one dies in a foreign land. In every divination about enlisting as a soldier, the Self is the Subject: if the Day generates the Self or the Self is thriving, one's service is accomplished, the profit is great, one's strength increases and one's body is hale; if it falls on the ten-day Void there is no one to oppose; if it meets Month-break there is much punishment and injury and it is greatly inauspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.125** 世爻随着官爻入墓，身遭厄难；助官鬼伤身，性命受殃。随军出战，所忌的是官爻。如果官爻临在世爻、身爻、命爻上而入墓，叫做随鬼入墓，必定有丧亡的凶难；官鬼适宜安静，如果遇到财爻发动、能帮助官鬼兴旺，又伤克身爻、世爻，叫做助鬼伤身，一定主身陷险地、性命倾覆，难以回到家乡。
  If the Self follows the star of office into the tomb, one's person meets disaster; if one helps Pressure to injure the Body, one's life suffers calamity. In going out to battle with the army, what is feared is the star of office. If it rests on the Self, the Body or the fate line and enters the tomb, it is called following Pressure into the tomb, and there will surely be the misfortune of death; Pressure is best quiet, and if Wealth moves and can help Pressure to flourish, and also injures the Body or the Self, it is called helping Pressure to injure the Body, and it surely means that one's person falls into danger, one's life is overturned, and it is hard to return to one's native village.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.126** 官鬼如果冲克世爻，不如不去，反而是好。军旅的忌讳，只在官鬼：它来冲世，不过是被对方克伐，还没有决出输赢；它如果来克世，大有刑伤，就是还没有沾到利禄，就先遭杀戮；不如安守本分，暂且守着故乡。
  If Pressure clashes with or controls the Self, it is better not to go, which is on the contrary good. The taboo of the army is nothing but Pressure: if it comes to clash with the Self, one is merely attacked by the other side and victory or defeat is not yet decided; if it comes to control the Self, there is much punishment and injury, and before one has shared in any pay or rank one is struck down; it is better to keep to one's lot and hold one's native place for now.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.127** 土属的官克世，遭坑陷；金属的官鬼冲身，被刀箭所伤；水属的官鬼，一定遇到风波险阻；火属的官，必主触犯火光；木爻值官鬼，多有刑责；福德（子孙）发动，最吉祥。克世的官是大凶之象，必须分它属于五行中的哪一类，才可以躲避：官鬼属土，要防对方挖陷坑，又要防疾病；金属的官鬼，忌讳锋利的刀箭，也防跌扑；水属的官鬼，必然设下背水的计谋，又被风波险阻；火属的官鬼，只要逃火患，又要顾虑营屯被烧的劫难；木属的官鬼，会有鞭打杖责之刑。子孙是和解之神，只要发动一次，万般祸患自然消散。
  If an Earth star of office controls the Self, one falls into a pit-trap; if a Metal Pressure clashes with the Body, one is hurt by knife and arrow; if a Water Pressure, one will surely meet the danger of waves; if a Fire star of office, one will surely run into the red glare; if a Wood line holds Pressure, there are many beatings and punishments; Output, the fortune virtue, moving is most auspicious. The star of office that controls the Self is an image of great ill, and one must tell which of the five kinds it belongs to in order to ward it off: if Pressure is of Earth, guard against the other side digging a trap-pit for one to fall into, and against illness; a Metal Pressure fears sharp blades and arrows, and one must guard against falls; a Water Pressure surely sets a back-to-the-river stratagem, and one is also blocked by wind and waves; with a Fire Pressure one need only flee the danger of fire and also fear the calamity of the camp being burned; a Wood Pressure brings the punishment of whip and cane. Output is the spirit of reconciliation, and if it moves even once, ten thousand calamities melt away of themselves.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.128** 世爻去克对方，适宜出战；应爻来克我，不要登场。世爻是我，应爻是对方：如果世克应爻，打仗必定胜过对方；应如果克世，对方一定很有本事。
  If the Self goes to control the other, it is fitting to go out and fight; if the Other comes to control me, do not enter the field. The Self is me and the Other is the enemy: if the Self controls the Other line, one will surely beat the other in battle; if the Other controls the Self, he is surely very able.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.129** 财旺相，饷银资助成倍得到；兄爻兴旺，实在怕兵粮被灭掉。财是口粮，旺相就丰足有余，休囚就不够；兄是耗损的祸祟，安静就可以，发动就有被克扣的忧患。
  If Wealth is thriving, pay and supplies are gained doubled; if Peer flourishes, one truly fears the soldier's rations being wiped out. Wealth is the ration, which when thriving is abundant and when resting or imprisoned is not enough; Peer is the baleful drain, and if quiet it is tolerable, and if it moves there is the trouble of deductions.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.130** 官爻持世、没有冲克，必定做先锋，辅佐帝王。官爻最适宜持世，又适宜安静旺相，这样就有奇特的才能和美好的爵位；如果遇到冲克，必有奸佞之人窃夺功劳权柄的祸患。
  If the Pressure line holds the Self with no clash or control, one will surely be a vanguard and assist the sovereign. The Pressure line is best holding the Self, and also best quiet and thriving, and then one has rare ability and a fine rank; if it meets clash or control, there will surely be the misfortune of treacherous men stealing one's merit and power.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.24** 担任将帅或征讨之官的人，平时占问，不适宜子孙发动，主有降职调任、贬责，也不适宜应爻发动克世爻，主有意想不到的变故；太岁、月建冲动官鬼或世爻，主有敕命征讨的事。如果临阵对敌占问，就喜欢子孙发动，必成剿捕的功绩，再得到太岁、月建生合，还有升赏；这时官鬼不当作爵位，要当作贼寇来论，世克应也吉。
  For one holding the post of commander or an officer of punitive expeditions, in divining in ordinary times it is not fitting that Output moves, which means demotion, transfer and rebuke, nor that the Other moves and controls the Self, which means an unforeseen change; if the Tai Sui or the Month clashes Pressure or the Self into motion, there will be an imperial order for a punitive expedition. If the divination is made facing the enemy, it is welcome that Output moves, and one will surely achieve merit in suppressing the bandits, and if the Tai Sui and the Month also generate or combine, there will be promotion and reward as well; Pressure is then not taken as rank but as the bandits, and the Self controlling the Other is also auspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.25** 镇守一方，不论文官武职，都适宜六爻安静；世应生合比和，日辰月建不互相冲克，就能安睡在边庭，安然没有警报，世应空亡也吉；如果遇到官鬼发动、世应冲克，必定多有侵扰，要通变着断。
  In guarding a region, whether in a civil or military post, it is fitting that the six lines are still; if the Self and the Other generate, combine or are in harmony and the Day and the Month do not clash or control each other, one sleeps soundly at the border, at ease and without alarm, and the Self and the Other Void is also auspicious; if Pressure moves and the Self and the Other clash or control each other, there will surely be many incursions, and one should judge flexibly.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.86** 占征战，世爻是我方，应爻是对方：世爻旺相、克住应爻就胜，应爻旺相、克住世爻就败。子孙是我方的将军，官鬼是对方的主帅，两边军师的强弱，就从这两个爻上分。
  In a question about a campaign the Self is our side and the Other is the enemy: if the Self is thriving or strong and controls the Other we win, and if the Other is thriving and controls the Self we lose. Output is our general and Pressure is the enemy commander, and the strength of the two sides' leaders is told apart from these two lines.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.87** 父母爻是旌旗。父母旺相发动，主兵马起动，旌旗遮满原野；金爻落空而动，就听到金鼓声喧闹震天，因为金落空了才会响。
  The Resource line is the banners. If Resource is thriving and moves, an army is rising and banners cover the plain; if a Metal line is Void and moves, one hears the din of gongs and drums shaking the sky, since Metal sounds when it is void.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.88** 妻财是粮草：旺相的粮草多，衰弱的粮草少，落空就是没有粮；兄弟是伏兵，也是抢夺粮草的神，不宜旺相发动。
  Wealth is grain and fodder: thriving means plenty, weak means little, and Void means none; Peer is the ambush, and also the spirit that seizes the grain, and should not be thriving and moving.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.89** 木是舟船：木爻发动来生扶世爻，或者水爻和子孙发动，适合乘船决战取胜；火是营寨：火爻旺相发动来生扶世身，扎寨必定得到地形优越的好地方。
  Wood is boats: if a Wood line moves to generate or support the Self, or a Water line moves together with Output, it is fit to fight a decisive battle in boats and win; Fire is the camp: if a Fire line is thriving and moves to generate or support the Self, the stockade will surely stand in a place of natural advantage.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.90** 父母爻持世发动，主帅不体恤士兵，上下离心，如果又带着兄弟或凶神，要防备内部自己生变；子孙持世、旺相发动，将军一定能在千里之外决定胜负。
  If Resource holds the Self and moves, the commander has no kindness for his soldiers and high and low are of different minds, and if it also carries Peer or a baleful spirit one must guard against a change from within; if Output holds the Self and is thriving and moves, the general will surely decide the victory from a thousand li away.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.91** 卦中水爻发动，或者世爻持着水爻，或者水爻发动克伤子孙，只要子孙旺相、得到月建日辰生扶，或者子孙旺相发动，就可以效法韩信背水列阵，在绝境里求生，反而取胜。
  If a Water line moves in the hexagram, or the Self holds a Water line, or a Water line moves and controls and injures Output, then so long as Output is thriving or strong and is generated and supported by the Month and Day, or Output is thriving and moves, one may follow Han Xin's battle with the river at his back, seeking life in the midst of death, and win instead.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.92** 兄弟是伏兵。兄弟在内卦旺相发动、克应爻，是我方设伏；克世爻，是对方设伏。兄弟在阳象适合白天设伏，在阴象适合夜里设伏，就像唐宪宗时李愬雪夜衔枚疾行，直捣蔡州城，擒住了吴元济。
  Peer is the ambush. If Peer in the inner trigram is thriving and moves and controls the Other, it is our ambush; if it controls the Self, it is the enemy's. If Peer is in a yang image the ambush is fit for the day and in a yin image for the night, like Li Su in the reign of Emperor Xianzong of Tang, who marched through a snowy night with his men holding sticks in their mouths for silence, struck straight at the city of Cai and captured Wu Yuanji.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.93** 世爻持着子孙，将领有才能，可以克敌；但如果被父母发动来克，就应当固守营垒、避开敌人的锋芒，不利于速战，就像汉景帝时七国造反，周亚夫驻军细柳，半夜军营受惊他也卧着不起，深沟高垒，几天后营里安定下来，终于击破了七国的军队。
  If the Self holds Output, the general has the ability to defeat the enemy; but if Resource moves to control it, one should hold the fort and avoid the enemy's edge, and a quick battle is not favourable, as when the seven states rebelled under Emperor Jing of Han and Zhou Yafu camped at Xiliu and lay still when the camp was alarmed at midnight, kept his ditches deep and his ramparts high, and after some days the camp settled and in the end he broke the armies of the seven states.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.94** 应爻持着旺相的官鬼，对方的将领有才能，我方难以对敌；就算卦中有子孙发动，最终也不能大胜，应当像司马懿那样，自知才能不如诸葛孔明，甘愿受送来妇人头巾的羞辱，坚守营垒不出战。
  If the Other line holds Pressure that is thriving or strong, the enemy general is able and it is hard for us to face him, and even if Output in the hexagram moves, in the end there will be no great victory; one should do as Sima Yi did, who judged that his ability did not match Kongming's, swallowed the insult of the women's head-cloths, and held his ramparts without fighting.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.95** 世爻虽然持着子孙，但子孙衰弱也难以战胜敌人；如果得到月建、日辰生扶，就可以效法秦始皇时王翦率六十万大军取得灭楚的功劳。
  Although the Self holds Output, a weak Output can hardly overcome the enemy; if it gets generation and support from the Month and Day, one may follow Wang Jian in the reign of the First Emperor, who with six hundred thousand men achieved the conquest of Chu.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.96** 卦里官鬼、父母虽然多，却安静休囚；子孙虽然少，却当令旺相而发动，这是以少胜多的象，就像晋朝的谢玄、刘牢之，以八千兵马渡江，击破秦王苻坚的九十万大军。
  If the hexagram has many Pressure and Resource lines that are still and resting or trapped, while Output is few but holds the season and is thriving and moving, it is the image of the few overcoming the many, like Xie Xuan and Liu Laozhi of Jin, who crossed the river with eight thousand men and broke the nine hundred thousand of Fu Jian, King of Qin.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.97** 子孙是将，世爻是国君。卦里有两个子孙旺相发动，生扶世爻或世身，主国家有两位将领合谋战胜敌人，就像唐朝的李光弼、郭子仪，两人同心，以忠义自勉，终于平定叛乱，复兴了唐朝。
  Output is the general and the Self is the ruler. If the hexagram has two Output lines that are thriving and moving and generate or support the Self, the state has two generals who plot together to overcome the enemy, like Li Guangbi and Guo Ziyi of Tang, who were of one heart, urged themselves on with loyalty and righteousness, and in the end put down the rebellion and restored the house of Tang.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.98** 两个子孙爻生旺发动，互相冲、刑，又都变入死、墓、绝、空，虽然能战胜敌将，但必定争权夺宠、互相残害，就像晋朝的钟会、邓艾带兵平定蜀国，蜀国平定之后彼此生出嫌隙，以致自相残杀，身家都丧。
  If two Output lines are thriving and moving, clash with and punish each other, and both turn into death, tomb, extinction or Void, then though they may overcome the enemy's generals they will surely struggle for power and favour and harm each other, like Zhong Hui and Deng Ai of Jin, who led troops to subdue Shu and, when Shu was settled, fell into mutual suspicion, until they killed each other and lost both their persons and their families.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.99** 子孙是我方的士兵。子孙发动变入死、墓、空、绝、败，应爻又来克世爻，或者官鬼、父母发动伤克世身，必定三军丧命、损兵折将，就像曹操夸耀水军八十万，乘势袭击吴国，被周瑜、黄盖的火攻打败。
  Output is our soldiers. If Output moves into death, tomb, Void, extinction or defeat, and the Other also controls the Self, or Pressure or Resource moves and injures the Self or Body, the three armies will surely lose their lives and the troops and officers be lost, like Cao Cao, who boasted of eight hundred thousand marines, attacked Wu in the same breath, and was beaten by the fire attack of Zhou Yu and Huang Gai.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.100** 世爻是一国之君、三军之帅，最好旺相、克住应爻才吉；如果被应爻刑、冲、克、害，世爻又休囚，或者发动变入死、墓、空、绝，主将必然不利，就像项羽百战百胜，一朝兵败星散，羞于去见江东父老，在乌江自刎。
  The Self is the ruler of a state and the commander of the three armies; it is best thriving or strong and controlling the Other. If it is punished, clashed with, controlled or harmed by the Other, and the Self is also resting or trapped, or moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, the chief commander is surely in trouble, like Xiang Yu, who won a hundred battles in a hundred, yet in one morning his troops scattered and, ashamed to face the elders east of the river, he cut his own throat at Wu River.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.101** 水爻的官鬼旺相发动，伤克世爻或世身，敌兵必定占到乘船渡江的便利，就像苻坚凭着能投鞭断流的大军，竟然败给了谢玄八千渡江的兵马。
  If a Water Pressure line is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Self or Body, the enemy troops will surely gain the advantage of crossing the river by boat, like Fu Jian, who with a host that could dam the river with the whips they threw in still lost to the eight thousand troops Xie Xuan brought across the river.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.102** 火爻带着官鬼，敌人的营寨一定很近；火鬼持世，要防被围困。这时如果子孙旺相发动，即使被围也能得胜，像田单下齐城；如果子孙衰弱、官鬼旺相，必定陷入围困，像汉高祖被匈奴围在平城，七天才解围。
  If a Fire line carries Pressure, the enemy's stockade is surely near; if a Fire Pressure line holds the Self, guard against being besieged. Then if Output is thriving and moves, one wins even when besieged, like Tian Dan's taking of the cities of Qi; if Output is weak and Pressure is thriving, one is surely besieged, like the founder of Han, whom the Xiongnu surrounded at Pingcheng until the siege was lifted after seven days.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.103** 应爻持着官鬼来克世爻，卦里又没有妻财，是粮食吃尽、困死的象，就像张巡被围在睢阳城。
  If the Other holds Pressure and controls the Self, and there is no Wealth in the hexagram, it is the image of running out of food and dying, like Zhang Xun besieged in Suiyang.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.104** 旺相的官鬼持世，是被围困的象；卦里又没有妻财、子孙又衰弱，世爻还去生合应爻，是兵少粮尽、向敌人投降的征兆，就像宋朝的吕文焕守襄阳，被元兵围了很久，贾似道隐瞒不发援兵，城中粮尽，于是投降。
  A thriving Pressure line holding the Self is the image of being besieged; if the hexagram also has no Wealth, Output is weak, and the Self also generates or combines with the Other, it is a sign of few troops, food run out and surrender to the enemy, like Lü Wenhuan of Song, who held Xiangyang while the Yuan troops besieged it for a very long time, Jia Sidao concealed the matter and sent no relief, and the city ran out of food and surrendered.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.105** 子孙在外卦发动、应爻克制世爻，是我军远征的象，起初虽然能得胜，最终必定失败；子孙又变成绝爻，免不了投降敌虏，就像汉武帝时李陵的往事。
  If Output moves in the outer trigram and the Other controls the Self, it is the image of our army on a distant campaign: at first it may win, but in the end it must lose, and if Output also turns into an extinct line it cannot avoid surrendering to the enemy, like Li Ling in the time of Emperor Wu of Han.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.106** 内卦的子孙发动，反而去生合应爻、伤克世身，是我方将领士兵背叛主人、投降敌人的征兆，就像燕国的将领乐毅背弃燕国、投奔赵国。
  If Output in the inner trigram moves and, instead, generates or combines with the Other and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is a sign that our generals and soldiers will betray their lord and surrender to the enemy, like Yue Yi, general of Yan, who turned his back on Yan and went over to Zhao.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.107** 官鬼爻虽然衰弱，但如果遇到动爻、日辰来生、扶、拱、合它，那就是敌兵虽然少，也一定有救援，不要去追击陷入穷途的敌人。
  Though a Pressure line is weak, if a moving line or the Day comes to generate, support, hold up or combine with it, then the enemy troops, though few, will surely have relief, and one should not chase a desperate foe.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.108** 子孙虽然旺相，但如果被父母、日辰、动爻克害，对方一定有计谋，不可急着进攻；进攻就必定受挫，即使不至于大败，也会损伤军威，应当缓缓图谋。
  Though Output is thriving, if it is controlled and harmed by Resource, the Day or a moving line, the enemy surely has a stratagem and one must not attack in haste; an attack will be broken, and even if it is not a great defeat it will damage the army's prestige, so one should plan it slowly.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.109** 官鬼爻本来安静，却被日辰冲动、又旺相，克害世爻或世身，必定像吴王被专诸刺杀；如果鬼暗动却被世爻克住，或者子孙发动来救，就像荆轲刺秦王没有刺中，反而自己被杀。
  If a Pressure line is still but the Day clashes it into motion and it is thriving and injures and controls the Self or Body, it is surely like the King of Wu stabbed by Zhuanzhu; if the Pressure line moves in secret but is controlled by the Self, or Output moves to rescue, it is like Jing Ke, who failed to stab the King of Qin and was himself put to death instead.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.110** 子孙变出官鬼，去生合应爻、克害世爻或世身，是我方的士兵谋杀主帅、想要投降敌人的象，就像后汉的张翼德，平时不体恤士兵，有一天被小兵范强、张达在帐下砍了头，顺流而下投奔孙权。
  If Output turns into Pressure that generates or combines with the Other and controls and harms the Self or Body, it is the image of our soldiers murdering their commander in order to surrender to the enemy, like Zhang Yide of the Later Han, who never cared for his soldiers and one day had his head cut off in his tent by the common soldiers Fan Qiang and Zhang Da, who went down the river to Sun Quan.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.111** 要知道用兵该用什么器械，要把五行和卦象一起推究：土是炮石，金是刀箭，水、木是舟船，火是营寨；乾、兑是刀，震、巽是弓马，坤是野战之类。哪个象里有能克应爻的神，就适合用这种器械去克敌；应爻的官鬼或父母在这个象里克世爻，就要反过来防备敌人用这种器械。
  To know the sharp tools for war, work out the five elements and the hexagram images together: Earth is cannon-stones, Metal is blades and arrows, Water and Wood are boats, Fire is the camp; Qian and Dui are blades, Zhen and Xun are bows and horses, Kun is open-field fighting and the like. Where an image holds a spirit that controls the Other, it is fit to use that tool against the enemy; where the Other's Pressure or Resource in that image controls the Self, one must instead guard against the enemy using that tool.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.09** 出兵交战，鬼是敌人（贼），财是粮草；鬼旺，敌人胜；子旺，我方强。以鬼为对方的贼寇，以子为我军；子孙旺相，必定获得全胜；（子孙）出现，宜先出战，伏藏，宜后出战；卦内凡是鬼、父旺相，或者独发，或者持世、持身，是大败之兆。如果六爻安静，世旺、克应，必胜。
  In sending out troops to give battle, Pressure is the enemy and Wealth is the provisions; when Pressure is thriving the enemy wins, and when Output is thriving I am strong. Take Pressure as the bandits on the other side and Output as my army; with Output thriving, one is sure to win entirely; if it appears it is right to go first, and if it is hidden it is right to go after; within the hexagram, if Pressure and Resource are thriving, or move alone, or hold the Self or the Body, it is a sign of great defeat. If the six lines are still, and the Self is thriving and controls the Other, one will surely win.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.10** 父母是城池、壕沟营寨、大旗；子孙是兵、将、军、马；兄弟是辕门、惊恐、伏兵；官鬼是敌兵和刀剑。世应空亡，主议和；世空，我军弱；应空，来攻的兵退；世爻被官鬼冲克，我军不利；兄弟独自发动，是凶。鬼不在爻中，兄弟化出官鬼来合世爻或身，主有奸细在军中；世下伏着鬼，也是这样。
  Resource is the city, the moat and stockade, and the great banner; Output is the soldiers, generals, army and horses; Peer is the camp gate, alarms and ambush troops; Pressure is the enemy soldiers and their swords. If the Self and the Other are both Void, it means peace; the Self Void, my army is weak; the Other Void, the troops marching against me withdraw; the Self line clashed or controlled by Pressure, my army is at a disadvantage; Peer moving alone is ominous. If Pressure is absent from the lines, and Peer changes into Pressure that combines with the Self line or the Body, it means a traitor in the army; with Pressure hidden under the Self it is the same.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.11** 凡是变爻冲克子孙，主损伤名将。冲克财爻，以及财持世又落空，主粮食受困。刑克父母，主战船、城寨有损失，指挥号令大为不宜。冲克官爻，对方的贼寇必败。凡是卦中动火（火爻发动）刑父母、克父母，必然火烧宫室；火冲克财，主火烧粮草。又，财有仓库（财的所在）：靠近子的，就近我军；靠近父的，就近壕堑；靠近鬼的，就近敌人所在。又，父母堵塞的方位，比如在坤宫就是西南方。
  Whenever a changed line clashes with or controls Output, it means a famous general is lost. Clashing with or controlling the Wealth line, or Wealth holding the Self and falling Void, means the provisions are in trouble. Punishing or controlling Resource means loss of warships, city and stockade, and command and orders are greatly unsuitable. Clashing with or controlling the Pressure line, the enemy bandits are sure to lose. Whenever in the hexagram a moving Fire line punishes or controls Resource, there will surely be fire burning the halls; Fire clashing with or controlling Wealth means fire burns the provisions. Also, Wealth has storehouses: near Output, they are near my army; near Resource, near the trenches; near Pressure, near the enemy's place. Also, the direction in which Resource stands as the barrier, as in the palace of Kun it is the south-west.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.24** 将星加刑，再看到登坛拜将的韩信。将星：寅午戌的将星是午，巳酉丑是酉，申子辰是子，亥卯未是卯。将星加白虎、羊刃而生世，必得强将；带贵人、青龙而生世，会得忠良的将领；加劫杀、发动来克世，要防弑君之祸，这样的将星爻名叫刺客。以上三节，都在旺相的年、月、日可以看到应验。
  The General star with punishment added: one sees again Han Xin ascending the altar. The General star: for Yin, Wu and Xu it is Wu; for Si, You and Chou, You; for Shen, Zi and Chen, Zi; for Hai, Mao and Wei, Mao. If the General star carries the White Tiger or the Goat Blade and generates the Self, a strong general is sure to be found; if it carries the Noble or the Azure Dragon and generates the Self, a loyal and good general is to be had; if it carries the Robbery Killing and moves to control the Self, guard against the calamity of regicide, and such a General-star line is called an assassin. The three sections above can all be seen in strong years, months and days.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.25** 大杀落在金鬼上、旺而发动，必起兵革，世去克它大概无大事，它克世必凶；土鬼带杀、发动旺相，四海皆乱，克世必有攻城的贼寇。卦中有杀无鬼，克世也凶；有鬼无杀，虽凶无咎；有神制住杀，鬼动也无妨；鬼位休囚，财兴有害；鬼空杀旺，纵乱也成不了；鬼旺杀空，虽凶无害。鬼带劫杀、或化为兄弟，不过是劫财的盗贼；加玄武，是暗中阴私加害的贼；卦中螣蛇、白虎不动，纵有大杀也不急迫；若官鬼和恶杀一起发动，实在是大害；再看生旺克害落在哪个方位，才可决断。
  A Metal Pressure line carrying a baleful spirit: war breaks out in one quarter; an Earth place meeting ill: weapons and spears everywhere under heaven. If the Great Baleful Spirit stands on a Metal Pressure line that is strong and moving, war is sure to break out; if the Self controls it there is probably no great matter, and if it controls the Self it is surely bad. If an Earth Pressure line carries a baleful spirit and is strong and moving, the four seas are all in chaos, and controlling the Self there will surely be bandits assaulting cities. If the hexagram has a baleful spirit but no Pressure, controlling the Self is also bad; with Pressure but no baleful spirit, though bad it brings no fault; if a spirit restrains the baleful spirit, a moving Pressure line does no harm; if the Pressure place is at rest or trapped, a thriving Wealth line does harm; with Pressure void and the baleful spirit strong, even a revolt does not succeed; with Pressure strong and the baleful spirit void, though bad it does no harm. If Pressure carries the Robbery Killing, or turns into Peer, it is no more than robbers who rob for wealth; if the Dark Tortoise is added, they are secret and covert harming bandits; if the Snake and the Tiger in the hexagram do not move, even with the Great Baleful Spirit it is not urgent; if Pressure and a vicious baleful spirit move together it is truly a great harm; then see in which direction the generation, strength, control and harm fall, and only then can one decide.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.26** 要遏止寇贼，全靠将帅专意出征。世是我方将领，应是对方统帅；又说子孙是我方将领和先锋。子孙得地、旺相，将军必胜；加青龙，是忠良的将领；加白虎，是勇悍威猛的将领；子孙持世，虽有鬼也无伤害；旺相发动，决胜千里之外；再得日辰扶持拱卫，有百战百胜之功。所嫌的是父母发动，即使有子孙也抵挡不了它的锋芒；若入墓、无气，虽动也无伤。
  To check bandits and raiders one relies wholly on a commander going out on campaign. The Self is our general and the Other their commander; another saying is that Output is our general and our vanguard. If Output is well placed and strong the general is sure to win; with the Azure Dragon added he is a loyal and good general, with the White Tiger a brave, fierce and mighty one; with Output holding the Self, even if Pressure is present there is no harm; strong and moving, victory is decided a thousand li away; and if the Day supports and gathers round it there is the merit of a hundred battles and a hundred victories. What is disliked is Resource moving, whose edge even Output cannot withstand; if it enters its tomb and has no qi, though it moves it does no harm.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.27** 要决定输赢，先看世和日辰相克的情形。专意出征讨伐，以世为我方、应为对方：世克应，我胜；应克世，对方胜；世入墓，只宜坚壁自守，不可妄动；世旺相、应入墓，对方必折损人丁；世空，我方有难；应空，对方有伤；世在阴宫，不宜先动；世在阳象，不利于后发；世动克应，我方必进；应动克世，对方要来攻；官鬼爻克世，对方胜我；官鬼爻克应，对方要退；鬼持世，要防被围困；金、火持世或应，两家流血交战；世、应比和而都旺相，是两强对手，胜败不能立刻定下，只能凭各自的所长来断；世、应相生相合，将帅有答应议和之心，士卒没有攻战之意。
  To decide victory or defeat, first examine how the Self and the Day control each other. In an expedition of conquest the Self is us and the Other is them: if the Self controls the Other we win, if the Other controls the Self they win; with the Self in its tomb one should only hold the walls firm and guard oneself and not move rashly; with the Self strong and the Other entering its tomb they are sure to lose men; the Self void, we are in difficulty, the Other void, they are wounded; with the Self in a yin palace one should not move first, with the Self in a yang image it is not favourable to rise later; the Self moving and controlling the Other, we are sure to advance, the Other moving and controlling the Self, they mean to come; the Pressure line controlling the Self, they beat us, controlling the Other, they wish to retreat; with Pressure holding the Self guard against being besieged; with Metal or Fire holding the Self or the Other both sides shed blood in the fight; if the Self and the Other are in accord and both strong they are two strong rivals, and victory and defeat cannot yet be decided, so judge only by what each is best at; if the Self and the Other generate and combine, the commanders are minded to agree to peace and the soldiers have no intention to attack.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.28** 妻财发动，该成夺旗易帜之功；兄弟交重，要防夺粮的祸患。父母是旗帜，旺相发动必然兴兵：在内卦动，是我方要兴兵；在外卦动，是对方行军出马；克世，是要来侵犯我；克应，是回头的旌旗，对方必自行退兵。父母不动而妻财动，该有夺旗易帜的事：财在内动克外面的父母，我方必夺对方的旌旗；妻财在外动克里面的父母，对方将夺我方的旗旌。财是粮草：卦里没有财必断粮，财空粮食将要缺乏，财无气粮食不多，财动是把粮食送给贼寇；若兄弟交重，要防备劫粮夺草。
  With Wealth moving, the merit of seizing the banners and changing the standards is to be achieved; with Peer moving heavily, one must guard against the trouble of grain being seized. Resource is the banner; strong and moving it surely means troops are raised: moving inside, we wish to raise troops; moving outside, they send out their army and horses; controlling the Self, they mean to invade us; controlling the Other, it is a banner turned back, and they will surely withdraw of themselves. If Resource does not move and Wealth moves, there will be the seizing of banners and changing of standards: Wealth moving inside and controlling Resource outside, we shall surely seize their banners; Wealth moving outside and controlling Resource inside, they will seize ours. Wealth is the grain and fodder: with no Wealth in the hexagram the grain is sure to be cut off; with Wealth void grain is about to run short; with Wealth lacking qi there is not much grain; with Wealth moving, grain is sent to the enemy; and if Peer moves heavily, guard against the raiding of grain and seizing of fodder.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.29** 要听战鼓如轰雷，就看金爻发动逢空；要看炮石砖块如雨，就看土位交重逢旺。土是炮石，要旺相、落在未上发动，才可以这样说；带大杀，必遭伤害；遇退神，要防自损。金空就鸣，发动之处逢冲鸣得更厉害；发动，必然金鼓轰雷，要防战斗。金爻发动而不空亡，刀刃森森明亮如雪；内卦动，我方器械齐备；外卦动，对方兵刃整齐。金爻发动逢刑克，兵甲虽坚却伤不了人；金爻无气又逢刑克，甲胄干戈不整齐。
  To hear war drums like rolling thunder, look for the Metal line moving and meeting the void; to see cannon stones and bricks like rain, look for the Earth place moving heavily and meeting strength. Earth is the catapult stone, and only when it is strong and moves while standing on Wei can this be said; if it carries the Great Baleful Spirit one is sure to suffer harm; if it meets a Retreating spirit, guard against harming oneself. Metal rings when void, and rings all the more where it moves and meets a clash; if it moves, gongs and drums are sure to thunder, and one must guard against fighting. A Metal line moving and not Void: blades gleam in rows, bright as snow; moving inside, our weapons are fully equipped; moving outside, their blades are in good order. A Metal line moving and meeting punishment or control: the arms and armour, though sturdy, injure no one; a Metal line without qi and meeting punishment or control: helmets, armour and halberds are not in order.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.30** 木是渡河的舟船桨楫。征战免不了登山涉水，没有舟船的方便，五月怎么能渡泸水？占这件事以木为舟楫：发动、旺相，必有渡河之利；木爻受冲，船必破漏；木爻受克，船车难行；卦里没有水爻，恐怕没有水；木爻逢空，必定没有船；木爻带鬼，是贼船。
  Wood is the boat and oar for crossing the river. Campaigns cannot avoid climbing mountains and wading rivers, and without boats how could one cross the Lu River in the fifth month? In this divination Wood is the boat and oar: moving and strong, it surely brings the advantage of crossing the river; if the Wood line is clashed the boat is sure to be broken and leaking; if controlled, boats and carts are hard to move; with no Water line in the hexagram there may be no water; a Wood line meeting the void means surely no boat; a Wood line carrying Pressure is a bandits' boat.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.31** 火是系马驻军的营寨。在野外作战免不了安营下寨，即使处在山谷的险要之地，也该占卜它的吉凶。占这件事以世为主将、火爻为营寨：与世爻相生相合，那个地方得利；与世爻相克相冲，不吉；火爻带鬼，贼寇的营寨必定靠近；火爻旺相，宜扎得宽阔；无气，宜小规模结营。苍屏说：水爻带着官鬼来克我，要防劫寨和攻营，再看有气还是无气，才能斟酌说败还是赢；假如火爻旺相、水爻无气，纵然来克世也没有伤害。
  Fire is the camp where horses are tethered and the army stays. In fighting in the open one cannot avoid pitching camp and setting up a stockade, and even in the peril of mountain valleys one should divine its good or ill fortune. In this divination the Self is the commanding general and the Fire line is the camp: if it generates or combines with the Self line the place is advantageous, and if it controls or clashes with the Self line it is not auspicious; if the Fire line carries Pressure, the bandits' stockade is sure to be near; a strong Fire line suits a broad camp, and without qi a small layout. Cangping says: a Water line carrying Pressure and coming to control me, guard against the raiding of the camp and the assault on the encampment; then see whether there is qi or none, and only then can one weigh and speak of defeat or victory; suppose the Fire line is strong and the Water line has no qi: even if it comes to control the Self there is no harm.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.32** 父母加白虎、杀，在外卦兴起，不要放纵军兵出阵。子孙是我方兵卒，官鬼是对方贼徒：子衰兵卒怯懦，鬼旺贼军强大；鬼空他必覆灭，子旺我必昌盛。怕的是父母带白虎在外卦动，必克子孙，宜守不宜战，战必伤军卒。父母若持世发动，主将不能抚养士卒，上下离散，心里没有相安之道；若带凶神，要防内部生变。亡神杀，正月从亥起顺着排；劫杀见后面。
  Resource carrying the Tiger and a baleful spirit and rising in the outer trigram: do not let the troops go out to the battle line. Output is our soldiers and Pressure is the enemy's bandits: with Output weak the soldiers are timid, with Pressure strong the enemy army is mighty; with Pressure void they are sure to be destroyed, with Output strong we are sure to prosper. What is feared is Resource carrying the White Tiger and moving outside, which surely controls Output; it is right to defend and not to fight, for fighting surely wounds the troops. If Resource holds the Self and moves, the commanding general cannot nurture his troops, high and low drift apart and hearts have no way to rest easy together; if a baleful spirit is carried, guard against a change from within. The Lost Spirit baleful spirit: begin at Hai in the first month and run forward in order; the Robbery Killing is given later.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.33** 官鬼带劫杀、亡神在内卦发动，要防奸细暗中混进城来。官鬼带两种杀在内发动，要防城池之内有奸细之人；若被世克，要去探听情况，不至于泄漏；世爻无气而鬼、杀旺，军机泄漏，祸终究要来；内外的鬼爻都发动克世，必有内应外合的祸患，子孙动就一定无妨；鬼爻不带杀，为害也不凶。
  Pressure carrying the Robbery and Lost-Spirit killings and moving inside: guard against spies secretly entering the city. If Pressure carries the two baleful spirits and moves inside, guard against spies within the city; if it is controlled by the Self, one must scout out the situation so that nothing leaks; if the Self line has no qi and Pressure and the baleful spirits are strong, military secrets leak and in the end disaster comes; if the Pressure lines inside and outside both move and control the Self, there is sure to be the trouble of insiders answering outsiders, and if Output moves it is surely harmless; if the Pressure line carries no baleful spirit its harm is not fierce either.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.34** 如果遇到暗动伤身，就要遇上阴谋刺客。凡是鬼爻暗动，又加劫杀、大杀，刑克世爻，主将要防刺客；卦内如果有子孙发动来救，刺客一定被擒，好比燕太子丹派荆轲刺秦王这一类事；如果日辰冲动、伏着的鬼伤了飞爻，要防自己手下有人谋害自己；卦中没有救助，祸将及身；在鬼旺的日子，一定应验。
  If one meets a hidden stirring that harms the self, one will meet a plotting assassin. Whenever the Pressure line moves in secret, with the Robbery Killing and the Great Baleful Spirit added and punishing or controlling the Self line, the commanding general must guard against assassins; if Output in the hexagram moves to rescue, the assassin is surely caught, like the matter of Crown Prince Dan of Yan sending Jing Ke to stab the King of Qin; if the Day clashes and moves and the hidden Pressure injures the flying line, guard against someone among one's own men plotting against one; if there is no rescue in the hexagram, calamity is about to reach one; on a day when Pressure is strong it will surely be confirmed.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.11** 国事再大，没有比用兵更大的，古代圣王对它很慎重：是尽人力来应天功，也曾用蓍草来谋划（对外之事）。出师的要点，是先分清彼此，形成主客，专以世应为凭据。统论安内攘外，就用福神和官鬼来断：世是我、应是敌，喜欢世强而制住应；福是兵、鬼是寇，宜福旺而克住官鬼，朝廷谋划的胜负在这里就看得出来了。兵占先看世应：世克应就胜，应克世就负，世应比和就胜负相等；世生应的，我方怯弱；应生世的，对方退让。再看子孙是我的兵，旺相发动就勇往直前，休囚空破就不能奋进；官鬼是对方的贼寇，旺相就强横，休囚就溃散。如果世坏，即使克应、动子孙，也只能抛甲而归；如果应坏，即使克世、动官鬼，也夺旗而返。
  Of all affairs of state none is greater than the use of troops, and the sage kings of old were careful about it: one exhausts what men can do to answer Heaven's work, and has also consulted the stalks when planning matters outside. The essential in sending out troops is first to distinguish them from us, to form host and guest, and to rely wholly on the Self and the Other. In judging both pacification and repelling, decide by Output and Pressure: the Self is me and the Other is the enemy, and one is glad for the Self to be strong and to control the Other; Output is the troops and Pressure is the bandits, and it is fitting for Output to be thriving and to control Pressure, and the court's planning of victory and defeat is seen in this. In divining about the army, first look at the Self and the Other: if the Self controls the Other one wins, if the Other controls the Self one loses, and if the Self and the Other are in accord, victory and defeat are even; if the Self generates the Other, my side is timid; if the Other generates the Self, the other side gives way. Then look at Output as my troops: if thriving and moving they press bravely forward, if resting, imprisoned, Void or broken they cannot press on; Pressure is the other side's bandits: if thriving they are violent and overbearing, if resting or imprisoned they are routed. If the Self is damaged, even if one controls the Other and moves Output one can only drag one's armour and come home; if the Other is damaged, even if it controls the Self and one moves Pressure, one still seizes their banner and returns.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.12** 问立营下寨，安危凭官鬼来断：内外卦不要入墓、绝、反吟、伏吟、空、破，犯了就军帐不安；内外两象不要冲击，犯了就将士离心，不可驻扎。占兵驻扎，最忌官鬼发动，恐怕被敌人劫掠；最厌内卦犯疾，以及六冲，上下乖离。
  In asking about setting up camp, safety or danger is judged by Pressure: the inner and outer must not meet tomb, extinction, Reversed Chant, Hidden Chant, Void or break, for if they do the commander's tent is uneasy; the two images must not clash, for if they do officers and men are at odds and the troops cannot be quartered. In divining about the quartering of troops, Pressure moving is most to be avoided, for fear of raiding by the enemy, and what is most disliked is the inner trigram being sick, and a six-clash, with high and low estranged.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.13** 问攻守城池、兵走水路还是陆路、作战的利钝快慢，以及遣将用计，志在必胜，都以世应来占。如果朝廷拜将，以及选人用间，这是三军的大任，必须仁、勇、通达应变，应当用宾位的应来辨别。以上六种占问，都是论胜负，只忌世坏和应克世，而随官入墓、助鬼伤身，主有覆军的失败。只有用间不同，类似嘱托：应爻空、破、散、绝，机谋就泄漏；应爻克冲世位，就是反间叛奸的人。君王用蓍草任命将领，也像用间的办法。古来用兵，没有比求将更大的，没有比用间更难的：或者内部结为骨肉，或者情谊兼有师友，所以用宾位的应。
  In asking about attacking or defending a city, whether the troops go by water or land, the speed and advantage of a battle, and sending out commanders and using stratagems, with the aim of certain victory, all are divined by the Self and the Other. If the court appoints a general, or a man is chosen to act as a spy, this is the great charge of the three armies, and he must be benevolent, brave and flexible; it should be judged by the Other, the guest's position. In these six kinds of question, victory and defeat are discussed, and only the Self being damaged and the Other controlling the Self are feared, while following the star of office into the tomb and helping Pressure to injure the Body mean the defeat of the army. Only using spies is different, being like entrusting a task: if the Other is Void, broken, scattered or extinct, the scheme leaks out; if the Other clashes with or controls the Self's place, he is a man who turns traitor. When the sovereign appoints a general by the stalks, it is also like the method of using spies. In the use of troops of old, nothing was greater than seeking a general and nothing harder than using spies: either one binds them as kin from within, or the feeling is that of teacher and friend, and so the Other, the guest's position, is used.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.14** 问守、问战，要以胜负分清彼此；问款（议和）、问降，要以忠奸来察看应爻。时日利于战，得时就对我有利；防守没有忧虑，官鬼一动就有来侵犯的。应克世是蚕食。得到应坏、世克应，就成百战百胜的功劳；世坏、应克世，就要忍受三战三怯的耻辱。敌人降于我，或我向敌人议和，都要察看应爻的真假，忌讳应克世。如果是卜日时的占：比如秋天用兵，就利于金爻持世；亥日出师，就利于火爻属应。
  In asking about defence and about battle, distinguish the two sides by victory and defeat; in asking about truce and surrender, examine the Other line by loyalty or treachery. If the day and hour favour battle, getting the right time benefits me; if defence is free from worry, Pressure moving means an invader is coming; the Other controlling the Self is nibbling away like a silkworm. If the Other is broken and the Self controls the Other, the merit of a hundred battles and a hundred victories is accomplished; if the Self is damaged and the Other controls the Self, one must bear the shame of three battles and three times of cowardice. When an enemy surrenders to me, or I seek a truce with an enemy, in both cases one examines whether the Other is true or false, and the Other controlling the Self is taboo. In divining the day and hour, for example if one uses troops in autumn it is favourable for a Metal line to hold the Self, and if one sends out the army on a Hai day it is favourable for a Fire line to belong to the Other.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.15** 防备刺客，以官鬼为刺客，厌恶它发动；去行刺，以应为被刺的人，喜欢它有疾（坏）。如果随墓、助伤，防刺就大凶；官鬼动、官鬼克世也凶，只有空、破才是吉。行刺时应坏，敌人命数尽了，可以得志；或者世坏、随墓助伤，敌人虽然灭了，我也跟着亡；或者官鬼兴旺而世旺，敌人虽然来刺我，我的命数还没有终结。吉里藏着凶，凶中有吉，要分辨。
  In guarding against assassins, Pressure is the assassin and its moving is disliked; in carrying out an assassination, the Other is the man to be stabbed and it is liked to be sick. If one follows into the tomb or helps Pressure to injure, guarding against an assassin is greatly ominous; Pressure moving, or Pressure controlling the Self, is also ominous, and only Void or break is auspicious. In carrying out an assassination, if the Other is damaged the enemy's number is used up and one can achieve one's aim; or if the Self is damaged and follows into the tomb or helps Pressure to injure, although the enemy is wiped out, I too die with him; or if Pressure flourishes and the Self is thriving, although the enemy strikes at me my number is not yet at its end. Ill lies hidden within good, and good within ill, so one must tell them apart.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.16** 用内应，如同用间；用火攻，如同用计；官鬼一动，谋划就败了。取粮于敌、截敌军粮饷于途中，财爻旺相，计谋就得手。内应可以委任，办法如同用间，只是不可官鬼发动，发动就功劳未成而先败；用火的办法如同用计，务求取胜，但水、火是百姓最大的祸害，也忌官鬼发动，否则谋划泄漏、祸患流布。
  Using an inside collaborator is like using a spy, and using fire is like using a stratagem; if Pressure moves, the scheme fails. In taking provisions from the enemy and intercepting the pay and grain on the road, if Wealth is thriving the plan succeeds. An inside collaborator can be appointed, and the method is like that of using spies, but Pressure must not move, for if it does the merit is not yet won and one is first defeated; the method of using fire is like that of a stratagem, and one must aim at victory, but water and fire are the greatest harms to the people, and Pressure moving is also to be avoided, otherwise the scheme leaks out and calamity spreads.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.17** 敌寇有据点的，用应来看；敌寇没有据点的，用官鬼来看。所以两军对阵，按世应的相克相生来断；四野围剿，则衡量官鬼和福神的强弱。如果敌人占据城郭，形成两军对阵之势，就考察世应的生克虚实来断胜负；如果是流窜无定的流寇，必须四面围剿消灭，就论福神与官鬼的强弱。
  If the bandits hold a base, look at the Other; if they have no base, look at Pressure. So when two armies face each other, follow the generation and control of the Self and the Other; when the countryside is being swept clean on all sides, weigh the strength of Pressure and Output. If the enemy holds a walled city and forms the posture of two armies, examine the generation, control, emptiness and fullness of the Self and the Other to decide victory and defeat; if they are roving bandits with no fixed place and must be wiped out from all four directions, then discuss the strength of Output and Pressure.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.18** 发檄文求救兵，应爻有疾（坏）就不救，应克世就是不轨。上书求济，卦是六冲、五疑，君象冲克世爻，就不允济。同仇相援，就如同用兵来断。应爻空、破、散，就没有周济急难的情意；应克世爻，不只是不救援，还有别的奸谋。五爻空、破、散、绝，叫做五疑，因为五爻是君，冲克世爻，恐怕逆耳不被允准。对方有困难、我去救援，断法如同用兵。
  In sending a dispatch by fast courier to ask for relief troops, if the Other is sick no help comes, and if it controls the Self it is disloyal. In petitioning in writing for aid, with a six-clash and the five doubts, if the sovereign image clashes with or controls the Self line, the aid is not granted. Mutual help between those who share a hatred is judged like using troops. If the Other is Void, broken or scattered, there is no feeling for relieving distress; if the Other line controls the Self, there is not only no help but some other treachery. If the fifth line is Void, broken, scattered or extinct, it is called the five doubts, because the fifth line is the sovereign and if it clashes with or controls the Self line, I fear what I say jars on his ear and is not granted. If the other side is in difficulty and I go to its rescue, it is judged like using troops.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.19** 占城池，担心被攻破，官鬼动、官鬼克世就危险；占敌人来的日期，应克应、应动就快；占军队被围困，世入墓、世受伤就凶。如果担心城被攻破，不用看父母，反而专看官鬼：官鬼发动克世就危险。
  In divining about a city, if one fears it will be broken, Pressure moving or Pressure controlling the Self is dangerous; in divining the date the enemy will come, the Other controlling or the Other moving means quickly; in divining about an army that has been besieged, the Self entering the tomb or being injured is ominous. If one fears a city will be broken, one does not look at Resource but looks only at Pressure: Pressure moving and controlling the Self is dangerous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.20** 兵法各家说法不一，但象和理只有一个：主帅（世）空，中军就折；贼首（应）空，敌人就逃。生我的动爻，喜它是来援的兵；克我的动爻，厌它是帮敌人的。还有一种：对方虽败而打不垮他，是因为他有生扶；我方虽伤而伤不到根本，是因为我有扶持。全盛而转衰，将倒而复起，进退反复，全看爻神的动静。
  The arts of war differ from school to school, but the image and the principle are one: if the commander (the Self) is Void the centre army is broken, and if the bandit chief (the Other) is Void the enemy flees. A moving line that generates me is welcome, being relief troops; one that controls me is hated, for it helps the enemy. There is also the case where the other side, though beaten, cannot be overcome, because it has generation, and where I, though hurt, am not harmed, because I have support. Fully thriving and then declining, about to fall and then rising, advancing and retreating back and forth: it all depends on the stirring and stilling of the lines.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.21** 官鬼是敌，有五件事不可：不可得时得势，怕它妨害成功；不可遇长生，怕它难以消灭；不可临胎、养，怕它正在兴起；不可暗动，再兼伏着的鬼克世，怕它谋划行刺；不可内外卦都有鬼克世，怕它内应外通。
  Pressure is the enemy, and there are five things it must not do: it must not obtain season and momentum, for fear it harms the success; it must not meet Birth, for fear it is hard to destroy; it must not stand at Foetus or Nourishment, for fear it is just rising; it must not move in secret, nor, with a hidden Pressure line, control the Self, for fear of a plot to stab; and it must not control the Self from both the inner and the outer trigram, for fear of collusion between inside and outside.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.22** 要看贼人布的什么策、用什么兵、走哪条路，我又该怎样应对、怎样克制、怎样夺取，全凭验五行八卦的消息。所以官鬼动在内卦，防间谋；发在外卦，虑野战；动在乾宫，伏兵在庙里；兴在巽卦，藏匿的随从在林中；坤宫的鬼，戒备受降；震宫的鬼，不该当作叛军已退；艮宫忌被困在冈陵；离宫忧市井受攻；兑宫不要信他的说辞；坎宫不要听他的诱语。书里自注：鬼显现之处，就是敌人谋划之处：坤是顺，鬼动则降不是真降；震是振，鬼摇则退下的叛军必变；兑是口、是悦，则说话必诈；坎是耳、是陷，则卑下的言辞必有谋。其余类推。
  To see what plan the bandits lay, what troops they use, what road they advance by, and how I shall answer, control and take them, one tests it all by the news of the five phases and the eight trigrams. So Pressure moving in the inner trigram, guard against spies and schemes; Pressure issuing in the outer trigram, take thought for a battle in the field; Pressure moving in the palace of Qian, troops lie in ambush in the temple; Pressure rising in the trigram Xun, followers lie hidden in the woods; Pressure in Kun, be on guard against accepting surrender; Pressure in Zhen, do not take the rebels as having withdrawn; Gen, beware of being trapped on hills and mounds; Li, fear an attack in the market towns; Dui, do not believe their talk; Kan, do not listen to their enticing words. The book's own gloss: where Pressure appears is what the enemy is planning. Kun is yielding, so when Pressure moves their surrender is not a real surrender; Zhen is shaking, so when Pressure sways the rebels who stood down will surely change; Dui is mouth and delight, so the talk must be deceit; Kan is ear and pit, so humble words must hide a scheme. The rest follows by analogy.
