- **LY.CAREER.YM.23** 火鬼动，不是劫寨，是谋划放火；水鬼动，不是水淹，是计谋用沙袋壅水；金是流矢之凶，木是胶舟之诈，「款」（疑是土）是平原之战；起于离的从南边来，发于坎的从北边犯；艮、坤的敌人出自山冈土阜，震、巽的寇从茂盛的树林里来；乾是用老弱来骗我军士的心，兑是用子女来惑我兵卒的志。取象取义，全凭用心去测度官鬼的情形。
  Fire Pressure moving is not a raid on the camp but a plan to set fires; Water Pressure moving is not a flood but a scheme of sandbags; Metal is the harm of flying arrows, Wood the deceit of a boat held fast, and 'Kuan' (presumably Earth) a battle on the open plain; what rises in Li comes from the south, what issues from Kan attacks from the north; with Gen and Kun the enemy comes out of hills and mounds, with Zhen and Xun the raiders come out of thick woods; Qian uses the old and weak to deceive my soldiers' hearts, and Dui uses sons and daughters to confuse my troops' will. Taking the image and taking the meaning all depends on gauging, with a deep mind, the condition of Pressure.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.24** 子孙动在水，利于水战；福神动在火，利于火攻；属土的靠炮石取胜，属金的靠弓箭取胜，属木的靠舟车取胜。
  Output moving in Water favours a battle on the water; Output, the fortune spirit, moving in Fire favours an attack by fire; Earth wins by catapult stones, Metal wins by bow and arrow, Wood wins by boats and carts.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.25** 子孙动在坤宫，有屯驻的兵来相助；福神兴在震卦，有壮士来归；乾是老成人的良谋，艮是少年的秘计；坎宫应当对外示弱，离宫应当对内示虚，兑宫应当用卑下的言辞来修饰，巽宫应当勇于进取。这些都是子孙发动才能取胜。
  Output moving in the palace of Kun, stationed troops come to help; Output rising in the trigram Zhen, brave men come over to me; Qian is the good plan of an elder, Gen the secret scheme of a youth; in Kan one should show weakness outwardly, in Li one should show emptiness inwardly, in Dui one should dress up one's words humbly, in Xun one should be bold in pressing forward. All of these win when Output moves.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.26** 父母是彼此双方的机关（计策，也是军师），妻财是双方的兵器甲胄，弟兄是双方的埋伏，鬼是贼众，子是将士；再考察生克衰旺，来分辨好坏。
  Resource is the machinery of both sides (the plans, and also the army's counsellor), Wealth is the weapons and armour of both sides, Peer is the ambush of both sides, Pressure is the bandit host and Output is the officers and men; then one examines generation, control, decline and thriving to tell the fine from the bad.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.27** 所以鬼（官鬼）临在吉神上，即使是吉也不吉；福（子孙）落在凶煞上，即使是凶也不凶。还有：子孙化官鬼的，是叛变；官鬼化子孙的，是投降；官鬼伏在飞爻下的，随灭随起；内外卦都有子孙的，屡战屡捷。阳是正兵，阴是奇兵，用子孙、官鬼来分辨它们的利弊。合就是结，冲就是绝；静就该守，动就该攻。
  So when Pressure stands on an auspicious spirit it is not auspicious even though it seems so, and when Output sits on an evil star it is not ominous even though it seems so. Moreover, Output turning into Pressure is mutiny, and Pressure turning into Output is surrender; Pressure hidden beneath a flying line wanes as it rises and rises as it wanes, and Output in both inner and outer trigram means repeated battles and repeated victories. Yang is the regular troops and yin the irregular troops, and Output and Pressure tell where the advantage lies. Combining is binding and clashing is severing; when still, one should hold, and when moving, one should attack.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.38** 投到将帅麾下效力：本卦六冲、鬼动，就不被录用；官爻空、破、散、绝，就不被任用；为名，官旺才好；为利，财、官并重；世不可病，官不可被克。入伍从军，以身体性命为要：世象强而好，冒着锋镝也能成功；官爻旺相，从行伍中被提拔、拜爵；官爻克世、助伤随墓，都不宜见到。
  In going to serve under a commander's banner: if the original hexagram is a six-clash or Pressure moves, one is not taken on; if the Pressure line is Void, broken, scattered or extinct, one is not employed; for the sake of a name, thriving Pressure is good; for profit, Wealth and Pressure count equally; the Self must not be sick, and Pressure must not be controlled. In enlisting in the ranks, body and life are what matter: if the Self image is strong and good, one braves blades and arrows and succeeds; if the Pressure line is thriving, one is raised from the ranks and granted rank; the Pressure line controlling the Self, helping the injury and following into the tomb should not be seen.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.50** 征战占总述：用兵凶险，打仗危险，关系人命生死和国家安危，所以要慎重。看八卦来知道方向；看世爻、应爻来知道彼此；看六亲来知道将帅和士卒；看五行来知道器械用具；看日辰来知道我方兵士的好坏。
  Overview of divining war. Arms are ill-omened and war is perilous, bearing on life and death and the safety of the state, so one must be careful. Look at the eight trigrams to know the direction; at the Self and Other to know them and us; at the six kin to know the commanders and the troops; at the five elements to know the arms and gear; and at the Day to know whether my soldiers are good or bad.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.51** 方向：内卦是我方的营寨，外卦是对方的军营。内卦旺相的，吉；胎、没次一等；死、囚、休、废的，凶。八卦的旺、相、胎、没、死、囚、休、废随八个节气排列：立春后艮旺、震相、巽胎、离没、坤死、兑囚、乾休、坎废；春分后震旺、巽相、离胎、坤没、兑死、乾囚、坎休、艮废；立夏后巽旺、离相、坤胎、兑没、乾死、坎囚、艮休、震废；夏至后离旺、坤相、兑胎、乾没、坎死、艮囚、震休、巽废；立秋后坤旺、兑相、乾胎、坎没、艮死、震囚、巽休、离废；秋分后兑旺、乾相、坎胎、艮没、震死、巽囚、离休、坤废；立冬后乾旺、坎相、艮胎、震没、巽死、离囚、坤休、兑废；冬至后坎旺、艮相、震胎、巽没、离死、坤囚、兑休、乾废。
  Direction. The inner trigram is my camp and the outer trigram is the enemy's. If the inner trigram is thriving or assisting it is auspicious; Tai and Mo come next; if dead, imprisoned, resting or abandoned it is ominous. The eight states of the trigrams run through the eight solar terms: after the Start of Spring Gen thrives, Zhen assists, Xun is Tai, Li is Mo, Kun is dead, Dui imprisoned, Qian resting and Kan abandoned; after the Spring Equinox Zhen thrives, Xun assists, Li is Tai, Kun is Mo, Dui is dead, Qian imprisoned, Kan resting and Gen abandoned; after the Start of Summer Xun thrives, Li assists, Kun is Tai, Dui is Mo, Qian is dead, Kan imprisoned, Gen resting and Zhen abandoned; after the Summer Solstice Li thrives, Kun assists, Dui is Tai, Qian is Mo, Kan is dead, Gen imprisoned, Zhen resting and Xun abandoned; after the Start of Autumn Kun thrives, Dui assists, Qian is Tai, Kan is Mo, Gen is dead, Zhen imprisoned, Xun resting and Li abandoned; after the Autumn Equinox Dui thrives, Qian assists, Kan is Tai, Gen is Mo, Zhen is dead, Xun imprisoned, Li resting and Kun abandoned; after the Start of Winter Qian thrives, Kan assists, Gen is Tai, Zhen is Mo, Xun is dead, Li imprisoned, Kun resting and Dui abandoned; after the Winter Solstice Kan thrives, Gen assists, Zhen is Tai, Xun is Mo, Li is dead, Kun imprisoned, Dui resting and Qian abandoned.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.52** 驻扎的方位：青龙、财、福神旺相发动的那一宫，可以驻扎；白虎、官鬼、飞廉、大杀等凶神和劫杀旺相发动的那一宫，不可驻扎；被日辰和动爻刑、害、克、破，或落旬空、月破，或发动变成死、墓、绝、胎的那一宫，不可驻扎；世爻处在死、墓、绝、胎的那一宫，不可驻扎。
  Where to camp. A palace where the Azure Dragon, Wealth and the fortune spirit are thriving and moving can be occupied; a palace where the White Tiger, Pressure, Feilian, great baleful spirits, other ill spirits and Robbery are thriving and moving cannot be occupied; a palace punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the Day and a moving line, or falling into decade Void or Month-break, or moving and turning into death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, cannot be occupied; a palace in which the Self is in death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage cannot be occupied.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.53** 出战还是固守，以及往哪个方位：内卦旺相、外卦衰弱的，可以出战；内卦衰弱、外卦旺相的，宜固守；内卦克外卦的，可以出战；外卦克内卦的，宜固守。内宫所在的方位，可战可攻；世爻生旺的方位，可遁走可躲避；应爻、官鬼爻生旺的方位，适宜避开对方的锋芒；败、死、绝的方位，适宜打击对方松懈之处；墓、胎的方位，适宜提防对方的埋伏。
  To fight or hold, and in which direction. If the inner trigram is thriving and the outer weak, one can go out and fight; if the inner is weak and the outer thriving, hold the line; if the inner controls the outer one can fight; if the outer controls the inner, hold the line. In the direction of the inner palace one can fight and attack; in the direction where the Self is born or thriving one can flee or evade; in the direction where the Other and the Pressure line are born or thriving, avoid their sharp edge; in the direction of spent, dead or extinct, strike where they are slack; in the direction of tomb or womb, guard against their ambush.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.54** 彼此强弱：世爻是我方主帅，应爻是对方将领。世爻落空，取子孙为用神；应爻落空，取官鬼为用神。世旺应衰，我强彼弱，宜进攻；世衰应旺，我弱彼强，宜固守。世克应，可以打；应克世，宜固守，而且要防贼来。世爻是阳爻而发动的，宜出兵；世爻是阴爻而安静的，宜坚壁固守。世爻落空，我方有难；应爻落空，对方必受损伤；世、应都落空，两方将领休兵。
  Strength of them and us. The Self is my commander and the Other is the enemy's general. If the Self is Void, take Output as the Subject; if the Other is Void, take Pressure as the Subject. If the Self is thriving and the Other weak, I am strong and they weak, and one should attack; if the Self is weak and the Other thriving, hold the line. If the Self controls the Other, one can fight; if the Other controls the Self, hold the line and guard against the bandits' coming. If the Self is yang and moves, march out; if yin and still, hold the walls. If the Self is Void, I am in trouble; if the Other is Void, they will surely be hurt; if both are Void, the two commanders rest their troops.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.55** 主将的才具和进退：世爻入墓、入胎的，宜退兵；世、应都旺相，或相生相合比和的，必不交锋，打起来也胜负难分；世、应持金爻、火爻的官鬼，旺相发动，两败俱伤；世爻持白虎、福神旺相的，决胜千里之外，再加日辰生扶，百战百胜；世爻临青龙、福神，再加将星发动，是良将；临白虎、福神是猛将；临勾陈、福神是秘密派人擒贼；临朱雀、福神是号令严明；临螣蛇、福神是变化莫测；临玄武、福神是善用囊沙、背水的计谋，或者巧于偷袭劫营；世爻加往亡、归忌、受死、大败、四废、月厌、飞廉、大杀、亡神、劫杀而发动的，宜坚壁不出，出则必败。
  The commander's talent, and advance or retreat. If the Self enters tomb or womb, it is fit to withdraw; if the Self and Other are both thriving or generate, combine or are in harmony, they will not cross swords and a fight would be hard to decide; if the Self and Other hold Metal or Fire Pressure lines, thriving and moving, both sides are badly hurt; if the Self holds the White Tiger and the fortune spirit, thriving, he wins decisively a thousand miles away, and with the Day generating or supporting, wins a hundred battles out of a hundred; if the Self sits on the Azure Dragon and the fortune spirit with the General star added and moving, he is a good general; on the White Tiger, a fierce general; on Hook Earth, one who secretly sends men to seize the bandits; on the Vermilion Bird, one whose orders are strict and clear; on the Coiling Snake, one whose changes cannot be fathomed; on the Dark Tortoise, one skilled in the stratagems of sandbags and fighting with the water behind, or clever at raiding camps; if the Self adds Wangwang, Guiji, Shousi, Dabai, the Four Wastes, Yueyan, Feilian, great baleful spirits, the Lost Spirit and Robbery and moves, hold the walls and do not go out, for going out means defeat.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.56** 官鬼看敌情：官鬼克世爻，对方胜；官鬼克应爻，他想退兵；官鬼持世爻，防围困。官鬼暗动，加大杀、劫杀、刑、刃克世爻的，防刺客，遇福神发动，刺客必被擒。日辰冲克世爻下伏的官鬼、刑害它的飞神的，防下面的人谋害我。没有子孙发动来救，官鬼生旺的日子必定见祸。世爻衰弱而官鬼和凶煞旺相发动的，军机泄露。内外两个官鬼发动克世爻的，防内应外合；世爻被日辰、动爻刑、害、克、冲的，防奇兵冲击。世爻变成空、死、墓、绝、胎的，主将不逃走就战死。世、应都旺相的，看日辰生合刑害克破哪一爻，来决定胜负。
  Reading the enemy by Pressure. If Pressure controls the Self, they win; if Pressure controls the Other, they want to withdraw; if Pressure holds the Self, guard against encirclement. If Pressure moves in secret, with a great baleful spirit, Robbery, punishment and the blade added, and controls the Self, guard against assassins, and if the fortune spirit moves, the assassin will surely be caught. If the Day clashes with or controls the Pressure line hidden under the Self, or punishes or harms its flying spirit, guard against those below plotting against me. With no Output moving to rescue, on the day Pressure is born or thriving there will surely be disaster. If the Self is weak and Pressure and baleful spirits are thriving and moving, military secrets leak. If both Pressure lines, inside and outside, move to control the Self, guard against collusion between inside and outside; if the Self is punished, harmed, controlled or clashed by the Day or a moving line, guard against a surprise force charging in. If the Self turns into Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, the commander either flees or dies. If the Self and Other are both thriving, see which line the Day generates, combines with, punishes, harms, controls or breaks, to decide victory.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.57** 六亲配军中的人和物，以及军师：父爻是军师，也是旌旗；财爻是粮草，子孙是先锋，兄弟是埋伏，官鬼是敌人。父爻下面伏着子孙，众将慑服，擒纵如意；伏着财爻，器械齐备粮饷充足、谋略周全，但要防左右的伏兵；伏着兄爻，军师贪财好色、狎侮身边的人；伏着官鬼，智谋短、计策拙，一行动就出疏漏。父爻临衰、墓，军师年老；临生旺，军师年轻力壮；临胎、养，不威严持重；临刑、害，不体恤众人。父爻旺相在外卦发动，对方兴兵；在内卦发动，我方出师；带白虎、凶煞在外卦发动，作战失利；在外卦发动克世爻，他来侵犯；克应爻，他自己退去。世爻持父爻发动，主将不爱惜士卒，再加大杀、劫杀发动，不爱惜士卒还要防兵变。
  Assigning the six kin to people and things in the army, and the strategist. The Resource line is the military adviser and also the banners; Wealth is provisions, Output the vanguard, Peer the ambush, and Pressure the enemy. If Output lies hidden under Resource, the generals are cowed and capture and release go as wished; if Wealth, arms are ready and rations plentiful and plans complete, but guard against hidden troops on both sides; if Peer, the adviser is greedy for money and women and treats those around him with contempt; if Pressure, his wit is short and his schemes clumsy, and any move shows a flaw. If Resource sits on weak or tomb, the adviser is old; on born or thriving, young and strong; on Tai or Yang, not awe-inspiring; on punishment or harm, he has no care for the many. If Resource is thriving and moves in the outer trigram, the enemy raises troops; in the inner trigram, I send out troops; with the Tiger and baleful spirits moving outside, the battle goes badly; moving outside to control the Self, they come to invade; controlling the Other, they withdraw of themselves. If the Self holds Resource and it moves, the commander does not spare his men, and with great baleful spirits and Robbery added and moving, he spares not his men and one must guard against mutiny.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.58** 旌旗：父爻旺相，旗帜就新；带青龙、朱雀，画的色彩鲜艳；父爻衰、墓，旗帜就旧，加勾陈、螣蛇、白虎、玄武，就破旧肮脏没有颜色。卦里没有父爻，或父爻落空、绝的，旗帜不齐备；父爻被刑、害、克、破的，宜改换旗帜。父爻安静、财爻发动的，功成之后拔旗（收兵）；内卦的财爻发动克外卦的父爻，我夺对方的旗；外卦的财爻发动克内卦的父爻，对方夺我的旗。
  The banners. If Resource is thriving the banners are new, and with the Azure Dragon and Vermilion Bird the colours are bright and fresh; if Resource is weak or in tomb the banners are old, and with Hook Earth, the Coiling Snake, the White Tiger and the Dark Tortoise they are tattered, dirty and colourless. If the hexagram has no Resource line, or it is Void or extinct, the banners are incomplete; if Resource is punished, harmed, controlled or broken, it is fit to change the banners. If Resource is still and Wealth moves, success is won and the banners are pulled up; if inner Wealth moves to control outer Resource, I seize their banners; if outer Wealth moves to control inner Resource, they seize mine.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.59** 粮草：财爻旺相，粮草丰足；衰败，没有余粮；临墓、胎，粮草发不下来；落空、绝，或卦里没有财爻，粮草断绝。子孙变出财爻的，向邻国借粮（呼庚）；财爻加玄武发动的，下面的人偷窃；内卦的兄爻发动克财爻的，督办粮饷的人侵吞中饱；外卦的兄爻发动克财爻的，敌人劫掠。
  Provisions. If Wealth is thriving, provisions are ample; if spent, there is no surplus; if on tomb or womb, provisions are not issued; if Void or extinct, or the hexagram has no Wealth, provisions are cut off. If Output turns into Wealth, one begs grain from a neighbouring state; if Wealth carries the Dark Tortoise and moves, those below steal; if an inner Peer line moves to control Wealth, the official in charge of supplies embezzles; if an outer Peer line moves to control Wealth, the enemy plunders.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.60** 先锋：子孙旺相，先锋有谋有勇；衰、墓，懦弱无能；落空、绝，先锋有难；变成败、死、墓、绝、胎，必定战败。子孙带大杀发动克应爻的，先锋冲突取胜；子孙旺相却受刑、害、克、冲的，不要轻易出战；子孙衰弱而得日辰、动爻生合的，以多取胜；子孙旺相发动克众多官鬼的，以少取胜；子孙变兄爻，将弱而成功；子孙变父爻，兵骄而致败。世爻下伏着子孙并合应爻的，先锋投降敌人；再加日辰生子孙的，必定被人唆使怂恿而投降仇敌。
  The vanguard. If Output is thriving, the vanguard has both wit and courage; if weak or in tomb, timid and incapable; if Void or extinct, the vanguard is in trouble; if it turns into spent, dead, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, it surely loses the battle. If Output carrying a great baleful spirit moves to control the Other, the vanguard wins by a charge; if Output is thriving but punished, harmed, controlled or clashed, do not go out lightly; if Output is weak but generated or combined by the Day or a moving line, win with numbers; if it is thriving and moves to control many Pressure lines, win with few; if Output turns into Peer, the commander is weak but succeeds; if Output turns into Resource, the troops grow proud and bring defeat. If Output lies hidden under the Self and combines with the Other, the vanguard goes over to the enemy; with the Day generating Output as well, he will surely be egged on to surrender to his enemy.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.61** 兄弟看埋伏：兄爻旺相发动，有埋伏；来冲世爻、冲子孙的，防劫营；兄爻暗动来冲的，防奸细。想知道哪天来进犯，就看兄爻生旺的日子、时辰。
  Peer and ambush. If the Peer line is thriving and moving, there is an ambush; if it comes to clash with the Self or Output, guard against a raid on the camp; if it moves in secret and comes to clash, guard against spies. To know on what day they will attack, look at the day or hour when the Peer line is born or thriving.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.62** 官鬼看敌人：官鬼衰弱安静，敌人胆怯；旺相发动，敌势强盛；再加财爻发动帮它，敌人诡计多端、变诈百出。官鬼落空，敌人必定灭亡；内外卦都没有官鬼，以及官鬼临墓、绝、胎的，敌人必定逃走；官鬼衰弱但得到扶助，不要追穷寇。官鬼带亡神、劫杀在内卦发动的，防奸细；如果世爻旺相发动、伤克官鬼，奸细必被擒获。世爻和官鬼发动、生合应爻的，因为没有救援而投降；卦里没有财爻，而应爻克世爻的，因为粮尽而毙命。
  Pressure and the enemy. If Pressure is weak and still, the enemy is timid; if thriving and moving, the enemy's force is strong, and with Wealth moving to help it, the enemy has many tricks and a hundred deceits. If Pressure is Void, the enemy is surely destroyed; if there is no Pressure in the inner or outer trigram, or Pressure sits on tomb, extinction or the womb stage, the enemy surely slips away; if Pressure is weak but supported, do not hound a desperate foe. If Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves in the inner trigram, guard against spies; if the Self is thriving and moving and injures or controls Pressure, the spy is surely caught. If the Self and Pressure move and generate or combine with the Other, he surrenders for lack of relief; if the hexagram has no Wealth and the Other controls the Self, he dies when the food runs out.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.63** 五行配兵器和舟船：木是舟船，火是营寨，土是炮石，金是刀刃，水是水源泉水。木爻旺相发动，利于水军；木旺，船大；木衰，船小；木落空、绝，没有船。船的各部分：青龙是船舵，在左；白虎是桅杆、帆、锚链，在右；勾陈是平基、跳板，在中舱；螣蛇是缆索；朱雀是烟灶，在船头；玄武是挠头、挡浪，在船尾。旺相，船新；休囚，船旧；落空，没有；被冲，破漏；受刑、害、克，以及木爻持发动的官鬼、木爻下伏着官鬼的，必定损伤。木下伏土爻官鬼，船搁浅；伏火爻官鬼，船干燥开裂；伏金爻官鬼，钉眼处损坏。又，木爻带官鬼的，是贼船。
  The five elements and arms and boats. Wood is boats and oars, Fire is the camp, Earth is catapult stones, Metal is blades, and Water is springs. If Wood is thriving and moving, it favours the navy; thriving Wood means a big boat, weak Wood a small boat, Void or extinct Wood no boat. The parts of the boat: the Azure Dragon is the rudder, on the left; the White Tiger is the mast, sail and anchor chain, on the right; Hook Earth is the flat base and gangplank, the middle hold; the Coiling Snake is the cables; the Vermilion Bird is the smoke and stove, at the bow; the Dark Tortoise is the scull-heads and wave-guard, at the stern. Thriving, the boat is new; resting or trapped, old; Void, there is none; clashed, it leaks; punished, harmed or controlled, or if Wood holds a moving Pressure line, or Pressure lies hidden under Wood, it is surely damaged. Earth Pressure hidden under it, the boat runs into the shallows; Fire Pressure, it dries and splits; Metal Pressure, the nail holes are damaged. Again, Wood carrying Pressure is the enemy's boat.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.64** 火看营寨：火爻发动生合世爻，立营得地利；发动冲、克、刑、害世爻，立营失地利。火爻官鬼持世发动的，被贼围困；火爻官鬼贴近世爻的，贼寨离得近。火旺，营大；火衰，营小；火落空，营危险；火绝，没有出路；火发动，利于迁营；火爻官鬼伤世爻，防劫营。加青龙，贼从左边来；加白虎，从右边来；朱雀从前面来；玄武从后面来；勾陈、螣蛇从四个角落来。
  Fire and the camp. If Fire moves to generate or combine with the Self, setting up camp gains the advantage of ground; if it moves to clash with, control, punish or harm the Self, the camp loses the advantage of ground. If a Fire Pressure line holds the Self and moves, one is besieged by bandits; if a Fire Pressure line is next to the Self, the bandits' camp is near. Thriving Fire means a big camp, weak Fire a small camp; Void Fire means a camp in danger; extinct Fire means no way out; if Fire moves, it favours moving camp; if a Fire Pressure line injures the Self, guard against a raid on the camp. With the Azure Dragon added, the bandits come from the left; with the White Tiger, from the right; the Bird, from the front; the Tortoise, from behind; Hook Earth and the Snake, from the four corners.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.65** 土看炮石和陷阱：阳土爻的官鬼旺相发动，带大杀来刑、害、克、冲世爻的，防炮石；阴土爻的官鬼旺相发动，带阴杀来冲、刑、克、害世爻的，防陷阱。世爻、子孙爻临阳土而伤应爻、伤官鬼的，利于使用炮石；临阴土而伤应爻与官鬼的，利于使用机关陷阱。应当在应爻、官鬼爻死、墓、绝、胎的右侧设下伏兵。
  Earth, catapult stones and pits. If a yang Earth Pressure line is thriving and moving, with a great baleful spirit, and comes to punish, harm, control or clash with the Self, guard against catapult stones; if a yin Earth Pressure line is thriving and moving, with a yin baleful spirit, and comes to clash with, punish, control or harm the Self, guard against pits. If the Self and Output sit on yang Earth and injure the Other and Pressure, it is favourable to use catapult stones; if on yin Earth and injuring the Other and Pressure, favourable to use mechanisms and pitfalls. One should set an ambush to the right of where the Other and Pressure lines are in death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.66** 金看兵甲：金爻旺相，兵器铠甲新，加青龙、朱雀、白虎的，光亮耀眼；金爻衰弱，兵甲旧，加勾陈、螣蛇、玄武的，钝坏没有光彩；金临胎、墓，弓入囊、戈收起（休兵）；金发动，将要打仗；金发动带刑、刃的，打起来必定伤人；金落空而发动，战鼓齐鸣，兵刃已经相接。
  Metal and arms. If Metal is thriving, weapons and armour are new, and with the Azure Dragon, the Bird or the Tiger added they are bright and dazzling; if Metal is weak, arms and armour are old, and with Hook Earth, the Snake or the Tortoise added they are blunt, worn and colourless; Metal on Tai or tomb, bows are put in their cases and spears put away; if Metal moves, a battle is about to be fought; if Metal moves carrying punishment and the blade, the fight will surely wound men; if Metal is Void and moves, war drums sound together and blades have already met.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.67** 水看水源和井：水旺相，水就满；衰、绝，水就干。申爻旺相发动的，有水，方位用八卦来定；初爻旺相发动的，有井，方位用十二地支来定（申爻是水源，初爻是井）。初爻是庚子水的，屋下有井；是甲子水的，门前有井。初爻是木爻发动的，树下有井；初爻值土，水浑浊；值火，井干；初爻是土爻落空的，有一个池塘。
  Water, springs and wells. If Water is thriving it is full; if weak or extinct, it runs dry. If the Shen line is thriving and moving, there is water, its direction fixed by the eight trigrams; if the first line is thriving and moving, there is a well, its direction fixed by the twelve branches (the Shen line is the source of water and the first line is the well). If the first line is Gengzi Water, there is a well under the house; if Jiazi Water, there is a well in front of the gate. If the first line is Wood and moves, there is a well beneath a tree; if the first line is on Earth, the water is muddy; on Fire, the well is dry; if the first line is Earth and Void, there is a pond.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.68** 水用于战：水爻的官鬼旺相发动、克世爻的，敌将会灌水淹我；水爻的子孙旺相发动、克应爻的，我可以用沙袋壅水，或者背水列阵；阴水爻的兄弟旺相发动、克应爻的，可以渡冰踏雪，在夜里晦暗时擒贼。
  Water in battle. If a Water Pressure line is thriving and moving and controls the Self, the enemy general will flood me; if a Water Output line is thriving and moving and controls the Other, I can use sandbags or draw up with the water behind me; if a yin Water Peer line is thriving and moving and controls the Other, one can cross the ice and tread the snow and seize the bandits on a dark night.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.69** 日辰看我方的兵：日辰代表我方的兵。日辰生合世爻、子孙爻的，士卒听命效力；冲克世爻、子孙爻的，兵骄横难以管束；世爻、子孙爻克日辰的，上面的人不爱惜下面的人；日辰加青龙、德神、喜神而发动的，将帅统御部下严肃；日辰克应爻、克官鬼的，我方兵胜；应爻、官鬼克日辰的，敌兵强。
  The Day and my troops. The Day stands for my soldiers. If the Day generates or combines with the Self or Output, the soldiers obey orders and serve; if it clashes with or controls the Self or Output, the troops are proud and hard to rein in; if the Self or Output controls the Day, those above do not spare those below; if the Day adds the Azure Dragon, Virtue and Joy and moves, the commander governs his men with strictness and dignity; if the Day controls the Other and Pressure, my troops win; if the Other or Pressure controls the Day, the enemy troops are strong.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.70** 日辰加六神，看我方兵的品性（在内卦是营内，在外卦是出征在外）：日辰加青龙发动、生世爻的，在内卦，兵守纪律，在外卦，行动停止都听号令；日辰加朱雀、喜神发动的，善用间谍；加朱雀、官鬼发动又逢空的，在内造谣生谤，在外打探的情报不实；日辰加勾陈、土爻官鬼发动的，在内阻挠号令不遵从，在外退缩不前进；加螣蛇、官鬼发动的，在内诡诈多端，在外兴妖惑众；加白虎、官鬼发动的，在内偏强难驯，在外轻敌坏事；加玄武、官鬼发动的，在内隔墙偷听，在外抢功图利；加玄武、官鬼，带天贼、天盗、劫杀旺相发动、克世爻的，在内偷窃符印，在外掠夺妇女财物。
  The Day carrying the six spirits, reading the character of my troops (inside means within the camp, outside means on campaign). If the Day carries the Azure Dragon, moves and generates the Self, then inside the troops keep discipline, and outside they stop and go at command; if the Day carries the Bird and Joy and moves, spies are used well; if it carries the Bird and Pressure, moves and meets Void, then inside they spread rumours and slander, and outside the intelligence gathered is untrue; if the Day carries Hook Earth and an Earth Pressure line and moves, inside they obstruct orders and do not comply, and outside they shrink back and do not advance; with the Snake and Pressure, inside they are crafty in many ways and outside they raise evil and delude the crowd; with the Tiger and Pressure, inside they are headstrong and hard to tame, and outside they despise the enemy and spoil the matter; with the Tortoise and Pressure, inside they listen at the wall and eavesdrop, and outside they grab credit for private gain; with the Tortoise and Pressure, carrying Tianzei, Tiandao and Robbery, thriving and moving and controlling the Self, inside they steal the tally and seal, and outside they plunder women and goods.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.26** 投军效用：官星要旺相，不能落空、被破、休囚；妻财和世爻两个爻，怕遇到刑、冲、克、坏。世爻与官星都要旺相又变出吉象，财是俸禄的供养，哪能让日月来刑冲？世爻失陷或发动变凶，自身尤其难保；官星失陷或发动变凶，难许成名；财爻失陷或发动变凶，缺粮没有俸禄，官星再旺也像厨房没有烟火。只喜欢官星持世、有财发动生它、财爻持世、官星发动与它相合，或官星与世爻临日月，以及太岁、月建、日辰相生，或世爻发动变吉，将来封官拜将可以期待。觉子说：要先识得来占的人的用意，为名利而问的这样断；如果问的是还没被任用时能不能被收用，官星就是收用他的那个官，官星来生合世爻的必收，官爻克世爻、或落空落破的不录用。
  On joining the colours: the star of office should be thriving and must not be Void, broken, or resting or trapped, and the Wealth line and the Self line fear punishment, clash, control and ruin. The Self and the star of office should both be thriving and turn into auspicious lines, and Wealth is the support of the salary, so how could the Day and Month punish or clash with it? If the Self is lost or moves and turns bad, one's own safety is all the harder to keep; if the star of office is lost or moves and turns bad, a name is hard to promise; if the Wealth line is lost or moves and turns bad, there is no grain and no salary, and even if the star of office is thriving no smoke rises from the kitchen stove. What is welcomed is the star of office holding the Self with moving Wealth generating it, the Wealth line holding the Self, the star of office moving to combine with it, the star of office and the Self sitting on the Day or Month, Tai Sui, the Month and the Day generating each other, or the Self moving and turning auspicious: then investiture and the rank of general may be hoped for. Juezi says: first understand the intent of the person who comes; for one asking for rank and profit this is how to judge; if he asks whether, not yet employed, he can be taken on, the star of office is the officer who takes him on: if it comes to generate or combine with the Self he will surely be taken on, and if the Pressure line controls the Self or is Void or broken he will not be enrolled.

## 国朝(The court and the state: ruler, ministers, the realm)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/国朝.md`

- **LY.CAREER.CA.16** 世是君、应是臣，上卦是君、下卦是臣；如果上下相生，君臣唱和，满堂和气；卦中世、应相生，又有奉命去训练士卒的差事，去了必定建功。
  The Self is the lord and the Other the minister, the upper trigram the lord and the lower the minister; if upper and lower generate each other, lord and ministers answer one another in song and the whole hall is in warm accord; when Self and Other generate each other and there is an order to go and drill troops, going will surely win merit.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.19** 太岁上卦，主入朝；卦里没有太岁，只能迁调；太岁与世爻合、与身位合，主入朝觐见天子。
  With Tai Sui in the hexagram there is entry to court; without Tai Sui one can only be moved or promoted; Tai Sui combining with the Self line and with the Body place means going into court and seeing the Son of Heaven.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.22** 占国家、占天子，从卦的本宫和世爻上取：本宫是国，世是君；本宫和世旺相比和，得圣明之主。六爻各有所主：初是民、二是士、三是大夫、四是公侯、五是君、六是宗庙社稷神；动静刚柔各要得当；六爻都要生合旺相，只要有一位不得其所，整个国家就不安宁。
  To divine for the state or the Son of Heaven, take from the palace of the hexagram and from the Self: the palace is the state and the Self is the lord, and when they are strong and in accord there is a sage ruler. The six lines each stand for a rank: the first the common people, the second the scholars, the third the grandees, the fourth the dukes and marquises, the fifth the lord, the sixth the ancestral temple and the altars of land and grain; moving or still, hard or soft, each should be in its right place; the six lines should all be generated, combined and strong, and if a single place is not where it should be, the whole country is unsettled.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.23** 国事占里，大杀是灾星：金主兵戈，水动主洪水之灾，土动主有修治城垒（或攻城）的事；大杀见于震、离、坎、兑四宫，应在边远的地方；见于坤、艮宫，国内的事情还没安宁；六爻不动、卦里没有杀，天下人民歌唱太平。
  In divining affairs of state the Great Baleful spirit brings calamity: Metal rules weapons, Water moving means flood, Earth moving means the repair of city walls (or the assault on a city); when it appears in the four palaces Zhen, Li, Kan and Dui, it falls on the border regions; in the palaces Kun and Gen affairs at home are not yet settled; with the six lines still and no baleful spirit in the hexagram, the people of the world sing of peace.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.09** 卦里没有太岁，只是移官调动；太岁相合的时候，能见天子。
  With no Tai Sui in the hexagram, only a transfer of office; when Tai Sui combines, one sees the Son of Heaven.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.01** 占朝廷国事，以世爻为主，应爻为辅；六合、子孙旺相的是吉，六冲、凶煞发动的是凶。国家太平、百姓安宁，靠文臣武将的匡正辅佐；想知道宗庙社稷的安危，须要审察易爻来衡量。
  In divining the affairs of the court and state, take the Self as the chief and the Other image as the aid; a six-combine and a thriving Output are auspicious, a six-clash and a moving baleful spirit are ominous. A state at peace and a people secure depend on the correction and aid of civil and military ministers; to know the safety or danger of the ancestral temple and altars, one must examine the lines of the Yi and weigh them.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.02** 亲宫（本宫）当作邦国，最忌空亡；本卦当作朝堂，最适宜旺相。本卦是所占得的那一卦的卦名，亲宫是本宫之卦，所以都当作邦国、朝堂，都适宜旺相兴隆，最忌空亡、墓、绝。
  The home palace is taken as the state, and Void is most to be avoided; the original hexagram is taken as the court hall, and thriving is best. The original hexagram is the name of the hexagram cast, and the home palace is the hexagram of the palace to which it belongs; so both are taken as the state and the court, both should be thriving and flourishing, and Void, tomb and extinction are most to be avoided.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.03** 初爻是百姓，吉星当权就能培固国家的根本；二爻是士人，文昌得令，文章就焕发光彩。初爻的百姓是国家的根本，喜欢临吉星；二爻的士人属文明，喜欢值文昌。
  The first line is the common people; if an auspicious star holds power, the root of the state is nurtured. The second line is the scholars; if Wenchang is in season, their writings shine. The people of the first line are the root of the state and like to sit on an auspicious star; the scholars of the second line belong to civilization and like to meet Wenchang.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.04** 喜神聚在三垣，郡守县令的贤能可比渤海的龚遂：喜，就是天喜，如果值在二爻，郡守、县令的贤能就和汉代的龚遂一样，龚遂做渤海太守时，教化百姓卖掉刀剑去买耕牛。贵人临在四爻，公侯的功绩超过汾阳王：贵，就是贵人星，如果临在四爻，卿相公侯的政绩就超过唐代的郭子仪，他以文武全才辅佐肃宗收复西京，立下中兴的伟绩，受封汾阳王。
  If Joy gathers at the Three Enclosures, the worth of prefects and magistrates equals Gong Sui of Bohai: 'Joy' is the Heavenly Joy, and if it sits on the second line the ability of prefects and magistrates is like that of Gong Sui of Han, who as prefect of Bohai taught the people to sell their swords and buy oxen. If the Noble sits on the fourth line, the achievements of dukes and marquises surpass the Prince of Fenyang: 'the Noble' is the Noble star, and if it sits on the fourth line the governing record of ministers and marquises exceeds that of Guo Ziyi of the Tang, who with his gifts in both letters and arms helped Emperor Suzong recover the western capital, achieved the great work of the restoration, and was enfeoffed Prince of Fenyang.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.05** 福德（子孙）当权，君子被进用、小人退下：福德就是子孙，用爻值在子孙上，正直的人在位，邪佞的人隐藏踪迹。青龙当道，战事止息，文教大兴。
  If Fortune Virtue holds power, gentlemen advance and petty men retire: Fortune Virtue is Output, and if the Subject line sits on it, upright men are in office and the crafty and flattering hide their traces. If the Azure Dragon holds the road, arms are laid down and culture and teaching flourish.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.06** 君主自己占，用世爻来安放帝座；臣民代占，还是用五爻来定君王。当今皇帝亲自占的，以世爻安帝座；臣民推占的，仍以五爻为天子；各有各的道理，断然不会出错。
  When the sovereign divines for himself, set the throne by the Self line; when ministers and commoners divine on his behalf, fix the ruler by the fifth line. When the present emperor divines in person, the throne is set by the Self; when ministers and commoners divine for him, the fifth line is still taken as the Son of Heaven; each has its principle, and there will surely be no error.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.07** 用爻不遇克、害、刑、冲，天下升平；遇到生、扶、拱、合，四方都来献玉帛。克害刑冲各有另章的起例；用爻不犯这些的，必定是四海承平、万民乐业；卦内相生相合、有拱有扶的，连远方绝域也都来朝贡。
  If the Subject line does not meet control, harm, punishment or clash, the realm is in peace; if it meets generation, support, flanking or combination, all quarters bring jade and silk. The rules for control, harm, punishment and clash are set out in other chapters; if the Subject line is not hit by them, the four seas are at peace and the people content in their work; if within the hexagram there is generation and combination, flanking and support, even distant lands and remote regions come to pay tribute.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.08** 宗庙由上爻掌管：上爻安静，国家就稳固。宗庙专管社稷，得到比和、安静，国家的版图就像磐石一样安稳。岁星（太岁）关系到世爻、光芒旺相的，国祚就绵长：岁星就是太岁，关系到治乱，光芒旺相；如果这样，国祚就久远无疆。
  The ancestral temple is governed by the top line: if it is still, the state is firm. The ancestral temple governs the altars of the state alone, and if it is in harmony and still, the realm is as secure as a rock. If the Year Star (Tai Sui) is linked with the Self and shines thriving, the dynasty's span is long: the Year Star is Tai Sui, it bears on order and disorder and shines thriving; if so, the dynasty lasts long without limit.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.09** 木爻遇到长生，前星（太子星）焕发光彩：木爻属东方，是青宫太子之象，前星也是太子之星，「焕彩」是说他得位，如果遇到长生又有气，他的德泽就广被而长久。金爻的官鬼逢到克制、陷落，就知道叛逆之人潜藏着：金爻属西方，主兵革之象，官鬼和凶煞是叛逆的人，如果被制服，或者随着空亡、墓、绝，不久就潜消。
  If a Wood line meets Birth, the Front Star (the heir's star) shines: Wood belongs to the east and is the image of the Azure Palace and the heir apparent; the Front Star is also the star of the heir; 'shines' says that he has his place, and if it meets Birth and has qi, his virtue spreads widely and lasts. If a Metal Pressure line meets restraint or sinks, then know that rebels lie hidden: Metal belongs to the west and is the image of war; Pressure and baleful spirits are the rebels, and if they are subdued, or follow Void, tomb or extinction, they soon vanish in secret.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.10** 粮饷看妻财，不要临败、绝：妻财是粮饷，兴旺则国家的根本充盈，衰败则京城的储备匮乏。城池看父母，不要变出刑伤：父母是城池，旺相则城池固若金汤，遇刑伤就有残缺的忧患。
  Provisions are judged by Wealth, which must not sit on spent or extinct: Wealth is the grain and pay, and if it flourishes the root of the state is full, and if it is spent the capital's stores run short. The city walls are judged by Resource, which must not change into punishment or injury: Resource is the walls and moats, and if it is thriving they are as firm as walls of metal and moats of boiling water, and if punished or injured there is the worry of breaches.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.11** 祸福关系在大象，要变通地洞察细微；吉凶离不开五行，要剖析精研衰旺。人能推究事物、获得知识，报应比影子、回声还快。祸福吉凶所关，易的大象离不开卦的五行，千姿百态哪里能都说尽？筮者应当审察五行的衰旺，自然要变通灵活，相机判断，没有不灵验的，就像回声应着声音。
  Fortune and misfortune turn on the great image, and one must adapt and perceive the faintest signs; good and ill do not leave the five elements, and one must analyse and study their strength and weakness. If a man can investigate things and gain knowledge, retribution is faster than shadow or echo. What fortune and misfortune turn on is that the great image of the Yi does not leave the five elements of the hexagram; of the thousand shapes and ten thousand states, how can all be told? The diviner should examine their strength and weakness and naturally adapt and be flexible, judging as the occasion offers, and there will be none that fail to prove true, as an echo answers a sound.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.89** 上书、启奏，以太岁和五爻为主，以文书爻、官鬼为凭据。启奏君王要看五爻：五爻是天子，太岁是君王，这两者只要有一个生合身爻、世爻，所奏的就如愿，得到宠遇；五爻和太岁如果来冲克身爻、世爻，恐怕触犯天威，就不要陈述情由；岁君克世爻，君王龙颜震怒；月将生官爻，官品爵位就高。另外，官爻多半是自己的本职：不落旬空、月破，又得日月生扶的，一定破格超升。
  For submitting a memorial, take Tai Sui and the fifth line as the chief, and the document and Pressure as the ground. To memorialise the sovereign look at the fifth line: the fifth line is the Son of Heaven and Tai Sui is the sovereign, and if either of the two generates or combines with the Body or the Self, what is memorialised goes as one wishes and one receives favour; if the fifth line and Tai Sui come to clash or control the Body and the Self, one fears offending the majesty of Heaven and should not present the circumstances; if Tai Sui controls the Self the dragon countenance is angry, and if the Month General generates the star of office, rank and title are high. Also, the star of office mostly stands for one's own post: if it does not fall on the ten-day Void or Month-break and is further generated and supported by the Day and the Month, one is surely promoted beyond the regulations.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.91** 朝见君王，以太岁和五爻为主，以官鬼、世爻为凭据。面君时官爻旺相、世爻没有受伤，就是折断栏杆、在朝廷上直言力谏也无妨；太岁、五爻如果冲克世爻，就不要趋步到殿前去招来天灾。面君的人，先推世爻，再察官爻：这两爻如果旺相、不落空亡，又不受君王之爻冲克，去见天子就没有污损伤害；世爻如果被天子之爻冲克，上朝一定遭殃，以安静守着为上。
  For an audience with the sovereign, take Tai Sui and the fifth line as the chief, and Pressure and the Self image as the ground. If the star of office is thriving and the Self is unhurt, even breaking the balustrade in a remonstrance at court is no matter; if Tai Sui or the fifth line clashes with or controls the Self, do not hasten to the palace steps to bring on a heavenly calamity. One who is to see the sovereign should first reckon the Self image and then examine the Pressure line: if these two lines are thriving, not on Void, and not clashed or controlled by the sovereign's line, then going before the Son of Heaven there will be no blemish or harm; if the Self line is clashed or controlled by the Son of Heaven's line, going to court will surely bring calamity, and keeping quiet is best.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.92** 日辰、月建生助身爻是吉，克世爻、伤官爻就都不祥；卦遇晋、升，蒙受君上的恩宠；爻成屯、蹇，哪里谈得上强？世爻和官爻即使逢休囚，只要得到日月或动爻生助，或者变出生扶它们的爻，都是好兆；如果动爻和日月伤官爻、伤世爻，都按凶来推。朝见君王卜得火地晋，晋是臣子遇见君王的象；又取地风升，升是往前、往上走，所以这两卦都吉；如果卜得水雷屯、水山蹇两卦，都是艰难，遇到的一定凶。
  If the Day and the Month generate the Body it is auspicious; if they control the Self or hurt the star of office, all is inauspicious. If the hexagram is Jin or Sheng, one receives the sovereign's favour; if the lines make Zhun or Jian, how could it be strong? Even if the Self line and the Pressure line are resting or imprisoned, if they get generation and aid from the Day and the Month or a moving line, or change out a line that generates and supports them, it is all a good omen; if a moving line and the Day and Month hurt the star of office or the Self, all is judged ominous. If in going to see the king one casts Jin, Fire over Earth, Jin is the image of a minister meeting his sovereign; Sheng, Earth over Wind, is also taken, for Sheng is advancing and going up, so these two hexagrams are both auspicious. If one casts Zhun, Water over Thunder, or Jian, Water over Mountain, both mean difficulty, and whoever meets them will surely have ill.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.26** 上奏陈情、进呈奏章、上书、进谏，以及奉召面君这类事，都忌讳动爻冲克太岁，也忌讳世爻被刑克；如果太岁、月建生合世爻，必定得到详细审议并获准；一旦遇到冲克，须提防意外的祸患；太岁衰弱安静又不带刑、害、白虎、螣蛇，只是不被采用，没有大害；动爻空亡或化空，也只是虚惊；有制伏、冲散、合住的，必有大臣出来申救；应爻发动冲克世爻，更要提防有人上奏弹劾。
  For anything such as a memorial in audience, presenting a petition, submitting a written report, remonstrating, or going to an audience with the sovereign at his summons, it is feared that a moving line clashes or controls the Tai Sui, and also that the Self is punished or controlled; if the Tai Sui or the Month generates or combines with the Self, it will surely be examined in detail and approved; once a clash or control comes, beware of an unforeseen misfortune; if the Tai Sui is weak and still and carries no punishment, harm, White Tiger or Coiling Snake, one is only not adopted and there is no great harm; if the moving line is Void or turns into Void, it is only a false alarm; if there is something that subdues, clashes away or combines it, a high minister will surely come forward to plead and rescue; and if the Other moves and clashes or controls the Self, beware all the more of someone submitting an impeachment.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.63** 占国家的兴衰，是把整个卦当作国运来读。国家有始有终，必有兴有废；国君宽厚，臣子就忠心；国家太平，百姓就安乐；这些祸福的来由，都可以用卦理推到根上、剖开判决。哪一爻代表什么，见下一条。
  A question about the fortunes of a state reads the whole hexagram as the fortune of the realm. A state has a beginning and an end and must rise and fall; when the ruler is forgiving the ministers are loyal, and when the state is at peace the people are happy; the source of such blessings and disasters can be traced and decided by the principles of the hexagram. Which line stands for what is in the next rule.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.64** 占国运，先分派各爻：本宫是国爻，太岁是君，和太岁相合的是后，月建是臣，日建是东宫（太子），子孙是百姓，父爻是国家。开国时占得本宫旺相，国运绵长，像周文王那样子孙享国八百年；本宫休囚、整个卦象又凶，国运短促，像秦始皇那样到二世就亡国。
  To ask about the fortune of a state, first assign the lines: the home palace is the line of the state, Tai Sui is the ruler, the branch combining with Tai Sui is the empress, the Month is the minister, the Day is the Eastern Palace (the crown prince), Output is the common people and Resource is the realm. If a state is being founded and the home palace comes out thriving or strong, its fortune is long, as with King Wen of Zhou, whose descendants held the realm for eight hundred years; if the home palace is resting or trapped and the whole image is also bad, its fortune is short, as with the First Emperor of Qin, whose state was lost with the second reign.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.65** 第五爻是君位：逢到阳象，又遇到青龙、妻财、子孙，是仁慈英明的君主；世爻临阳象、带吉神也一样。第四爻是臣位：子孙旺相、克世爻，是敢于直言进谏的臣子；兄弟、官鬼生合世爻，是阿谀奉承的佞臣。
  The fifth line is the ruler's seat: if it meets a yang image and also meets the Azure Dragon, Wealth and Output, the ruler is benevolent and enlightened, and so it is if the Self sits in a yang image with auspicious spirits. The fourth line is the ministers' seat: if Output is thriving or strong and controls the Self, the minister dares to remonstrate plainly; if a Peer or Pressure line generates or combines with the Self, he is a flattering and treacherous minister.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.66** 本宫衰弱死绝，又被太岁克，这一年国家有动乱灭亡的征兆，就像陈后主在宫里日夜歌舞饮宴，后来被隋朝灭掉。
  If the home palace is weak or dead and is also controlled by Tai Sui, the state shows signs of disorder and ruin that year, like the last ruler of Chen, who gave himself to songs and revels in the palace and was later destroyed by Sui.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.67** 国君自己占卜：太岁来刑、冲、克、害世爻，主国君生病，或者宫廷内部将有祸乱；如果世爻同时又衰弱，就要担心国君驾崩。
  When the ruler casts for himself: if Tai Sui punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, the ruler will be ill or trouble will break out within the court; if the Self is also weak, there is cause to fear the ruler's death.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.68** 世爻落在沐浴上，或者妻财爻、应爻带着沐浴发动、来克合世爻，多半是国君好色，就像夫差迷恋西施、拒不听伍子胥的劝谏，后来被越王勾践灭掉。
  If the Self sits on Bathing, or the Wealth line or the Other line carries Bathing, moves, and controls or combines with the Self, the ruler is surely fond of women, like Fuchai who doted on Xishi, refused the remonstrance of Zixu, and was later destroyed by Goujian, King of Yue.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.69** 应爻是皇后。应爻带咸池，皇后一定不检点；又落在第五爻，就是皇后居于至尊之位，像汉朝的吕后临朝称制；如果它再旺相发动、刑冲克害世爻，世爻又逢空亡，就像唐朝的武后废掉中宗、降为庐陵王，改唐为周。
  The Other line is the empress. If it carries Xianchi, the empress is sure to be licentious; if it also sits in the fifth place, the empress holds the seat of honour, like Empress Lü of Han presiding at court and issuing edicts; if it is also thriving and moving, and punishes, clashes with, controls or harms the Self, while the Self may be Void, it is like Empress Wu of Tang, who deposed Emperor Zhongzong, made him Prince of Luling, and turned the Tang mandate into Zhou.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.70** 国君自占得游魂卦，是迁都、四处巡游的象，像周穆王巡游天下；如果再带凶煞克世，或世爻发动、变入死、墓、空、绝，就像虞舜南巡，死在苍梧的野外，一去不回。
  When the ruler casts for himself, a Wandering Soul hexagram is the image of moving the capital and touring the realm, like King Mu of Zhou who toured the whole land; if a baleful spirit is added that controls the Self, or the Self moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is like Shun of Yu, who went south on a tour and died in the wilds of Cangwu, never to return.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.71** 国君自占得归魂卦，又遇凶神恶煞发动来克世爻或卦身，就像秦始皇到海上求仙，回国后死在沙丘。
  When the ruler casts for himself, a Returning Soul hexagram that meets baleful spirits moving to control the Self or the Body is like the First Emperor of Qin, who sought immortals by the sea and died at Shaqiu after returning to his state.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.72** 子孙是臣。子孙逢空发动，或者第四爻（臣位）落空而动、又被世爻克害，是国君想害臣子、臣子想躲开国君，就像张良借口辟谷、辞去官职，跟随赤松子出游。
  Output is the minister. If Output meets Void and moves, or the fourth line, the ministers' seat, is Void and moves and is also controlled and harmed by the Self, the ruler wants to harm the minister and the minister wants to avoid the ruler, like Zhang Liang, who pleaded fasting from grain, gave up office and went roaming with Chi Songzi.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.73** 将星按占卦那天的地支来找，例如寅、午、戌日占，午爻就是将星。将星临妻财、子孙，必是忠良有智勇的将领；值官鬼、白虎，是强悍的将领；临父母爻，是年老的将领；遇兄弟，不过是平庸的将领。将星持官鬼来克害世爻，就像曹操、桓温挟天子以令诸侯，往往篡位自立；将星被动爻或第四爻动来克害，就像岳飞遇到秦桧这样的权奸而被害死。
  Find the General star from the day branch: on a Yin, Wu or Xu day, the Wu line is the General star, and so on for the others. A General star on Wealth or Output means a loyal, able, wise and brave general; meeting Pressure or the White Tiger, a fierce and stubborn one; on Resource, an old general; meeting Peer, no more than a commonplace one. If the General star holds Pressure and controls and harms the Self, it is like Cao Cao and Huan Wen, who held the emperor and gave orders to the lords and mostly ended by usurping the throne; if the General star is controlled and harmed by a moving line, or by a moving fourth line, it is like Yue Fei of Song, who was killed by the harm of the powerful and treacherous Qin Hui.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.74** 应爻是皇后。应爻旺相、生合世爻，又临妻财或子孙，皇后相貌美、有智谋、天性仁慈，能规劝国君的过失，引导国君向善，像汉朝的马皇后、宋朝的宣仁皇后。
  The Other line is the empress. If the Other line is thriving or strong, generates or combines with the Self, and sits on Wealth or Output, the empress is beautiful, wise and kind by nature, and can correct the ruler's faults and lead him to the good, like Empress Ma of Han and Empress Xuanren of Song.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.75** 别的宫的子孙旺相，又遇日辰生扶拱合，而世爻落在胎、养、空、绝上，主国君年幼，有大臣摄政，像周公辅佐成王；本宫的子孙生旺，又得日辰扶助，是国君厌倦了大位、想要传位，太子主持国政、代行天子的事。
  If the Output of another palace is thriving or strong and is further generated, supported or combined by the Day, while the Self falls on Womb, Nurture, Void or extinction, the ruler is young and a great minister acts as regent, like the Duke of Zhou assisting King Cheng; if the Output of the home palace is thriving and gets support from the Day, the ruler is tired of the great seat and wants to hand it on, and the crown prince takes charge of the state and acts in the Son of Heaven's stead.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.76** 本宫的子孙（太子）被第四爻冲、克、刑、害，必定被进谗的臣子诽谤；世爻克本宫的子孙，子孙又落入死、墓、空、绝，太子遭谗言陷害，就像唐玄宗听信李林甫的谗言，把太子瑛、鄂王瑶、光王琚都废为平民，后来又赐死在城东驿。
  If the Output of the home palace (the crown prince) is clashed, controlled, punished or harmed by the fourth line, he is surely slandered by treacherous ministers; if the Self controls the Output of the home palace and that Output also falls into death, tomb, Void or extinction, the crown prince meets slander and is harmed, like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang, who trusted Li Linfu's slander, demoted Crown Prince Ying, Prince E (Yao) and Prince Guang (Ju) to commoners and later granted them death at the Chengdong post station.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.77** 本宫的子孙带着凶煞、旺相发动、克害世爻，是太子有篡位的征兆，就像隋朝的杨广弑杀父亲、自立为皇帝。
  If the Output of the home palace carries a baleful spirit, is thriving and moves, and controls and harms the Self, the crown prince shows signs of usurping the throne, like Yang Guang of Sui who killed his father and set himself up as emperor.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.78** 卦里没有子孙，或者子孙休囚，又发动落入墓、绝、空、死，必定是国家没有太子，就像宋仁宗没有儿子而叹息。
  If there is no Output in the hexagram, or Output is resting or trapped and moves into tomb, extinction, Void or death, the state has no crown prince, like Emperor Renzong of Song who sighed because he had no son.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.79** 第四爻是臣位。它旺相发动、伤克本宫的子孙（太子），就像秦朝的太子扶苏被赵高假传诏书赐死。
  The fourth line is the ministers' seat. If it is thriving and moves and injures and controls the Output of the home palace (the crown prince), it is like Fusu, the crown prince of Qin, who was ordered to die by Zhao Gao's forged edict.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.80** 世爻或卦身持着官鬼，带着凶煞、旺相发动，必定发展到杀害兄弟，是兄弟不能相容的征兆，就像唐太宗在玄武门埋伏士兵，射死建成、元吉，血流满了宫中。
  If the Self or the Body holds Pressure with a baleful spirit and is thriving and moving, it ends in killing brothers; it is a sign that brothers cannot tolerate each other, like Taizong of Tang, who set troops in ambush at the Xuanwu Gate and shot Jiancheng and Yuanji dead, so that blood flowed in the palace court.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.81** 世爻持着落空亡的兄弟，和应爻相生相合，又有吉神发动来克，是兄弟之间互相推让天子之位的象，就像泰伯假托采药逃到荆蛮，把位置让给季历。
  If the Self holds a Void Peer line, generating or combining with the Other, and an auspicious spirit also moves to control, it is the image of brothers yielding the throne to one another, like Taibo, who on the pretext of gathering herbs fled to the Jing barbarians and gave the succession to Jili.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.82** 官鬼、凶煞发动来生合世爻，必定是奸佞的臣子阿谀奉承、国君信任他，就像唐玄宗信任李林甫十九年，养成了天下大乱。
  If a Pressure line or a baleful spirit moves to generate or combine with the Self, a treacherous minister is surely flattering the ruler and the ruler trusts him, like Emperor Xuanzong of Tang who trusted Li Linfu for nineteen years and bred great disorder in the realm.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.83** 第四爻是臣。四爻持着妻财或子孙，却被世爻发动来克，是敢于冒犯君主、当面直谏的臣子，遇到了剖人心的君主，就像比干尽忠而被商纣王杀害。
  The fourth line is the minister. If the fourth line holds Wealth or Output and is moved and controlled by the Self, it is a minister who dares to offend the ruler's face with plain advice meeting a ruler who cuts out hearts, like Bi Gan, who was loyal to the end and was put to death by King Zhou of Shang.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.84** 整个卦象都凶，世爻、应爻互相克害刑冲，或者世爻发动入死、墓、空、绝，是国君遭灾死亡的征兆；离宫变入坎宫，离是南方、坎是北方，由南入北，就像宋朝的徽宗、钦宗被金人掳走，囚在五国城，老死在北方的沙漠地带。
  If the whole image is bad, the Self and the Other control, harm, punish and clash with each other, or the Self moves into death, tomb, Void or extinction, it is a sign that the ruler will meet calamity and die; a Li-palace hexagram turning into a Kan-palace hexagram goes from south to north (Li is south, Kan is north), like the Song emperors Huizong and Qinzong, who were carried off by the Jin, held at Wuguo City, and grew old and died in the desert land.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.85** 乾宫变巽宫，又有吉曜，就像刘备、孙权在东南建国，和魏国三足鼎立。
  A Qian-palace hexagram turning into a Xun-palace hexagram, with an auspicious star, is like Liu Bei and Sun Quan founding states in the south-east and standing with Wei like the three legs of a tripod.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.06** 占朝廷、国家，世应相得，君臣就同心用力；六位没有相克，万国都安宁。世是帝王，应是功臣，本宫是都城。内外卦比和、旺相，是天生圣明的君主，刚柔动静有常规，大地出奇才；最宜吉神，切忌大煞。金是兵戈，忌动；土是城垒，宜安；水是泛滥，火是炎热晴天，木爻是恶风；吉神是祥瑞。震、离、坎、兑是四方，艮、坤两卦是中土。
  In divining about the court and the state, if the Self and the Other are in accord, ruler and ministers are of one mind; if none of the six places controls another, all the states are at peace. The Self is the sovereign, the Other is the meritorious minister, and the home palace is the capital. If the inner and outer trigrams are in accord and strong, Heaven has produced a sage ruler, firmness and softness, movement and stillness keep their constancy, and the earth brings forth rare talents; auspicious spirits are most suitable, and great baleful stars are strictly to be avoided. Metal is arms and the spear, and it must not move; Earth is the city wall and rampart, and it should be at peace; Water is flooding, Fire is blazing heat, the Wood line is wicked wind; auspicious spirits are good omens. Zhen, Li, Kan and Dui are the four quarters, and the two trigrams Gen and Kun are the central earth.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.07** 五爻是至尊，加上吉神、太岁，是仁慈的君主；带煞、白虎，是暴虐的君主；（五爻）与吉神生合，必定亲近贤人、任用能人，远离佞臣、去除奸邪。初爻安静，吉神持世或生世，万民心悦诚服。本象的二爻是侍臣，带吉神，左右必定得到贤人；加凶煞的，多是奸邪谄佞。四爻会合吉神，克世或生世，必定向上忠于君主，向下安定百姓。
  The fifth line is the supreme one; with an auspicious spirit and the Tai Sui added, he is a benevolent ruler; carrying a baleful star and the White Tiger, he is a tyrannical ruler; if it generates and combines with an auspicious spirit, he will surely draw near the worthy and employ the able, keep far from flatterers and dismiss the treacherous. If the first line is still and an auspicious spirit holds or generates the Self, the people are all pleased and submit. In the image, the second line is the attending minister; with an auspicious spirit, he will surely have worthy men about him; with a baleful star added, they are mostly treacherous and flattering. If the fourth line meets an auspicious spirit and controls or generates the Self, he will be loyal to the sovereign above and bring peace to the common people below.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.08** 子孙是储君、郡主，宜旺相不空；如果大煞发动、刑冲克它，恐怕有废立的祸患。子孙在初爻动，克三爻或克世的，有士人百姓上书直言利害；在二爻动，必有有才有德、口才好的臣子进呈密封奏事；在三爻动，有贤能的诸侯到门前直言劝谏；在四爻动（「艾」疑是「爻」之误），身边近臣必定尽忠、冒死直谏。
  Output is the crown prince and the princesses; it should be strong and not Void; if a great baleful star moves to punish, clash or control it, I fear there is the trouble of deposition and installation. If Output on the first line moves to control the third line or the Self, scholars and commoners submit memorials speaking directly on benefit and harm; moving on the second line, there will be ministers of talent and virtue and a ready tongue who present sealed memorials; moving on the third line, a worthy and able lord comes to the gate to remonstrate frankly; moving on the fourth line (the 'ai' is presumably a slip for 'yao', line), the courtiers at his side will surely be wholly loyal and remonstrate at the risk of death.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.19** 占国家，以本宫为国、世为君；又说五爻是天子。五爻带财、福、青龙、贵人的，是仁君；临太岁、月建、勾陈的，威震天下，是守正之君；加大杀、白虎的，是暴虐之君；带咸池的，是淫乱之君；加劫杀的，在位不久。五爻与吉神相生相合，必能亲近贤者；吉神来克它，必有贤臣辅佐；它动而克凶神，必能远小人、去奸邪。若与凶杀相生相合，或凶杀发动来克，辅佐的宰相就不得其人，都是奸佞在辅佐，君爻纵然遇上吉神，也不能去掉奸佞。
  In divining for the state, the palace of the hexagram is the state and the Self is the lord; it is also said that the fifth line is the Son of Heaven. A fifth line carrying Wealth, Output, the Azure Dragon or the Noble is a benevolent lord; on Tai Sui, the Month or Hook Earth it shakes the world and is a lord who keeps to the right; with the Great Baleful Spirit or the White Tiger it is a tyrant; with Xianchi, a debauched lord; with the Robbery Killing, a lord who will not hold his seat long. If it is generated or combined by auspicious spirits it will surely draw near the worthy; if an auspicious spirit comes to control it, there will be worthy ministers to assist; if it moves and controls a baleful spirit it will surely keep the petty at a distance and remove the wicked. If it is generated or combined by baleful spirits, or baleful spirits move to control it, the chancellors are the wrong men and all are flatterers assisting, and even if the lord's line meets an auspicious spirit it cannot remove the flatterers.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.20** 三公九卿的位置都在四爻。四爻临吉神、旺相而克世，是敢言敢谏的社稷之臣；加鬼、杀、旺相而克世，是欺君僭上、蒙蔽君主把持权柄的人；世值吉神，大概没有大害。四爻与世相生，是阿谀奉承的臣子，纵然带吉神也不能规谏君主的过错，带凶神也害不了君主的正道。四爻动生初爻，必有忧虑百姓之心；初爻生四爻，百姓有仰慕之意；四爻加凶杀克初爻，百姓受无辜之害；初爻发动克四爻，百姓看他如同仇敌。四爻生合二爻，必能招揽贤人、接纳士人；二爻生四爻，士人有景仰羡慕之心；四爻生克三爻，必能任用百官、进用贤人罢退不肖之人。
  The places of the Three Councillors and the Nine Ministers are all on the fourth line. If it stands on an auspicious spirit, strong, and controls the Self, he is a pillar of the state who dares to speak and to remonstrate; if Pressure and baleful spirits are added, strong, and it controls the Self, he is one who deceives the lord, oversteps, keeps his master in the dark and seizes power; if the Self is on an auspicious spirit there is probably no great harm. If the fourth line and the Self generate each other, he is a flattering minister, who even with an auspicious spirit cannot admonish the lord's faults or set right his errors, and with a baleful spirit cannot damage the lord's uprightness or ruin his virtue. The fourth line moving to generate the first, he surely has a heart that grieves for the people; the first generating the fourth, the people look up to him; the fourth with a baleful spirit controlling the first, the people suffer undeserved harm; the first moving to control the fourth, the people regard him as a bitter enemy. The fourth generating or combining the second, he can surely call in the worthy and receive scholars; the second generating the fourth, the scholars admire him; the fourth generating or controlling the third, he can surely employ the hundred officials, advancing the worthy and dismissing the unworthy.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.21** 占国家，以子孙为国家的继承人。子孙得日辰扶助相合，或动爻来生它，或落在月建上旺相，以及加青龙、贵人，如果东宫（太子）已立，将来要登宝位，就不会再有更改；子孙带咸池，不是正宫所生；福德落在劫杀、大杀上，恐怕有变更，再被四爻伤克，必有赵高谋害扶苏那样的事；卦里没有子孙，就没有国家的继承人。
  In divining for the state, Output stands for the heir to the state. If Output gets support and combination from the Day, or a moving line generates it, or it stands on the Month and is strong, or the Azure Dragon or the Noble is added, then if the Eastern Palace (the crown prince) has already been established, when he is due to ascend the throne there will be no further change; if Output carries Xianchi, he is not born of the principal consort; if Output stands on the Robbery Killing or the Great Baleful Spirit there may be a change, and if it is further injured and controlled by the fourth line there will surely be a matter like Zhao Gao's plot against Fusu; if there is no Output in the hexagram, there is no heir to the state.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.22** 世爻和五爻如果带咸池、玄武，在上的君主必定荒淫；卦里有咸池却被君爻克住，虽有西施那样的美人，也无害于国政；应爻加咸池、飞入五爻旺相，又加上世爻空亡，必有像武氏乱唐那样的祸患，以后必定见到女主治理天下。再加吉神，天下大概可以安定，再带凶神，四海必乱。君位如果合咸池、带杀、旺相，必有女祸，就像唐皇与杨贵妃的故事。
  If the Self line and the fifth line carry Xianchi and the Dark Tortoise, the ruler above is sure to be dissolute; if the hexagram has Xianchi but it is controlled by the lord's line, then though there is a beauty like Xishi it does no harm to the government; if the Other line carries Xianchi and flies into the fifth line, strong, and the Self line is also Void, there will surely be the calamity of a Lady Wu throwing Tang into disorder, and later a female ruler will surely rule the world. If an auspicious spirit is added the world will perhaps be at peace, and if a baleful spirit is added the four seas are sure to be in disorder. If the lord's place combines with Xianchi and carries a baleful spirit, strong, there is sure to be calamity through a woman, like the story of the Tang emperor and Yang Guifei.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.23** 福神（子孙）克世，又见到折槛直谏的朱云。子孙发动、伤克世爻，主有正直的大臣上奏，很像朱云折槛的风骨；若与日辰相生相合，上面一定准他的奏章；与日辰刑冲，皇帝大怒，终究遭到贬责。子孙落在太岁、月建上，发动克四爻、生五爻，在子孙旺的日子，必有御史弹劾权臣；四爻的鬼、杀旺相而不受克，要防备有变。
  Output controlling the Self, one sees again Zhu Yun who broke the railing. If Output moves and injures and controls the Self line, there is an upright minister presenting a memorial, much like Zhu Yun breaking the railing; if it is generated or combined by the Day, the sovereign will surely grant the memorial, and if it is punished or clashed by the Day the dragon countenance is greatly angered and he is in the end demoted and reprimanded. If Output stands on Tai Sui or the Month and moves to control the fourth line and generate the fifth, on a day when Output is strong there will surely be a censor impeaching a powerful minister; if the fourth line's ghost and baleful spirit are strong and not controlled, guard against a change.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.35** 白虎、螣蛇都动，正是离乱的时候；世、应都空，适宜见到升平的安乐。大杀如果发动，加白虎、螣蛇，必是大乱，一时难以平息；大杀落在胎、养、长生的位置上，是丧乱刚刚萌发的时候；在临官、帝旺的地方，正当大乱，过了这个阶段才渐渐平息；落在墓、绝的位置上，将要太平。离乱已经过去的，要知道哪一年最厉害、哪一年稍稍平息，依长生法来断。六爻没有鬼、卦里没有杀，世、应都空，就是太平。
  With the Tiger and the Snake both moving it is just the time of dispersal and disorder; with the Self and the Other both void it suits seeing the joy of peaceful times. If the Great Baleful Spirit moves and the White Tiger and the Coiling Snake are added, it is surely great disorder, hard to quiet soon; if the Great Baleful Spirit falls on the Embryo, Nurture or Birth stage it is the time when calamity and disorder are just sprouting; if it is at the Office or Peak stage it is just the height of disorder, which then gradually subsides; if it falls on the Tomb or Extinction stage peace is coming. For a disorder already passed, to know in which year it was at its worst and in which it eased a little, judge by the method of the Birth stages. With no Pressure among the six lines and no baleful spirit in the hexagram, and the Self and the Other both void, there is peace.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.01** 占国事，要义是进用忠良、为万民造福，所以福爻（子孙）不能失；要退去奸邪、除去四海的疾患，所以鬼象（官鬼）不能动。凡是占国事，都喜欢福神临到、厌恶官鬼发动，这是占国事的大象。本章细讲迁都、建庙、择后、立储、改制、享国各问。
  In divining about the state, the essential is to advance the loyal and good and to bring blessing to the myriad people, so the fortune line, Output, must not be lost; one must also remove the treacherous and rid the four seas of their sickness, so the Pressure image must not move. In every divination about the state one is glad to see the fortune spirit arrive and dislikes Pressure moving, and this is the great image of such divinations. The chapter treats in detail the questions of moving the capital, building a temple, choosing an empress, naming an heir, changing the institutions, and how long the dynasty will last.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.02** 内卦是国都和邦畿，外卦是人民；卦宫安稳旺相，国运就长；爻象和合，宗庙社稷就长久。冲不要出现在正卦和之卦（本卦和变卦）里，合不要被破、被害，这样天下就和平，国家就安定了。接着还要再看旺衰、动静、合冲。
  The inner trigram is the capital and its domain and the outer is the people: if the palace of the hexagram is secure and thriving the dynasty is long, and if the lines combine harmoniously the ancestral temple and altars last long. If clash does not appear in the original or the changed hexagram, and a combination is not broken or harmed, the world is at peace and the state is secure. Next look at strength and weakness, movement and stillness, combination and clash.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.03** 皇帝亲自占卜，以世爻为主象，适宜得到日月的生扶；臣下为国占卜，以九五爻当作尊位，忌讳随墓、空、破。皇帝亲占而应爻合世的，皇后的德行像樛木那样昭著；臣下占卜而二爻持福神的，皇后的母仪在后宫居首。臣下占卜，以五爻为君、二爻为后，所以这样说。
  When the emperor divines in person, the Self line is the chief image and it is fitting for the Day and Month to generate and support it; when a minister divines for the state, the ninth-fifth line is the honoured position and following into the tomb, Void and break are to be avoided. If the emperor divines in person and the Other combines with the Self, the empress's virtue shines like that of the drooping tree; if a minister divines and the second line holds the fortune spirit, the empress's model of motherhood heads the harem. A minister divining takes the fifth line as the sovereign and the second as the empress, hence what is said.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.04** 推爻位：初爻是国本，二爻是士人，三爻是各地的守牧（地方长官），四爻是九州的屏藩，上爻是宗庙之爻；都厌恶官鬼、喜欢福神，喜欢安稳旺相、厌恶动摇。推六亲：官鬼是国家的祸患，又是大盗；福神是国家的储君，又是良臣；妻财是国家的赋税，又是宦官；兄弟是国家的聚敛，又是宗室；父母是山川城郭，又是诏令。看它们临在什么位置、属于哪一宫，比如官鬼发动在乾宫，西北有事；福神临在三爻，地方守牧得人，等等。
  Reckoning by the line positions: the first line is the root of the state, the second the scholars, the third the governors of all the regions, the fourth the screens and bulwarks of the nine provinces, and the top line the line of the ancestral temple; all dislike Pressure and like the fortune spirit, like quiet and thriving and dislike moving and shaking. Reckoning by the six kin: Pressure is the plague of the state and also a great bandit; Output, the fortune spirit, is the heir of the state and also a good minister; Wealth is the revenue of the state and also the palace gatekeepers; Peer is the state's levying and hoarding and also the imperial clan; Resource is the mountains, rivers and city walls and also the edicts and orders. Look at where they sit and what palace they belong to: for example, if Pressure moves in the Qian palace there is trouble in the north-west, and if the fortune spirit sits on the third line the governors are well chosen, and the like.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.05** 官鬼在内卦落空，就没有内乱；官鬼在外卦发动，就有外患要抵御；官鬼靠近五爻，要防佞臣；依附三爻，要戒酷吏；祸患在二爻，是士人横行；祸患在初爻，是百姓困苦。白虎官如果不在金乡发动，就没有战事；玄武官如果不在水地张扬，就没有水涝；火属官鬼带螣蛇，当年忧火灾；土官带朱雀，当年虑蝗灾。凡是官鬼发动，在内还是在外、哪一宫、哪一位、属于哪一类，都应当谨慎；整饬弊端要防奸邪，朝廷的谋划应当预先。
  If Pressure is Void in the inner trigram there is no disorder within; if Pressure moves in the outer trigram there are outside enemies to repel; if Pressure is near the fifth line guard against treacherous ministers; if it attaches to the third line beware of cruel officials; if the trouble is on the second line the scholars run riot, and if on the first line the people are in distress. If a White Tiger Pressure does not move in a Metal palace there is no war; if a Dark Tortoise Pressure does not show itself on watery ground there is no flooding; a Fire Pressure with the Coiling Snake means anxiety about fire in the year, and an Earth star of office with the Vermilion Bird means worry about a plague of locusts. Wherever Pressure moves, inside or outside, in which palace, in which position and of which element, one should be careful; in setting right abuses guard against treachery, and the court's planning should be done in advance.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.06** 子孙遇木而旺相，当年桑麻丰收；妻财遇土而兴隆，当年田野丰收；父母爻值巳、午而出现，当开启文明。子孙在青龙，文士彬彬有礼；子孙临白虎，武功烈烈；动在朱雀，善于弹劾的奏章；摇在玄武，美在宫闱的德行；勾陈得田官的喜庆，螣蛇靠使臣的力量。凡是福神临在五行、六亲、六神上，都是吉祥的应验，所以福神摇在玄武，也是后宫的美事。
  If Output meets Wood and thrives, the year's mulberry and hemp are plentiful; if Wealth meets Earth and flourishes, the fields yield well in the year; if Resource falls on Si or Wu and appears, civilisation is opened up. If Output is on the Azure Dragon, the scholars are refined; if Output is on the White Tiger, military exploits blaze; moving on the Vermilion Bird, good memorials of impeachment; swaying on the Dark Tortoise, fine virtue in the inner palace; on Hook Earth it enjoys the delight of the field officers, and on the Coiling Snake it relies on the strength of envoys. Wherever the fortune spirit rests, on the five elements, the six kin or the six spirits, it is an auspicious response, so the fortune spirit swaying on the Dark Tortoise is also a fine thing for the inner palace.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.07** 问迁都，如同迁居：以外卦旺相为凭据，官鬼兴旺是忌。问建庙，如同供奉神灵：以三冲、六坏、鬼动、鬼疾、鬼克为忌。迁都、建庙，都忌贞冲、悔冲、合处逢冲这几种情形。六爻是太庙所属，所以不可破散；官鬼是太灵（祖先神灵），所以不可鬼动、鬼空、鬼散，以及鬼克世。
  For moving the capital, which is like moving house, the outer being thriving is the ground and Pressure flourishing is the taboo; for building a temple, which is like enshrining the spirits, the taboos are the three clashes, the six breakages, and Pressure moving, being sick or controlling. In moving the capital and building a temple, the taboos are the clash of the original hexagram, the clash of the changed hexagram, and a combination meeting a clash. The six lines belong to the great temple, so it must not be broken or scattered; Pressure is the great spirit, so Pressure must not move, be Void or scatter, nor control the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.08** 选皇后如同婚姻：财爻在哪一宫，就应在哪个地方的女子。如果财在中间的爻，应得晋地的女子；财在内卦，应在本乡。立储君，喜欢子孙跨在日、月上；改制，忧虑官鬼在强横的位置上发动。
  Choosing an empress is like a marriage: in whichever palace Wealth lies, the match corresponds to that place. If Wealth is in a middle line, the match should be a woman of Jin; if Wealth is in the inner trigram, it should correspond to the home district. In naming an heir one is glad for Output to straddle the Day and Month; in changing the institutions one worries about Pressure moving in the place of the violent and overbearing.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.57** 面见皇上上书、叩阍陈情献策，不可遇到遁卦。见六冲，就上下隔阂；受五爻的克，就触犯君主的逆鳞；五位受伤，就忠言逆耳。如果是向将相逐条陈述意见，喜官旺而忌克世。
  In presenting a memorial to the sovereign in person, or knocking at the palace gate to offer a plan, the hexagram Dun must not be met. Meeting a six-clash, high and low are estranged; suffering the control of the fifth line, one touches the reversed scale on the sovereign's throat; the fifth place being injured, loyal words grate on the ear. If one is presenting views item by item to a general or chief minister, thriving Pressure is welcome and controlling the Self is feared.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.63** 衙门裁撤革除，只察看官爻；公议的短长，只求鬼象；国家政务行止，专从五位。官旺就不裁；公议将要实行的事，鬼破就中止；兴办、革除、减少、增加，以得到君命才行，五爻克世，就不能行。
  Whether a government office is abolished, one looks only at the Pressure line; whether public opinion carries or fails, one seeks only the Pressure image; whether a state affair goes ahead or stops, one follows only the fifth place. Thriving Pressure means it is not abolished; of a matter public opinion is about to carry out, broken Pressure stops it; to set up, abolish, reduce or increase anything, it goes ahead only if the sovereign's order is obtained, and if the fifth line controls the Self, it does not go.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.106** 太岁值福或值鬼的年份：身命有福神持世，一生平安快乐；家宅有鬼会合，岁月多艰难；仕宦，官职高到台阁；词讼，事情惊动宪司。事情关系朝廷，喜太岁生合世爻，忌冲克身象。凡是上疏、面圣、请封等事，都喜太岁生合世爻，就上合君心；太岁冲身象，就担忧受严厉的谴责。
  When the Tai Sui sits on Output or on Pressure: for a person's fate, with the fortune spirit holding the Self one's whole life is peaceful and happy; for the household, with Pressure joining, the months and years are hard; for an official career, the post rises to the cabinet; for a lawsuit, the matter reaches the censorate. For a matter that concerns the court, it is welcome for the Tai Sui to generate or combine with the Self line, and it is feared for it to clash or control the body image. For memorials to the throne, audience with the sovereign, and asking for conferment of titles, it is always welcome for the Tai Sui to generate or combine with the Self line, which accords above with the sovereign's heart; the Tai Sui clashing the body image is a cause to fear severe censure.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.31** 占朝廷的事和占臣子百姓自己的事不同：以世爻所在的那个单卦当作国家；世爻和五爻都是君主，君主自己占取世爻为用神，臣子百姓代他占取五爻；应爻和二爻是皇后；子孙是储君，妻财是妃嫔也是国库，父母是城池，兄弟是劫神，官鬼是奸贼；初爻是百姓，二爻是士人，三爻是大夫，四爻是公卿，五爻是天子，六爻是宗庙朝廷。察看各爻的生克旺衰，国家的利弊兴衰就看得清清楚楚；至于占国家传几世、延续几年，另有一种方法。
  Divining for the court differs from divining for ministers and commoners: the trigram in which the Self line sits stands for the state; the Self and the fifth line are both the ruler, and when the ruler divines for himself the Self is taken as the Subject, while ministers and commoners divining on his behalf take the fifth line; the Other line and the second line are the empress; Output is the heir apparent, Wealth is the consorts and also the treasury, Resource is the city walls and moats, Peer is the robber spirit, and Pressure is the traitors and bandits; the first line is the common people, the second the scholars, the third the great officers, the fourth the ministers of state, the fifth the Son of Heaven, and the sixth the ancestral temple and court. Look at the generation, control, strength and weakness of each line and the gains and losses and rise and fall of the state are as clear as the palm of one's hand; as for divining how many reigns or years the dynasty will last, there is another method.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.32** 大象，国运的总势：世爻所在的那个单卦就是国家。它旺相，国家治理得好，天下太平；胎、没次一等；死、囚、休、废，国运就衰落下去。它落空亡，君主巡游没有节制；被冲破，疆界不稳固；被刑克，奸邪作乱的人暗中起事；受害，臣子百姓离心。太岁生旺这一宫，国运炽盛；太岁克制衰弱的卦，国家的宗庙社稷将要倾覆。
  The great image, the general trend of the state's fortune. The trigram in which the Self line sits is the state. If it is thriving, the state is well governed and the world at peace; the stages Tai and Mo come next; if it is dead, imprisoned, resting or abandoned, the dynasty declines. If it falls into Void, the sovereign tours without restraint; if it is clashed or broken, the borders are not secure; if it is punished or controlled, treacherous rebels rise in secret; if it is harmed, ministers and commoners lose heart. If Tai Sui generates a thriving palace, the state's fortune is blazing; if Tai Sui controls a weak trigram, the ancestral temple and altars of the state are about to fall.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.33** 大象，灾变：官鬼带亡神、劫杀，在内卦发动的，要提防奸细；官鬼带亡神、劫杀，暗中发动而伤身爻、世爻的，有阴谋。金爻的官鬼带大杀，临在旺相的宫里发动，兵祸起；水爻的，有洪水浸泡之灾；火爻的，旱魃肆虐；木爻的，庄稼受害；土爻的，瘟疫流行。官鬼落空而发动的，虽然凶也没有大害。
  The great image, disasters and upheavals. If Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves in the inner trigram, beware of spies; if Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves secretly to injure the Body and Self, there is a plot. If a Metal Pressure line carrying a great baleful spirit sits in a thriving palace and moves, war arises; if Water, flood disaster; if Fire, the drought demon rages; if Wood, the crops are blighted; if Earth, pestilence breaks out. If Pressure is Void and moves, though it is ominous it does no harm.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.34** 大象，方位、祸乱的阶段和迁徙：想知道事情在哪个方向，用八卦来定。官鬼和凶煞值胎、养、长生，祸乱刚开始；值临官、帝旺，祸乱正炽盛；值死、墓、绝，祸乱将要平息。离卦发动变坎卦，君主将要北狩；兑卦发动变震卦，想往东迁；坎卦发动变离卦，想往南渡；震卦发动变兑卦，必定西征。卦遇六合，安静的太平；遇六冲、乱动的，变故很多。
  The great image, direction, the stage of the disorder, and moving the court. To know in which direction, fix it by the eight trigrams. If Pressure and a baleful spirit are in the stages of Tai, Yang or Birth, the disorder is just beginning; in Official Post or Emperor's Peak, the disorder is at its height; in death, tomb or extinction, the disorder is about to cease. Li moving and turning into Kan: the sovereign will go on a northern hunt; Dui moving and turning into Zhen: he thinks of moving east; Kan moving and turning into Li: he wishes to cross to the south; Zhen moving and turning into Dui: he will surely march west. If the hexagram is a six-combine and still, there is peace; if it meets the six-clash and moves wildly, there are many upheavals.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.35** 国君的品性和寿数：世爻与五爻旺相，又在阳宫的阳爻上的，是明君；旺相而安静的，是有福的君主；带青龙、喜神、德神、合神、福、贵人而发动的，是仁君；加上刑杀，威严震动臣下和邻国；带白虎、官鬼、大杀、刑、刃，旺相发动的，暴虐无道，肆意诛杀夷灭；带玄武、咸池发动的，荒淫无度；带兄爻、耗神、羊刃发动的，多奢靡而不节制；带白虎、官鬼、死符、病符发动的，身体常常倦怠，懒于政事；休囚，又被太岁冲克，以及随着官鬼入墓的，不久就驾崩。
  The character and span of the sovereign. If the Self and the fifth line are thriving and sit on a yang line in a yang palace, he is a wise ruler; if thriving and still, a fortunate ruler; if they carry the Dragon, Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune and the Noble and move, a benevolent ruler; with punishing baleful spirits added, his majesty shakes ministers and neighbours; if they carry the Tiger, Pressure, a great baleful spirit, punishment and the blade, thriving and moving, he is cruel and lawless and gives free rein to executions and exterminations; if they carry the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi and move, he is dissolute without limit; if they carry a Peer line, the Waste spirit and the Goat Blade and move, he is extravagant and without restraint; if they carry the Tiger, Pressure, the Death Tally and the Illness Tally and move, he is often in poor health and tired of government; if they are resting or trapped and are clashed or controlled by Tai Sui, or follow Pressure into the tomb, he will not last long.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.36** 国君身边的祸患：土爻的官鬼旺相发动、伤世爻的，贼寇攻城；财爻带阴贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，后宫妇人和宦官专权，蛀蚀国家；父爻带禄、贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，功臣外戚尾大不掉；兄爻带阴贵人、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，皇亲国戚倚仗恩宠骄横放纵；子孙爻带华盖、大杀发动，伤世爻、五爻的，妖僧和方士蛊惑君主的心。
  Dangers about the sovereign. If an Earth Pressure line is thriving and moving and injures the Self, bandits attack the city; if the Wealth line carrying the Yin Noble and a great baleful spirit moves to injure the Self and the fifth line, women of the palace and eunuchs seize power and eat away at the state; if the Resource line carrying Emolument, the Noble and a great baleful spirit does so, the meritorious ministers and imperial in-laws become too big to control; if the Peer line carrying the Yin Noble and a great baleful spirit does so, the imperial kin presume on favour and are arrogant and unbridled; if the Output line carrying Canopy and a great baleful spirit does so, evil monks and alchemists bewitch the sovereign's mind.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.37** 后妃：应爻、二爻与世爻、五爻相生、相合、比和的，是好配偶；应爻克世爻，二爻克五爻的，皇后摘下环佩进言规劝；应爻、二爻带青龙、喜神、德神、合神、福、贵人，旺相的，是女中的尧舜；带玄武、咸池的，是艳妻煽动祸乱当权的格局；带父母爻旺相的，专攻书史；带子孙爻旺相的，像螽斯一样子孙众多；子孙爻发动生财爻的，能恩惠及下；应爻落在五爻的阳位上，又加太岁、紫微、华盖、贵人、驿马，旺相，都聚在一爻的，垂帘听政。
  Consort and empress. If the Other and the second line generate, combine or are in harmony with the Self and the fifth line, they are a fine match; if the Other controls the Self and the second line controls the fifth, the empress takes off her ring and gives admonition; if the Other and the second line carry the Dragon, Joy, Virtue, Combination, Fortune and the Noble and are thriving, she is a Yao and Shun among women; if they carry the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi, it is the pattern of the beautiful wife who stirs up trouble and holds sway; if they carry Resource thriving, she devotes herself to books and histories; if they carry Output thriving, her descendants are as many as katydids; if Output moves to generate Wealth, she is bounteous to those below; if the Other sits on a yang position of the fifth line with Tai Sui, Ziwei, Canopy, the Noble and the Horse added, thriving and all gathered in one line, she rules from behind the curtain.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.38** 太子（青宫）：以子孙为用神。三传（太岁、月建、日辰）、动爻生合子孙，或者子孙带青龙、德神、贵人、驿马、福、禄而旺相的，太子贤而有德；福神临在木爻上，生旺的，储君的德行必定深厚（青宫属木）；世爻生合子孙的，太子得到君主的眷顾；世爻冲、克、刑、害子孙的，太子违逆君主的心意；子孙爻临劫杀、大杀而发动的，太子必定被更换；子孙爻被四爻刑、害、克、破的，要提防篡逆；四爻带青龙、德神，生合子孙爻的，太子的师保贤明；四爻带玄武、官鬼、咸池，又合福神的，师保奸邪谄佞；子孙爻带咸池的，太子是偏宫所生。
  The Azure Palace, the heir apparent. Take Output as the Subject. If the three rulers or a moving line generate or combine with Output, or Output carries the Dragon, Virtue, the Noble, the Horse, Fortune and Emolument and is thriving, the heir is worthy and virtuous; if the fortune spirit sits on a Wood line and is born or thriving, the heir's stored virtue is surely deep (the Azure Palace belongs to Wood); if the Self generates or combines with Output, the heir wins the sovereign's regard; if the Self clashes with, controls, punishes or harms Output, the heir goes against the sovereign's mind; if Output sits on Robbery and a great baleful spirit and moves, the heir will surely be replaced; if Output is punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the fourth line, beware of usurpation; if the fourth line carrying the Dragon and Virtue generates or combines with Output, the heir's tutors are worthy; if the fourth line carrying the Dark Tortoise, Pressure and Xianchi combines with the fortune spirit, the tutors are wicked and flattering; if Output carries Xianchi, the heir is born of a secondary palace.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.39** 进谏的官员：以子孙为用神。子孙爻发动克世爻的，正直的臣子匡正、告诫君主；子孙爻发动刑、冲世爻的，正直的臣子冒死触犯君主的逆鳞；子孙爻发动合世爻的，谄谀的臣子顺从迎合。子孙爻临太岁、月建而发动，克四爻、生合五爻的，到子孙生旺的日子，必有正直的臣子弹劾权臣。子孙爻发动，克四爻、五爻，又得日辰生合的，君主准其奏章；与日辰刑、害、克、冲的，奏疏既不被允准，反而遭到贬官责罚。
  The remonstrating officials. Take Output as the Subject. If Output moves and controls the Self, an upright minister corrects and warns the sovereign; if it moves to punish or clash with the Self, an upright minister touches the dragon's reversed scales; if it moves to combine with the Self, a flattering minister goes along. If Output sits on Tai Sui or the Month and moves, controlling the fourth line and generating or combining with the fifth, then on a day when Output is born or thriving there will surely be an upright minister impeaching a powerful one. If Output moves and controls the fourth and fifth lines and the Day generates or combines with it, his memorial is approved; if the Day punishes, harms, controls or clashes with it, the memorial is not allowed and he is instead demoted and rebuked.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.40** 城池：以父母为用神。父母爻旺相又遇生合，城池坚固；休囚又遇伤克，防守疏漏；父爻下面伏着官鬼，或父爻发动变官鬼的，须提防奸细到城下。
  City walls and moats. Take Resource as the Subject. If it is thriving and meets generation or combination, the walls are firm; if it is resting or trapped and meets injury or control, the defence is lax; if Pressure lies hidden under the Resource line, or Resource moves and turns into Pressure, beware of spies at the city.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.41** 国库：以妻财为用神。财爻旺相又遇生合，国库丰盈、封存牢固；衰败、死、绝、落空，又遇刑、害、克、破，国库就空虚，像漏了的酒器一样不断外泄；兄爻旺相发动，多被侵吞蚕食；官鬼爻旺相发动，多有耗费。
  The treasury. Take Wealth as the Subject. If it is thriving and meets generation or combination, the treasury is full and securely sealed; if it is spent, dead, extinct or Void and meets punishment, harm, control or breaking, the treasury is empty, like a leaking vessel; if the Peer line is thriving and moving, there is much embezzlement; if the Pressure line is thriving and moving, there is much expenditure.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.42** 奸贼：以官鬼为用神。官鬼带凶煞和刃发动，在乾卦、坤卦的，祸乱起于宫中；在坎、离、震、兑的，祸乱起于四方。木爻的官鬼，是东方的寇盗；金爻的，是西方的贼；火爻的，是南方的盗；水爻的，是北方的酋首；土爻的，是四方散起的。
