  Traitors and bandits. Take Pressure as the Subject. If Pressure carrying baleful spirits and the blade moves in Qian or Kun, the trouble arises within the palace; in Kan, Li, Zhen or Dui, it arises in the four quarters. A Wood Pressure line is brigands from the east; Metal, rebels from the west; Fire, robbers from the south; Water, a chieftain from the north; Earth, risings in scattered places on all sides.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.43** 分官（一），按爻位看百姓、士人和官吏：初爻临官鬼，百姓有灾；落空，百姓有离心，生旺，百姓安居物产丰盛，衰弱受伤，百姓遭涂炭。二爻临官鬼，读书人有灾；落空，读书人不乐意归附；金爻落空而发动，读书人多乱发议论；生旺又加文昌，文运昌盛；衰弱受伤，读书人多受压抑。三爻持兄爻、官鬼，官吏多贪婪酷虐；落空，没有好官吏；生旺，带青龙、喜神、贵人、驿马、进神而发动的，多有善政传到上面，官员晋级、越级升迁；衰弱受伤，再加白虎、螣蛇、刃、劫杀、退神而发动的，多有贪污失德，被降级革职。
  Dividing the offices (1), reading the people, scholars and officials by line. When Pressure sits on the first line, the common people suffer; if it is Void the people lose heart, if thriving and born the people are at peace and goods abundant, if weak and injured the people fall into misery. When Pressure sits on the second line, the scholars suffer; if Void the scholars are unwilling to attach themselves; if a Metal line is Void and moves the scholars voice many wild opinions; if thriving and with Wenchang added, literary fortune flourishes; if weak and injured the scholars are often suppressed. When the third line holds a Peer line or Pressure, the officials are often greedy and cruel; if Void there are no good officials; if thriving and carrying the Dragon, Joy, the Noble, the Horse and an Advancing spirit and moving, there is much good government reported upward and officials are advanced and promoted out of turn; if weak and injured, with the Tiger, the Coiling Snake, the blade, Robbery and a Retreating spirit added and moving, there is much corruption and officials are demoted and dismissed.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.44** 分官（二），四爻公卿和六爻宗庙：四爻持官鬼，公卿多奸诈虚伪；落空，职位空缺，不称职；临在子孙爻上，多忠义之臣；子孙爻落空而发动，忠义之臣挂冠而去；生旺，带贵人、驿马、青龙、德神，又得四直（年月日时）生扶的，老成持重，内外四方都畏服；休囚，遇螣蛇、白虎、官鬼、凶煞、亡神、劫杀，又被四直刑、害、克、破的，模棱两可、尸位素餐，朝野失望。（五爻是天子，见前。）六爻旺相又逢生合，宗庙安定，朝廷肃穆安宁；发动、衰弱、受伤，鬼神震动惊恐，王法纲纪顿时松弛；落空，祭祀不举行，朝觐的礼废弛。
  Dividing the offices (2), the ministers of the fourth line and the ancestral temple of the sixth. When the fourth line holds Pressure, the ministers are often crafty and false; if Void, posts stand vacant and the holders are unequal to them; if it sits on Output, there are many loyal and righteous ministers; if Output is Void and moves, the loyal and righteous hang up their caps and leave; if thriving and born, carrying the Noble, the Horse, the Dragon and Virtue and supported by the four rulers, the elders are steady and weighty and the Chinese and foreign are awed; if resting or trapped, meeting the Coiling Snake, the Tiger, Pressure, baleful spirits, the Lost Spirit and Robbery and punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the four rulers, they are noncommittal and fill their seats, and court and country are disappointed. (The fifth line is the Son of Heaven, see above.) When the sixth line is thriving and meets generation or combination, the ancestral temple is secure and the court solemn and calm; if it moves, is weak or injured, the spirits are shaken and afraid and the royal rules and bonds suddenly slacken; if Void, sacrifices are not held and the rites of court audience fall into disuse.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.45** 《万金赋》穿断法，看君、官、民：五爻发动生初爻，君主忧虑百姓；三爻发动生初爻，官吏体恤百姓；四爻发动生初爻，宰相打动百姓；初爻生五爻，百姓拥戴君主；初爻合三爻、四爻，百姓服从官员；三爻、四爻、五爻带白虎、凶煞发动克初爻的，百姓受害；初爻冲克三爻、四爻、五爻的，百姓一同进谗毁谤。
  The Chuanduan method of the Wanjin Fu, reading ruler, officials and people. If the fifth line moves to generate the first, the sovereign worries for the people; if the third line moves to generate the first, the officials care for the people; if the fourth moves to generate the first, the minister moves the people; if the first generates the fifth, the people support the sovereign; if the first combines with the third and fourth, the people submit to the officials; if the third, fourth or fifth line carrying the Tiger and baleful spirits moves to control the first, the people suffer harm; if the first clashes with or controls the third, fourth or fifth, the people all slander.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.46** 《万金赋》穿断法，看君、士、臣：五爻生合二爻，君主礼敬士人；三爻、四爻生合二爻，臣下贤明；三爻、四爻合二爻又生五爻的，臣子向君主举荐贤才；二爻生三爻、四爻的，在上的人如影随形地追随；二爻临父母爻克五爻的，书生上书进奏；四爻生克三爻的，大臣能统率百官，提拔贤才、罢黜奸邪；三爻持财爻发动生世爻的，下面的人贿赂上面；五爻生合三爻，君主奖励、资助良吏；五爻生合四爻，君主把心思托付给执政大臣；四爻生合五爻，大臣竭诚报效君主。
  The Chuanduan method, reading sovereign, scholars and ministers. If the fifth line generates or combines with the second, the sovereign honours the scholars; if the third and fourth generate or combine with the second, the ministers are worthy; if the third and fourth combine with the second and generate the fifth, the ministers recommend the worthy to the sovereign; if the second generates the third and fourth, those above follow like a shadow; if the second sits on Resource and controls the fifth, a scholar presents a memorial; if the fourth generates or controls the third, the great minister can direct the hundred officials, promoting the worthy and dismissing the wicked; if the third holds Wealth and moves to generate the Self, those below bribe those above; if the fifth generates or combines with the third, the sovereign rewards and supports good officials; if the fifth generates or combines with the fourth, the sovereign entrusts his mind to the minister in charge; if the fourth generates or combines with the fifth, the minister gives his sincerity to repay the sovereign.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.47** 《万金赋》穿断法，看奸佞和忠良：四爻持螣蛇、白虎、兄弟、官鬼、亡神、劫杀、刑、刃，而五爻发动克它的，君主罢黜、远离奸佞；四爻持凶煞，而五爻发动生合它的，君主信任谗佞邪恶之人；四爻持金爻、白虎、官鬼、凶煞，发动克五爻的，大臣谋害君主，得日辰、动爻合住或制住它，大概没有大的祸害；四爻带玄武、咸池，生合五爻的，阿谀逢迎取悦君主；五爻持父母爻发动克四爻的，忠言逆耳；四爻加福德、将星，被五爻刑、害、克、破的，良将冤死。
  The Chuanduan method, reading traitors and the loyal. If the fourth line holds the Coiling Snake, the Tiger, a Peer line, Pressure, the Lost Spirit, Robbery, punishment and the blade, and the fifth moves to control it, the sovereign dismisses and keeps away the flatterers; if the fourth holds baleful spirits and the fifth moves to generate or combine with it, the sovereign trusts slanderers and the wicked; if the fourth holds Metal, the Tiger, Pressure and baleful spirits and moves to control the fifth, the great minister plots against the sovereign, and if the Day or a moving line combines with or restrains it, perhaps there is no great harm; if the fourth carries the Dark Tortoise and Xianchi and generates or combines with the fifth, it is flattery to win favour; if the fifth holds Resource and moves to control the fourth, loyal words grate on the ear; if the fourth adds Output and the General star and is punished, harmed, controlled or broken by the fifth, a good general dies wronged.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.48** 什么卦利于做国家的什么事（鄱阳汪所性的占例）：比、屯、豫利于分封建国；涣、萃利于祭告宗庙；益、随利于郊祭天；升利于封禅；晋适宜接受朝觐；屯、豫利于立君；豫、既济、师、离、谦利于出兵征伐；乾利于即位；师、解、巽利于田猎，比、屯、恒不利于田猎；涣利于发号施令；革利于改定历法；夬利于除去小人；师利于赏战功；家人适宜纳后；归妹适宜嫁妹；蛊利于求嗣；井利于迁改城邑；剥、遁适宜选宫嫔、宦官；鼎利于确定名位称号；姤、巽适宜颁布抚恤的诏书；大有、丰、泰，天下太平；否，国运艰难；大过，国祚危险。
  Which hexagram favours which act of state (the divination examples of Wang Suoxing of Poyang). Bi, Zhun and Yu favour enfeoffment; Huan and Cui favour reporting to the ancestral temple; Yi and Sui favour the suburban sacrifice to Heaven; Sheng favours the Feng and Shan sacrifices; Jin is fit for receiving court audiences; Zhun and Yu favour setting up a ruler; Yu, Jiji, Shi, Li and Qian (Modesty) favour military campaigns; Qian (Heaven) favours accession; Shi, Xie and Xun favour hunting, while Bi, Zhun and Heng are unfavourable for it; Huan favours issuing proclamations and orders; Ge favours changing the calendar; Guai favours removing petty men; Shi favours rewarding military merit; Jiaren is fit for taking an empress; Guimei is fit for marrying off a sister; Gu favours seeking an heir; Jing favours moving a city; Bo and Dun are fit for selecting palace consorts and eunuchs; Ding favours settling titles and ranks; Gou and Xun are fit for issuing edicts of relief; Dayou, Feng and Tai mean the world at peace; Pi means the state's course is hard; Daguo means the dynasty is in danger.

## 求职(Finding work: a tutor's post, an apprenticeship, hiring)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/求职.md`

- **LY.CAREER.EL.52** 求馆（谋求教书的馆席），以世爻、应爻为主，以妻财、子孙为凭据。求馆、受聘最怕世爻落空，应爻落空就遇不到东家；父爻落空，这个馆终究不是好馆；财爻陷入空亡，束修（馆金）很不丰厚。世爻、应爻都不落空，就必定有希望；如果变成冲散，就一定做不长久。六亲各取一象：世是自己，应是东家，父是书馆，子是书生，财是束修，鬼是荐馆的人，兄是同胞之士。落空各有所缺：世空遇不到尊重、听从自己的人，应空没有东家接纳，父空难遇好馆，财空束修菲薄，子空学生不多，鬼空无人举荐，兄空无人来争，身空住处不安；其余的空还可以稍微放过，只要世应不空，终究有希望。
  In seeking a school post, take the Self and the Other as the chief and Wealth and Output as the ground. To seek a post and be engaged, the Self falling Void is the thing most to be feared, and if the Other is Void one cannot meet the host; if Resource is Void the school is in the end no fine one; if Wealth is sunk into Void the fee is very meagre. If neither the Self nor the Other is Void there must be hope; if it changes into a scattering clash there will surely be no lasting end. The six kin each take one image: the Self is oneself, the Other the host, Resource the school, Output the pupils, Wealth the fee, Pressure the one who recommends a post, Peer the men of one's own kind. A Void in each leaves something lacking: if the Self is Void one meets no one who honours and follows one, if the Other is Void there is no host to take one in, if Resource is Void it is hard to meet a good post, if Wealth is Void the fee is slight, if Output is Void the pupils are few, if Pressure is Void no one recommends one, if Peer is Void no one competes, and if the Body is Void the place one lives is unsettled; the other Voids may be set aside somewhat, and if the Self and the Other are not Void there will in the end be hope.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.53** 凡是遇到六冲的卦，或者卦里没有官鬼，一定没有馆席；合的地方遇到冲，或者变出六冲，即使看着已经成了，那一年也一定做不到头。游魂卦，改换去别处另谋是吉；归魂卦，是守着旧馆，胜过东奔西走。所以问迁移更换的，游魂卦大为有利；占守旧馆的，反倒要归魂卦。
  Whenever the hexagram is a six-clash, or there is no Pressure line in it, there will surely be no post; if where there is a combination it meets a clash, or it changes out a six-clash, then even if the post seems to be settled, that year it will surely not last to the end. With a Wandering Soul hexagram, to move and change and plan for another post is auspicious; a Returning Soul hexagram is for the old post, better than going east and west. So for asking about moving to a change, a Wandering Soul is greatly favourable; for divining about keeping the old post, a Returning Soul is on the contrary what is wanted.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.71** 援监、纳吏，以及一切依照成例交银买官的，以官爻为主，以财爻为凭据，又都把世爻和官爻当主要来看，两个都不适宜落空：落空就办不成，即使办成也不长久。世爻落在旬空，不能长久；福神（子孙）安静而旺相，永远没有祸患。子孙虽然是福德，也不可以发动，一动就伤克官爻；也不可以没有，没有就少得利益。
  In purchasing a place in the Academy, purchasing a clerkship, and every case of buying office by paying silver according to the statute, take the Pressure line as the chief and the Wealth image as the ground, with the Self line and the Pressure line as the principal things, and neither should be Void: if Void it does not succeed, and even if it succeeds it does not last. If the Self falls on the ten-day Void it will not be long-lasting; if Output, the fortune spirit, is quiet and thriving there is never any calamity. Output, though it is Fortune Virtue, must not move, for if it moves it hurts the star of office; and it must not be absent, for without it there is little profit.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.72** 官鬼克制身爻，就遭责罚；财爻生世爻，就一定荣昌。身爻、世爻这两爻，如果被官鬼来冲克，反而受它的祸害；如果官鬼爻持世，再加上财爻发动来生它，一定名成利就。
  If Pressure controls the Body line one suffers reproof and punishment; if Wealth generates the Self image one will surely prosper. As for the Body and Self lines, if Pressure comes to clash or control them, one suffers its calamity instead; if the Pressure line holds the Self and Wealth moves to generate it, one will surely win name and profit.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.73** 官（夫）爻化成子孙，就难以出来做官；妻财变成兄弟，财路就被伤。官爻如果化成子孙，又难出仕；妻财如果变成兄弟，或者卦中兄爻发动，不但没有所得，反而主亏损受伤。
  If the husband line, Pressure, changes into Output, it is hard to come out into office; if Wealth, the wife, changes into Peer, the source of profit is hurt. If the Pressure line changes into Output it is again hard to come out into office; if Wealth changes into Peer, or a Peer line moves in the hexagram, one gains nothing, and on the contrary suffers loss and injury.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.74** 买官、纳吏、选阴阳官，以及僧官、道官、医官，都要仔细审察；僧官、道官、阴阳官、医官、使典这一类，都适宜官鬼旺相、财爻兴旺；爻里有官鬼没有财，有名无实；有财爻没有官鬼，名和利都落空。
  Buying office, buying a clerkship, being selected as a Yin-Yang official, and the monk, Daoist and medical officials, are all to be examined in detail; for monk-officials, Daoist officials, Yin-Yang officials, medical officials, clerks and the like, it is fitting that Pressure be thriving and Wealth flourishing; if there is Pressure among the lines but no Wealth, there is a name without substance; if there is Wealth but no Pressure, both name and profit are empty.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.75** 吏典，财爻、官爻适宜旺相；贡生，父爻、官鬼最忌空亡；入太学，父爻兴旺、官爻旺相的地方，一定声名显扬、传遍四方。恩贡、选贡、岁贡、监生这一类，虽然用的是官爻，还要凭父母爻，这两个象都不可落空；如果父爻、官爻有气，一定名传京城。
  For a clerk of the establishment, Wealth and the star of office should be thriving; for a tribute student, Resource and Pressure must avoid Void; for the Imperial Academy, where Resource flourishes and the star of office is thriving, fame will surely be made manifest and spread in all quarters. For the grace tribute, selected tribute, annual tribute, Academy students and the like, though the Pressure line is the one used, one must rely also on Resource, and these two images must not be Void; if Resource and the star of office have qi, the name will surely be known throughout the capital.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.76** 官员选缺、吏典参房，都以官爻和世爻为主来细看：这两个象里只要有一个遇到月破或旬空，官员就得不到这个缺，吏就不能参这个房。世爻即使不空，如果被发动的应爻、或发动的兄弟克了世爻，一定有同僚来抢夺，要提防兄弟兴旺、竞争激烈。
  For an official selected to a vacancy and a clerk joining a section, take the Pressure line and the Self line as the chief and examine them in detail: if either of these two images falls in Month-break or the ten-day Void, the official cannot obtain the vacancy and the clerk cannot join the section. Even if the Self is not Void, if the Other moves to control the Self, or Peer moves to control the Self, there will surely be colleagues scrambling for it, and one must be on guard against Peer flourishing and the competition being strong.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.77** 世爻如果遇到空、冲、绝，费尽辛勤劳苦，又怎么能兴旺？不遇六冲、官有气，就稳稳地得到好的官位，姓名远扬。世爻如果变到绝地，或者被日辰来冲，或者卜得六冲的卦，即使去谋求，也一定难以成功；世爻不空、官爻不陷，应爻和兄爻即使发动，只要不来克世，所求的一定遂意，也不会更改。
  If the Self meets Void, clash or extinction, however much toil one spends, how can one prosper? If one does not meet a six-clash and the star of office has qi, one will securely receive a fine rank and one's name will be known. If the Self changes into a place of extinction, or is clashed by the Day, or the hexagram cast is a six-clash, then even if one goes about scheming it will surely be hard to succeed; if the Self image is not Void and the Pressure line is not sunk, then even if the Other and the Peer line move, as long as they do not come to control the Self, what one seeks will surely be attained and there will be no change.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.78** 参房求利，适宜财爻旺相，官鬼之象不要来克世爻；选缺求名，怕的是福德（子孙）发动，以及随着官爻入墓，恐怕遭殃。参房和选缺看法不同：参房如果官和财都旺相，所得一定不寻常，官鬼克世身，难逃罪责；选缺也要官爻兴旺，最嫌子孙发动，官鬼克世身反而不必畏忌；只是遇到随官入墓，恐怕遭灾患。
  For joining a section and seeking gain, Wealth should be thriving and the Pressure image must not come to control the Self; for selection to a vacancy and seeking a name, what is dreaded is Output, the fortune virtue, moving, and following the star of office into the tomb, for fear of calamity. Judging joining a section differs from judging a selection to a vacancy: for joining a section, if the star of office and Wealth are both thriving, what is gained will surely be extraordinary, and if Pressure controls the Self or the Body one cannot escape blame; for a selection to a vacancy the star of office must also flourish and Output moving is most disliked, whereas Pressure controlling the Self or the Body need not be feared; but if one meets following the star of office into the tomb, calamity is to be feared.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.117** 要卖身投靠主人（鬻身投主），以世爻、应爻为主，以父母爻为凭据。命运不顺，必定要卖身来延续岁月；年成饥荒，还得投靠主人来度过早晚。要决断一生的事，应当寻找持世的爻：不破不冲，一百年都可以得到辅助；落空落陷，一年也难以相从。卖身投主的人是出于不得已，必须选择良善的主人：世爻旺相、得到生扶，可以勉强过活；世爻落空亡，或者遇到日冲、月破，虽然是暂时安顿，毕竟一生落寞，即使勉强维持，终究不久。
  In selling oneself into service under a master, take the Self and the Other as the chief and Resource as the ground. When one's fate runs against one, one must sell oneself to prolong one's days; in a famine year one must still take a master to get through morning and evening. To decide the affairs of a lifetime, one should seek the line that holds the Self: if it is neither broken nor clashed, a hundred years can be aided; if it falls to Void or into a pit, it is hard to follow even for one year. Those who sell themselves do so out of necessity and must choose a good and kind master: if the Self line is thriving and supported one can eke out a living; if the Self falls on Void, or meets the Day's clash or Month-break, then although one is temporarily settled, in the end one is desolate all one's life, and even if one makes do it will not last.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.118** 官鬼立在世爻上，灾殃缠身、多受烦扰；空亡临在应爻上，主人没有照顾自己的情意。招祸招灾的，无非是官鬼；如果它来持世，就灾生祸扰。应爻是主人的象，如果遇到空、绝，只能苟延岁月，没有养育厚待的情分。
  If Pressure stands on the Self, calamity entangles one's person with trouble; if Void rests on the Other, the master has no feeling of caring for oneself. What brings misfortune and disaster is nothing but Pressure, and if it comes to hold the Self then disaster arises and misfortune troubles one; the Other is the image of the master, and if it meets Void or extinction one can only drag out one's days, for there is no generous feeling of nurture.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.119** 父母爻生身，蒙受上辈的维持和宠爱；兄弟克世，有被同辈诽谤陷害的忧虑。父母爻是主人家里的长辈尊者，如果生身、生世，常常被青眼相看；兄弟是同辈的伙伴，如果来克世，他们就放肆地谋划嫉妒、妨碍。
  If Resource generates the Body, one receives the favour of support from one's elders; if Peer controls the Self, one has the worry of being slandered and trapped by one's fellows. Resource is the honoured elder of the master's household, and if it generates the Body or the Self, one is constantly looked on with a favourable eye; Peer are fellows of one's own rank, and if it comes to control the Self, they give free rein to jealous plots to hinder one.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.120** 世爻冲了父爻的位置，主仆没有缘分；财爻与身爻、官爻相合，日常起居有利。父爻是主人、世爻是我，不可互相伤害；如果遇到冲克，就像没有缘分、合不来，难以互相得利；财爻是衣食俸禄，如果生合世爻，衣禄就没有亏缺，利益资财有指望。
  If the Self clashes with Resource's position, master and servant have no affinity; if Wealth combines with the Body and the star of office, daily life is favourable. Resource is the master and the Self is me, and they must not injure each other; if they meet clash or control, it is as if there were no affinity and no accord, and it is hard to profit one another; Wealth is clothing and pay, and if it generates or combines with the Self line, clothing and pay will not be lacking and profit and funds can be hoped for.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.121** 子孙旺相、财爻分明，积蓄就多；兄爻兴旺、官鬼发动，就染上灾祸是非。子孙发动生财，财得子孙相助，就是积蓄丰余、源源不断；官鬼和兄爻一起发动，官鬼耗损、招官灾，就是灾祸生起、钱财散去，件件都不踏实。
  If Output is thriving and Wealth is clear, there are many savings; if Peer flourishes and Pressure moves, one is stained with calamity and wrangling. If Output moves to generate Wealth, with Wealth aided by Output, it means abundant savings flowing unceasingly; if Pressure and Peer move together, Pressure wastes substance and brings official disaster, so calamity arises and wealth scatters, and nothing proves solid.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.122** 官鬼发动、并同日辰一起伤克世爻、身爻，会遭天降的灾殃；父爻兴旺、与应位一起合财爻（妻室），会遭主人玷污。卦中最忌的是官鬼：如果它发动、又同日辰一起克世，须防不测的灾祸。财就是妻：如果父爻和应爻一起合财爻，就是主人和婢女淫乱混杂。
  If Pressure moves and, together with the Day, hurts the Self or the Body, one suffers a calamity sent by heaven; if Resource flourishes and, together with the Other's position, combines with Wealth, the wife line, one suffers dishonour from the master. What is most feared in the hexagram is Pressure: if it moves and, together with the Day, controls the Self, one must guard against an unforeseen disaster. Wealth is the wife: if the Resource line and the Other line together combine with Wealth, it means lewd mixing between the master and the maidservant.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.123** 多是多非，是因为动爻临朱雀；常来常往，是因为主人的象值游魂。朱雀主口舌，如果值动爻，就整天被吵闹、睡吃不安；游魂是游荡，如果卜得这个卦，就是流荡忘返、萍踪浪迹的人。再推之卦（变卦）的合冲，才能决断这件事从头到尾是远是近；「之卦」就是变卦，细细辨别其中的冲合，可以断一生的吉凶。
  Many quarrels and much wrangling are because a moving line sits on the Vermilion Bird; constant coming and going is because the master image falls on a Wandering Soul. The Vermilion Bird governs wrangling, and if it falls on a moving line one is clamoured at all day long and neither sleep nor food is in peace; the Wandering Soul is wandering, and if this hexagram is cast one is a drifter who wanders and forgets to return, like duckweed's trace and a wave's track. Then reckon the combination or clash in the changed hexagram, and only then decide how far or near it is from beginning to end; the 'zhi gua' is the changed hexagram, and by carefully distinguishing the clash and combination in it one can judge the good or ill of one's whole life.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.140** 学艺（学一门手艺、行业），以妻财为主，以世爻、应爻为凭据。行业没有大小、贤愚之分，哪能不拜师；事情有败有成、有去有留，还须占卜。凡是学三教一类的，师父如同伯叔：如果椿萱（父母爻）旺相，教法必定讲解详细；如果逢旺相，或者来合世、生身，必定得到全传。学各种技艺，先看兄弟爻，再看应爻，这两个象如果有一个空亡，就没有所承受。
  In learning a craft or art, take Wealth as the chief and the Self and the Other as the ground. No trade is great or small, wise or foolish, yet how can one not follow a teacher; matters have failure and success, leaving and staying, and one must still consult the oracle. In learning among the Three Teachings, the teacher is like an uncle: if Resource, the parents, is thriving, the method will surely be taught in detail; if it meets thriving, or comes to combine with the Self or generate the Body, one will surely receive the whole transmission. In learning the various crafts, look first at Peer and then at the Other line; if either of these two images is Void, there is nothing to be received.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.142** 靠本钱做生意谋生的，子孙发动、财爻分明，终究发达；靠空手手艺谋生的，财爻发动、官鬼旺相，必定兴家；给官僚当助手的，世爻怕官鬼爻冲克；在行会里开名头的，身爻适宜福德生扶。凡人学业各有各的分别：靠本钱的，财是主象，子孙是元神；空手的，官是用爻，财爻帮助主象；如果是在官府当差役的，虽然适宜官鬼兴旺，但官鬼冲克世身是大忌；牙行生意，最要子孙旺：旺就能广招千里的客人，空就门可罗雀；卦中虽然有子孙，应爻如果逢空，也没有客人上门。
  Those who live by trade with capital will in the end prosper if Output moves and Wealth is clear; those who live by bare-handed skill will surely make the household prosper if Wealth sways and Pressure is thriving; for one who serves as an assistant to officials, the Self fears the Pressure line clashing with or controlling it; for one who opens his name in a trade guild, the Body should be generated and supported by Output, the fortune virtue. In learning a business each person has his own division: for those with capital, Wealth is the chief image and Output the Support; for those with bare hands, the star of office is the Subject line and Wealth aids the chief image; if one serves as a runner in an office, though it is fitting for Pressure to flourish, Pressure clashing with or controlling the Self or the Body is the great taboo; in the brokerage trade, Output thriving is what matters most: if thriving one draws guests from a thousand miles, and if Void the gate is empty enough to net sparrows; even if there is Output in the hexagram, if the Other meets Void no customer comes.
- **LY.CAREER.HZL.01** 科举功名，是为了求进职，都以官爻为主，旺相就一定得到。凡是占赴考、谒见贵人、面见君主、参谒官员、到部里谋办事情的，看世爻上有没有文书（父母爻）：父母旺相，可以允许；官爻旺相，就吉；忌子孙持世，不中。占赴任，子孙持世或独发，必定做不满任期。为什么要看世爻上有没有文书？因为这是专以官为主、用爻为辅，所以要父母在世爻上。
  Examinations and a name in office are for seeking advancement in post, and the Pressure line is taken as the main one in all of them; if it is thriving it is certainly obtained. In divining about going to an examination, calling on a great man, seeing the sovereign, attending an official, or going to a ministry to see to business, look at whether there is the document line, Resource, on the Self: if the Resource line is thriving, it may be granted; if the Pressure line is thriving, it is auspicious; Output holding the Self is feared, and it does not hit the mark. In divining about going to take up a post, if Output holds the Self or moves alone, one will surely not complete the term. Why look at whether there is Resource on the Self? Because this takes the official alone as the master and the Subject line as the aid, and so one wants Resource on the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.29** 问馆（教书的馆席）有没有、成不成，喜六合、喜生世，恶三冲、恶应空。本章分十三问：在什么地方、什么时候、就馆、开馆、附学、代馆、辞馆、荐馆、攘馆、选文刻书、卜徒、幕馆、商馆。
  In asking whether a teaching post exists and whether it will come off, Six-Combine and generation of the Self are welcome, while the three clashes and a Void Other are hated. The chapter takes thirteen questions in turn: where, when, taking up a post, opening a school, pupils coming to attach themselves, standing in for another, resigning, recommending, snatching a post, choosing texts for cutting, divining a disciple, a secretary's post, and a merchant's post.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.35** 问选文刻书，用神有两个：求名用父母，求利用妻财。卜徒弟的好坏，用神也是两个：儒、释、道三教的师徒情谊同父子，所以用子孙；百工是为谋利学的，所以凭应象。不过执贽（行了拜师礼）之后，才有用子孙还是用应象的分别；束修还没送，就都从应象，这是三教、百工相同的。应克世，这个人矫饰欺罔；应空、破、散，这个人下劣；三冲，没有恒心；财空，没有礼金礼物。
  In asking about choosing texts and cutting blocks, the Subject is of two kinds: for fame Resource is used, for profit Wealth. In divining whether a disciple is good or bad the Subject is also of two kinds: the three teachings, Confucian, Buddhist and Daoist, have a bond of teacher and pupil like father and son, so Output is used; the hundred crafts are learned for gain, so the Other image is relied on. But it is after the formal presenting of gifts that there is the division between Output and the Other image; before the tuition gift is offered, all follow the Other image, and in this the three teachings and the hundred crafts are the same. The Other controlling the Self, and the person is deceitful; the Other Void, broken or scattered, and the person is base; the three clashes, and he has no constancy; Wealth Void, and he has no gift of money or goods.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.36** 占在衙门里做幕友，以及和富豪同业共事，看财、官、世、应，喜它们生、合，忌它们空、冲。占在什么地方遇到馆，就不取应爻，而取财：财长生的方位、财动所在的宫，才是可以去图谋的；财属哪一行，就是它的象。再参看六神、诸星。财动按宫来论，如财动在乾宫，宜往西北去；财静按长生来论，如财属金爻（金长生在巳，在东南），利涉东南；妻财临水，遇鱼盐一类的行业、江海之滨；妻财临木，遇花果的行当、山林之处；土是田野，火是城市，金是玉石；玄武主水、青龙主木，白虎主兵，朱雀主市，螣蛇主商，勾陈主医；天医主医使，华盖主僧从，都用财来考察。接受人家的聘请，以世应为先；替人家办事，以财利为务。
  In divining about a post as a secretary in a government office, or about working in the same trade as a rich man, look at Wealth, Pressure, the Self and the Other: it is good for them to generate and combine, and bad for them to be Void and clashed. In divining where one will meet a post, one does not take the Other line but takes Wealth: the quarter where Wealth is born and the palace where Wealth moves are where one can make plans, and the phase Wealth belongs to is its image. Then consult the six spirits and the stars. When Wealth moves, judge by the palace: if Wealth moves in the palace of Qian, go to the north-west; when Wealth is still, judge by Birth: if Wealth is a Metal line (Metal is born at Si, in the south-east), the south-east is favourable. Wealth on Water meets trades of fish and salt and the shores of river and sea; Wealth on Wood meets the flower-and-fruit trade and the mountain forest; Earth looks for fields, Fire for towns, Metal for jade and stone; the Dark Tortoise is water, the Azure Dragon is wood, the White Tiger is soldiers, the Vermilion Bird is markets, the Coiling Snake is merchants and Hook Earth is doctors; the Heavenly Doctor is physicians and messengers, the Canopy is monks and followers; all are examined by Wealth. In accepting a person's invitation the Self and the Other come first; in serving a person's affairs, Wealth and profit are the business.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.54** 补授官职、服满起复、依例捐纳的，要戒备世空，反而退缩不成。图差使、代役、选缺、参房，问成不成：冲不利图谋，世不利空，官不利坏；问财，就不利财虚；问受辱，就不利官克世。
  For being appointed to a vacant post, being recalled after mourning, or buying office by precedent, beware of the Self being Void, for one will on the contrary draw back. For seeking a commission, taking another's place in a duty, selecting a vacancy or entering the clerks' office, in asking whether it comes off: clash is unfavourable to the plan, Void is unfavourable to the Self, damage is unfavourable to Pressure; in asking about money, empty Wealth is unfavourable; in asking about disgrace, it is unfavourable for Pressure to control the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.95** 所以择师，同（延）师占：父临日辰、月建，是名师；世空，恐怕我不能受教。收徒传法，同继嗣占：子逢生旺，是法器（可造之材）；命墓，恐怕他不能传持（继承）。云游同出行占，但有访道和求利的区别，各自兼用相应的象。
  So choosing a teacher is divined like hiring a teacher: the father line on the Day or Month is a famous teacher; the Self Void, I fear I cannot be taught. Taking disciples and transmitting the teaching is divined like continuing a line of descent: the child line meeting generation and thriving is a vessel of the Dharma (a promising disciple); the life line in the tomb, I fear he cannot carry it on and transmit it. Wandering as a cloud is divined like travelling, but there is a difference between seeking the Way and seeking profit, and each also uses its corresponding image.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.79** 觅馆（找教书的馆），六亲的取象：世爻是自己，应爻是东家；父爻是书馆，子孙是学生，财爻是束修（馆金），官鬼是推荐人，兄弟是谋夺（这个馆）的人。世爻、应爻、父爻落空、绝的，没有馆；父爻值墓、胎，没有好馆；财爻空、墓、绝、胎的，束修菲薄；子孙空、墓、绝、胎的，学生少；官鬼空、墓、绝、胎的，没有推荐人；兄弟空、墓、绝、胎的，没有人来谋夺。
  Seeking a teaching post, the images of the six kin. The Self is oneself and the Other is the host; the Resource line is the schoolroom, Output the pupils, Wealth the fee, Pressure the one who recommends, and Peer the one who schemes to take the post. If the Self, the Other and the Resource line are Void or extinct, there is no post; if Resource meets tomb or womb, there is no good post; if Wealth is Void, in tomb, extinct or in womb, the fee is slight; if Output is Void, in tomb, extinct or in womb, the pupils are few; if Pressure is Void, in tomb, extinct or in womb, there is no one to recommend; if Peer is Void, in tomb, extinct or in womb, there is no one to scheme for the post.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.80** 觅馆，卦象和进退：游魂卦，宜另作打算；归魂卦，宜仍留旧馆；六冲卦没有馆，六合卦有馆；六合卦遇冲的，被人破坏；六合变六冲的，起初应承、最后拒绝。官星加贵人三合世爻、应爻的，有贵人推荐；兄弟发动克世爻的，有人争夺；财爻发动变兄弟的，推荐的人要分走束修；世爻发动变官鬼的，要央求别人转荐；官鬼发动变兄弟的，推荐人先索要谢礼；子孙爻合而遇冲的，学生聚了又散；子孙衰弱而得到帮助的，学生起初少、后来增多。
  Seeking a teaching post, the hexagram and advance or retreat. In a Wandering Soul hexagram it is fit to look elsewhere; in a Returning Soul hexagram, to stay in the old post; a six-clash hexagram has no post and a six-combine hexagram has one; a six-combine meeting a clash is broken by someone; a six-combine changing into a clash is agreed at first and refused in the end. If the star of office with the Noble forms a three-harmony with the Self and Other, a patron recommends; if Peer moves to control the Self, someone contends for the post; if Wealth moves and turns into Peer, the one who recommends takes a share of the fee; if the Self moves and turns into Pressure, one must ask another to pass it on; if Pressure moves and turns into Peer, the recommender first demands a gift of thanks; if Output combines and meets a clash, the pupils gather and then scatter; if Output is weak but helped, the pupils are few at first and grow later.

## 应期(Timing: when the post comes, changes or ends)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/应期.md`

- **LY.CAREER.CA.04** 占得坎、离二卦，宜在冬季三个月（坎阳离阴，水火相济）；占得震、巽二卦，应在申、酉年——震、巽属木，得申、酉的金作官，官星旺。
  With the two trigrams Kan and Li, the three winter months suit (Kan is yang and Li is yin, water and fire balancing each other); with Zhen and Xun, look to the years of Shen and You, for Zhen and Xun are Wood and the Metal of Shen and You gives them a strong official.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.12** 问求官在什么时候，答：以扶助官鬼的时候作为期限；官鬼值火的时候，应验得快；如果碰上水的日子，事情就迟。
  Asked when the office will come, the answer is: take the time that supports Pressure as the term; when Pressure meets a Fire time the answer is quick, and if it meets a Water day the matter will be slow.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.14** 要知道得官的年、月、日期：有爻持着官鬼，就以那一爻的时间为期；没有这样的爻，就取官鬼得生、得旺的时候；旺相就快，休囚就迟。先看本宫，以旺的为正，比如震取春分，其余照此类推。
  To know the year, month and term when the office is gained: if a line carries Pressure, that line's time is the term; if there is none, take the time when Pressure is born or strong; strong, it is quick, and at rest or trapped, slow. First look at the palace of the hexagram, taking the strong as the standard, for Zhen for example the Spring Equinox, and the rest likewise.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.15** 占迁官（调任升迁）的日月期限，举例：占得纯乾卦，日月是寅，戌土持世，官鬼爻午火得生、旺相，断定正月、五月得官调动，没有疑问。
  For the day and month of a transfer of office, an example: with the hexagram pure Qian, the day and month at Yin, Xu Earth holding the Self, and the Pressure line Wu Fire born and strong, one judges that the first and fifth months will bring the transfer, without doubt.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.54** 要知道哪一天定下这个馆，要细看父母爻：卦里有父爻而且旺相，取它入墓、逢合的时候；父爻衰弱，取它旺相、得生的日子；卦里没有父爻，要等到它当值的那一天才算在。还要看哪一天生世爻，世爻落空的也可以在那一天成。到了扶助、生合世爻的那一天，宾（师）主（东家）两方就会相见。
  To know on which day the engagement is settled, one must look closely at Resource: if Resource is in the hexagram and thriving, take the time when it enters the tomb or meets a combination; if it is weak, take the day when it thrives or is generated; if it is not in the hexagram, it is present only when the day on which it is on duty comes. Look also for the day that generates the Self; even a Void Self can then be settled. On the day that aids, generates or combines with the Self, guest and host come to meet each other.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.61** 要知道官职在哪个月升，遇到官爻当值、得生的月份，就有喜报。要断定什么时候迁转，就推断官鬼的旺衰：官鬼旺相，高升就在眼前；衰弱，就等它得生、旺相的时候。如果六爻里没有官鬼，再查哪个月临到官，就能知道升迁的决断了。
  To know in which month one is promoted, when the line meets its own month or the month that generates it, joyful news is reported. To decide when the promotion or transfer will come, weigh the strength of Pressure: if it is thriving, the high promotion is near at hand; if weak, wait for the time when it is generated or thriving. If there is no Pressure among the six lines, then check which month falls on the star of office, and one will know the decision of the promotion.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.85** 占官凭（官员的委任凭据），只要文书爻逢上旺相的气；占凭据哪一天到，要远远去找父母爻遇上生它的时候。另外附带占文凭到的日子，要看父母爻，等它临到生、旺的时候，这份凭据一定到；卦里没有父爻，要等到父爻当值的日子，凭据才会来；父爻如果衰弱又落空，即使等候，凭据也还没有到。
  To divine about the official credentials, the document line need only meet a thriving qi; to divine on what day they arrive, look far for Resource meeting the time that generates it. Also appended: for the day the credentials arrive, look at the Resource line and wait for the time when it meets generation or thriving, and the credentials will surely arrive; if there is no Resource in the hexagram, one must wait for the day when the Resource line is on duty, and only then will the credentials come; if Resource is weak and also Void, even if one waits the credentials have not arrived.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.09** 占国运能延续多久，以太岁为纪年：有太岁就是一位一元，再有也是一位一元；但必须考察本宫的旺衰、官鬼之象的动静，才能推定。太岁管年历国运：比如己亥年，得丁亥太岁，有六元的运数，还要用内卦参看，以官鬼发动来权衡，才可以推。
  To divine how long the dynasty will last, take the Year as the reckoning: if the Tai Sui is present it is one position, one cycle; with another it is again one position, one cycle; but one must still examine the strength or weakness of the home palace and the movement or stillness of the Pressure image before it can be reckoned and settled. The Year governs the calendar and the dynasty's fortune: for example, in a Jihai year, getting the Dinghai Tai Sui, there is a destiny of six cycles, and one also consults the inner trigram and weighs it by Pressure moving, and only then can it be reckoned.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.31** 问什么时候能得到馆，看填实世爻（世爻落空时）、生世、合世的日子，那就是它的日期。
  In asking when a post will be obtained, the days that fill the Self, generate the Self or combine with the Self are its date.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.43** 它什么时候升？以官爻自己的月份（值月）为法：逢生旺就会被会推（联名推举），遇扶助就高升。再占确切的日期：世爻实、官爻崇旺，就不是别的日子，就是那一天。如果有两个官爻、一强一弱，那个弱的值月就升，例如亥月壬戌日占调迁得丰卦，到季冬才升。只有一个官爻，一定要求它值月，可以占验；官在长生、临官而有气，也可以升迁，生它、助它的月份也一样，但都不如值月最好，长生次之。
  When will the promotion come? The rule is to take the month in which the Pressure line is on duty (its own month): meeting generation and thriving, one is put forward by joint recommendation; meeting support, one is raised high. To divine again for the exact date: when the Self is solid and Pressure exalted, it is no other day, but that very one. If there are two Pressure lines, one strong and one weak, the promotion comes when the weak one meets its month; for example in a Hai month on a Ren-Xu day, divining about a transfer, one got Feng, and the promotion came only in the last month of winter. With a single Pressure line one must seek its own month, and it can then be tested; if it is at Birth or Taking Office with force, one can also be promoted, and the months that generate and support it are the same, but none is as good as its own month, with Birth second.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.93** 哪一年道成？世临日、月的时期。哪个地方法事兴隆？世临生合的地方。卦象乱动，不是清修之象；游魂，没有戒定（守戒、入定）的心。道业成就自身，专凭世的位置；世病、乱动、游魂，都忌。
  In which year is the Way accomplished? In the period when the Self is on the Day or the Month. In which place does the teaching flourish? In the place where the Self meets generation and combination. A hexagram of disorderly movement is not the image of pure practice; Wandering Soul has no heart of precepts and meditation. The monastic vocation making the man depends wholly on the position of the Self; a sick Self, disorderly movement and Wandering Soul are all feared.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.08** 想知道哪一天得官：官星旺相发动的，远的以它当值的年、合它的月为期，近的以当值的月、合它的日为期；官星旺相而安静的，以冲动它的年、月、日为期；官星伏藏的，以它当值的年、月、日为期；官星临火发动，选得快，临水发动，选得慢。
  To know on what day one gets office: if the star of office is thriving and moving, a distant date is set by the year it rules and the month that combines with it, and a near date by the month it rules and the day that combines with it; if it is thriving but still, the date is the year, month or day that clashes it into motion; if it lies hidden, the date is the year, month or day in which it rules; if the star of office moves on Fire the selection is quick, and if on Water, slow.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.09** 再看卦宫来定选官的节气：卦宫是艮的，立春以后选；震，春分以后；巽，立夏以后；离，夏至以后；坤，立秋以后；兑，秋分以后；乾，立冬以后；坎，冬至以后。
  Look also at the palace of the hexagram to fix the solar term of the selection: if the palace is Gen, the selection comes after the start of spring; Zhen, after the spring equinox; Xun, after the start of summer; Li, after the summer solstice; Kun, after the start of autumn; Dui, after the autumn equinox; Qian, after the start of winter; Kan, after the winter solstice.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.24** 被人替代：官星旺相的，到官星遇死、墓、绝的日子被人替代；子孙旺相发动的，立刻就可以被替代；卦里没有官星的，到官星绝的日子可以被替代；外卦是阳的，替代得快，是阴的，替代得慢。占接替自己的官员什么时候来，以应爻生旺、世爻入墓入绝的日子为期，或者以官爻生旺的月、日为期。
  Replacement. If the star of office is thriving, he is replaced on the day when it meets death, tomb or extinction; if Output is thriving and moving, he can be replaced at once; if the hexagram has no star of office, he can be replaced on the day of its extinction; if the outer trigram is yang the replacement is quick and if yin, slow. To divine when the official who will take over comes, take as the term the day when the Other is born or thriving and the Self goes into tomb or extinction, or the month or day when the Pressure line is born or thriving.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.28** 官职退复，失去职位：子孙旺相发动，父爻休囚落空，官星入墓入绝的，必定退职。想知道哪年哪月退职，只以卦身为主：从卦身所在的地支顺数到占卜的那个月，数过几个位置，就失职几个月——丑月占得噬嗑，卦身在戌，戌、亥、子，三位，失职三个月；午月占得艮，卦身在巳，一位，一个月；辰月占得蛊，卦身在寅，两位，两个月。其余照这样推。
  Loss and recovery of office, losing the post. If Output is thriving and moving, the seal line is resting and Void, and the star of office is in tomb or extinct, he surely loses his post. To know the year and time of losing it, take the hexagram Body as the main thing: count from the branch where the Body lies to the month of the divination, and the number of places passed is the number of months of the post lost. In a Chou month, getting Shihe with the Body at Xu: Xu, Hai, Zi, three places, the post is lost for three months; in a Wu month, getting Gen with the Body at Si: one place, one month; in a Chen month, getting Gu with the Body at Yin: two places, two months. The rest follow this.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.49** 占国家传几世、延续几年（易学主青）：天子自己占，以世爻为主。世爻发动变出的，就从世爻数到变爻，来定他传几世、几年：比如纯乾卦世爻壬戌发动，变成泽天夬卦的丁未爻，就从壬戌数到丁未，是四十六世，年数就是四十六年。卦变了、世爻不动的，就用本卦和变卦这两个世爻来数：比如乾变姤，从壬戌数到辛丑世爻，是四十世，年数就是四十年。六爻安静的，就从占卜那一年数到世爻：比如庚寅年占，从庚寅数到壬戌，是三十三世，年数就是三十三年。臣子百姓代占的，以五爻为主：比如甲子年占得乾卦，五爻是壬申，从甲子数到壬申，占世是九世，占年是九年。其余照这样推。
  Divining how many reigns or years the dynasty will last (Yixue Zhuqing). When the Son of Heaven divines for himself, take the Self as the chief. If the Self line moves and changes, count from the Self line to the changed line to fix the number of reigns and years: for example, in pure Qian the Self line Renxu moves and turns into the Dingwei line of Guai (Marsh over Heaven); count from Renxu to Dingwei, and it is forty-six reigns, and the years are forty-six. If the hexagram changes but the Self does not move, count between the two Self lines of the original and the changed hexagram: Qian changing into Gou, from Renxu to the Xinchou Self line, is forty reigns and forty years. If all six lines are still, count from the year of the divination to the Self line: divining in a Gengyin year, from Gengyin to Renxu is thirty-three reigns and thirty-three years. When ministers and commoners divine on his behalf, take the fifth line as the chief: divining in a Jiazi year and getting Qian, the fifth line is Renshen, and from Jiazi to Renshen is nine reigns and nine years. The rest follow this.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.72** 问文书什么时候可以拿到：文书爻旺相的，等它入墓的月日；衰弱的，等它生旺的月日；旺相发动的，等逢合的月日；旺相安静的，等冲动它的月日；文书爻伏藏的，等文书当值的月日才能得到。
  To ask when a document can be had: if the document line is thriving, wait for the month or day it enters tomb; if weak, wait for the month or day it is born or thriving; if thriving and moving, the month or day it meets a combination; if thriving and still, the month or day that clashes it into motion; if the document line lies hidden, it is had in the month or day in which the document rules.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.81** 想知道聘书（关约）什么时候来：以父母爻为用神。卦里没有父爻的，在（父爻）死、当值的日子来；父母爻旺相的，在合它的日子来；衰弱的，在它生旺的日子来；空、墓的，在冲它的日子来。又，父母爻生世爻、合世爻的日子，也可以成关、得到聘约。
  To know when the engagement (contract) will come: take Resource as the Subject. If the hexagram has no Resource line, it comes on the day it dies and rules; if Resource is thriving, on the day that combines with it; if weak, on the day it is born or thriving; if Void or in tomb, on the day that clashes with it. Again, on the day Resource generates or combines with the Self, the engagement can also be made and obtained.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.88** 快慢：以用爻为主。发动的快，安静的慢；旺相、出现的快，囚、死、伏藏的慢；阳变阴的快，阴变阳的慢；发动临在震、巽的快，临在坎、艮的慢；用爻临卯、酉的快（卯酉是日月出入的门户），临辰、戌的慢（辰戌是天罗地网，是天涯海角）；初爻、二爻发动的快，三爻、四爻发动的犹豫迟疑，五爻发动的慢，六爻发动的更慢。用爻临太岁，不出一年；临月将，不出一月；临日辰，就在当日；临日建，就在当时。尹逢头的断法说：用爻旺相，还要等入墓的日子来定；用爻休囚，在生旺的日子应当成；用爻伏藏，只看用爻当值的日子；发动而被合住的，等冲它的日辰。
  Slow or fast. Take the Subject line as the chief. Moving is quick, still is slow; thriving and appearing is quick, imprisoned, dead and hidden is slow; yang changing into yin is quick, yin changing into yang is slow; moving on Zhen or Xun is quick, on Kan or Gen is slow; the Subject on Mao or You is quick (Mao and You are the gates of sun and moon), on Chen or Xu is slow (Chen and Xu are the net of heaven and the snare of earth, the ends of the earth); a first or second line moving is quick, a third or fourth moving hesitates and delays, a fifth moving is slow, and a sixth moving slower still. If the Subject sits on Tai Sui, it is within a year; on the Month General, within a month; on the Day, that same day; on the Day branch, that very hour. The Chuan Yin Feng method of judgment says: if the Subject is thriving, fix it by the day it enters tomb; if resting, it should be done on the day it is born or thriving; if hidden, look only at the day the Subject rules; if moving and held by a combination, wait for the day that clashes it.

## 取象(Reading the images: what kind of post, which office, which place)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/取象.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.09** 用官鬼做用神，最好它伏在父母爻下；官鬼伏在子孙爻下，就一定没有成就；伏在兄弟爻下，怕有阻隔的阻挠。用父母做用神伏在妻财下都是祸患。
  When Pressure is the Subject, it does best hidden under Resource; hidden under Output, it will have no accomplishment; hidden under Peer, one fears the hindrance of obstruction. When Resource is the Subject, hidden under Wealth is all trouble.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.02** 求官时，初爻代表吏；官鬼落在初爻、来克世身，说明这人将来是廉明、慈惠、贤明的官员。
  In seeking office the first line stands for the clerk; Pressure on the first line, coming to control the self, marks one who will be an upright, merciful and discerning official.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.17** 要知道卦里是做什么官，先仔细看身：火是文章、喉舌之位（翰林一类）；土是守、令（修治城垒）；水是江边巡守的职事；金带兵、刑和生杀之权；木主工科，管建造创设；官爵大小都随动爻的休囚旺相来说。
  To know what office the hexagram holds, first look carefully at the Body: Fire is the place of letters and of the throat and tongue of the court (an office of the Hanlin kind); Earth is the prefect or magistrate (repairing city walls); Water is a patrol-and-guard post along rivers and shores; Metal carries soldiers, punishments and the power of life and death; Wood governs the works section, in charge of building and founding; and the rank, high or low, follows whether the moving line is thriving or weak.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.30** 爻乱动而去坐关（闭关），是大忌；八纯卦倒好去参玄；艮、坤只能栖身岩穴山谷；离、巽适合隐居市井；龙虎交驰，适合访道；坎、离汇聚，适合安心坐禅；游魂卦带杀，别去云游；一世卦遇休囚，不要置买田产。
  With lines moving in disorder, going into retreat is the great taboo; an Eight-Pure hexagram is on the contrary good for studying the mysteries; Gen and Kun suit only a dwelling among crags and ravines; Li and Xun suit hiding in the marketplace; the Dragon and Tiger racing against each other suit seeking the Way; Kan and Li converging suit settling into meditation; a Wandering Soul hexagram with a baleful spirit, do not go wandering like cloud and water; a First-Generation hexagram resting or trapped, do not buy fields.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.01** 占仕宦，按鬼谷子的爻位法：初爻是吏人，二爻是曹官，三爻是长官，四爻是监司，五爻是朝仕（在朝的官），六爻是执政。
  In divining official careers, by the Guigu line-position method the first line is the clerk, the second the bureau officer, the third the head of the office, the fourth the supervising commissioner, the fifth the court official and the sixth the minister in power.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.07** 要知道卦里是做什么官，先仔细看身：火是文章、喉舌的位置；土是守、令；水是排岸、监司一类的职事；金代表兵、刑，握有重权；木是工部和作院（军器作坊）；官职小大，都随爻的旺相来说。
  To know what office the hexagram holds, first look carefully at the Body: Fire is the place of letters and of the throat and tongue of the court; Earth is the prefect and the magistrate; Water is a post such as embankment supervisor or commissioner; Metal stands for soldiers and punishments and holds heavy power; Wood is the Ministry of Works and the arsenal; and whether the post is small or large is said according to strength.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.33** 占趋谒（拜谒贵人），按鬼谷子的爻位法：初爻是乡里的贵人，二爻是县里的贵人，三爻是州里的贵人，四爻是朝中的贵人，五爻是内廷的贵人，六爻是大贵人。
  In divining a call on a noble, by the Guigu line-position method the first line is the noble of the township, the second the noble of the county, the third the noble of the prefecture, the fourth the noble of the court, the fifth the noble of the inner court and the sixth the great noble.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.47** 占求事（谋事），按鬼谷子的爻位法：初爻是心里的事，二爻是自身的事，三爻是家里的事，四爻是人事往来，五爻是官府的事，六爻是国家的事。
  In divining an undertaking, by the Guigu line-position method the first line is the matter of the heart, the second a personal matter, the third a household matter, the fourth dealings with people, the fifth a matter of the officials and the sixth a matter of the state.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.141** 做金、银、铜、钱的工匠，遇到乾、兑、酉、申最吉；做竹、木、芦、藤的匠人，得到震、巽、寅、卯为好；制造、贩卖鱼、盐、酒、醋，利益进入坎宫；裁制衣袖、绢帛、绫罗的，喜欢遇到离象；采石、上山砍柴，须见艮；做土工、泥作，要逢坤。
  For metalworkers in gold, silver, copper and coin, meeting Qian, Dui, You and Shen is the most auspicious; for craftsmen in bamboo, wood, reed and rattan, getting Zhen, Xun, Yin and Mao is good; in making and selling fish, salt, wine and vinegar, profit enters the Kan palace; for cutting garments and silks, one is glad to meet the Li image; for quarrying stone and cutting wood on the mountain one must see Gen; for earthwork and plastering one must meet Kun.
- **LY.CAREER.EL.143** 学经书，父爻必须生旺；求官职，官鬼不可空无；祝巫大利官爻兴旺，僧道只适宜子孙旺。学儒业的，父母爻是用爻；求功名的，官鬼是用爻；祝巫一类，迎神召将、照马、关亡之类，也用官爻；只有僧道一流，以子孙为用。凡是用爻都适宜旺相，各自忌空亡：旺就事事有成，空就样样不成。各种道术、一切生计，都把财当作衣食俸禄、养命的源头，并且要推子孙是福神、发家的主；各种艺业，都适宜财神（财爻）兴隆；财如果空亡，利润资财一定没有指望了。
  In studying the classics, Resource must be thriving; in seeking office, Pressure must not be empty; for diviner-priests and shamans it is greatly favourable for the star of office to flourish, and for monks and Daoists only Output thriving is fitting. For the scholarly calling, Resource is the Subject line; for those seeking fame and office, Pressure is the Subject; for shamans, those who greet gods and summon generals, 'look at the horse' and 'close the gate on the dead' and the like, the Pressure line is also used; only for the monks and Daoists is Output the Subject. Every Subject line should be thriving and each fears Void: if thriving everything succeeds, and if Void nothing is accomplished. For every kind of Daoist art and every livelihood, Wealth is taken as the source of food, clothing, pay and the sustaining of life, and Output must also be reckoned as the fortune spirit and the master of making a household prosper; in every kind of art and trade it is fitting for Wealth to flourish, and if Wealth is Void, profit and funds are surely without hope.
- **LY.CAREER.HDY.01** 父母爻代表尊长、文书、吏员，印绶、衣服、轿车船，契约、本事，还有文章、印绶、官职事业、图籍号令、差遣的札子和条文敕令；官鬼爻代表神邪、疾病、鬼祟、盗贼，失物、输赢、灾祸，还有君主、职位清要、公庭。
  The Resource line stands for elders, documents and clerks, the seal and ribbon, clothing, sedan chairs, carts and boats, contracts and skills, and also essays, the seal and ribbon, office and career, maps and registers, orders and commands, dispatch notes, regulations and decrees; the Pressure line stands for spirits and evil, illness, hauntings and thieves, lost things, winning and losing and disaster, and also the sovereign, clear and weighty posts, and the public court.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.19** 父母旺相，衙门一定大；休囚，衙门一定小。官鬼旺而父母衰，不是小去处，而是冷落清闲的衙门，因为官旺所以职位高；官鬼衰而父母旺，职位虽然卑微，却在大衙门里办事；官和父都衰，职位低、衙门小，必定不是风宪之地。
  If Resource is thriving, the office is surely large; if resting or trapped, the office is surely small. With Pressure thriving and Resource weak, it is not a small place but a cold and quiet office, the post being high because Pressure is thriving; with Pressure weak and Resource thriving, the post, though lowly, is in a large office; with both Pressure and Resource weak, the post is low and the office small, and it is surely not a place in the censorate.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.20** 官鬼旺相而不临月建，一定不是风宪的职位；如果临月建，又得到扶助而出现在世爻上，一定是风宪的任命，必不是府县的官，再在日辰的生旺之地，更是风宪；如果带刑爻，多是镇守边陲的职位，或者掌握兵权，或者在刑部，在外地也是主管刑狱的职位。
  If Pressure is thriving but does not rest on the Month, it is surely not a post in the censorate; if it rests on the Month and is supported so as to appear on the Self, it is surely an appointment in the censorate and not an officer of a prefecture or county, all the more if it also stands in a thriving place of the Day; if it carries a punishment line, it is mostly a post guarding the frontier, or holding military power, or in the Board of Punishments, and outside the capital a post in charge of punishments.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.21** 旧注说：官鬼在世爻、应爻上，或者带月建、日辰的，必是掌印的正官；如果被世爻合住，或者在旁边的爻上，就是佐贰的职位；六爻没有官鬼而动爻化出官鬼的，也是这样。王洪绪说：官鬼临子午卯酉是正任的官，临寅申巳亥是佐贰的职官，临辰戌丑未是杂职官；如果临月建、日辰，就是掌印的官。
  The old commentary says: if Pressure is on the Self or the Other, or carries the Month or the Day, it is surely the principal officer who holds the seal; if it is combined by the Self or is on a side line, it is a post as an assistant; and if there is no Pressure among the six lines but a moving line turns into Pressure, it is the same. Wang Hongxu says: Pressure on Zi, Wu, Mao and You is a principal officer, on Yin, Shen, Si and Hai an assistant post, and on Chen, Xu, Chou and Wei a miscellaneous post; and if it rests on the Month or the Day, it is the officer who holds the seal.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.27** 僧官、道官、医官和阴阳官，都要子孙出现、有气不空才吉。父母发动，必有灾祸悔恨；父母带太岁、月建、日辰，就不是自身有灾病，而是外来的祸；子孙自己空亡，也有大难；但子孙只适宜安静，官鬼不适宜空伏，两样都要顾到，要仔细。王洪绪说：僧官、道官、医官都以子孙为用，父母发动就伤害他们，用药不灵，反而不好。
  Monk-officials, Daoist officials, physician-officials and officials of yin-yang divination all need Output to appear, with breath and not Void, to be auspicious. If Resource moves, there will surely be calamity and regret; if Resource carries the Tai Sui, the Month or the Day, it is not calamity or illness in oneself but a misfortune from outside; if Output is Void by itself, there is also a great calamity; but Output should only be still and Pressure should not be Void or hidden, and both must be satisfied, with care. Wang Hongxu says: monk-officials, Daoist officials and physician-officials all take Output as the Subject, and if Resource moves it injures them, medicines do not work and on the contrary it is bad.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.30** 占馆，应爻代表聘请先生的东家，看应爻临什么来断这家人：临官爻，是官吏、户役人家（加白虎有病人，加玄武与盗贼往来）；临父母，加勾陈是种田人家，加朱雀是读书人家，加白虎是宰杀人家，加螣蛇是工艺人家；临子孙属金，是僧道作主；临财爻在阴宫阴爻又没有官鬼的，是妇人作主，有官鬼就是富贵人家，财化财、财化子是做买卖的人家，被月建日辰动爻制伏的是奴仆作主；临兄弟，是平常人家，加朱雀是赌博人家。
  In divining a school post, the Other stands for the host family that engages the teacher, and one judges the household from what the Other rests on: on Pressure, an official's or clerk's household (with the White Tiger, a sick person; with the Dark Tortoise, dealings with thieves); on Resource, a farming household with Hook Earth, a scholars' household with the Vermilion Bird, a slaughterers' household with the White Tiger, a craftsmen's household with the Coiling Snake; on Output belonging to Metal, a monk or Daoist is the master; on Wealth in a yin palace and on a yin line with no Pressure, a woman is the master, with Pressure a rich and noble household, with Wealth turning into Wealth or Output a trading household, and if subdued by the Month, the Day or a moving line, a servant is the master; on Peer, an ordinary household, with the Vermilion Bird a gambling household.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.31** 应爻临临官、帝旺，主人必定年壮强盛，临墓库必是高龄；应爻的五行没有气，他家贫乏，旺相就富厚，再加上财爻、子孙，必然是巨富；他的品德性情，要按五类和六神一起参断。
  If the Other rests on the Official-Post or Peak stage, the master is surely in his prime and strong, and if on a tomb he is surely advanced in years; if the element of the Other has no breath, his family is poor, if thriving, rich, and with Wealth and Output added he is surely enormously rich; his character is to be judged by the five relations and the six spirits together.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.32** 一个卦里系着两个人时，两人的六亲要用相生的道理来定：占馆以应为东家，应爻属土，火生土，不论卦里有没有火、也不论是哪一类六亲，都当作他的父母；应爻属金，金生水，水就当作他的子孙。火爻旺而空、冲空、动空，父母不全，衰而空就没有父母；火化火有两重父母；带官鬼旺相是贵，衰是病，带兄弟是好赌、无廉耻，带财是做买卖，带子孙是慈善。不能把六爻总起来看，在应宫的爻是对方的六亲，在世宫的爻是我的六亲。
  When two persons are tied to one hexagram, their relations are fixed by the principle of generation: in divining a school post the Other is the host, and if the Other belongs to Earth, Fire generates Earth, and whether or not there is Fire in the hexagram and whatever relation it is, it is taken as his parents; if the Other belongs to Metal, Metal generates Water, and Water is taken as his children. If the Fire line is thriving but Void, clashed Void or moved Void, his parents are not both alive, and if weak and Void he has no parents; Fire turning into Fire means two sets of parents; carrying Pressure that is thriving means nobility, weak, illness; carrying Peer means gambling and shamelessness; carrying Wealth means trade; carrying Output means kindness. The six lines are not to be looked at all together: lines in the Other's palace are the other's relations, and lines in the Self's palace are all my relations.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.50** 子孙临青龙，他的学生必然聪明俊秀，再遇月建、日辰生合、又临金、水爻的，必有颖悟非常的孩子；临白虎，就多是顽劣不驯的，发动则性情粗野，难以教训；遇到青龙，必定有礼貌，遇到白虎，必定不尽礼。
  If Output rests on the Azure Dragon, his pupils are surely clever and handsome, and if the Month or the Day also generates or combines with it and it rests on a Metal or Water line, there will surely be an exceptionally quick-witted child; if it rests on the White Tiger, they are mostly unruly and untamed, and if it moves their nature is wild and hard to teach; with the Azure Dragon they are surely courteous, and with the White Tiger they surely fall short in manners.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.51** 子孙在阳宫阳爻，又临胎、养以及金、水两爻，旺相不空，有扶助的，他的学生必有出类拔萃的，像刘恕那样的神童在门下；如果在阴宫阴爻，又化出财爻的，必有女孩来受学，不化出财爻而在兑宫的，也有女学生。
  If Output is in a yang palace on a yang line and rests on the Embryo or Nurture stage or on a Metal or Water line, thriving and not Void and supported, there will surely be an outstanding pupil among them, a child prodigy like Liu Shu at the door; if it is in a yin palace on a yin line and also turns into Wealth, there will surely be girls who come to study, and if it does not turn into Wealth but is in the Dui palace, there are also girl pupils.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.62** 占学馆，世爻如果衰绝无气，而遇到子孙发动变化、父母生扶、合起世爻的，主先生的才学本来不够丰富，因为教导子弟，他的才思日渐进益；卦里有财爻发动，就不是这样。
  In divining a school, if the Self is weak and in extinction without breath, but Output moves and changes and Resource generates and supports and combines the Self up, it means the teacher's learning was not at first abundant, but through teaching the youngsters his talent and thought advance day by day; if a Wealth line moves in the hexagram, it is not so.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.16** 要认识做官人的性情好恶，必须凭卦象仔细推详。托别人来占、要知道这位官员的为人、形貌、性情，只把官鬼爻来论：看它在哪一宫，论他的形体，再合上六兽的形貌，性情就清清楚楚。
  To know an official's likes and dislikes one must reckon in detail from the hexagram image. If the question is asked through another's hand and one wants to know the official's character, appearance and temper, reckon only from the Pressure line: see in which palace it lies, reckon his build, and together with the looks of the six beasts his nature can be seen clearly.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.17** 世在离宫，这人眼睛外露、聪明、性急；身在乾象，这人脸圆、正大、仁慈。离属火，为目；乾属金，为首；各取它的形体和德性来说。
  With the Self in the palace of Li the man's eyes show, he is clever and quick-tempered; with the Body in the Qian image his face is round and he is upright and magnanimous and benevolent. Li belongs to Fire and is the eye; Qian belongs to Metal and is the head; each is taken by its bodily form and its virtue.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.18** 金是武职的官，掌握生杀的大权；火是文章之士，探求礼义的根源。金有决断制裁的意思，所以取为武职；火是文明的象，必定职掌儒业。
  Metal is the military officer, holding the heavy handle of life and death; Fire is the man of letters, who probes the source of ritual and righteousness. Metal has the sense of judging and restraining, so it is taken for military office; Fire is the image of civilization and brightness, so the office is surely over the Confucian scholarly profession.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.30** 问馆在什么地方，以应爻为方位：应在坎、兑宫，近水；在震、巽，入林；在坤、艮，是山野；在离，是城市；在乾，是大都会或灵宫（神庙）。应落在父母，是诗书礼乐之家；落在官鬼，是做官的、书吏衙役的住宅；落在妻财是商贾，落在兄弟是同辈同行，落在子孙就是清闲讲道德的门第。用阴阳分是男是女来聘，用内外分是远是近来请，用游魂、归魂分本地还是他乡；用六神看四周的景色，用诸星看人事。应带青龙，馆在长林丰草之间；带玄武，在水边池旁；带朱雀，在闹市或有炊烟的村子；带白虎，在荒僻的乡下；带勾陈，近田野旧坟；带螣蛇，依在来往的工匠、舟车一带。贵人是做官的，驿马是经商的，华盖是出家人，天财是商贩，天猪是屠户，其余照这样类推。
  In asking where the post is, the Other gives the direction: the Other in Kan or Dui is near water, in Zhen or Xun it goes into woods, in Kun or Gen it is mountain wilds, in Li it is a town, and in Qian it is a great city or a hall of the spirits (a temple). The Other on Resource is a house of poetry, books, rites and music; on Pressure, the residence of officials, clerks and runners; on Wealth, merchants; on Peer, one's fellows of the same trade; on Output, a quiet, leisured house of morals and the Way. Yin and yang tell whether a man or a woman engages me, inner and outer whether the invitation is near or far, Wandering Soul and Returning Soul whether it is home ground or another region; the six spirits give the scenery around and the stars give the human affairs. The Other with the Azure Dragon: the place is among long woods and thick grass; the Dark Tortoise: a house by water, beside a pond; the Vermilion Bird: a busy market or a smoky village; the White Tiger: a barren, remote countryside; Hook Earth: near fields and old graves; the Coiling Snake: by the traffic of craftsmen, boats and carts. The Noble is an official, the Post Horse a merchant, the Canopy a monk, Heavenly Wealth a trader, the Heavenly Boar a butcher, and the rest are sought by analogy.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.49** 总的占该得什么官，专门察看官爻，用八卦分它属于哪个部曹：乾是天官（古称冢宰），管朝廷禁内的杂职、厩马；坎是冬官（古称司空），管总河、总漕之务和台谏，属天下水利的官；艮是阍寺，管晨门的官吏、防御守卫的僚属、治狱禁，属矿藏、宝库、钱局一类；震是春官（古称宗伯），是太子东宫的师傅，或总试、总裁，以及管宗庙祭祀、礼乐文章的官；巽是天使，以及管上林苑、奉命向四方宣布的上差的僚佐；离是夏官（古称司马），专管耳目之权、纠弹之事，又一说是端门的辅臣、修史的文明之官；坤是地官（古称司徒），管天下农事，是有土之官，担疆场社稷之任，是皇太后的内臣；兑是秋官（古称司寇），管言语、主刑罚、通章疏、主皇上膳食之事。
  To divine in general what office one will get, look only at the Pressure line, and tell by the eight trigrams the department it belongs to: Qian is the Heavenly Office (anciently called the Grand Steward), governing miscellaneous posts within the forbidden palace and the stable horses; Kan is the Winter Office (anciently the Minister of Works), governing the affairs of the river-conservancy and grain-transport directorates and the censors and remonstrators, officials of the waterworks of the realm; Gen is the palace gatekeeper, governing the officers of the dawn gate, the officials of defence and guard, the management of prisons, and things like mines, treasuries and mints; Zhen is the Spring Office (anciently the Elder of the Ancestral Temple), the tutors of the Crown Prince's Eastern Palace, or chief examiners and chief compilers, and the officials who manage the ancestral temples, sacrifices, rites, music and letters; Xun is the Heavenly Envoy, and those who manage the Shanglin park and the assistants of senior envoys sent with orders to the four quarters; Li is the Summer Office (anciently the Marshal), governing the power of ears and eyes and the work of impeachment, or also the supporting ministers of the Duan Gate and the civilised office of compiling histories; Kun is the Earth Office (anciently the Minister of the Multitude), governing the farming of the realm, officials with land, who carry the charge of the borders and the altars of the state, and the inner servants of the Empress Dowager; Dui is the Autumn Office (anciently the Minister of Crime), governing speech, in charge of punishments, passing memorials, and having the charge of the sovereign's meals.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.50** 用六爻来分辨官位的高低：官爻在初爻，是散缺（闲散的缺额），或是本乡的官守；在二爻，是县邑，或是谏议大臣、相国学士；在三爻，是守牧（知府、知州一类），或是三司的首领，以及大衙门里随从的官；在四爻，是司道，或是九卿六曹，以及近君的臣子；五爻是君象，不是臣子所居，只有代天巡狩的官和奉钦命的钦差才当得起；六爻是贵戚之乡，以及宗庙功勋之臣、高尚之士、辅相王府的官、四围的股肱。武途的封职，意义相类似。
  By the six lines one tells the height or lowness of the rank: the Pressure line in the first line is a scattered vacancy (an idle post), or an official guard of his home district; in the second line, a county or town, or a remonstrating minister, a chief minister or academician; in the third line, a prefect or governor, or the head of the Three Offices, and officials attending in a great yamen; in the fourth line, a commissioner or circuit intendant, or the Nine Ministers and the Six Boards, and ministers near the sovereign; the fifth is the sovereign's image and not where a subject dwells, and only an official who makes tours of inspection in Heaven's stead or one sent by imperial command can fill it; the sixth line is the home of the noble kin, and of ministers of merit to the ancestral temple, men of lofty virtue, officials who assist a prince's household, and the limbs and trunk on all four sides. For appointments on the military path the meaning is similar.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.51** 用五行来分这个官的职掌：金官必管兵事，或执掌刑罚法度；木官必管工务、礼乐、苑囿、车舆舟楫，以及桑麻、制造之事；水官必管江淮河海，以及水利盐务、税饷漕储；火官必管文章、弹劾、祭祀，以及冶铸、喉舌的权柄；土官必管土地农田、社稷边疆，以及陵寝、屯守的权柄。
  By the five phases one divides the duties of the office: a Metal official must govern military affairs, or hold the scales of punishment; a Wood official must govern public works, rites and music, parks, carriages and boats, and the affairs of mulberry, hemp and manufacture; a Water official must govern the rivers, the Huai, the Yellow River and the seas, and waterworks, the salt administration, tax and army supplies, and grain transport; a Fire official must govern letters, impeachment and sacrifice, and the powers of smelting and casting and of being the sovereign's mouthpiece; an Earth official must govern land and farmland, the altars of the state and the borders, and the powers over imperial tombs and garrison farms.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.52** 用六神和诸星（「诸监」疑是「诸星」之误）来参看这个官的政务：青龙，政务清明显耀，是文章诗赋、清华的官署；朱雀，政务是剖断，谏议善恶，折辨是非，是言路之官；勾陈，政务在田土，兴教劝农，治寇保民，是一方的牧守；螣蛇，政务是传宣，往来奔走，上下体察，是差遣之利；白虎，政务是威武，掌生杀之权，秉军旅之事，是刑罚的主宰；元武，政务是水利，是司捕、司谋之象。文昌是翰林，官符是有司，天马是钦差，天狱是禁吏；斗木獬是巡方之官，尾火虎是兵权之主，星日马是圣传之司，亢金龙是文章之相。大抵以五行、六爻、八宫为经，以六神、诸星为纬来参看。
  By the six spirits and the various stars ('jian' here is presumably a slip for 'xing', stars) one consults the government business of the office: with the Azure Dragon the government is clear and bright, the offices of letters, poetry and distinguished rank; with the Vermilion Bird the government is judging, remonstrating on good and evil, deciding right and wrong, an official of the channels of speech; with Hook Earth the government is in land and soil, promoting teaching and urging farming, quelling bandits and protecting the people, a shepherd of one region; with the Coiling Snake the government is relaying proclamations, going to and fro at speed, looking into things high and low, the advantage of commissions; with the White Tiger the government is awesome force, holding the power of life and death, in charge of military affairs, the master of punishments; with the Dark Tortoise the government is waterworks, the image of arresting and of planning. The Literary Star is the Hanlin, the Official Charge is the competent department, the Heavenly Horse is the imperial envoy, the Heavenly Prison is the prison officer; the Dipper Wood Xie-beast is an official who tours the regions, the Tail Fire Tiger is the master of military power, the Star Sun Horse is the office of transmitting the imperial word, the Neck Metal Dragon is the minister of letters. In general the five phases, the six lines and the eight palaces are the warp, and the six spirits and the various stars the weft that is consulted along with them.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.53** 日、月来象征它，世、应来度量它，君、臣来定它的位置。所以官爻独发的论宫，官爻只有一位的论爻位，官爻两现的求它所属，都没有的就考伏和变：伏的占飞神，变的占八宫。伏、变的办法也可以分出位置和所属。要知道当世的官守，用象去求，即使各代名称有更改，道理相差不远。官临日辰月建，不是社稷之臣，一定是直节的科道官；官爻持世是正印，临应是左右辅佐之职；五爻是君，不是御史就是当宠幸之臣；二爻是大臣，是宰相，是国家的仪表。卦里没有官鬼，就求伏下的飞神，用位和所属来分辨；卦有动象，就求所变的官爻，用八宫来区别。
  The Day and Month image it, the Self and the Other measure it, ruler and minister fix its place. So when the Pressure line moves alone one discusses the palace; when there is only one Pressure line one discusses the line position; when two Pressure lines show one seeks the department they belong to; when there is none one examines the hidden and the changed: for a hidden one one divines the flying line, for a changed one the eight palaces; this method of hidden and changed lines can also separate out the place and the department. To know the offices of the present age, one seeks them by the image, and though the names have been changed from age to age, the principle is not far off. When Pressure sits on the Day or Month branch, if he is not a minister of the altars of the state, he is surely an upright censor or supervising official; the Pressure line on the Self is the principal seal, and on the Other it is a post at the side, an assistant; the fifth line is the sovereign, and if not a censor he is a minister favoured by the throne; the second line is a great minister, a chief minister, the model of the state. If there is no Pressure line in the hexagram, one seeks the flying line above the hidden one and tells it by position and department; if there is a moving image, one seeks the Pressure line it changes into and tells it by the eight palaces.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.66** 六亲推一个人的品性：父母是才能，弟兄是诡诈，子孙是善良，妻财是术技，官鬼是强梁。先忠后佞的，是子化官；先奸后义的，是鬼化财。必须依据生克，之后才说吉凶。
  The six relations judge a man's character: Resource is talent, Peer is trickery, Output is goodness, Wealth is skill and craft, and Pressure is the bully. One who is first loyal and then flattering is Output turning into Pressure; one who is first treacherous and then righteous is Pressure turning into Wealth. One must go by generation and control before speaking of good or ill.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.67** 六神推人：带青龙而生世，就得到弹铗那样的贤人；随青龙而克世，就只有藏刀的诈。白虎生世的，刚而直；白虎克世的，勇而暴。朱雀生世的，能辩而忠；朱雀克世的，佞而奸。玄武生世的，智而敏；玄武克世的，巧而贼。螣蛇的生、克，分出他对我的损益是真是虚；勾陈的克、生，分出他善不善是否迟钝滞涩。如果来生世，虽然（神）凶也吉；如果来克世，虽然（神）吉也成凶。
  The six spirits judge a man: with the Azure Dragon and generating the Self, one gets a worthy like the man who strikes his sword-hilt; following the Azure Dragon and controlling the Self, there is only the deceit of the hidden blade. The White Tiger generating the Self is firm and straight, the White Tiger controlling the Self is bold and violent. The Vermilion Bird generating the Self is eloquent and loyal, the Vermilion Bird controlling the Self is glib and treacherous. The Dark Tortoise generating the Self is wise and quick, the Dark Tortoise controlling the Self is cunning and thievish. The Coiling Snake's generating and controlling tell whether the gain or loss to me is real or empty; Hook Earth's controlling and generating tell whether his goodness or lack of it is sluggish and stuck. If one comes to generate the Self, even if ominous it is auspicious; if one comes to control the Self, even if auspicious it becomes ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.68** 用爻动在八宫，就察八宫的性情；坐在五行，就推五行的性质；星煞详细看，衰旺仔细考，这个人是贤还是不肖，就可以借蓍草得知。八宫只论用爻单独发动：乾，生世是刚健，克世是骄恣；坤，生世是柔顺，克世是阿谀逢迎；震，生世是正直，克世是暴戾；巽，生世是伶俐，克世是随风倒；坎，生世是智巧，克世是奸险；离，生世是明白识事，克世是浮躁；艮，生世是笃实，克世是疑滞；兑，生世是和悦，克世是谄媚奸佞。五行、六神、诸星只论用爻所落的：水之贤叫智，不肖叫贼；火之贤叫急，不肖叫纵；木之贤叫慈，不肖叫懦；金之贤叫刚毅，不肖叫强横；土之贤叫敦信，不肖叫愚蠢。驿马生世，勤于奔走，克世，频繁来往；天财生世，善于生息，克世，善于贪占；官符生世，长于智谋，克世，长于讼斗；咸池生世，能于工技，克世，沉溺酒色。生世则对自己有益而是贤，克世则对自己有损而是不肖。
  When the Subject line moves in an eight-palace trigram, one examines the nature of that palace; when it sits in a phase, one infers the character of the phase; the stars and spirit-stars are examined in detail and strength and weakness carefully weighed, and whether a man is worthy or unworthy can be learned by the stalks. The eight palaces are discussed only when the Subject line moves alone: Qian, generating the Self is firm and strong, controlling the Self is arrogant and wilful; Kun, generating the Self is gentle and yielding, controlling the Self is fawning; Zhen, generating the Self is upright, controlling the Self is violent and perverse; Xun, generating the Self is clever, controlling the Self is going with the wind; Kan, generating the Self is shrewd and skilful, controlling the Self is treacherous and dangerous; Li, generating the Self is clear-sighted, controlling the Self is flighty and rash; Gen, generating the Self is solid and honest, controlling the Self is suspicious and stuck; Dui, generating the Self is harmonious and pleasant, controlling the Self is flattering and glib. The phases, the six spirits and the stars are discussed only by where the Subject line stands: Water, as worthy called wisdom, as unworthy called thievery; Fire, as worthy called ardour, as unworthy called licence; Wood, as worthy called kindness, as unworthy called weakness; Metal, as worthy called firmness, as unworthy called brutality; Earth, as worthy called steadfast good faith, as unworthy called stupidity. The Post Horse generating the Self is diligent in running about, controlling it is coming and going often; Heavenly Wealth generating is good at making money breed, controlling it is good at grabbing; the Official Charge generating is strong in wit and plans, controlling it is strong in lawsuits and quarrels; the Peach Blossom generating is able in crafts, controlling it is sunk in wine and women. Generating the Self, it benefits oneself and he is worthy; controlling the Self, it harms oneself and he is unworthy.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.70** 察他的性情，专看用象：金刚、火强、水智、木仁、土信。青龙能文，朱雀能言，白虎暴戾而玄武奸诈，勾陈迟钝而螣蛇浮躁。用八宫来辨别，用诸星来配合。这是用爻独发时来论的：在乾志向高，在坤性顺，震直、巽奸，坎暗、离明，艮讷、兑佞；角宿有蛟龙之志，尾宿有虎性，奎宿有狼心，星宿有骏马的德性。随生克衰旺来说吉凶。
  To examine his nature, look only at the Subject image: Metal firm, Fire strong, Water wise, Wood kind, Earth faithful. The Azure Dragon is able in letters, the Vermilion Bird is able in speech, the White Tiger is violent and perverse and the Dark Tortoise is treacherous, Hook Earth is slow and dull and the Coiling Snake is flighty. Tell it by the eight palaces and match it with the stars. This is for a Subject line moving alone: in Qian the ambition is high, in Kun the nature is yielding; Zhen is straight, Xun is crafty; Kan is dark, Li is bright; Gen is slow of speech, Dui is flattering. The Horn mansion has the dragon's will, the Tail mansion the tiger's nature, the Legs mansion the wolf's heart, and the Star mansion the virtue of the steed. Good or ill is told by generation, control, strength and weakness.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.98** 金木，是释（佛）、道；升降，是成败。西方属金，是肃杀之地，所以用释氏来象征它；况且佛从西边来，当属乾、兑、申、酉，所以金爻以及乾、兑之宫，福动就吉，鬼动就凶。东方属木，是化育之所，所以用道宗来象征它；况且道行于东土，当属震、巽、寅、卯，所以木爻以及震、巽之宫，官动是恶，子动是善。卦有升降之爻：如八月得升卦，则世是升爻；八月得泰卦，则世是降爻，这是秋分后四阴升而三阳降的缘由。世居升爻，可以入圣，叫做成；世居降爻，不能超越凡俗，叫做败；然而无病就吉，有病就凶。
  Metal and Wood are the Buddhists and the Daoists; ascent and descent are success and failure. The west belongs to Metal, a place of austerity and killing, so the Buddhist is imaged by it; and since the Buddha came from the west, it belongs to Qian, Dui, Shen and You, so for a Metal line and the palaces of Qian and Dui, Output moving is auspicious and Pressure moving is ominous. The east belongs to Wood, a place of transformation and nurture, so the Daoist school is imaged by it; and since the Way goes in the eastern land, it belongs to Zhen, Xun, Yin and Mao, so for a Wood line and the palaces of Zhen and Xun, Pressure moving is bad and Output moving is good. A hexagram has lines of ascent and descent: if one gets Sheng in the eighth month, the Self is a line of ascent; if one gets Tai in the eighth month, the Self is a line of descent, which is why after the autumn equinox four yin rise and three yang fall. The Self on an ascending line may enter sagehood, and this is called success; the Self on a descending line cannot rise above the ordinary, and this is called failure; but without sickness it is auspicious, and with sickness ominous.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.99** 子（孙）、官（鬼），是善与恶；父母动，得典籍策书的名誉；兄弟动，犯贪嗔的戒；妻财发，有牵挂家室的心。子孙动，前世就有善根，或者因缘福德深厚，或者有高贤徒众侍奉；官鬼动，原本有恶业，或者灾障缠身，或者调达（提婆达多那样的）搅扰；父母带吉神动，我的书文以后得名誉，带凶神动，主法嗣多聚多散；弟兄吉动，当遇良朋善友，但道风清苦，凶动，常有贪嗔的牵累；妻财吉动，道场供养有盈余，凶动，有妇女缠绊。
  Output and Pressure are good and evil; Resource moving gains the name of scripture and learning; Peer moving breaks the precept against greed and anger; Wealth issuing brings a heart that clings to home. Output moving means one had roots of goodness from the past, or great karmic merit, or the service of eminent worthies and disciples; Pressure moving means one had evil karma originally, or entanglement by calamity and hindrance, or the harassment of a Devadatta; Resource moving with an auspicious spirit, my writings later win renown, with an ominous spirit, the heirs of the teaching gather and scatter much; Peer moving auspiciously, one will meet good friends but the way of life is austere, ominously, there is often the entanglement of greed and anger; Wealth moving auspiciously, the offerings to the place of practice have a surplus, ominously, women entangle.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.100** 螣蛇，是尘世的障碍不除；勾陈，是旧缘不断；白虎、元武，是灾祸盗贼的由头；朱雀、青龙，是有才有名的人；大概必须因鬼动才说凶，福动才说吉。例如朱雀带着摇动的鬼，是是非；朱雀动福，是名誉，之类。
  The Coiling Snake means the hindrances of the dust of the world are not removed; Hook Earth means old ties are not cut; the White Tiger and the Dark Tortoise are the cause of calamity and theft; the Vermilion Bird and the Azure Dragon are men of talent and name; generally one says ominous only because Pressure moves, and auspicious only because Output moves. For example, the Vermilion Bird with a moving Pressure is disputes; the Vermilion Bird with Output moving is renown, and so on.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.105** 凡看（卦身、世身）二身，先要没有病，然后用六亲、六神、星煞来详看；功名仕宦，喜欢文书、官鬼、青龙、朱雀、贵人临在身上，而子孙、勾陈是忌。
  In looking at the two bodies, they should first be free of sickness, and only then are the six relations, the six spirits and the stars examined in detail; for fame and office, it is welcome for Resource, Pressure, the Azure Dragon, the Vermilion Bird and the Noble to rest on the body, and Output and Hook Earth are taboo.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.108** 天马，就是乾马，正月起在午，循环着走；这颗星只利于升迁的占问。正月用乾卦四爻为天马，二月用五爻（申），依次循环使用。口诀说：正月、七月在午，二月、八月在申，三月、九月在戌，四月、十月在子，五月、十一月在寅，六月、十二月在辰。
  The Heavenly Horse is the horse of Qian; it starts at Wu in the first month and goes round in turn; this star is of benefit only in divinations about promotion. In the first month the fourth line of Qian is taken as the Heavenly Horse, in the second month the fifth line, Shen, and so on in turn. The formula says: first and seventh months at Wu, second and eighth at Shen, third and ninth at Xu, fourth and tenth at Zi, fifth and eleventh at Yin, sixth and twelfth at Chen.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.02** 断官职用鬼谷分爻：六爻各配一类官，依次是执政、朝仕、监司、长官、曹官、吏人；看所占的是哪一类官，就在对应的那一爻上取用，来断吉凶。
  To judge an office use Guigu's division of the lines: the six lines are matched with six kinds of official, in order the ruling minister, the court official, the supervising commissioner, the head of an office, the departmental officer and the clerk; see which kind of official is being asked about, take the matching line, and judge good or ill from it.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.03** 断是什么官：四直、贵人、驿马聚在身爻、世爻上，又有德神、合神相扶，落在官星、印星的阳爻上，必是宰相之位；再加刑、刃、大杀，是出将入相；再加太岁或五爻生合，必荣获赐宴。太岁与五爻生合世身，是在朝为官；冲克世身，是外任；世爻、官爻在内卦发动是外任，在外卦发动是朝官（五爻是君位，四爻六爻都挨近君位）。官星、贵人持世，在阳宫阳爻的是文职，在阴宫阴爻的是武官。世爻持岁破，在朝是六部等大衙门，外任是布政司、按察司、都司；持月建，是巡方、安抚、行人这类；持日建，是郡县的长官。
  To tell what office it is: if the four rulers, the Noble and the Horse gather on the Body and Self lines, and Virtue and combination also support them, and they sit on a yang line of the star of office and seal, it is surely the post of chief minister; with the punishing blade and great baleful spirits added, one goes out as a general and comes in as chancellor; with Tai Sui or the fifth line generating or combining, he is surely honoured with a gift of banquet. If Tai Sui and the fifth line generate or combine with the Self and Body, it is an official at court; if they clash with or control the Self and Body, a post outside; if the Self or the Pressure line moves in the inner trigram it is a post outside, and in the outer trigram a court official (the fifth is the ruler's seat, and the fourth and sixth are both near the ruler). If the star of office and the Noble hold the Self in a yang palace and on a yang line it is a civil post, and in a yin palace and on a yin line a military officer. If the Self holds the Year-break, at court it is in the great offices such as the Six Ministries, and outside it is in the provincial administration, judicial or military commissions; holding the Month it is an inspecting commissioner, a pacification commissioner or an envoy; holding the Day it is the head of a prefecture or county.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.04** 世爻持官星、贵人、禄、驿马，又得太岁、月建、文书来生合，是有威权的监察之职（风宪）；世爻持官星、印星而旺相，但是靠旁边的爻带着贵人、禄、驿马来扶助的，只是正官，不是风宪。
  If the Self holds the star of office, the Noble, Emolument and the Horse, and Tai Sui, the Month and the document also generate or combine with it, it is a post of authority in the censorate; if the Self holds the star of office and seal and is thriving, but it is a side line carrying the Noble, Emolument and the Horse that supports it, he is only a regular official and not in the censorate.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.05** 世爻持官星、贵人、禄、驿马，不落空、不被刑克破，再按它所在爻的五行定部门：金爻旺相，京官是兵部尚书、刑部尚书、大理寺和都察院中丞，衰弱的是尚宝司和兵部的武选、车驾、职方、武库各司郎官，武职是五军都督府和锦衣卫，外任旺相的是总制、巡抚、按察、观察、总兵，衰弱的是恤刑、司理、参将游击、把总；木爻旺相，京官是工部尚书，衰弱的是营缮司、虞衡司，外任旺相的是关津主税，衰弱的是税课、茶水之职；水爻旺相，京官是吏部尚书，衰弱的是文选、稽勋、验封、都水各司郎官，外任旺相的是盐政、漕运、河道、操江，衰弱的是水利、盐运、粮运之职；火爻旺相，京官是礼部尚书、詹事府、学士、国子监祭酒司业、太常寺、翰林这类官，衰弱的是中书，旺相又加朱雀的是科道官，衰弱的是鸿胪寺，在午爻上的旺相是兵部尚书、衰弱的是苑马寺，外任带文昌、驿马、又加月建的，不是主考官，必是督学官，衰弱的是学官和炉冶坑场之职；上爻旺相，京官是户部、京兆尹、巡城御史、仓场院，加青龙德神的是光禄寺，外任旺相的是布政使、屯田督运督粮，衰弱的是知府、知县和开矿、采石、垦荒的差使。
  When the Self holds the star of office, the Noble, Emolument and the Horse, and is not Void, punished, controlled or broken, the department is fixed by the element of the line it sits on.
