On a Metal line, thriving: a capital official is the Minister of War, the Minister of Justice, or the head of the Court of Judicial Review or a vice censor-in-chief; weak: the Seal Office and the directors of the bureaus of the Ministry of War, and for the military the Five Commissions and the Embroidered-Uniform Guard; serving outside, thriving: a governor-general, governor, judicial commissioner, intendant or regional commander; weak: a commissioner for mercy in punishments, a judicial clerk, a deputy or flying column commander, a company captain. On a Wood line, thriving: the Minister of Works; weak: the Yingshan and Yuheng bureaus; outside, thriving: a customs and tax master; weak: a post for taxes and the tea and water duties. On a Water line, thriving: the Minister of Personnel; weak: the directors of the Wenxuan, Jixun, Yanfeng and Dushui bureaus; outside, thriving: salt, grain transport, river works or the river patrol; weak: a post for water conservancy, salt transport or grain transport. On a Fire line, thriving: the Minister of Rites, the Supervisorate of the Heir, a scholar, the Imperial Academy, the Court of Imperial Sacrifices or the Hanlin Academy; weak: a secretary of the Secretariat; thriving with the Vermilion Bird added: a supervising secretary or censor; weak: the Court of State Ceremonial; on the Wu line, thriving: the Minister of War, weak: the Pasturage Court; outside, carrying the Wenchang star, the Post Horse and the Month: not an examiner but surely an education commissioner; weak: a school instructor or a post in the furnaces, smelting works and mines. On the top line, thriving: a capital official is the Minister of Revenue, the governor of the capital prefecture, the city-patrol censor or the granary office, and with the Dragon and Virtue added, the Court of Banqueting; outside, thriving: a provincial administration commissioner or an officer of military farms, transport and grain; weak: a prefect, a county magistrate, or an envoy to open mines, quarry stone or clear land.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.06** 世爻持官星、贵人、禄而没有印绶（父母），是佐贰官；世爻持衰弱的官星，得到日辰、动爻的财来生合，或者财伏藏、化出财的，是管仓场、府库、驿站这类的官；世爻持官星、印星而没有财的，不是正式选授的官，而是差遣委任的官（筮诀说，卦里若没有财，必定不是正式选授）；官星衰弱、印星旺相的，是代理掌印的官。
  If the Self holds the star of office, the Noble and Emolument but has no seal and ribbon (Resource), it is an assistant official; if the Self holds a weak star of office and Wealth from the Day or a moving line generates or combines with it, or Wealth lies hidden or turns into Wealth, it is an official of the granaries, treasuries, post stations and the like; if the Self holds the star of office and seal but has no Wealth, it is not a regularly selected official but one dispatched or commissioned (the Divination Secret says: if the hexagram has no Wealth, it is surely not a regular selection); if the star of office is weak and the seal thriving, he is an acting seal-holder.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.25** 为官贪还是廉：官星、印星都旺相，财爻不出现的，清廉；世爻持兄爻、旺相发动的，贪；世爻下伏着兄爻、又变成兄爻的，贪。
  Greed or integrity in office. If the star of office and the seal are both thriving and the Wealth line does not appear, he is incorruptible; if the Self holds a Peer line that is thriving and moving, he is greedy; if a Peer line lies hidden under the Self and turns into a Peer line, he is greedy.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.26** 为官的品性：世爻持白虎、官鬼，再加刑、刃、劫杀发动的，酷；世爻持官鬼，再加玄武、咸池、荒芜、四废杀发动的，疲软无能；世爻持白虎、官鬼，再加雷火、卒暴杀发动的，浮躁；世爻带天解、天赦、喝散发动的，能为积压的冤狱昭雪。
  The character of the official. If the Self holds the Tiger and Pressure and the punishing, blade and Robbery spirits are added and move, he is cruel; if the Self holds Pressure and the Dark Tortoise, Xianchi, Huangwu and the Four Wastes baleful spirits are added and move, he is feeble and soft; if the Self holds the Tiger and Pressure and the Thunder-Fire and Sudden-Violence baleful spirits are added and move, he is rash and restless; if the Self carries Tianjie, Tianshe and Hesan and they move, he clears and redresses cases long held up in prison.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.27** 清名与属吏：财爻落空、官鬼旺相的，清廉的名声很响，而口袋是空的；父爻落空、官星旺相的，爵位高，身边只有琴和鹤相随；父母爻是行李。初爻代表属吏和百姓：初爻克官星、克世爻的，主这个官贤明，属吏百姓亲近他；世爻、官爻克初爻的，主这个官苛刻，属吏百姓畏惧躲避他；生初爻的，主这个官慈爱属吏百姓。
  A clean name, and the clerks and people. If Wealth is Void and Pressure thriving, his name for incorruptibility rings out while his purse is empty; if the seal line is Void and the star of office thriving, his rank is high while only the zither and the crane follow him; the Resource line is the baggage. The first line stands for clerks and people: if the first line controls the star of office or the Self, he is wise and enlightened and the clerks and people are close to him; if the Self line or the Pressure line controls the first line, he is harsh and the clerks and people fear and avoid him; if it generates the first line, he is kind and loving towards them.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.53** 僧官、道纪、医官、杂职、阴阳等官：别人占僧人、道士、医生，以子孙为用神，子孙旺相则道业高强，父母兴旺则平庸愚笨、懦弱；他们自己占自己的道术，也以子孙为用神，子孙旺相能降伏鬼神。但他们自己占功名时，子孙发动就克官星了，不能再当用神，应当仍然看官爻：官星持世，或日月动爻来生，或父母持世、有旺相的官星来生，功名必定成；子孙发动或子孙持世，一定不成名；阴阳官和各种杂职官尤其嫌子孙发动；其余的和普通人求名同样断。
  Monastic, Daoist, medical, minor and yin-yang officials: when another person divines about a monk, Daoist or doctor, Output is the Subject, and if Output is thriving his practice is high and strong, if Resource flourishes he is mediocre, dull and timid; when they divine about their own art, Output is again the Subject, and a thriving Output can subdue ghosts and spirits. But when they divine about their own rank, a moving Output controls the star of office and can no longer be the Subject, so one must still look at the Pressure line: if the star of office holds the Self, or the Day, Month or a moving line generates it, or Resource holds the Self and a thriving star of office generates it, the rank will surely be made; if Output moves or holds the Self, the name will surely not be made; yin-yang officials and all minor posts especially dislike a moving Output; otherwise judge as for an ordinary man seeking a name.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.11** 爻乱动，务必不要入宝；八纯卦倒好去参玄；艮、坤只好栖身岩穴山谷；离、巽偏适合隐居市井；龙虎交驰，适合访道；坎、离汇聚，适合安心坐禅；游魂卦带杀，别去云游；一世卦休囚，不要置买田产。
  With lines moving in disorder, by all means do not enter the 'treasure'; an Eight-Pure hexagram is on the contrary good for studying the mysteries; Gen and Kun are fit only for dwelling among crags and valleys; Li and Xun are better for hiding in the marketplace; the Dragon and Tiger racing against each other suit seeking the Way; Kan and Li converging suit settling into meditation; a Wandering Soul hexagram with a baleful spirit, do not go wandering like cloud and water; a First-Generation hexagram resting or trapped, do not buy fields.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.14** 六位例按六爻到初爻的次序列各行：仕宦——六爻隐逸、五爻王公、四爻诸侯、三爻大夫、二爻士人、初爻庶民；占事——六爻国事、五爻心事、四爻官事、三爻家事、二爻身事、初爻人事；转官——六爻任所、五爻宣命、四爻差遣、三爻保举、二爻己身、初爻解由；僧道——六爻祖师、五爻师父、四爻檀越、三爻法眷、二爻己身、初爻徒弟。
  The table of the positions of the six lines lists each row in order from the sixth line down to the first: official careers: the sixth line the recluse, the fifth princes and dukes, the fourth feudal lords, the third grandees, the second scholars, the first commoners; matters divined: the sixth the state, the fifth the heart, the fourth officials, the third the household, the second oneself, the first dealings with people; transfer of office: the sixth the place of the post, the fifth the proclaimed commission, the fourth the assignment, the third the sponsorship, the second oneself, the first the discharge; monks and Daoists: the sixth the patriarch, the fifth the master, the fourth the donor, the third the Dharma kin, the second oneself, the first the disciple.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.15** 八卦法用例里和事业有关的几行，按乾、坤、震、巽、坎、离、艮、兑的次序：君臣——王公、宰相、诸侯、大夫、公吏、士人、庶民、伶伦；人事——官事、家事、身事、公事、忧事、心事、闲事、喜事；各业——士、农、工、商、武、文、医、卜；寻访——贵人、主人、客人、行人、吏人、士人、商人、匠人；转官——朝廷、任所、差遣、宣命、印绶、保明、复任、解田。
  The rows of the 'uses of the eight trigrams' bearing on careers, in the order Qian, Kun, Zhen, Xun, Kan, Li, Gen, Dui: lord and ministers: prince and duke, chancellor, feudal lord, grandee, public clerk, scholar, commoner, musician; human affairs: officials, household, oneself, public business, worries, the heart, idle matters, joyful matters; trades: scholar, farmer, artisan, merchant, soldier, literary man, doctor, diviner; seeking and visiting: the noble, the host, the guest, the traveller, the clerk, the scholar, the merchant, the craftsman; transfer of office: the court, the place of the post, the assignment, the proclaimed commission, the seal and ribbon, the certification, reinstatement, discharge.

## 风险(Risks: obstruction, demotion, impeachment, plots, a bad year)

### 六爻 → `chapters/六爻/事业/风险.md`

- **LY.CAREER.BSQS.04** 随官入墓是身、世或本命跟着官鬼入了墓。求官遇到它，事情难成，就算成了也振作不起来；入的如果是杀墓（丁未、戊戌），就更是大凶。
  Following Pressure into the tomb means the Body, the Self or one's birth year follows the Pressure line into its tomb. In seeking office, meeting it makes the matter hard to achieve, and even if achieved one cannot rise; if it enters a killing tomb (Ding-Wei or Wu-Xu) it is greatly inauspicious.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.05** 占得坎、咸、复这三个卦，求官的人心里疑惑、犹豫不决。
  With the hexagrams Kan, Xian or Fu, the seeker of office is left in doubt and hesitation.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.08** 旺相的爻去克应，不好；应入墓的时候，要担心有人死亡；世爻被克，遭贬谪、责罚；世爻去克别的爻，俸禄加倍。
  A strong line controlling the Other is not good; when the Other enters its tomb, beware of death; the Self being controlled brings demotion and banishment, and the Self controlling another line doubles one's pay.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.09** 初爻是人吏，落空亡，即使做官清正，吏员没力量，谁替他奉行，所以难以立足；内外卦全是阴，阴柔立不起来，怎么振作？所以名声不扬。
  The first line is the clerk; if it falls Void, even an upright official has clerks without strength to carry out his orders, like a tree without branches, and it is hard to stand firm; if the inner and outer trigrams are both wholly yin, the soft cannot stand up and the name is not spread abroad.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.10** 子孙爻克伤官鬼，就没有官禄；福德（子孙）爻发动，反而不昌盛；子孙持世、子孙发动，是求官的大忌，一定落到毁官的地步。
  Output controlling Pressure leaves no official emolument; with the Output line stirring there is on the contrary no flourishing; Output holding the Self, or moving, is the great taboo in seeking office and surely ruins the post.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.12** 白虎爻旺相发动，惊恐的事马上就到。
  With the White Tiger line strong and moving, fright and alarm arrive at once.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.28** 世临白虎，官司缠绕；身遇勾陈，心事牵缠；勾陈带杀，会遭魔障；玄武临财，被盗贼冤害；兄弟和勾陈同在一位发动，要提防同门法眷在外面勾结牵连。
  The Self on the White Tiger, lawsuits dog one; the Body meeting Hook Earth, worries entangle the heart; Hook Earth carrying a baleful spirit meets demonic obstruction; the Dark Tortoise on Wealth, one is wronged by thieves; Peer and Hook Earth moving together in the same place, beware of Dharma kin colluding with outsiders.
- **LY.CAREER.CA.31** 子孙发动、妻财交动，是要还俗的人；忽然有负债纠结、财和身一起发动，免不了去偿还俗世的债和冤；世、应不和、身又乱动，想了身达命要等到驴年（也就是永远等不到）。
  Output moving with Wealth also moving: a man who will return to lay life; if suddenly there is a debt-knot and Wealth and the Body move, one cannot avoid paying back worldly debts and grievances; with Self and Other at odds and the Body moving wildly, to finish with the body and reach one's destiny one must wait for the Year of the Donkey (that is, for ever).
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.04** 初爻是吏，落空亡，即使官员清正，吏员没力量，难以立足；内外卦全是阴，名声不扬；子孙爻克官鬼，就没有官禄；福德（子孙）爻惊动，哪里还会吉昌；卦克世、身，职位不安；身去克初爻的吏，吏员都畏惧躲避，百样忧愁聚集；白虎动时惊恐就到；子孙持世，必定掉官。
  The first line is the clerk, and if it falls Void, even if the official is upright the clerks have no strength and it is hard to find a footing; with inner and outer trigrams wholly yin, the name is not made known; Output controlling Pressure, there is no official emolument; with the Output line startled into moving, how could there be fortune; the hexagram controlling the Self and the Body, the post is not secure; the Body controlling the first line, the clerk, all the clerks shrink away in fear and a hundred anxieties gather; when the White Tiger moves, fright and alarm come; Output holding the Self surely drops the office.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.13** 官鬼落空亡或被冲破，世、应又都遇上这种情形，尤其难以指望；官爻带杀克身，是凶，纵然得到官也会反过来中止。例子：假定正月占得损之临，上九的寅爻带阴杀，举这一爻作为定例，不必再久久思量。
  With Pressure Void or clashed and broken, and the Self and the Other both meeting the same, it is all the harder to count on; with the Pressure line carrying a baleful spirit and controlling the self it is bad, and even if the office is gained it will turn back and stop. For example, suppose in the first month the hexagram Sun changing to Lin, with the top line, a nine, Yin carrying a yin baleful spirit: take this one line as the fixed example, and there is no need to ponder longer.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.18** 占官员能否得到接替（卸任）：如果没有官鬼爻，官就能得到接替，也就能离去、没有滞留；子孙爻发动也是这样；财动，也一定照这个算。
  On divining whether an official will be replaced: if there is no Pressure line, the official will be replaced and can go without being held back; Output moving is the same, and with Wealth moving it is sure to be reckoned in the same way.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.19** 占现任官还能做多久：子孙动克官鬼，官位就丢；财发动、印（父母）亡，要担忧俸禄品秩；要知道丢官位要多久，退度在二、三个月或日子。注文说：子孙爻动克官鬼，有失官位的兆头；财爻发动克父母，可能被追缴印绶。
  On divining how long a serving official can remain: Output moving and controlling Pressure, the post is lost; with Wealth moving and the seal lost, worry for the salary and rank; to know how long until the post is lost, the retreating degree is in two or three months or days. The note says: Output moving and controlling Pressure is a sign of losing the post; Wealth moving and controlling Resource may lead to the seal and ribbon being recalled.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.20** 占在任的官有没有疾病：卦变成囚、死之地，在任的人身体有病以至衰亡；无气又带杀，终究有祸，再遇上空亡必有丧事；官鬼发动而遇休、废之地，这个人退位后终究没有官位；胎、没再并上，世就自然衰微，再加刑、杀，就是衰败。
  On divining whether an official in post will fall ill: when the hexagram changes into a trapped or dead place the one in office falls ill, even to decline and death; without qi and carrying a baleful spirit there is in the end calamity, and meeting Void as well there is surely a death; if Pressure moves and meets a place of rest or abandonment, the man leaves his post and in the end has none; with Embryo and Sunk joined to it the Self is naturally weak, and with punishment and baleful spirits added it is decline.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.27** 求官，官鬼是官科；子孙动，求官步步叹息。父母发动的时候是印绶；财爻发动，破了官科。空亡落在鬼上，怕空手而回；劫杀遇上官，失官又能怎么办。
  In seeking office Pressure is the official department; with Output moving, seeking office is a matter of sighs at every step. When Resource moves it is the seal and ribbon; when the Wealth line moves it breaks the official department. Void falling on Pressure, fear returning empty-handed; the Robbery Killing meeting the office, what can be done about the loss.
- **LY.CAREER.DYTJ.28** 杀在官鬼里来克世，人因为官鬼而损身的很多；官临旺相，如高山峻岭；位入休囚，如浅水之河。
  A baleful spirit within the Pressure line coming to control the Self: many are those who lose themselves through Pressure; Pressure on a strong place is like high mountains and ridges, and in a place at rest or trapped like a shallow stream.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.03** 子孙是伤官的煞，最喜欢它空亡墓绝，尤其适宜安静受制才吉。它如果在卦里发动，所谋的事必不遂意：没做官的得不到除授选用，已经做官的有被夺官革职的祸患；官鬼再无气，必定被除名、削去官籍，不只是贬责降罚。
  Output is the killer that harms office. It is best when Void, in a tomb or in extinction, and above all fitting when still and held in check. If it moves in the hexagram, what is planned will surely go against one's wish: one not yet in office cannot be appointed or selected, and one already in office has the calamity of being deprived of office and stripped of post; and if Pressure has no breath as well, one will surely be struck from the roll and removed from the register of office, which is more than being rebuked and demoted.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.04** 兄弟是劫财的神。它空亡、不上卦，或者临死绝而安静，就吉；如果发动，免不了花费钱财、招来诽谤，俸禄也不称心；再和子孙一起发动，或者就化成子孙，必有被除粮、减俸的事；持世、临身都不利。
  Peer is the spirit that robs wealth. If it is Void, absent from the hexagram, or in death or extinction and still, it is auspicious; if it moves, there is bound to be spending of money and slander, and the salary will not be to one's liking; if it moves together with Output, or turns directly into Output, there will surely be grain struck off and salary cut; holding the Self or settling on the Body is in every case unfavourable.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.09** 世爻无缘无故自己落空亡又没有救助，不管已经任职还是没有任职，必有大难，甚至死亡；想谋求办事的，主不成。
  If the Self falls Void by itself without cause and there is no rescue, whether one is already in office or not, there will surely be a great calamity, even death; and if one wants to seek or manage something, it will not succeed.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.11** 占官，不能没有财爻，也不能让它发动。如果官鬼有气，又得到财爻发动来扶起，就必须用钱财去谋求打点，才能升迁；如果父母衰弱，又遇到这一爻加临白虎旺相发动，必有丁忧（为父母守丧）的事；财化子孙、子孙化财，或财临世发动，或子孙发动而父母无故自己空亡的，都主丁忧。
  In divining an office, Wealth cannot be missing, nor should it move. If Pressure has breath and is raised up by a moving Wealth, one must use money to seek and arrange it before one is promoted; if Resource is weak and this line, settled on the White Tiger, moves in strength, there will surely be a mourning for a parent; and if Wealth turns into Output, or Output into Wealth, or Wealth settles on the Self and moves, or Output moves while Resource falls Void by itself without cause, in all these cases there will be mourning.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.12** 日辰刑冲克世爻，必定招来诽谤，按带的是哪一类六亲来推：带兄弟是因为贪贿赂或者征税太急，带财爻是因为调度无方或者赋税收不起来，带子孙是嗜酒好游、疏于政事，带父母是事务繁重料理不过来，带官鬼是酷刑或者同僚不和。以上都招诽谤，声名不能振作；如果世爻临月建，即使有诽谤也不能为害。
  If the Day punishes, clashes or controls the Self, slander will surely be invited, and one infers by which relation it carries: with Peer it is because of greed for bribes or collecting taxes too hastily, with Wealth because of poor management or revenue that cannot be raised, with Output because of fondness for wine and wandering and neglect of government, with Resource because affairs are heavy and cannot be managed, with Pressure because of harsh punishments or discord among colleagues. All of these invite slander and one's reputation cannot rise; but if the Self rests on the Month, even though there is slander it cannot do harm.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.13** 官鬼发动，生合世爻的是用神，伤克世爻的是鬼煞。用神扶助世爻，必有进取；鬼煞伤到世身，必有凶祸。按它化出的六亲来断：化出子孙有被贬谪的忧虑，化出财有女人带来的祸患，化出兄弟主失财，化出父母要忧虑小孩；如果都不是这样，自己一定有灾病，得世爻空亡躲开才不妨。
  When Pressure moves, if it generates or combines with the Self it is the Subject, and if it harms or controls the Self it is a killer. When the Subject supports the Self there will surely be advancement; when a killer harms the Self and the Body there will surely be misfortune. Judge by the relation it turns into: into Output, the worry of being demoted and exiled; into Wealth, the calamity of a woman; into Peer, loss of wealth; into Resource, worry for the children; and if it is none of these, one will surely have calamity or illness oneself, and it is harmless only if the Self is Void and so avoids it.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.15** 太岁出现、发动伤克世爻，必定遭到贬责；再加上刑、害、白虎、螣蛇等煞，必有被锁拿擒捕的耻辱；世爻入墓，必受囚禁，得到动爻、日辰来救，或者可以无事；但怕化出子孙，罪终究免不了。月建也照这样看。
  If the Tai Sui appears and moves to injure or control the Self, one will surely be demoted and rebuked; if punishment, harm, the White Tiger, the Coiling Snake and other killers are added, there will surely be the disgrace of being chained and seized; if the Self enters a tomb, one will surely be imprisoned, and if a moving line or the Day comes to the rescue one may perhaps come to no harm; but if it turns into Output, the offence cannot in the end be escaped. The Month is looked at in the same way.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.42** 世爻、身爻被月建、日辰、动爻刑克，虽然有可成的象，日后也不称心；如果是官鬼爻刑克，会有官非、疾病之类的事。
  If the Self and the Body are punished or controlled by the Month, the Day or a moving line, then though there is the image of its coming about, it will not be to one's liking later; if it is Pressure that punishes or controls, there will be lawsuits, illness and the like.
- **LY.CAREER.HJC.60** 月建、日辰、动爻、变爻等爻，都来刑克世爻的，占馆一定不能成；占束修，恐怕被学生的父兄呵责，应该谨慎。王洪绪说得更重：占馆有意想不到的凶险。
  If the Month, the Day, the moving lines and the changed lines all come to punish or control the Self, the school post cannot come about; in divining the fee, one may be scolded by the pupils' fathers and brothers, and care should be taken. Wang Hongxu puts it more heavily: in divining a school post there is an unforeseen ill.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.06** 子孙发动，即使已在任上也要休官；子孙是剥官的神，一发动，虽在任所也该休致，求仕的人就更不用说了。
  With Output moving, even one already in office will be dismissed; Output is the spirit that strips office, and if it moves even one at his post should retire, to say nothing of one seeking office.
- **LY.CAREER.TXF.07** 劫杀交重发动，正当受恩的时候反而损伤自己；官鬼落在劫杀上发动，必定因为做官而损命。劫杀见后面神杀章；它是五行混浊的神，阴气特别毒的叫做杀，遇到都不是吉兆。
  With the Robbery Killing moving heavily, just when one is to receive favour one is harmed; if Pressure stands on it and moves, one is sure to lose one's life through office. The Robbery Killing is dealt with in the later chapter on baleful spirits; it is a spirit of the five phases muddied and turbid, yin qi that is especially poisonous being called a 'killing', and meeting it is never an auspicious sign.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.41** 自己占的，世爻不可空：空了，就是该升而没有迁、有位而无职司、在任而闲居、带着罪而立功；替别人占就不是这样。随官入墓、助鬼伤身，是仕宦所忌的，不论衰旺都凶。六合、六冲不是仕宦所忌的：冲而旺相，只是三个月的耽搁；合而休囚，就是五年的停滞。还有晋、升两卦为吉，屯、蹇两卦为凶，也不可遗漏。
  In divining for oneself the Self line must not be Void: if it is Void, one who should be promoted is not moved, one holds rank without office, is in post but idle, or serves to atone while under a charge; for another it is not so. Following Pressure into the tomb and helping Pressure to injure the Body are what an official career fears, and whether weak or thriving they are ominous. Six-Combine and six-clash are not what an official career fears: if clashed but thriving, it is only a delay of three months; if combined but resting or imprisoned, it is a stagnation of five years. Moreover, Jin and Sheng are auspicious and Zhun and Jian are ominous, and these must not be left out either.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.58** 在任上防被参劾、处罚的，厌子孙动而喜官旺，世空就是要离任；离任后担心留下后患的，喜子孙动而厌官旺，世空就没有事。
  For one in office guarding against impeachment and penalty, Output moving is hated and Pressure rising is welcome, and the Self Void means leaving the post; for one who has left office and fears trouble afterwards, Output moving is welcome and Pressure rising is hated, and the Self Void means no trouble.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.71** 游魂卦而用爻空，就有逃亡的变故；归魂卦而用爻破，就有被回赎（被人赎回去）的嫌疑。卦里有两个父、两个官，事情可能两头牵系；三刑、三破，自身常有四体的灾。世空，是我自己疑惑；间爻动，要防阻隔。三破，说的是岁破、月破、日破。
  With a Wandering Soul hexagram and the Subject line Void, there is a change of flight and absconding; with a Returning Soul hexagram and the Subject line broken, there is the suspicion of being redeemed and taken back. If the hexagram has two Resource lines and two Pressure lines, the matter may be tied at both ends; with three punishments or three breaks the person often has a calamity of the limbs. The Self Void means I am in doubt; a between line moving means guard against obstruction. The 'three breaks' are the break of the year, of the month and of the day.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.73** 想要他走，应克世，他就不走；想要他来，应受阻，他就不来。这个人可以信任了，会不会被人夺走？应克世，就被人争。这个人可以用了，会不会遭受是非？官动、官克，就被告。这个人可以用了，会不会生病？命墓、应坏、鬼动，就患病。
  If one wants him to go, with the Other controlling the Self he does not go; if one wants him to come, with the Other obstructed he does not come. The man can be trusted: will he not be taken from me? With the Other controlling the Self, he is fought over. The man can be used: will he not meet with slander and trouble? With Pressure moving and Pressure controlling, he is sued. The man can be used: will he not fall ill? With the life line in the tomb, the Other damaged and Pressure moving, he falls ill.
- **LY.CAREER.YM.107** （随墓的害处）求官的人走上仕途，就回不来了；出行的人走在平路上却遇到屯难；行人病在他乡，产妇厄在暗室；当差的有枷锁之辱，兴造的有触犯妨碍之凶；偷关越险的陷入罗网，离开宗族谋差使的受羁绊。随墓是通忌；如果是兴造、偷关逾险，空、破还稍微可以。
  (The harm of following into the tomb:) one who seeks office walks the official road and does not return; one who travels walks on a level road and meets trouble; the traveller falls ill in a strange land, the woman in childbirth is in distress in a dark room; the runner suffers the disgrace of fetters, the builder meets the ill-omen of offence; one who slips past a pass and crosses a danger falls into the net, and one who leaves his clan to seek a post is bound. Following into the tomb is a taboo in all cases; but for building and for slipping past a pass and crossing a danger, Void or broken is somewhat tolerable.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.19** 在任，俸禄、弹劾和降职：财爻变出子孙，加俸加秩；财爻变出兄爻，被罚俸禄钱粮；月建、日辰刑、害、克、破官爻、世爻，或者贵人、官星发动伤身爻、世爻的，被人弹劾；官星、印星遇到空、死、墓、绝、胎，或被刑、害、克、破，或者发动变出子孙、变成空、死、墓、绝、胎、变成刑、害、克、破的，都降职；世爻、官星落空而发动的，将要失去职位；官星变成退神（括号里是退神的四个干支）。
  In office, salary, impeachment and demotion. If Wealth turns into Output, his salary and rank are raised; if Wealth turns into a Peer line, his salary and grain are docked; if the Month and Day punish, harm, control or break the Pressure line or the Self line, or the Noble and star of office move to injure the Body and Self, he is impeached; if the star of office and the seal meet Void, death, tomb, extinction or the womb stage, or are punished, harmed, controlled or broken, or move and turn into Output, or into Void, death, tomb, extinction, the womb stage, punishment, harm, control or breaking, he is demoted; if the Self and the star of office are Void and moving, he is about to lose his post; the star of office turning into a Retreating spirit is also listed, followed in brackets by the four ganzhi of the Retreating spirit.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.20** 在任，退休、避罪和死在任上：游魂卦变归魂卦的，适宜退休（致仕）；世爻落空、凶煞发动的，适宜避罪；世爻持官星加大杀发动，或者官鬼带亡神、劫杀发动伤到衰弱的世爻，或者世爻、身爻、命爻都遇空、死、墓、绝的，都会死在任所。
  In office, retirement, evading a charge and dying at one's post. If a Wandering Soul hexagram turns into a Returning Soul hexagram, it is fitting to retire from office; if the Self is Void and a baleful spirit moves, it is fitting to evade a charge; if the Self holds the star of office and a great baleful spirit is added and moves, or Pressure carrying the Lost Spirit and Robbery moves to injure a weak Self, or the Self line, the Body line and the Life line are all Void, dead, in tomb or extinct, in every case he dies at his post.
- **LY.CAREER.YY.30** 丁忧起复，遭父母之丧和重新起用：财爻带白衣杀，或财爻加白虎杀，又会合月建，发动来刑克父母爻的，阳爻是遭父亲的丧，阴爻是遭母亲的丧；二爻、五爻（二爻是母，五爻是父）带白衣杀旺相发动，伤世爻、克官星的，二爻是母亲的丧事，五爻是父亲的丧事；后来遇到生合官星、印星的年月，必定起复，或者官星、印星生旺的年月起复做官。
  Mourning and recall. If the Wealth line carries the White Garment baleful spirit, or Wealth carries the Tiger spirit and meets the Month, and moves to punish or control the Resource line, then if it is yang he is in mourning for his father and if yin for his mother; if the second or fifth line (the second is the mother and the fifth the father) carries the White Garment spirit, thriving and moving, and injures the Self and controls the star of office, the second line means mourning for the mother and the fifth line mourning for the father; later, in the year or month that generates or combines with the star of office and seal, he is surely recalled, or in the year or month when the star of office and seal are born or thriving he is recalled to office.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.14** 日月来冲克，在任上多招诽谤：日月克官星或克世爻，或者朱雀、螣蛇克世而世爻休囚，招来的祸不小；世爻旺相、有敕命的，也多招是非，要检点、勤勉谨慎处理政事，以免过失；官鬼克世爻，但世旺官衰的，也主有诽谤；世爻衰弱、官鬼旺相，如果又临螣蛇、朱雀，必定被弹劾。
  When the Day and Month clash with or control, slander is much invited in office: if the Day and Month control the star of office or the Self, or the Vermilion Bird or the Coiling Snake controls the Self while the Self is resting or trapped, the misfortune is not slight; if the Self is thriving and one holds an imperial edict there is still much gossip and trouble, so one should be careful and diligent and prudent in affairs of government to avoid fault; if Pressure controls the Self though the Self is thriving and Pressure is weak, there is slander too; if the Self is weak and Pressure thriving and it also sits on the Coiling Snake or the Vermilion Bird, one will surely be named in an impeachment.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.17** 世爻被破、官鬼落空，当官不会长久；世爻或官星被破、落空，在任上待不了几天；世爻衰弱发动变出官鬼，以及变出回头克的，要防备寿命不牢靠。
  If the Self is broken and Pressure is Void, one will not hold office long; if the Self or the star of office is broken or Void, one cannot stay long in the post; if a weak Self moves and turns into Pressure, or into a returning control, guard against a life that is not firm.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.21** 财爻持世，或者卦中财爻发动，或者父母爻休囚、落空、被破的，如果家里有尊长亲人，不要只当财来断，要防备孝服（父母之丧）。
  If the Wealth line holds the Self or moves in the hexagram, or the Resource line is resting or trapped, Void or broken, then if one has elders and parents, do not judge it as wealth alone, but guard against mourning dress (the death of a parent).
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.30** 财爻持世或发动，会克破文书，是没有益处的奏章；但如果父母持世，财和官一起发动，又是好的，因为财动生官、官动生世。
  If the Wealth line holds the Self or moves, it controls and breaks the document, and the memorial is of no use; but if Resource holds the Self and Wealth and the star of office move together, that is good again, because Wealth moves to generate the star of office and the star of office moves to generate the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.31** 上书陈奏时子孙持世或发动：子孙是克官星的神，有官职的人上奏陈事，不宜见到它，轻的降职受罚，重的罢官；没有官职的人要看来占者心里的念头：本来是为了求功名的，见子孙必定不成名；担心会有祸患的，见子孙必定没有祸。
  When Output holds the Self or moves in a memorial: Output is the spirit that controls the star of office, and for an official with a post who submits a memorial it is not good to see it: at the lightest there is demotion and punishment, at the worst dismissal; for a man without office one must look at the thought of the one who comes: if he does this in order to seek a name, seeing Output means he will surely not gain a name; if he fears that it may bring him misfortune, seeing Output means there will surely be none.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.32** 上书时世爻临空、临破，难以挽回圣意：世爻临旬空、月破，以及临绝、变绝、入三墓的，得不到批准，应当早点察觉时机；世爻发动变凶，或者太岁、五爻、日月、动爻冲克世爻的，不但难以挽回，而且要防备想不到的祸患。
  In a memorial, if the Self falls on Void or break, it is hard to turn heaven round: if the Self falls on Void or Month-break, or on extinction or turns into extinction, or enters the three tombs, the request will not be approved, and one should read the moment early; if the Self moves and turns bad, or Tai Sui, the fifth line, the Day, the Month or a moving line clashes with or controls the Self, it is not only hard to turn heaven round but one must guard against unforeseen misfortune.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.34** 上书最忌官爻克世爻，也嫌弃帮助官鬼伤害自身：官鬼克世已经预兆凶象，再加上财发动来帮助官鬼的，灾祸难免；世爻变出官鬼、变出回头克、随官鬼入墓、卦得反吟、卦变成绝、世爻被冲克的，事情如果不是重大的，要赶紧停止。
  In a memorial what is most to be avoided is the Pressure line controlling the Self, and also disliked is helping Pressure to injure the Body: when Pressure controls the Self it already foretells ill, and if Wealth also moves to help Pressure the misfortune cannot be avoided; if the Self turns into Pressure, or into a returning control, or follows Pressure into the tomb, or the hexagram gets Reversed Chant or changes into extinction, or the Self is clashed or controlled, unless the matter is grave it should be stopped at once.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.35** 太岁生世必须在卦爻里出现才算数，不在卦爻中就生不了世；五爻生世当作吉的，也要防它受克、落空、衰弱、被破、入绝，有生的名义没有生的实际；六爻恍惚不定就不要上书。野鹤说：真有益于国的事，舍身为国也不辞，不必占卜；事情可做可不做之间的，必须世爻与父爻都没有刑冲克破，而且太岁、五爻、日月相生，才可以做；爻中乱动、用神元神非旺非衰、克多生少的，就应当停止。
  For Tai Sui to generate the Self it must appear among the lines, and if it is not among the lines it cannot generate the Self; taking the fifth line generating the Self as auspicious, one must also guard against its being controlled, Void, weak, broken or extinct, which would give the name of generation and not the substance; if the six lines are vague and unsettled, do not submit the memorial. The Old Man of Yehe says: if the matter truly benefits the state one gives oneself for the country and refuses nothing, and there is no need of divination; for a matter that could go either way, it may be done only if the Self and the Resource line are free of punishment, clash, control and break and Tai Sui, the fifth line, the Day and the Month generate them; if the lines move in disorder, the Subject and Support are neither thriving nor weak, or controls outnumber generations, it should be stopped.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.37** 为求功名而上书，忌讳子孙发动，忌讳兄弟临身：父母持世、文书再旺，只要子孙一动伤官，为名的人文书旺也没用；旺相的文书生世、但兄弟持世，上司的奏章可以上去，自己的钱财终究要破，销账不准。
  When one submits a memorial to seek a name, Output moving is to be avoided and Peer on the Body is to be avoided: if Resource holds the Self and the document is extremely thriving, once Output moves to injure the star of office a thriving document is of no use to one who seeks a name; if a thriving document generates the Self but Peer holds the Self, the superior's memorial goes up but one's own money is bound to be lost and the write-off is not approved.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.38** 防备被参劾、担心大计考核、案子还没结的人：世爻与官星都旺相，再有日月、动爻生扶，或者世爻、官爻临在日月上，或者发动变吉，就没有什么可忧虑的：防参劾的无忧，已被参劾而未结案的无忧，担心大计的不但无忧，还一定被评为卓异；凡是旺相的官星持世，也同样推断。
  For one who guards against impeachment, fears the great evaluation, or has a case not yet closed: if the Self and the star of office are both thriving, and the Day, Month or a moving line also generates and supports them, or the Self or the Pressure line sits on the Day or Month, or they move and turn auspicious, there is nothing to fear: one guarding against impeachment has nothing to fear, one already impeached whose case is not closed has nothing to fear, and one who fears the great evaluation not only has nothing to fear but will surely be rated outstanding; whenever a thriving star of office holds the Self, judge in the same way.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.39** 子孙持世、子孙在卦中发动、世爻发动变出子孙，官星被破或落空，以及发动变出回头克，都主被罢职、休官。
  If Output holds the Self, or Output moves in the hexagram, or the Self moves and turns into Output, or the star of office is broken or Void, or it moves and turns into a returning control, all mean dismissal and removal from office.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.40** 被参劾的情形：子孙发动、财落空，减俸、罚俸；兄弟持世或发动，或者财被破、财落空，以及财发动变凶，如果官爻旺相，不过罚俸而已；官星如果失陷，那么利益和功名都丧失了。
  In the case of impeachment: with Output moving and Wealth Void, there is a cut in salary and a fine; if Peer holds the Self or moves, or Wealth is broken or Void, or Wealth moves and turns bad, then if the Pressure line is thriving it comes to no more than a fine; if the star of office is lost, both gain and rank are lost.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.41** 世爻衰弱、官鬼来克，遭贬责、受凌辱。世爻衰弱又得不到生扶，又有太岁、五爻、日月、动爻刑冲克世，或者官星发动克世，或者世爻随官鬼入墓，世爻发动变鬼、变克，以及白虎、螣蛇刑克世爻的，轻的贬官受罚，重的坐牢。轻重要看世爻有没有救：世爻有救，是轻的；世爻休囚又被刑伤冲克，是重的。
  With the Self weak and Pressure controlling it, there is demotion, reproof and abuse. If the Self is weak and gets no generation or support, and Tai Sui, the fifth line, the Day, the Month or a moving line punishes, clashes with or controls the Self, or the star of office moves to control the Self, or the Self follows Pressure into the tomb, or the Self moves and turns into Pressure or into a control, or the White Tiger or the Coiling Snake punishes or controls the Self, then at the lightest there is demotion and punishment, at the worst prison. Light or heavy depends on whether the Self has a rescue: it is light when the Self has a rescue, and heavy when the Self is resting or trapped and is also punished, injured, clashed or controlled.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.43** 世爻失陷但遇到生扶，凶煞兴旺也没有妨碍。古人有「身空煞动是避祸的征兆」的说法，增删卜易认为不对：忌神在卦中发动，世爻落空，等到世爻出空的月份日子，必定遭它的害，怎么能避开？只有世爻落空而遇到元神发动来生它，卦中即使有忌神发动也没有妨碍，因为等到世爻出空那天，忌神反而去生元神，元神再生世爻。
  If the Self is lost but meets generation, there is no harm even when baleful spirits flourish. The ancients said 'a Void Body and a moving baleful spirit are a sign of avoiding disaster', which the Zeng Shan Bu Yi holds to be wrong: when the Adversary moves in the hexagram and the Self is Void, in the month or on the day the Self leaves the Void it will surely be harmed, so how could it avoid it? Only when the Self is Void and meets the Support moving to generate it is there no harm even though the Adversary moves, because by the day the Self leaves the Void the Adversary instead generates the Support and the Support generates the Self.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.44** 官星兴旺而世爻失陷，自身受辱但官位还在：凡是遇到凶兆，如果官爻持世，或者官星伏在世爻下面而且旺相，虽然受到责罚，官职还保留（古说：身边伏鬼若不落空，官职还在）。野鹤说：世爻被五爻所克，只要官星旺相、生合世爻，或者旺相的官星持世，虽然遭到羞辱，仍然会有官职。
  With the star of office flourishing and the Self lost, one is humiliated but keeps the office: whenever an ill omen comes, if the Pressure line holds the Self, or the star of office lies hidden under the Self and is thriving, one is censured and punished but the office remains (the old saying: if Pressure lies hidden beside the Body and is not Void, the office remains). The Old Man of Yehe says: if the Self is controlled by the fifth line but the star of office is thriving and generates or combines with the Self, or a thriving star of office holds the Self, one meets disgrace but still has office.
- **LY.CAREER.ZS.45** 自身实在、官星失陷，官位丢了但人平安：世爻旺相、不受伤克，而官星被破、落空，或被刑冲克害，或发动变凶，官职俸禄虽然丢了，人不会受伤害。
  With the Body solid and the star of office lost, the post goes but the person is safe: if the Self is thriving and not injured or controlled, while the star of office is broken or Void, or punished, clashed, controlled or harmed, or moves and turns bad, the office and salary are lost but the person is not harmed.
- **LY.CAREER.ZSZ.05** 遇到履卦（莫问官方）、大过卦（求官不达）、蹇卦（官贵艰难）、归妹卦（贵官休问）、小过卦（官阻病忧）、坤卦（功名特远），求官不利，要安分守常。
  Meeting the hexagram Lü ('do not ask about official business'), Daguo ('seeking office does not succeed'), Jian ('officials and nobles are hard to reach'), Guimei ('do not ask about nobles or office'), Xiaoguo ('office is blocked and illness is a worry') or Kun ('fame and rank are especially distant'), seeking office is unfavourable and one should keep to one's lot.
